TL;DR: 本文提出Region Token Interface (RTI)方法，通过希尔伯特排序将图像补丁排列成一维序列，在特征变化最大处切割并将连续区域合并为区域令牌，从而减少扩散模型在平坦区域的令牌浪费。该方法在匹配预算下优于现有缩减方法，在2.0×加速下保持密集质量，在2.6×加速下接近密集质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然图像的大部分细节集中在图像的小部分区域，但扩散模型在每个补丁、每个层、每个时间步都花费完整的令牌。像素空间模型没有自动编码器来先吸收低级冗余，导致大量计算浪费在平坦区域。

Method: RTI方法首先对图像补丁进行希尔伯特排序，使连续位置始终是图像邻居，从而任意连续片段都是连通区域。然后在模型特征变化最大的位置进行切割，将每个连续区域池化为一个区域令牌。在微调期间随机抽取区域数量，使单个检查点适用于任意计算预算。

Result: RTI在匹配预算下优于现有缩减方法（相似性合并、潜在瓶颈、跳过等方法），在2.0×加速下达到密集质量，在2.6×加速下仍能保持接近密集的质量。

Conclusion: 通过利用图像内容的空间连续性，以几何感知的方式合并冗余令牌，RTI有效地减少了像素空间扩散模型的计算浪费，同时保持生成质量。

Abstract: Natural images concentrate their detail in a small fraction of the frame, yet diffusion models spend a full token on every patch, in every layer and at every timestep. The waste is largest in pixel-space models, with no autoencoder to absorb low-level redundancy first. Probing a pretrained pixel text-to-image transformer, we find its middle-block tokens redundant wherever the image is flat. The redundancy occupies connected, content-shaped regions, and exploiting it requires tokens with the same geometry. Cutting a Hilbert ordering of the patches provides them. Consecutive positions are always image neighbours, so any contiguous run is a connected region whose size and shape follow the content, and grouping in two dimensions becomes a cut in one. Existing reductions each lose part of this. Similarity merging scatters its groups, latent bottlenecks discard position, and skipping deletes what it should summarize. We cut where the model's features change most and pool each run into one region token. Our Region Token Interface (\method{}) adapts a diffusion model to these tokens, with the region count drawn at random during fine-tuning so one checkpoint serves every budget. \method{} leads prior reduction methods at matched budgets, matches dense quality at $2.0\times$ the speed, and stays close at $2.6\times$. The code and models are open-sourced at https://eduardzamfir.github.io/rti

</details>


### [89] [3D-MRL: Nested Multimodal 3D Representations via Matryoshka Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29285)
*Márcus Lobo,Vitor Matias,Jeová Farias,Moacir Ponti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出3D-MRL框架，基于Matryoshka表示学习实现对点云计算的多模态3D预训练，通过将点云与冻结的CLIP图像和文本嵌入对齐，并在多个嵌入维度上施加对比监督，使单一模型能够无需重新训练即可生成不同维度的3D表示。

在Objaverse-LVIS、ModelNet40和ScanNet数据集上的实验表明，3D-MRL在零样本和少样本3D识别任务上取得了竞争性性能，并在Objaverse-LVIS上将Top-1准确率从46.8%提升至50.9%。此外，该学习到的表示支持在单一模型内跨不同嵌入维度进行检索，且不同维度能够产生不同层次的语义和几何特异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态3D预训练方法生成固定维度嵌入，需要为不同计算预算分别训练单独模型，缺乏灵活性和效率。

Method: 提出3D-MRL框架，基于Matryoshka表示学习，将点云与冻结的CLIP图像和文本嵌入对齐，在多个嵌入维度上施加对比监督。Matryoshka目标仅应用于3D编码器，使单一模型可生成不同维度的表示而无需重新训练。

Result: 在Objaverse-LVIS、ModelNet40和ScanNet数据集上，零样本和少样本识别任务取得竞争性性能；在Objaverse-LVIS上Top-1准确率从46.8%提升至50.9%；支持跨维度检索且不同维度体现不同层次的语义与几何特异性。

Conclusion: 3D-MRL通过Matryoshka表示学习实现了灵活高效的多模态3D预训练，在保持识别性能的同时提供多维度嵌入能力，为不同计算预算下的3D理解任务提供了统一的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models align point clouds with image and text embeddings, enabling zero-shot recognition, retrieval, and open-vocabulary understanding of 3D shapes. Existing multimodal 3D pre-training methods produce fixed-dimensional embeddings, requiring separate models for different computational budgets. We propose 3D Matryoshka Representation Learning (3D-MRL), a multimodal 3D pre-training framework based on Matryoshka Representation Learning. 3D-MRL learns nested 3D representations by aligning point clouds with frozen CLIP image and text embeddings while applying contrastive supervision across multiple embedding dimensions. The Matryoshka objective is applied only to the 3D encoder, allowing a single model to produce representations at different dimensionalities without retraining. Experiments on the Objaverse-LVIS, ModelNet40, and ScanNet datasets show that 3D-MRL achieves competitive performance on zero-shot and few-shot 3D recognition tasks. In addition, the learned representations support retrieval across different embedding dimensions within a single model. On Objaverse-LVIS, 3D-MRL improves Top-1 accuracy from 46.8% to 50.9%. Retrieval experiments further show that different embedding dimensions yield varying levels of semantic and geometric specificity.

</details>


### [90] [PERSIST: Persistent-State Discrimination for Shot Boundary Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29287)
*Tingyu Lin,Christian Stippel,Armin Dadras,Jakob Zenzmaier,Florian Kleber,Wolfgang Aigner,Robert Sablatnig*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PERSIST框架，将镜头边界检测（SBD）从局部视觉不连续检测重构为边界语义判别，通过FiLM条件化正弦表示网络和结构化判别器（结合局部变化、瞬态冲激、回归趋势三个语义线索）检验持续变化，有效消除手持抖动、光照闪烁、运动模糊等假阳性，在各项基准上达到与最强公开检测器相当的性能，同时大幅降低伪事件误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SBD方法将镜头边界视为局部视觉不连续点，但大量假阳性（如手持抖动、光照闪烁、运动模糊、遮挡、档案材料损伤）同样产生尖锐局部变化却并未引入新镜头，导致检测精度受限。

Method: 将SBD重构为边界语义判别问题，核心标准是"持续性检验"：仅当局部变化伴随视频潜在时间状态的持续更新时才判定为边界，而非暂时性偏离又回归趋势。通过FiLM条件化正弦表示网络构建连续潜在状态，并采用结构化判别器在双速率时间骨干上将局部变化、瞬态冲激、回归趋势三个语义线索融合为单一可解释的逐帧信号。

Result: 在2,727视频的分类型诊断中，相较同训练的目标检测器，PERSIST消除了33-80%的闪光、文字叠加和档案假阳性；在匹配真实转换召回率下，比TransNetV2减少约一半的伪事件误报（诊断集）和约四分之一的误报（ClipShots），同时保持召回率。在在线、广播、短视频和历史档案迁移评估中达到与最强公开检测器相当的性能，且训练条件更严格（仅使用ClipShots真实转换，对比方额外使用85%合成转换的语料）。

Conclusion: PERSIST通过持续性判别准则将SBD的每个决策变为可检查的：持久性标准被训练进分类器，逐帧效果可从门控三元组读取，学习到的潜在状态具有可测量的边界判别性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Shot boundary detection (SBD) is widely treated as the localisation of local visual discontinuities, yet many false positives such as hand-held shake, illumination flicker, motion blur, occlusion, and damaged archival material produce equally sharp local change without introducing a new shot. We reformulate SBD as boundary semantic discrimination: a frame is favoured as a boundary only when its local change evidence is accompanied by a persistent update of the video's latent temporal state, rather than a transient excursion that returns to the surrounding trend. This persistence test is operationalised with a continuous latent state from a FiLM-conditioned sinusoidal representation network and a structured discriminator that combines three semantic cues, local change, transient impulse, and return-to-trend, into a single interpretable per-frame signal over a dual-rate temporal backbone. The resulting framework, PERSIST, turns every decision into an inspectable one: the persistence criterion is trained into the classifier, its per-frame effect stays readable from the gate triple, and its learned latent state is measurably boundary-discriminative. On a 2,727-video per-subtype diagnostic it removes 33-80% of flash, text-overlay, and archival false positives relative to an identically trained cue detector, and at matched true-transition recall it roughly halves TransNetV2's pseudo-event false positives on that diagnostic and cuts its false positives on ClipShots footage by about a quarter, while preserving recall. It does so while reaching parity with the strongest public detector across online, broadcast, short-form, and historical-archive transfer evaluations, under markedly stricter training: it learns from ClipShots real transitions only, whereas the anchor draws on additional corpora whose transitions are 85% synthetic. Code is available at https://github.com/linty5/PERSIST.

</details>


### [91] [AOI-Net: Structural Face AOI-Guided Eye-Gaze Track Representation Learning for Autism Spectrum Disorder Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29289)
*Zhanpei Huang,Binbin Sun,Jialiang Chen,Yiou Wang,Taochen Chen,Yuzhu Ji,Yiqun Zhang,Yiu-Ming Cheung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AOI-Net网络，联合建模短时时间动态与AOI层级结构组织，用于自闭症谱系障碍（ASD）眼动筛查，在包含1300+参与者的大规模临床眼动数据库上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有眼动分析方法主要基于离散凝视轨迹和注视事件，仅捕捉短时时间动态，且忽略了注视行为在语义上有意义的感兴趣区域（AOI）之间的结构关系，限制了模型对长程依赖和结构线索的建模能力。

Method: 提出结构面部AOI引导的眼动轨迹网络（AOI-Net），通过网络门控机制自适应融合短时时间动态表示与AOI层级结构表示；并采用类别分布感知学习缓解ASD与典型发育个体之间的类别不平衡问题。

Result: 在包含八个刺激子集和超过1300名参与者的大规模临床眼动数据库上，AOI-Net一致优于现有先进方法，同时支持可解释的眼动行为建模。

Conclusion: AOI-Net为真实医疗场景中可扩展的AI驱动的ASD筛查提供了实用基础，且代码已开源。

Abstract: Eye-movement tracking has emerged as a promising non-invasive approach to Autism Spectrum Disorder (ASD) screening, with systematic differences in attentional allocation and revisit behaviors observed during socially interactive tasks. Existing computational methods typically characterize eye-movements using discrete gaze trajectories and fixation events, yielding representations dominated by short-range temporal dynamics and limiting models that primarily emphasize long-range dependencies. Meanwhile, gaze behavior is naturally organized across semantically meaningful Areas of Interest (AOIs), whose attention allocation and transitions provide important structural cues, yet their relationships are rarely modeled explicitly. To address these limitations, we propose a structural face AOI-guided Eye-Gaze Track Network (AOI-Net) that jointly models short-term temporal dynamics and AOI-level structural organization. A network gating mechanism adaptively integrates the complementary temporal and structural representations according to their contributions to gaze-behavior characterization. To mitigate the pronounced class imbalance commonly encountered between individuals with ASD and Typically Developing (TD) participants in clinical datasets, class-distribution-aware learning is further employed to facilitate discriminative embedding learning under skewed class distributions. Experiments on a unique and large-scale clinical eye-tracking database comprising eight stimulus subsets and more than 1,300 participants show that AOI-Net consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods. The proposed framework also enables interpretable gaze-behavior modeling and provides a practical basis for scalable AI-driven ASD screening in real-world healthcare. The code is available at https://github.com/Zhanpei-ai/CIM-AOI-Net/tree/main/Code

</details>


### [92] [Hyper3-CLIP: Hierarchy-Conditioned Hyperbolic Vision-Language Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29313)
*Matin Mahmood,Antonio Rueda-Toicen,Mohamed ElBassat,Seifeldin Elkerdany,Weixing Wang,Gerard de Melo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Hyper3-CLIP，一种层级条件化的双曲视觉语言模型，结合全局、局部和全局-局部对比学习与查询条件化的视觉池化，通过从文本构造轻量级查询层级（完整标题、句子片段、局部部件描述和提取短语）来训练，支持图像-文本、整体-部件和父-子蕴含损失，在COCO和Flickr上的R@5/R@10检索以及VOC和COCO上的多标签分类方面均有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的CLIP类视觉语言模型通过对比目标学习全局表示，但欧几里得嵌入和全局池化无法编码部分-整体和父-子等关系结构。双曲VLM虽然通过蕴含目标解决这一差距，文本条件化变体虽然改善了细粒度对齐，但这两条研究路线仍彼此分离：双曲VLM使用静态图像和区域特征，而查询条件化方法缺乏层级几何结构。

Method: Hyper3-CLIP将全局、局部和全局-局部对比学习与查询条件化的视觉池化相结合。从文本构造轻量级查询层级，包括完整标题、句子片段、局部部件描述和提取短语。每个查询条件化视觉补丁的池化过程，生成的表示支持图像-文本、整体-部件和父-子蕴含损失。查询条件化池化仅在训练期间激活。

Result: Hyper3-CLIP在COCO和Flickr上提升了R@5和R@10检索性能，在VOC和COCO上改进了多标签分类，同时保持层级度量的竞争力。还对固定提示机制下的零样本提示敏感性进行了审计，并研究了训练的局部GRIT部件预算的影响。

Conclusion: 通过将双曲几何结构与查询条件化的池化机制相结合，Hyper3-CLIP成功弥合了双曲VLM与文本条件化方法之间的鸿沟，在检索和多标签分类任务上取得提升，同时保持层级关系建模能力，证明了将层级几何与查询条件化结合的有效性。

Abstract: CLIP-like vision-language models (VLMs) trained with contrastive objectives learn strong global image-text representations, but their Euclidean embeddings and global pooling fail to encode relational structure such as part-whole and parent-child relations. Hyperbolic VLMs address this gap with entailment-based objectives, and text-conditioned variants improve fine-grained alignment through sentence- and phrase-level queries. However, these two lines of work remain separate: hyperbolic VLMs use static image and region features, while query-conditioned methods lack hierarchical geometric structure. We present Hyper3-CLIP, a hierarchy-conditioned hyperbolic VLM that combines global, local, and global-local contrastive learning with query-conditioned visual pooling. To train the model, we construct lightweight query hierarchies from text, comprising full captions, sentence fragments, localized part descriptions, and extracted phrases. Each query conditions the pooling of visual patches, and the resulting representations support image-text, whole-part, and parent-child entailment losses. Query-conditioned pooling is active only during training. Hyper3-CLIP improves R@5 and R@10 retrieval on COCO and Flickr, as well as multi-label classification on VOC and COCO, while remaining competitive on hierarchy metrics. We also audit zero-shot prompt sensitivity under fixed prompt regimes and study the effect of the localized GRIT part budget used during training. Code is available at https://github.com/Hyper3Labs/hyper3-clip.

</details>


### [93] [Test-Time Scaling for Video Diffusion Models via Diagnosis-Guided Candidate Recycling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29322)
*Hangzhou He,Lunhao Duan,Shanshan Zhao,Kaiwen Li,Qing-Guo Chen,Weihua Luo,Yanye Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GEARS是一个无需训练的框架，通过"诊断引导的候选回收"机制将视频测试时扩展（TTS）从传统的"生成即丢弃"范式转变为"生成-评估-编辑"循环，在相同NFE预算下使1.3B模型达到接近14B模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散模型依赖闭源系统或大规模基础设施，而测试时扩展方法多停留在噪声搜索范式，生成低分候选后直接丢弃，浪费了计算资源和候选样本中已编码的部分运动、布局或外观结构。

Method: GEARS包含两个协作组件：Stage-Aware Scheduler负责决策何时修复、修复什么以及哪些候选应保留、回收或丢弃；Candidate Recycler通过关键帧和多维奖励反馈诊断可恢复的失败，生成候选特定的修复提示，并通过流形感知的潜在SDEdit修复候选，将修复后的候选回收进搜索池形成精化路径。

Result: 在匹配NFE预算下，GEARS在VBench基准上持续优于现有视频TTS方法，使1.3B模型达到与14B模型相当的总分；消融实验验证了自适应调度、诊断条件编辑和流形感知重降噪的必要性。

Conclusion: 通过将低分候选转化为可编辑的先验进行回收利用，而非简单丢弃，GEARS在保持训练免费的同时显著提升了轻量视频生成模型的性能上限，为视频TTS提供了新的范式方向。

Abstract: Recent video diffusion models have achieved remarkable generation quality, but high-fidelity results still largely depend on closed-source systems or costly large-scale infrastructure. Test-time scaling (TTS) offers a training-free way to improve lightweight generators by spending additional inference compute, yet existing methods mostly remain within a noise-search paradigm: they sample, select, or perturb denoising trajectories and discard low-scoring candidates after expensive generation. This generate-and-discard process wastes not only computation but also the partial motion, layout, or appearance structure already encoded in recoverable samples. We present \textbf{GEARS} (\textbf{G}uided \textbf{E}diting for \textbf{A}daptive \textbf{R}ecycling \textbf{S}earch), a training-free framework that introduces {diagnosis-guided candidate recycling} into video TTS by turning such candidates into editable priors through a generation-evaluation-editing loop. GEARS consists of two collaborative components. The \textbf{Stage-Aware Scheduler} determines what to repair, when to repair it, and which candidates should be preserved, recycled, or discarded. The \textbf{Candidate Recycler} diagnoses recoverable failures from keyframes and multi-dimensional reward feedback, derives candidate-specific repair prompts, and repairs the corresponding candidates through manifold-aware latent SDEdit. The repaired candidates are recycled into the search pool, creating refinement paths beyond standard noise perturbation while preserving useful structure. Under matched NFE budgets, GEARS consistently outperforms existing video TTS methods on VBench, bringing a 1.3B model to a total score comparable to a 14B counterpart, and ablations verify the necessity of adaptive scheduling, diagnosis-conditioned editing, and manifold-aware re-denoising. Code is available on GitHub.

</details>


### [94] [Neural video codecs quality assessment dataset and benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29331)
*Nikolay Safonov,Nikita Gornostaev,Alexandra Dubonos,Dmitriy Vatolin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个大规模主观视频质量评估数据集，包含神经视频编解码器和传统编解码器压缩的视频，通过众包成对比较收集主观评分，旨在为神经视频编解码器的质量评估研究提供基准资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频流量占全球网页流量很大比例，神经视频编解码器（NVCs）作为新兴的深度学习压缩方法带来了新的时间压缩范式，传统质量评估方法难以充分评估其效果，因此需要专门的评估数据集和基准。

Method: 构建大规模主观数据集，包含神经和传统视频编解码器压缩的视频样本，通过众包成对比较的方式收集主观评分，并公开数据集供后续研究使用。

Result: 提出了一个公开可用的大规模主观视频质量数据集，为面向神经视频编解码器的视频质量指标开发和基准测试提供了有价值资源。

Conclusion: 该数据集可作为神经视频编解码器质量评估研究的重要基础设施，有助于推动该领域的进一步发展和改进。

Abstract: Video traffic constitutes a significant share of global web traffic. To reduce its volume, video codecs have been developed and continuously improved. While the industry has achieved substantial progress in traditional video coding, neural video codecs (NVCs) have recently emerged as a new approach that applies deep learning to video compression. This creates new challenges for compression quality assessment, which is essential for the further development and improvement of such codecs. In particular, it is important to evaluate the novel temporal compression paradigms introduced by NVCs. In this work, we present a large-scale subjective dataset of videos compressed with both neural and traditional video codecs. The subjective scores were collected through crowd-sourced pairwise comparisons. The proposed dataset provides a valuable resource for the development and benchmarking of video quality metrics tailored to neural video codecs. The dataset is available at the following link: https://videoprocessing.github.io/nvc-dataset-benchmark

</details>


### [95] [GenFirst: Generation Before Reconstruction for Stable End-to-End Latent Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29335)
*Guangting Zheng,Yiyuan Zhang,Tao Yang,Yunpeng Chen,Rui Zhu,Jiajun Deng,Yanyong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GenFirst，一种生成优先于重建的端到端潜变量生成模型训练策略。通过分析发现KL散度中的熵项对防止潜变量坍塌至关重要，且生成与重建存在不对称学习动态，因此采用先生成后重建的调度，实现了无需潜变量坍塌的直接端到端训练，在ImageNet-256和文本到图像生成上取得SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜变量生成模型通常采用两阶段流水线（先训练VAE重建，再在冻结潜空间上训练生成模型），但重建优化的潜空间不一定利于生成。端到端联合训练虽然更有吸引力，却面临潜变量坍塌和生成-重建冲突两大挑战，此前一直难以解决。

Method: 首先分析不同目标函数如何塑造潜空间，得到两个关键洞见：(1) KL散度中的熵项对防止坍塌至关重要，重建和先验拟合倾向于收缩后验，而熵保留了非退化潜变量的不确定性；(2) 重建和生成具有不对称学习动态，重建快且强监督，生成慢且难以优化。基于此提出GenFirst策略：生成目标先在弱重建压力下塑造潜空间，然后逐步加强重建以恢复视觉细节，实现无坍塌的直接端到端训练。

Result: 在ImageNet-256上，SiT达到gFID 0.97（带CFG）和1.45（无CFG）；MMDiT在文本到图像生成上达到GenEval 0.90。框架还扩展到共享视觉潜变量的生成与表征学习，以及连续统一文本-图像生成。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明了稳定端到端潜变量学习在各类生成先验和模态上的通用性，验证了GenFirst策略和熵项防止坍塌的有效性。

Abstract: Latent generative models typically follow a two-stage pipeline, training a variational autoencoder for reconstruction and then a generative model on the frozen latent space. Since reconstruction-optimized latents are not necessarily generation-friendly, jointly training both models is an appealing alternative. However, direct end-to-end training remains challenging, as it is prone to latent collapse and faces a generation-reconstruction conflict. We revisit this problem by analyzing how different objectives shape the latent space and identify two key insights. First, the entropy term in the Kullback-Leibler divergence objective is essential for preventing collapse: reconstruction and prior fitting tend to shrink the posterior, while entropy preserves non-degenerate latent uncertainty. Second, reconstruction and generation exhibit asymmetric learning dynamics: reconstruction is fast and strongly supervised, whereas generation is slower and harder to optimize. Based on these insights, we achieve the first direct end-to-end training without latent collapse and propose GenFirst, a simple generation-before-reconstruction strategy. The generative objective first shapes the latent space under weak reconstruction pressure, after which reconstruction is progressively strengthened to recover visual details. We validate GenFirst with continuous autoregressive priors with exact likelihoods and SiT priors with implicit likelihoods. With our end-to-end objective and GenFirst, SiT achieves a gFID of 0.97 with CFG and 1.45 without CFG on ImageNet-256, while MMDiT reaches a GenEval score of 0.90 on text-to-image generation. Beyond image generation, we extend the framework to shared visual latents for generation and representation learning, and to continuous unified text-image generation. These results demonstrate the generality of stable end-to-end latent learning across generative priors and modalities.

</details>


### [96] [FISICA: A Deployed Service for Plantar-Pressure and Posture Assessment with Ontology-Grounded Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29336)
*Juhwan Song,Heejung Kim,Juntae Noh,Jonghak Ryu,Huiju Park,Junseong Lee,Dohyeon Ahn,Byungwoo Jo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FISICA是一个已投入生产的身体评估与推荐服务系统，通过站立会话中的两张照片提供足底负荷测量、姿势坐标、3D虚拟化身、视觉报告以及鞋类和运动推荐。系统使用定制刻度板（1cm网格上含634个力敏元件和4个称重传感器）进行测量，基于规则的评估器控制所有推荐，语言模型仅负责解释结果。方法核心创新在于虚拟化身生成——不是将测量角度映射到骨架，而是用与受试者相同的函数测量化身并迭代求解直至两者一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有身体评估系统在将测量数据映射到3D化身时通常采用调谐增益（tuned gain），导致测量精度损失；同时推荐服务需要兼顾实时性、可靠性和可解释性，纯AI模型难以保证结果可信。

Method: 采用规则驱动与语言模型解释相结合的架构（rule-based evaluator控制推荐，LLM仅解释）。核心方法创新在于化身生成算法：使用与受试者相同的度量函数测量虚拟化身，通过迭代求解使两者在采样不变的脊柱度量上达成一致，取代传统的单关节增益映射方式。

Result: 在脊柱度量上，该方法将正常与脊柱后凸记录的区分度提升至7.2度（对比单关节公式的0.9度）。生产环境中通用API中位响应时间0.023s，足底压力分析0.45s，推荐服务2.16-2.26s（规则部分每次均在1秒内）。关键点图在公共数据上达到0.960 PCK@0.2。目录包含699款鞋和10,500条类型化事实。

Conclusion: FISICA验证了规则驱动+LLM解释的生产级身体评估架构的可行性，其采样不变的化身度量方法显著提升了姿势区分精度，系统各项性能指标满足实时生产需求；放射学参考验证研究已获批，后续验证工作即将开展。

Abstract: FISICA is a body-assessment and recommendation service running in production. One standing session with two photographs returns foot-loading measures, posture coordinates, a driven 3D avatar, a visual report, and ranked shoe and exercise candidates. Measurement comes from a purpose-built scale carrying 634 force-sensitive elements on a 1 cm grid and four load cells, and a rule-based evaluator controls every recommendation while a language model only explains the stored result. The method contribution is the avatar. Instead of mapping a measured angle onto a rig through a tuned gain, we measure the avatar with the same function used on the subject and solve until the two agree, on a sampling-invariant spinal metric that separated a normal from a kyphotic record by 7.2 degrees against 0.9 degrees for a single-joint formulation. In production, general APIs respond at a 0.023 s median, plantar-pressure analysis at 0.45 s, and recommendation at 2.16 s to 2.26 s with the rule-based portion under one second in every trial. The served keypoint graph reaches 0.960 PCK@0.2 on public data, and the catalog holds 699 shoes with 10,500 typed facts. An approved study supplies the radiographic reference for the validation still ahead.

</details>


### [97] [GSPotential: Camera Potential Field for Sparse-View 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29346)
*Zeyuan An,Yanghang Xiao,Zhiying Leng,Yijun Feng,Xiaohui Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GSPotential框架，通过相机势场量化视图空间的监督不平衡性，识别监督不足的区域，并从两个互补角度引导3D高斯溅射重建：一是概率球形采样策略在低势区域放置虚拟相机提供几何引导，二是利用方向覆盖线索对弱覆盖空间区域进行保守的高斯更新。实验表明该方法在不牺牲训练效率的前提下实现了高重建保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D高斯溅射在稀疏视图场景下因光度监督不足而严重过拟合并产生几何伪影。现有方法引入外部先验（如深度、点云、扩散模型）进行正则化，但忽略了监督在视图空间中的非均匀分布，导致先验利用和基元控制缺乏针对性。

Method: 提出相机势场（Camera Potential Field）来量化视图空间监督不平衡性，识别"监督低谷"区域。在此基础上，设计两个互补机制：1）概率球形采样策略，在低势区域放置信息丰富的虚拟相机，利用其点云渲染提供定向几何引导；2）利用势场提供的方向覆盖线索，对弱覆盖空间区域执行保守的高斯参数更新。

Result: 大量实验证明，GSPotential在保持具有竞争力的训练效率的同时，实现了较高的重建保真度，有效缓解了稀疏视图下的过拟合和几何伪影问题。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模视图空间的监督不平衡性并加以利用，GSPotential为稀疏视图3D高斯溅射提供了一种高效且有效的正则化方案，验证了监督分布感知在三维重建中的重要性。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting has achieved remarkable success in photorealistic rendering, yet it suffers from severe overfitting and geometric artifacts in sparse-view scenarios due to the inherent deficiency of photometric supervision. Recent advances have attempted to regularize optimization by incorporating external priors, such as depth, point clouds, or diffusion models. However, these methods typically overlook the non-uniform distribution of supervision across the viewing space, resulting in limited specificity in prior use and primitive control. In this paper, we propose GSPotential, a framework that quantifies view-space supervision imbalance using a Camera Potential Field. Our key insight is to identify supervision valleys where photometric constraints are most deficient, and use the potential field to guide reconstruction from two complementary aspects. First, we devise a probabilistic spherical sampling strategy that places informative virtual cameras in low-potential regions. Point-cloud renderings from these views then provide targeted geometric guidance. Second, the same field provides a directional coverage cue for conservative Gaussian updates in weakly covered spatial sectors. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GSPotential achieves high reconstruction fidelity while maintaining competitive training efficiency.

</details>


### [98] [Sketch2Inspire: Structure-Sensitive Evaluation for Product Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29364)
*Ge Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Sketch2Inspire，一个用于早期产品设计检索的诊断性资源，它基于Amazon Berkeley Objects的策划子集构建，包含文本查询、基于边缘的草图代理查询以及文本-草图融合查询。通过人类评分参考协议，该资源区分了类别级检索与结构敏感的类别内检索。实验表明，多模态晚期融合在所有相关性定义下均取得最佳性能（nDCG最高达0.9962），但检索增益取决于相关性如何定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有产品图像资源和通用图像-文本检索基准很少能将类别检索与类别内结构匹配分开评估，而早期产品设计检索需要同时匹配语义意图和粗略结构线索，现有方法无法满足这一需求。

Method: 从Amazon Berkeley Objects中构建策划子集，配备对齐的文本查询、基于边缘的草图代理查询和文本-草图融合查询；将检索任务分为类别级检索和结构敏感类别内检索；采用基于预训练CLIP系列编码器的轻量级参考系统，比较仅文本检索、仅草图检索、加权晚期融合和文本优先重排序等策略。

Result: 在广泛相关性下，晚期融合获得最高nDCG=0.9962；在自动结构敏感相关性下，晚期融合获得最高nDCG=0.7015，超过仅文本检索的0.5912；在人工评分结果中，晚期融合获得最高nDCG@10=0.9133，仅文本检索位居第二（0.9030）。

Conclusion: 多模态输入的检索增益取决于相关性的定义方式。Sketch2Inspire为评估模态贡献提供了诊断资源，支持开发具有独立人工标注的结构感知产品检索协议。

Abstract: Early-stage product design retrieval often requires more than category recognition: designers may need reference examples that match both a short semantic intent and a rough structural cue. Existing product-image resources and generic image--text retrieval benchmarks rarely separate category retrieval from within-category structural fit. We present Sketch2Inspire, built from a curated subset of Amazon Berkeley Objects with aligned text queries, edge-based sketch-proxy queries, and fused text--sketch queries. The resource separates broad category-level retrieval from structure-sensitive within-category retrieval and includes a human-graded reference protocol for calibration. We evaluate a lightweight reference system based on pretrained CLIP-family encoders, comparing text-only retrieval, sketch-only retrieval, weighted late fusion, and text-first reranking without updating model weights. Under broad relevance, late fusion obtains the highest score (nDCG = 0.9962). Under automatic structure-sensitive relevance, late fusion again obtains the highest score (nDCG = 0.7015), exceeding text-only retrieval (nDCG = 0.5912). In the human-graded results, late fusion obtains the highest nDCG@10 (0.9133), while text-only retrieval ranks second (0.9030). These results show that the retrieval gain from multimodal input depends on how relevance is defined. Sketch2Inspire therefore provides a diagnostic resource for evaluating modality contribution and supports the development of structure-aware product-retrieval protocols with independent human annotation.

</details>


### [99] [Think, Look, and Revise: Inconsistency-Aware Visual Self-Correction in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29374)
*Yu Cheng,Arushi Goel,Hakan Bilen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ReVISE框架，通过为多模态大语言模型配备验证和动态错误恢复机制，解决工具增强推理中工具输出不可靠的问题，在多个基准上取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具增强的多模态推理方法很少验证工具输出，无法检测工具失败并从错误中恢复，限制了复杂视觉任务中的推理可靠性。

Method: ReVISE包含两部分：(1) 构建训练数据集，监督模型的反思行为，使其能够验证工具证据、在视觉不匹配时重新表述查询、在工具不可靠时回退到内在规则；(2) 基于强化学习的目标奖励机制，鼓励内部反思并惩罚空间错位。

Result: 在多个基准上的实验表明，ReVISE相较现有方法取得了一致的性能提升，验证了错误检测与纠正在工具增强多模态推理中的重要性。

Conclusion: 在工具增强的多模态推理中引入验证和错误恢复机制能显著提升模型表现，为未来设计更可靠的工具使用策略提供了方向。

Abstract: Tool-augmented multimodal reasoning integrates external tools (e.g., object detection, depth estimation) into multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to address perceptual bottlenecks in complex visual tasks. However, existing approaches rarely verify tool outputs, limiting their ability to detect and recover from tool failures. We propose ReVISE, a framework that equips MLLMs with verification and dynamic error recovery for tool-augmented reasoning. ReVISE introduces (1) a curated training dataset that supervises reflective behaviors, enabling models to validate tool-derived evidence, reformulate queries when visual mismatches arise, and fall back to intrinsic grounding when external tools are unreliable; and (2) a reinforcement learning based targeted rewards that encourage internal reflection and penalize spatial misalignment. Experiments on several benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over existing methods, highlighting the importance of error detection and correction in tool-augmented multimodal reasoning.

</details>


### [100] [FiLM-GPNet: Geometry-Aware Pseudo-Supervised Phase Restoration with Zero-Shot Generalization for Large Temporal InSAR Stacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29384)
*Getnet Demil,Muhammad Farhan Humayun,Tomi Westerlund,Jukka Heikkonen,Mourad Oussalah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FiLM-GPNet，一种基于几何条件的地理自适应网络，用于SAR干涉相位恢复。通过特征级线性调制和7D几何描述符显式适配采集几何差异，在三个Capella数据集上与Goldstein基线相比，时间残差降低66-68%，闭合误差降低10-13%，并展现出良好的零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集商用SAR时间序列使得时序InSAR分析成为可能，但固定的经典滤波器在异构采集几何条件下表现不佳，导致相位质量下降和时间一致性受损。现有方法缺乏对采集几何差异的显式建模。

Method: 提出FiLM-GPNet，采用特征级线性调制（FiLM）机制和7D逐对几何描述符，使网络能够显式适应采集几何差异。通过Goldstein滤波干涉图的伪监督进行训练，利用三重闭合一致性进行干涉物理正则化，同时估计逐像素偶然不确定性。

Result: 在IEEE GRSS 2026数据融合竞赛的三个Capella数据集上，FiLM-GPNet相对Goldstein基线将时间残差降低68%（夏威夷）和66%（西澳大利亚），闭合误差分别降低10%和13%。在西澳大利亚地区，解缠成功率和DEM NMAD分别提升7.7个百分点和31%。在洛杉矶堆栈上展现出无需重训练的强零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 几何条件化相位恢复可作为跨异构堆栈固定经典滤波的有效替代方案，在相位质量和时间一致性方面均取得显著提升，且具备跨区域泛化能力。

Abstract: The growing availability of dense commercial Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) time series enables temporal Interferometric SAR (InSAR) analysis, but fixed classical filters fail under heterogeneous acquisition geometries, degrading phase quality and temporal consistency. We propose FiLM-GPNet, a geometry-conditioned network for wrapped-phase restoration that explicitly adapts to acquisition differences using Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM) and a 7D per-pair geometry descriptor. The model is trained with pseudo-supervision from Goldstein-filtered interferograms and regularized by interferometric physics via triplet-closure consistency, while also estimating per-pixel aleatoric uncertainty. Experiments on three Capella Spotlight stacks from the IEEE GRSS 2026 Data Fusion Contest show that FiLM-GPNet reduces temporal residual by 68% (Hawaii) and 66% (Western Australia) relative to the Goldstein baseline, alongside closure error reductions of 10% and 13%, respectively. In Western Australia, it further improves unwrapping success rate by 7.7 percentage points and Digital Elevation Model (DEM) Normalized Median Absolute Deviation (NMAD) by 31%. The model also shows strong zero-shot generalization to a geographically and geometrically distinct third stack (Los Angeles) without retraining, supporting geometry-conditioned restoration as an effective alternative to fixed classical filtering across heterogeneous stacks.

</details>


### [101] [A Visual Question Answering Model to Automate Nondestructive Evaluation Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29408)
*Mehrdad Shafiei Dizaji,Hoda Azari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种专门用于无损评估（NDE）应用的视觉问答（VQA）模型，将ResNet-50图像特征提取与GPT-2语言生成相结合，使检测人员能够通过自然语言问答方式交互式查询NDE图像（如询问是否存在裂纹或缺陷位置），从而提升检测效率并减少人为错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统NDE检测依赖人工判读图像，效率低且容易出错。VQA模型可以让检测人员通过自然语言直接交互式查询图像内容，获得精准答复，从而提升检测效率、降低错误率并增强实际场景中的可用性。

Method: 该系统采用深度学习与自然语言处理相结合的技术路线：使用ResNet-50模型进行图像特征提取，使用GPT-2模型进行语言生成，将视觉与语言两种模态整合到一个VQA框架中，实现对NDE图像的问答交互。

Result: 该VQA模型能够准确回答针对NDE图像提出的问题（如是否存在裂纹、缺陷位置在哪里），提供精确且信息丰富的反馈，在检测效率和准确性方面均有提升。

Conclusion: 所提出的VQA模型通过交互式问答机制显著改善了NDE检测流程，减少了潜在错误，提升了实际现场场景的可用性，为无损评估领域提供了新的智能化检测手段。

Abstract: This study introduces a Visual Question Answering model designed specifically for nondestructive evaluation applications. VQA models allow inspectors to interactively query NDE images, asking targeted questions like, Is there a crack or Where is the defect located and receive precise answers from the model. Leveraging deep learning and natural language processing, the developed system integrates image feature extraction (via a ResNet-50 model) and language generation capabilities (via GPT-2) to provide accurate, informative feedback. By enabling direct question-and-answer interactions, this VQA model significantly improves inspection efficiency, reduces potential errors, and enhances usability in practical field scenarios.

</details>


### [102] [Polis: 3D Self-Supervision at City Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29426)
*Alexander Rusnak,Sophia Kovalenko,Jingru Wang,Ismail Moudden,Xiru Wang,Frédéric Kaplan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Polis，首次将Sketched Isotropic Gaussian Regularization (SIGReg)应用于原生点云编码器，结合几何匹配的余弦不变性、VICReg-style防坍缩项和重力保持的空间视图采样，在14个城市及建筑规模数据集上进行冻结特征基准评估。Polis在城市规模场景中显著优于基线（23.8% vs 16.3% mIoU），但在精细地面捕捉场景中排名反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市级大规模3D模型的语义表示对城市分析、基础设施监测、自动驾驶和遗产保护至关重要，但空中测量获取的城市场景与大多数3D自监督模型预训练所使用的室内、物体级和自动驾驶LiDAR数据存在显著差异，现有预训练模型难以直接迁移到城市尺度场景。

Method: Polis结合三种核心组件：(1)几何匹配的余弦不变性；(2)SIGReg（Sketched Isotropic Gaussian Regularization）目标函数；(3)VICReg-style防坍缩项。此外使用12.8k场景的室外预训练混合数据和重力保持的空间视图采样策略，并采用冻结特征基准评估。

Result: 在三个预训练不相交的城市数据集上，Polis达到23.8%的平均mIoU，优于次优编码器的16.3%（高容量冻结探针）；在匹配点和体素预算下为17.3%对16.1%。城市规模优势在预训练已见过的数据上同样保持，但在细粒度地面采集数据上排名反转。

Conclusion: 分布正则化的联合嵌入架构能够成功应对具有挑战性的城市尺度3D场景；当自监督设计贴合特定领域的采集几何和空间上下文时迁移效果更好，但也揭示了这种专业化的局限性——精细地面捕捉场景中性能不如通用方法。

Abstract: Reliable semantic representations derived from city-scale 3D models are increasingly important for urban analysis, infrastructure monitoring, autonomous systems, and heritage conservation. However, urban scenes of large spatial extent captured through aerial surveying differ substantially from the indoor, object-level, and self-driving LiDAR data used to pretrain most 3D self-supervised models. We introduce Polis, to our knowledge the first application of Sketched Isotropic Gaussian Regularization (SIGReg) as an objective for a native point cloud encoder, and evaluate it through a frozen-feature benchmark spanning fourteen city- and building-scale corpora. Polis combines geometrically matched cosine invariance, SIGReg, and VICReg-style anti-collapse terms with a 12.8k-scene outdoor pretraining mixture and gravity-preserving spatial view sampling. Controlled ablations show that this objective outperforms student--teacher architecture alternatives, as well as Polis versions without anti-collapse terms, on the same representative outdoor corpus. On three pretraining-disjoint city datasets, Polis reaches $23.8\%$ mean mIoU versus $16.3\%$ for the next-best encoder under high-capacity frozen probing, and $17.3\%$ versus $16.1\%$ at a matched point and voxel budget. The same city-scale lead holds on datasets whose training sets were seen in pretraining. On localized terrestrial captures with fine-grained facade and streetscape labels, the ranking reverses. Our results show that distributionally-regularized joint embedding architectures can be successful on challenging city-scale 3D scenes, and that transfer improves when self-supervision is designed for the capture geometry and spatial context of this domain while also revealing the limits of this specialization.

</details>


### [103] [Calibration and Comparative Analysis of Forward-Looking Sonar and 3D Sonar for Enhanced Underwater Object Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29433)
*Aditya Penumarti,Khanh Dong,Zi-Hao Zhang,Yongkyoon Park,Zhenqi Wu,Trung Dong,Shahriar Negahdaripour,Xiaomin Lin,Jane Shin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用两种声呐模态（2D强度图像和3D点云）配合自动校准来滤除噪声特征，从而提升稀疏点云中的特征提取效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声呐产生大量噪声，且在新型3D点云技术下噪声被放大，导致稀疏点云难以识别用于导航、识别或重建的特征。

Method: 采用双模态声呐方案：一种产生2D强度图像，另一种生成3D点云，通过自动校准在两种模态间滤除噪声特征以增强特征提取。

Result: 自动校准相比手动校准性能提升5%，滤波后特征提取相对原始点云提升超过40%。

Conclusion: 所提出的自动校准与滤波方法能有效提升声呐点云的特征提取质量，且代码和数据集已公开。

Abstract: Sonars generate a significant amount of noise. With the advent of new technology capable of producing full 3D point clouds, the noise is amplified in sparse point clouds, making it challenging to recognize features for navigation, recognition, or reconstruction. To address this challenge, we propose using two different sonar modalities: one that produces a 2D intensity image and another that generates a 3D point cloud. By implementing auto-calibration, we can filter out noisy features between the modalities to enhance feature extraction. Experiments demonstrate that auto-calibration improves performance over manual calibration by 5% and that filtering enhances feature extraction by more than 40% relative to the raw point cloud. Code and datasets are given at https://theaprilab.org/fls-3d-calibrator

</details>


### [104] [Text-Guided Diffusion-Based Adversarial Attacks on Chest X-Ray Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29456)
*Basudha Pal,Arjun Narayanan,Neha Ajith,Vikas R Bhat,Muhammad Umair*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于文本引导扩散模型的对抗攻击框架，通过优化可学习的文本条件而非直接像素扰动来生成对抗性胸部X光图像，在多种分类器架构上优于FGSM、PGD和CW攻击，虽然显著降低了模型AUROC（0.3885-0.5646），但95.9%的二元和73.8%的多病种对抗图像在临床医生眼中保持不变，揭示了人机解读之间的关键差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学影像鲁棒性评估主要依赖像素空间攻击，这些攻击引入的扰动在数值上受限，但不一定能代表放射学上合理的变化。尤其在多病种胸部X光分类中，这种局限性更加突出，因为模型需要同时评估多个重叠病理，而对抗失败可能改变多个诊断预测。因此需要探索更贴近临床现实的生成式威胁模型。

Method: 提出文本引导扩散对抗框架，核心思路是优化可学习的文本条件嵌入，同时保持扩散生成器和目标分类器冻结，通过学习的图像先验而非直接像素操纵来生成对抗样本。在二元肺不张和多病种胸部X光分类设置下，对多种分类器架构进行评估，并与FGSM、PGD和Carlini-Wagner攻击进行对比。

Result: 该方法在所有比较的攻击中产生最大的分类器性能下降：二元分类AUROC降至0.3885-0.5646，多病种设置降至0.4441-0.4878；同时保持了优越的图像保真度（SSIM 0.9080, LPIPS 0.1670, FID 51.23）。最重要的是，95.9%的二元和73.8%的多病种对抗图像对临床医生的解读未发生改变，但模型预测却有显著变化。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了人工智能与人类临床解读之间存在重要差异，表明医学AI鲁棒性评估需要从传统像素空间攻击扩展到生成式威胁模型，以暴露在视觉上和临床上合理的图像变化下的潜在失败模式，对于临床安全部署具有重要警示意义。

Abstract: As artificial intelligence is increasingly integrated into chest X-ray (CXR) interpretation, triage, and clinical decision support, understanding its vulnerability to adversarial manipulation is critical for safe deployment. Existing robustness evaluations, however, predominantly rely on pixel-space attacks that introduce numerically constrained perturbations but may not represent plausible radiographic variation. This limitation is particularly important in multi-disease CXR classification, where models simultaneously evaluate multiple overlapping pathologies and adversarial failures may alter several diagnostic predictions. We propose a text-guided diffusion-based adversarial framework that optimizes learnable text conditioning while keeping the diffusion generator and target classifier frozen, enabling adversarial generation through a learned image prior rather than direct pixel manipulation. We evaluate the framework across multiple classifier architectures in both binary atelectasis and multi-disease CXR classification and compare it with FGSM, PGD, and Carlini-Wagner attacks. Our approach consistently produced the greatest degradation in classifier performance, reducing AUROC to 0.3885-0.5646 in binary classification and 0.4441-0.4878 in the multi-disease setting, while achieving superior image fidelity (SSIM 0.9080, LPIPS 0.1670, FID 51.23). Importantly, clinician interpretation remained unchanged for 95.9% of binary and 73.8% of multi-disease adversarial images despite substantial changes in model predictions. These findings reveal a clinically important discrepancy between human and machine interpretation and demonstrate the need to extend medical AI robustness evaluation beyond conventional pixel-space attacks toward generative threat models that can expose failures under visually and clinically plausible image variations.

</details>


### [105] [Co-Evolutionary Prompt Optimization with Cross-Category Transfer for Zero-Shot Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29467)
*Sisi Zhu,Changwei Yu,Renshuai Tao,Zhenliang Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoEvoAD是一个用于零样本异常检测的协同进化框架，通过进化算法在离散自然语言空间中进行提示搜索，并引入跨类别迁移目标（CCTO）来提升跨类别泛化能力，在多个异常检测数据集上取得了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于CLIP的零样本异常检测方法多采用连续提示嵌入进行提示优化，将语义编码在潜在向量中，缺乏可解释性和可扩展性。

Method: CoEvoAD采用协同进化框架实现离散提示选择：通过进化算法在离散自然语言空间中进行提示搜索，迭代地生成、评估和选择候选提示，保持自然语言的可解释性和可组合性；同时引入Cross-Category Transfer Objective（CCTO），将保留的源类别作为未见类别的代理，根据估计的跨类别迁移性对提示规则进行评分，从而改善跨类别泛化。

Result: 在多个异常检测数据集上进行的大量实验验证了CoEvoAD的有效性，结果显示其在多个异常检测数据集上达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: CoEvoAD通过将提示搜索从连续向量空间转换到离散自然语言空间，并结合跨类别迁移目标，成功解决了现有CLIP方法在可解释性和跨类别泛化方面的不足，为零样本异常检测提供了新思路。

Abstract: Zero-shot anomaly detection (ZSAD) has gained significant attention for its practical value in industrial inspection. Recently, CLIP-based approaches have been widely adopted in ZSAD due to their strong vision-language generalization capabilities. However, existing methods commonly employ continuous prompt embeddings for prompt optimization and encode semantics in latent vectors, which lack interpretability and scalability. To this end, we propose CoEvoAD, a co-evolutionary framework for discrete prompt selection. CoEvoAD performs prompt search in the discrete natural-language space using an evolutionary algorithm. Candidate prompts are iteratively generated, evaluated, and selected throughout population evolution, thus preserving the interpretability and composability of natural language. Furthermore, we introduce a Cross-Category Transfer Objective (CCTO), which treats held-out source categories as proxies for unseen categories and scores prompt rules based on their estimated cross-category transferability, effectively improving cross-category generalization. Extensive experiments are conducted to validate the effectiveness of CoEvoAD, and the results show that it achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple anomaly detection datasets. The code is available at https://github.com/rstao-bjtu/CoEvoAD.

</details>


### [106] [Seeing Through Extreme Visual Sparsity: Surface Understanding from a Single Random Visual Patch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29475)
*Sindhuja Penchala,Sudip Mittal,Noorbakhsh Amiri Golilarz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了稀疏表面理解框架（SSUF），一个统一的双任务学习框架，将四种预训练架构（ConvAE、ViT、Swin Transformer和MAE）适配用于表面重建和材料分类。在Touch-and-Go数据集上仅保留10%可见信息的稀疏观测协议下，实验表明Swin Transformer在分类性能上最优（准确率89.21%），MAE在重建质量上最佳（PSNR 16.06 dB），ViT在重建与分类之间取得最佳平衡，所有模型均实现实时推理（<5ms/图）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从不完整视觉观测中进行表面材料识别是机器人感知和环境理解中的挑战性问题，现有方法难以在严重视觉稀疏条件下同时完成表面重建和材料分类任务。

Method: 提出SSUF统一双任务学习框架，将四种预训练架构（ConvAE、ViT、Swin Transformer和MAE）适配为同时进行表面重建和材料分类的模型。为保证公平比较，重建导向模型被扩展分类头，分类导向模型被增加重建解码器，并在稀疏观测协议（仅10%像素可见）下评估。

Result: Swin Transformer分类最优（准确率89.21%、F1 0.8922、ROC-AUC 0.9813）；MAE重建质量最高（PSNR 16.06 dB、SSIM 0.4501）；ViT在两项任务间取得最佳平衡；所有模型推理时间均小于5ms，实现实时性能。

Conclusion: 预训练架构在严重视觉稀疏条件下能够支持材料识别任务，但精确的图像重建仍然具有挑战性，不同架构在分类和重建任务上各有优势。

Abstract: Surface material recognition from incomplete visual observations remains a challenging problem in robotic perception and environmental understanding. This paper discusses Sparse Surface Understanding Framework (SSUF), a unified dual-task learning framework that adapts four pretrained architectures-Convolutional Autoencoder (ConvAE), Vision Transformer (ViT), Swin Transformer, and Masked Autoencoder (MAE) for si-multaneous surface reconstruction and material classification. Experiments were conducted on the Touch-and-Go dataset using a sparse observation protocol in which only 10% of the original image remained visible while the remaining regions were masked. To enable a fair comparison, reconstruction-oriented models were extended with classification heads, whereas classification- oriented models were augmented with reconstruction decoders. The resulting architectures were assessed using reconstruction quality, classification performance, model complexity, and in-ference efficiency metrics. Experimental results revealed distinct strengths across the models. Swin Transformer achieved the best classification performance with an accuracy of 89.21%, an F1-score of 0.8922, and a ROC-AUC of 0.9813. In contrast, MAE produced the highest reconstruction scores among evaluated models, with a PSNR of 16.06 dB and an SSIM of 0.4501, while ViT provided the best overall balance between reconstruction and classification performance. Furthermore, all models achieved real-time inference, requiring less than 5 ms per image. Over-all, the results show that pretrained architectures can support material recognition under severe visual sparsity, while accurate image reconstruction remains challenging.

</details>


### [107] [GeoAgent: Evaluating VLM Geolocalization Through Embodied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29483)
*Arka Mukherjee,Soham Roy,Kartikeya Trivedi,Shreya Ghosh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了GeoAgent，一个基于具身导航的智能体环境基准，用于评估视觉语言模型在图像地理定位任务中的表现。研究发现现代VLM在国家和大洲级别的定位上表现良好，但在区域级别上存在困难；智能体导航比静态图像基线能显著提升定位精度，同时还暴露了模型在发达/发展中国家间的严重偏见及较差的自我改进能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像地理定位研究大多局限于静态图像检索、分类和预测，缺乏对真实任务场景的忠实还原。作者认为地理定位本质上应涉及具身导航——多模态智能体需要自主探索环境、收集观测后才做出预测，因此需要一个支持智能体导航的评估基准。

Method: 引入GeoAgent基准，让智能体在街景环境中进行具身导航，通过序列推理逐步精化地理定位预测。论文将智能体导航结果与静态图像基线进行对比分析，并评估模型在区域模式识别、地域偏见和自我修正能力等方面的表现。

Result: 现代VLM在国家和大洲级别的预测表现良好，但在区域级别模式的识别上存在困难；与静态图像基线相比，智能体导航显著提升了各指标下的定位精度；前沿模型在发达国家/发展中国家背景下表现出严重偏见，且在给定错误先验时的自我改进能力较差。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了具身导航和地理空间推理方面的挑战，并公开发布了代码和GeoAgent环境供社区使用。

Abstract: Modern Vision-Language Models (VLMs) perform well above the human baseline in image geolocalization, a task critically important in disaster response, OSINT verification, and location privacy. However, most efforts to study AI behavior on the task remain limited to static image-based retrieval, classification, and predictions. We argue that faithful recreation of the task should involve embodied navigation, where a multimodal agent autonomously explores its surroundings to gather observations before submitting a prediction. To this end, we introduce \textbf{GeoAgent}, an agentic environment-based benchmark that requires agents to navigate Street View environments to refine their geolocalization through sequential reasoning. Our analysis shows that modern VLMs struggle to discern regional patterns while succeeding at country- and continent-level predictions. When compared to static image-based baselines, agentic navigation significantly improves accuracy across established metrics. We also note severe bias in a developed/developing region context across frontier model architectures and poor self-improvement capabilities given incorrect priors. Overall, our work establishes the challenges of embodied navigation and geospatial reasoning. We publicly release our code and the GeoAgent environment: https://geoagent-benchmark.github.io

</details>


### [108] [ARMOR: Manifold-Oriented Training for Adversarially Robust Aerial Object Detection under Data Scarcity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29510)
*Haoran Wang,Matthew Lau,Alec Helbling,Matthew Hull,ShengYun Peng,Mansi Phute,Martin Andreoni,Willian T. Lunardi,Duen Horng Chau,Wenke Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ARMOR（流形导向训练的对抗鲁棒性），一种面向低数据场景下航空目标检测器的防御方法，通过掩码背景保留物体相关特征并在物体上注入随机补丁来增强特征鲁棒性，在物理可实现的对抗补丁攻击下显著提升模型鲁棒性，同时保持良好清洁性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空目标检测模型容易受到物理通用对抗补丁的攻击，同时防御者面临训练数据稀缺的实际约束（航空图像采集和标注成本高，部署场地通常只有数百张图像），而现有对抗鲁棒性基准假设需要数万张训练图像，这促使需要一种在低数据条件下有效的防御方法。

Method: ARMOR借鉴流形对抗训练（OMAT）的核心思想——建模数据流形以学习和增强特征，但在数据稀缺条件下采用数据高效策略：(i) 掩码图像背景以保留与物体相关的特征，(ii) 在物体上注入随机补丁以提升特征鲁棒性。该方法复用检测任务已提供的标签，避免了OMAT中训练大型生成模型和对抗训练等数据密集型操作。

Result: 在低数据实验中，ARMOR针对物理可实现的对抗补丁攻击（包括查询无关的迁移攻击和防御感知攻击）进行评估，在保持超过0.90模型置信度的强清洁性能的同时，相比最先进的防御方法在模型置信度上最高提升0.32的对抗鲁棒性。物理实验证实这些鲁棒性提升在真实部署中仍然有效。

Conclusion: ARMOR成功地将流形训练的思想转化为数据稀缺条件下可用的防御方法，有效弥补了基于流形的训练方法与实际部署数据限制之间的差距，为航空目标检测器的对抗鲁棒性提供了一种实用可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Aerial object detection is increasingly deployed in real-world applications, but models remain vulnerable to physical, universal adversarial patches that cause them to miss objects. Furthermore, defenders face the practical constraint of training data scarcity: aerial imagery is costly to collect and label, so a deployment site typically yields hundreds of images rather than the tens of thousands that adversarial robustness benchmarks assume. To tackle model vulnerability and training data scarcity, we propose Adversarial Robustness with Manifold-Oriented Training (ARMOR), a novel defense that realizes the core insights of on-manifold adversarial training (OMAT) in low-data regimes. ARMOR builds on the insight of OMAT to model the data manifold - the compact structure capturing the data's relevant features - to learn and robustify these features during training. While OMAT relies on the data-intensive operations of training large generative models and adversarial training to achieve this, ARMOR adopts a data-efficient approach that reuses labels the detection task already supplies: ARMOR (i) masks image backgrounds to retain object-relevant features, and (ii) injects randomized patches on objects to improve feature robustness. Our low-data experiments with physically-realizable adversarial patches evaluate both query-free transfer attacks and defense-aware attacks. ARMOR maintains strong clean performance of over 0.90 model confidence, while improving adversarial robustness by up to 0.32 in model confidence over state-of-the-art defenses. Physical experiments with printed patches confirm that these gains survive deployment. Overall, ARMOR translates insights from manifold-based training to defend object detectors amidst training data scarcity.

</details>


### [109] [FuncRoom-Agent: Sequential Feed-Forward 3D Functional Indoor Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29519)
*Hao Feng,Zhi Zuo,MingJian Liang,Jingyu Hu,Xiaowei Hu,Liupengfei Wu,Dian Zhang,Guoxin Fang,Zhengzhe Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出功能房间生成（Function-Room Generation）新任务，通过递归领域特定语言（DSL）表示功能房间为可执行程序，并采用序列前馈场景构建框架与执行导向的过程奖励机制（ScenePRM），实现了无需昂贵生成-评估-修正循环的高效功能房间生成，在功能完整性、关系正确性、几何可执行性和生成效率上达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有室内三维场景生成方法虽能生成视觉合理的布局，但难以满足明确的功能性目标；已有的智能体方法和可执行方法虽提升可控性，却依赖耗时的测试时"生成-评估-修正"循环，导致功能房间生成速度慢、计算开销大。

Method: 三个技术贡献：(1) 设计递归领域特定语言（DSL），将房间表示为含显式几何与功能关系的分阶段可执行程序，组织从房间结构到密集支撑面及嵌套小物体的层级对象组合；(2) 提出序列前馈场景构建框架，将递归构建轨迹蒸馏为场景构建专家，推理时专家分阶段生成可执行DSL代码，由确定性执行器直接实例化，无需教师智能体、在线评判或迭代修复；(3) 提出ScenePRM，一种基于执行反馈的过程奖励框架，通过强化学习整合功能、几何、关系和未来可构建性反馈来改进专家。

Result: 建立了功能导向的基准测试，在通用室内场景生成和功能房间生成任务上均取得最先进性能，实现了更强的功能完整性、关系正确性、几何可执行性和生成效率。

Conclusion: 通过将功能房间建模为可执行程序并结合执行导向的强化学习，该方法有效解决了功能场景生成中可控性与效率之间的矛盾，为功能性室内场景生成提供了新的范式。

Abstract: We introduce Function-Room Generation, a new indoor 3D scene generation setting that creates rooms supporting explicit functional goals rather than merely visually plausible layouts. Existing agentic and executable methods improve controllability, but often depend on costly test-time generate--evaluate--revise loops, making functional room generation slow and computationally expensive. We address this challenge with three technical contributions. First, we design a recursive domain-specific language to effectively organize the hierarchical object compositions required by functional rooms, from room structure and major furniture to dense support-surface and nested small objects. It represents rooms as staged executable programs with explicit geometric and functional relations. Second, we propose a sequential feed-forward scene construction framework that distills recursive construction traces into a scene construction expert. At inference time, the expert writes executable DSL code stage by stage, and a deterministic executor directly instantiates each stage without teacher agents, online critics, or iterative repair. Third, we introduce ScenePRM, an execution-grounded process reward framework that improves the expert through reinforcement learning with functional, geometric, relational, and future-constructability feedback. We further establish a function-oriented benchmark and show state-of-the-art performance on both general indoor scene generation and function-room generation, achieving stronger functional completeness, relation correctness, geometric executability, and generation efficiency.

</details>


### [110] [NepScript Genesis: Neural Architecture Search for Handwritten Devanagari Digit Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29540)
*Mausam Gurung,Prabin Neupane,Sajjan Acharya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了NepScript Genesis，一个用于自动GAN发现的NAS框架，应用于条件式天城文手写数字合成。最佳策略Adaptive Exploration相比DCGAN基线将FID从332.28降至79.12（提升76.19%），并在低资源场景下将CNN分类准确率从91.0%提升至96.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手写数字识别在低资源语言（如天城文）中面临真实训练数据稀缺的挑战，而传统GAN架构设计依赖人工经验且效率低下。现有NAS方法缺乏针对脚本特定结构（script-specific）的领域感知评估指标，导致早期模式坍塌问题，亟需一种自动化、高效的GAN架构搜索框架来提升合成数据质量，从而服务实际识别任务。

Method: 提出NepScript Genesis NAS框架，采用两阶段流水线进行架构选择，由新颖的领域感知评估指标（Enhanced Score）指导。比较五种NAS策略，并与精心构建的DCGAN基线（FID=332.28）对照。在搜索阶段融入脚本特定结构启发式规则以预防早期模式坍塌。最终通过最佳NAS模型生成数据，在低资源场景下对CNN分类器进行数据增强验证。

Result: Adaptive Exploration策略取得了最优的质量-效率权衡，FID达到79.12（相对基线提升76.19%），Recall=0.531（NAS策略中模式覆盖率最高），且GPU训练时间不足1小时。在低资源下游评估中，用250个真实样本/类加NAS生成数据增强后，CNN分类准确率从91.0%提升到96.5%（+5.5个百分点）。

Conclusion: NAS优化的GAN合成能够生成足够质量的天城文手写数字，在真实数据稀缺时有效提升实际识别流水线的性能。将脚本特定结构启发式融入搜索阶段是防止早期模式坍塌的关键，而Adaptive Exploration提供最优的质量-效率平衡。

Abstract: This paper introduces NepScript Genesis, a Neural Architecture Search (NAS) framework for automated Generative Adversarial Network (GAN) discovery, applied to conditional Devanagari handwritten digit synthesis. We compare five NAS strategies against a carefully constructed Deep Convolutional GAN (DCGAN) baseline (FID=332.28). Architecture selection utilizes a two-stage pipeline guided by a novel domain-aware evaluation metric (Enhanced Score). Results demonstrate that Adaptive Exploration achieves the optimal quality-efficiency trade-off, attaining an FID of 79.12 -- a 76.19% improvement over the baseline -- and the highest mode coverage among the NAS strategies (Recall=0.531) in under one GPU-hour. Furthermore, we demonstrate that incorporating script-specific structural heuristics into the search phase prevents early-stage mode collapse. In a downstream low-resource evaluation, augmenting 250 real training samples per class with GAN-generated digits from the best NAS model improves CNN classification accuracy from 91.0% to 96.5% (+5.5 percentage points), demonstrating that NAS-optimized synthesis produces digits of sufficient quality to benefit practical recognition pipelines when real data is scarce.

</details>


### [111] [TRINITY: A Multi-Perspective Benchmark for Personal-Style Video Highlight Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29577)
*Qianqian Chen,Hyun Bin Kim,Denzel Elden Wijaya,Yang Yi,Bo Liu,Yangkai Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TRINITY基准，将视频高光显著性分解为事件、情感和自然三个互补维度，并基于共享骨干的多分支架构进行多视角并行预测，在多个数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视频高光检测基于狭窄的事件中心显著性定义，难以泛化到无约束的个人视频，因为这类视频中高光是异构且依赖视角的。

Method: 提出TRINITY多视角基准，将高光显著性分解为事件（Event）、情感（Emotion）和自然（Nature）三个互补维度，统一在时间框架内；并提出共享骨干的多分支架构，通过视角专属专家进行并行多视角预测。

Result: 在Mr. HiSum上分别获得+7.15/3.62 mAP（rho=15%/50%）的提升，在YouTube Highlights上获得+10.82 mAP的提升，显著优于现有最优基线。

Conclusion: 多视角建模为视频显著性提供了更稳健和更全面的表述，尤其适用于复杂的真实世界场景。

Abstract: Traditional video highlight detection relies on a narrow, event-centric definition of saliency, which often fails to generalize to unconstrained personal videos where highlights are heterogeneous and perspective-dependent. To address this, we introduce TRINITY, a multi-perspective benchmark that decomposes highlight saliency into three complementary dimensions, Event, Emotion, and Nature, within a unified temporal framework. Leveraging this multi-faceted view, we propose a shared-backbone multi-branch architecture designed for parallel multi-perspective prediction via view-specific experts. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving gains of +7.15/+3.62 mAP (rho=15%/50%) on Mr. HiSum and +10.82 mAP on YouTube Highlights. These results validate that multi-perspective modeling provides a more robust and comprehensive formulation of video saliency, especially for complex real-world scenarios. The benchmark and relevant codes will be released upon acceptance. The benchmark is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/vanilladucky/TRINITY and the code is available at https://github.com/vanilladucky/TRINITY.

</details>


### [112] [Guardrail-Agnostic Societal Bias Evaluation in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29590)
*Yusuke Hirota,Michael Ross Boone,Arun George Zachariah,Jibin Rajan Varghese,Yu-Chiang Frank Wang,Boyi Li,Ryo Hachiuma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向强安全防护大视觉语言模型（LVLMs）的社会偏见评估方法，通过将评估任务与所描绘人物解耦，避免模型拒绝回答偏见探测问题，从而实现对20个最新开源和专有LVLMs的可靠偏见测量。结果发现所有模型在人物无关任务中都会不期望地使用用户人口统计信息，且专有模型偏见程度相对较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏见评估基准依赖直接询问模型推断图片中人物属性的提示词（如"此人是CEO还是秘书？"），但在具有强安全防护的LVLMs（如GPT、Claude）下，模型往往会拒绝回答这类可能导致偏见暴露的提示，导致评估不可靠，亟需新的评估范式。

Method: 提出"任务-人物解耦"评估策略：使用不询问图片中人物的提示（如虚构故事创作、术语解释、考试问答），将图像作为临时用户信息附带以隐含地提供人口统计线索，然后比较模型对不同用户人口统计特征的输出差异。该策略在三个任务（故事生成、术语解释、考试式问答）上实例化。

Result: 对20个最新LVLMs（包括开源和专有模型）评估发现：所有模型都在人物无关任务中不期望地使用了用户人口统计信息（如故事中男性用户对应机械师、女性用户对应护士角色）；专有模型（如GPT-5）虽仍存在偏见但低于开源模型。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了强安全防护时代LVLMs偏见评估的可行性方案，并指出专有模型偏见较低可能与其持续监控和改进流程有关，强调了持续模型监测对减少偏见的重要性。

Abstract: We propose a societal bias evaluation method for large vision-language models (LVLMs) in the era of strong safety guardrails. Existing benchmarks rely on prompts that ask models to infer attributes of people in images (e.g., "Is this person a CEO or a secretary?"). However, we find that LVLMs with strong guardrails, such as GPT and Claude, often refuse these prompts, making evaluations unreliable. To address this, we change the prior evaluation paradigm by decoupling the task from the depicted person: instead of inferring person's attributes, we use prompts that do not ask about the person (e.g., "Write a fictional story about an imaginary person.") and attach the image as provisional user information to implicitly provide demographic cues, then compare outputs across user demographics. Instantiated across three tasks --- story generation, term explanation, and exam-style QA --- our method avoids refusals even in guardrailed LVLMs, enabling reliable bias measurement. Applying it to 20 recent LVLMs, both open-source and proprietary, we find that all models undesirably use user demographic information in person-irrelevant tasks; for instance, characters in stories are often portrayed as mechanic for male users and nurse for female users. Although still biased, proprietary models like GPT-5 show lower bias than open-source ones. We analyze potential factors behind this gap, discussing continuous model monitoring and improvement as a possible contributor for reducing bias.

</details>


### [113] [SnapBench: Benchmarking Snap-and-Ask Multimodal Retrieval for Mobile Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29607)
*Zirong Chen,Fuda Ye,Kuan Zhang,Enjun Du,Junfu Pu,Xinlei Wang,Xinyu Zuo,Lisheng Duan,Jin Ma,Yongqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SnapBench，首个针对"拍照-提问"（snap-and-ask）多模态检索场景的配对鲁棒性基准，包含1,145个查询、9,085个图库项和53种受控损坏条件，并评估了16种多模态检索器，进一步提出MOOR自适应融合方法以提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动AI的"拍照-提问"检索已成为常见入口，但现实场景中照片可能模糊、文本可能拼写错误。现有基准只测试干净输入或未隔离配对鲁棒性，缺乏针对该场景的受控评估平台。

Method: 构建SnapBench配对鲁棒性基准（53种损坏条件+人工标注），系统评估16种多模态检索器（含双塔编码器和基于嵌入的VLM），并提出MOOR（Modality-anchored, Outlier-aware, Optimal Reweighting）自适应融合方法进行可靠性感知的模态校准。

Result: 图像损坏显著降低检索性能；文本损坏主要影响纯文本检索但对联合检索影响有限；干净的纯图像检索常优于联合检索，揭示了粗粒度文本的拖累效应以及嘈杂输入下缺乏跨模态回退机制的问题。

Conclusion: SnapBench为snap-and-ask场景提供了受控的鲁棒检索评估平台，MOOR方法验证了可靠性感知的模态校准在提升该场景检索鲁棒性方面的必要性。

Abstract: Mobile AI acts as a visual oracle, empowering users to snap a picture of something and ask for information. Snap-and-ask retrieval is now one of the most common entry points for mobile AI, yet photos are often blurry, while text questions may be short or mistyped. Existing benchmarks only test on clean inputs or do not isolate paired robustness in snap-and-ask retrieval. Therefore, we introduce SnapBench, the first paired benchmark for robust snap-and-ask multimodal retrieval, spanning 1,145 queries, 9,085 gallery items under 53 controlled corruption conditions with human annotations. We evaluate 16 multimodal retrievers, covering dual-tower encoders and embedding-based VLMs. Results show that image corruptions substantially degrade retrieval, while text corruptions mainly affect text-only retrieval and have limited impact on joint retrieval. Clean image-only retrieval often outperforms joint retrieval, indicating the coarse-text drag and the lack of cross-modal fallback under noisy inputs. SnapBench provides a controlled testbed for evaluating robust retrieval in snap-and-ask scenarios. We further propose MOOR (Modality-anchored, Outlier-aware, Optimal Reweighting), a simple adaptive fusion approach, highlighting the need for reliability-aware modality calibration in snap-and-ask retrieval.

</details>


### [114] [nnMNet: Baseline for Martian Terrain Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29609)
*Ming-Han Lee,Chi-Yeh Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了nnMNet，一个基于nnWNet改进的Martian地形语义分割基线模型，通过引入线性注意力、轻量卷积和空间感知融合块（SAFB），并在三个标准化数据集上取得了新的最先进性能（86.61%、83.25%和88.24% mIoU）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 火星表面语义分割是理解火星的重要任务，但由于其表面高度非结构化且复杂，精确的像素级预测和细粒度标注十分困难。该领域缺乏鲁棒、公开可复现的基线和统一基准，难以进行公平评估。

Method: 在nnWNet基础上，集成线性注意力以更好地捕捉全局上下文，使用轻量卷积降低计算开销；提出空间感知融合块（SAFB）弥合局部与全局表征之间的差距，增强并融合具有不同特征的特征图；同时构建了一个包含三个数据集的统一评测基准。

Result: nnMNet在SynMars-TW、SynMars-Air和MarsScapes三个数据集上分别取得86.61%、83.25%和88.24%的mIoU，均达到新的最先进水平。

Conclusion: nnMNet作为新的基线和统一的基准框架，为Mars地形语义分割提供了鲁棒、公开可复现的解决方案，代码、模型和数据集均已开源。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation is a crucial task for understanding Mars, the most Earth-like planet in our solar system. However, it is challenging because the Martian surface is highly unstructured and complex, making accurate pixel-level prediction and fine-grained annotation difficult. Recent advancements in deep learning have introduced numerous methods and datasets to address these challenges. Nevertheless, the field lacks a robust, publicly available, and reproducible baseline, as well as a unified benchmark to facilitate fair evaluations. In this work, we present nnMNet, a new baseline model designed for Martian terrain semantic segmentation. Building upon nnWNet, we integrate linear attention to better capture global context and employ lightweight convolutions to reduce computational overhead. To bridge the gap between local and global representations, we introduce the Spatially-Aware Fusion Block (SAFB), which augments and combines features with diverse characteristics. Furthermore, we establish a new benchmark by curating and standardizing three high-quality datasets for thorough evaluation. nnMNet achieves new state-of-the-art 86.61%, 83.25%, and 88.24% mIoU on SynMars-TW, SynMars-Air, and MarsScapes, respectively. Our code, models, and datasets are publicly available at https://github.com/dereklee0310/nnMNet.

</details>


### [115] [See the Change, Keep the Flow: Unsupervised Action Segmentation via Spectral-Temporal Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29611)
*Yun Li,Jun Xiao,Cong Zhang,Kin-Man Lam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SpecT-OT，一种基于谱-时间表示学习框架的无监督动作分割方法。通过谱重参数化投影器（SRP）和时序亲和正则化（TAR）两个组件，在最优传输（OT）伪标签框架下同时提升判别特征建模和局部时序稳定性，从而生成更可靠的伪标签用于迭代表示学习。在四个基准上13/15个指标达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于最优传输的无监督动作分割方法，其伪标签质量严重依赖于构建传输代价的表示空间。作者认为可靠的OT伪标签需要表示几何同时具备两个特性：对判别性动作变化敏感，并且沿局部时间进程保持连贯性。现有方法未能同时满足这两点要求。

Method: SpecT-OT包含两个核心组件：1）谱重参数化投影器（SRP），用固定傅里叶基和可学习系数参数化投影器权重，以更好地建模快速变化的判别性特征；2）时序亲和正则化（TAR），对逐帧亲和度施加距离感知、无标签约束，以稳定局部时序结构。二者共同产生更具判别力和时序稳定性的传输代价，进而生成更可靠的伪标签用于迭代表示学习。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上与最先进方法相比表现优异，在15个指标中的13个上取得最佳结果。在Breakfast上MoF提升4.1个百分点，在Desktop Assembly上F1提升7.4个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过同时优化表示空间的判别性和时序连贯性，可以显著提升最优传输伪标签的质量，进而有效改进无监督动作分割的性能。

Abstract: Unsupervised action segmentation aims to discover latent action categories and their temporal organization without action annotations. Optimal transport-based methods provide structured frame-to-action assignments, however, their pseudo-label quality is fundamentally conditioned on the representation space used to construct the transport cost. We argue that reliable OT pseudo-labeling requires a representation geometry that is simultaneously sensitive to discriminative action changes and coherent along local temporal progressions. Based on this insight, we propose SpecT-OT, a spectral-temporal representation learning framework built upon an unbalanced optimal transport pseudo-labeling concept. SpecT-OT introduces a Spectral Reparameterization Projector (SRP), which parameterizes projector weights with fixed Fourier bases and learnable coefficients to improve the modeling of rapidly varying discriminative features, and Temporal Affinity Regularization (TAR), which imposes distance-aware, label-free constraints on pairwise frame affinities to stabilize local temporal structure. The two components jointly produce more discriminative and temporally stable transport costs, yielding more reliable pseudo-labels for iterative representation learning. Experiments on four benchmarks demonstrate strong performance compared with state-of-the-art methods. SpecT-OT achieves the best results on 13 of 15 metrics, including 4.1-point MoF and 7.4-point F1 gains over the baseline on Breakfast and Desktop Assembly, respectively.

</details>


### [116] [SPLG-Mamba: Structure-Preserving Local-Global Mamba Network for Salient Object Detection in Optical Remote Sensing Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29626)
*Yi Xu,Ruichao Hou,Tongwei Ren,Gangshan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SPLG-Mamba网络，通过SDR、层级感知的Local-Global Mamba和门控跨尺度融合，解决遥感图像显著性检测中的结构退化问题，在三个基准数据集上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ORSI-SOD方法虽能定位显著区域，但预测结果常出现前景响应碎片化、不完整或局部缺失等结构退化问题，其根本原因与层级特征传播有关（浅层细节引入纹理背景响应、深层语义过度平滑弱结构、跨尺度融合扰动连贯区域）。

Method: 提出SPLG-Mamba网络，包含三个核心组件：Smooth-Detail Recalibration (SDR)在状态空间建模前对平滑响应和细节残差进行重校准；层级感知的Local-Global Mamba将局部建模分配给浅层特征、全局建模分配给深层特征；Gated Cross-Scale Fusion (GCSF)在解码过程中控制跨尺度细节注入。

Result: 在ORSSD、EORSSD和ORSI-4199三个数据集上取得最先进结果，并改善了结构完整性和连续性。

Conclusion: SPLG-Mamba通过针对性的结构保持设计有效缓解了显著性检测中的结构退化问题，验证了层级化局部-全局建模和受控跨尺度融合在遥感显著性检测中的有效性。

Abstract: Salient object detection in optical remote sensing images (ORSI-SOD) requires dense predictions that preserve object completeness and structural continuity under complex backgrounds, scale variation, and irregular object shapes. Existing methods often localize salient regions, but their predictions may still suffer from structural degradation, including fragmented, incomplete, or locally missing foreground responses. This degradation is closely related to hierarchical feature propagation, where shallow details can introduce texture-induced background responses, deep semantics may over-smooth weak structures, and uncontrolled cross-scale fusion can disturb coherent regions. To address this issue, we propose a novel Structure-Preserving Local-Global Mamba Network, SPLG-Mamba, for ORSI-SOD. Specifically, SPLG-Mamba integrates Smooth-Detail Recalibration (SDR), hierarchy-aware Local-Global Mamba, and Gated Cross-Scale Fusion (GCSF). SDR recalibrates smoothed responses and detail residuals before state-space modeling, Local-Global Mamba assigns local modeling to shallow feature levels and global modeling to deep feature levels, and GCSF controls cross-scale detail injection during decoding. Experiments on ORSSD, EORSSD, and ORSI-4199 demonstrate state-of-the-art results and improved structural completeness and continuity. The code is available at https://github.com/yxu9910/SPLG-Mamba

</details>


### [117] [Conducting Stylistic Analysis of Paintings through an Art-History Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29644)
*Marc S. Walton,Astrid Harth*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自动化风格分析的AI框架，通过视觉Transformer编码画作特征，利用稀疏字典学习分解为可共享的视觉特征，再借助大语言模型将这些特征转化为符合艺术史语义的描述性语言，弥合了传统艺术风格分析与AI概率分类之间的方法论鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统艺术史中的风格分析依赖人工视觉观察与描述，而现有的AI模型仅提供无法解释的概率分类结果，二者之间存在方法论断层。作者旨在构建一个既能利用大规模图像数据、又能产出符合艺术史语义解释的AI框架。

Method: 采用视觉Transformer（ViT）在大量带元数据的画作上训练，将艺术史特定信息编码为嵌入向量；通过稀疏字典学习将这些嵌入分解为一组训练集中反复出现的共享特征；随后用大语言模型（LLM）通过检索相关画作及策展人撰写的文本，将每个特征综合为反映风格属性的描述；最后，一个自主协调LLM采用推理与行动（ReAct）框架对这些特征进行加权、测试与精炼，生成对单件作品或作品间比较的连贯描述。

Result: 该框架成功将细粒度的视觉特征转化为艺术史语义术语，有效解决了艺术史领域"图像即数据"与"人文学者语义关切"之间的对接难题。

Conclusion: 这一方法建立了一种以视觉为基础的计算艺术史研究范式，为证据收集、发现和验证提供了增强基础，为未来该领域的拓展指明了方向。

Abstract: Attributing an artwork to an artist has traditionally relied on detailed visual observations and descriptions, known as stylistic analysis in art history. By contrast, current artificial intelligence (AI) models used in the field offer only unexplained probabilistic classifications. To bridge this methodological gap, we present an AI framework that automates stylistic analysis of paintings, providing a foundation for enhancing evidence collection, discovery, and verification. By training a vision transformer (ViT) on a large corpus of paintings with metadata, our system encodes this art history-specific data as embeddings. These representations are factorized via sparse dictionary learning into a shared set of features that recur across the training set. A large language model (LLM) then interprets each feature by retrieving associated artworks and their accompanying curator-written texts, and synthesizes them into descriptions that reflect their stylistic attributes. Finally, an autonomous coordinator LLM applies a reasoning-and-action (ReAct) framework to weight, test, and refine these features into cohesive descriptions of an artwork, or comparisons of artworks. This approach converts detailed visual features into descriptive terms, addressing a key challenge in art history. It thus connects the use of images as data with the semantic concerns of humanists, establishing vision-based computational art history as an area for future growth.

</details>


### [118] [PhysVR: Vision-Language Model Guided Interference-aware Temporal Feature Refinement for Remote Physiological Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29663)
*Zixu Li,Jianjun Qian,Hang Shao,Daoheng Li,Lei Luo,Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PhysVR，一种由视觉语言模型引导的干扰感知时间特征精炼框架，用于远程光电容积描记（rPPG）估计。该方法通过融合生理可靠性证据和视觉干扰证据，利用时间交叉注意力构建干扰感知上下文，并结合共享校正单元与四个干扰特异性专家进行特征精炼，在五个公开基准上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有rPPG方法主要在学习阶段抑制干扰，但鲜有研究探讨学习到的时间特征是否仍受干扰影响，以及如何在rPPG估计前进一步抑制此类干扰。作者旨在填补这一空白，提出在特征层面进行干扰感知精炼的方案。

Method: PhysVR框架包含三部分：（1）生理主干网络生成全局时间特征和粗略rPPG预测，并从局部时间特征中构建信号衍生的生理可靠性证据；（2）冻结的视觉语言模型在干扰导向提示下处理采样的面部帧，证据头提取视觉干扰证据；（3）时间交叉注意力整合两类证据与全局特征构建干扰感知上下文，由共享时间校正单元进行通用精炼，四个干扰特异性专家通过自适应路由选择性抑制不同干扰，精炼后的特征用于最终rPPG估计。

Result: 在五个公开基准数据集上的大量实验表明，PhysVR在数据集内和跨数据集评估协议下均持续优于代表性方法。

Conclusion: 通过引入视觉语言模型引导的干扰感知时间特征精炼，PhysVR有效解决了已有方法忽视时间特征层干扰残留的问题，为rPPG估计提供了一种新的干扰抑制思路。

Abstract: Remote photoplethysmography (rPPG) enables contactless physiological measurement from facial videos, yet its subtle pulse-related variations are easily affected by illumination variation, head motion, facial blur, and region-of-interest instability. Existing methods mainly suppress interference during feature learning, while whether the learned temporal features remain affected by interference and how to further suppress such interference before rPPG estimation are rarely examined. To address this limitation, we propose PhysVR, a vision-language model guided interference-aware temporal feature refinement framework for rPPG estimation. Specifically, a physiological backbone produces global temporal features and a coarse rPPG prediction, from which signal-derived physiological reliability evidence is constructed from local temporal characteristics. In parallel, a frozen vision-language model processes sampled facial frames under an interference-oriented prompt, and an evidence head extracts visual interference evidence from the VLM output. Temporal cross-attention integrates the physiological and visual evidence with the global temporal features to construct interference-aware temporal context. Guided by this context, a shared temporal correction unit performs general refinement, while four interference-specific experts selectively suppress different interference through adaptive routing. The refined temporal features are then used for final rPPG estimation. Extensive experiments on five public benchmarks demonstrate that PhysVR consistently outperforms representative methods under both intra-dataset and cross-dataset evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [119] [MedSegBenchmarker: A Raw-Count-First Framework for Controlled 2D Medical Image Segmentation Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29677)
*Vanessa Borst,Lukas Horn,Daniel Grillmeyer,Thomas Prantl,Samuel Kounev*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了MEDSEGBENCHMARKER (MSB)，一个配置驱动的2D医学图像分割基准测试框架，支持重复图像检测、分组数据划分、YAML实验配置、可恢复训练、超参数优化、交叉验证和基于检查点的评估。MSB导出样本级和类别级的像素计数及预测结果，而非仅保留聚合性能指标，从而支持无需重复推理的事后分析。案例研究表明，在六个数据集-分辨率组合中，对相同预测的重新聚合在三个组合中改变了排名第一的架构，证明评估设置中的微小选择会影响基准结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分割（MIS）领域的模型比较面临数据集异构、评估协议不一致、架构快速演进等挑战。现有比较通常隐含假设模型排名对数据划分、预处理、指标聚合、不确定性估计和计算约束是不变的，但缺乏统一可扩展的评估框架，难以系统性地调查新模型、数据集和训练范式。

Method: MSB是一个配置驱动的基准测试框架，包含以下核心组件：重复及近似重复图像检测、分组感知数据划分、YAML研究规范、可恢复训练、超参数优化、交叉验证和基于检查点的评估。与仅保留聚合性能指标不同，MSB导出样本级和类别级的像素计数及预测，同时记录评估上下文，使研究者能够进行事后分析而无需重新推理。

Result: 在三个异构2D数据集和多个MIS及通用视觉模型上，以256和512像素输入分辨率进行案例研究。对相同预测的重新聚合在六个数据集-分辨率设置中的三个改变了排名第一的架构，尽管聚合策略之间的排名相关性很高。输入分辨率的提高产生依赖于模型和数据的性能增益或损失，需结合实测推理复杂度进行综合考量。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，评估和实验设置中看似微小的选择会影响基准结论。MSB作为开源框架，为明确且可复现的基准条件和评估选择提供了实用且可扩展的基础，促进医学图像分割模型的公平比较。

Abstract: Despite rapid advances in MIS, fair and reproducible comparisons of segmentation models remain challenging due to heterogeneous datasets, inconsistent evaluation protocols, and rapidly evolving architectures. In particular, comparisons often implicitly assume that model rankings are invariant to data partitioning, preprocessing, metric aggregation, uncertainty estimation, and computational constraints. The lack of extensible and unified evaluation frameworks further limits systematic investigation of new models, datasets, and training paradigms. We present MEDSEGBENCHMARKER (MSB), a configuration-driven framework for controlled benchmarking of 2D MIS. It integrates duplicate and near-duplicate image detection, group-aware data splitting, YAML study specifications, resumable training, hyperparameter optimization, cross-validation, and checkpoint-based evaluation. Rather than retaining only aggregate performance measures, MSB exports sample- and class-level pixel counts and predictions together with the evaluation context. These elementary artifacts enable post-hoc analyses without repeated inference. We demonstrate MSB in a case study involving three heterogeneous 2D datasets and multiple MIS and general-purpose vision models evaluated at 256- and 512-pixel input resolutions. Reaggregation of identical predictions changes the top-ranked architecture in three of six dataset-resolution settings, despite high rank correlations between aggregation strategies. Increasing input resolution produces model- and dataset-dependent performance gains and losses that must be considered alongside empirically measured inference complexity. These results show that seemingly minor choices in evaluation and experimental setup can affect benchmark conclusions. MSB, available at GitHub, provides a practical and extensible basis for making benchmark conditions and evaluation choices explicit and reproducible.

</details>


### [120] [GeoRay: Gauge-Aware Feed-Forward Satellite 3D Reconstruction in the Geodetic Frame](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29680)
*Zhe Dong,Wanqing Wu,Yuzhe Sun,Haochen Jiang,Yuchen Ma,Lecheng Ren,Tianzhu Liu,Yanfeng Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoRay方法，解决卫星摄影测量中绝对大地坐标系下的稠密地表高程重建问题。针对RPC(有理多项式相机)与非中心投影的挑战，设计了射线一致适配器、显式基准机制和校准逆方差融合，在绝对框架基准上取得2.99m绝对MAE和91.9%覆盖率，显著超越现有前馈式基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈式3D基础模型擅长单次重建透视场景，但卫星摄影测量需要不同的产品：在绝对大地坐标框架下重建稠密地表高度，且需处理非中心有理多项式相机(RPCs)。现有域自适应方法无法解决此问题，因为透视预训练特征沿RPC高度射线不可靠观测，绝对高程存在低阶基准-传感器偏差耦合，单目与多视线索在不同区域失效。

Method: 方法包含三个核心组件：(1)轻量级射线一致适配器，使冻结骨干网络沿原生RPC射线可匹配；(2)显式基准机制，将相对地形与绝对水平分离，对垂直原点等变，一个模型支持零/单/稀疏控制点推理；(3)校准逆方差融合，结合单目与多视两个地形信息流。同时构建了GeoBench绝对框架基准，涵盖域内、跨数据集、跨城市三个评测层级。

Result: 在26个US3D测试瓦片上达到2.99m绝对MAE，91.9%覆盖率；在完整性感知精度上较最强合规前馈基线提升46.4个百分点；在两种迁移偏移下仍保持最高精度；每瓦片模型前向时间仅24秒。

Conclusion: GeoRay通过针对RPC几何的专门设计，成功将前馈式基础模型扩展至卫星绝对高程重建场景，兼顾精度、鲁棒性和效率，代码与模型已开源。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D foundation models reconstruct perspective scenes in one pass. Satellite photogrammetry needs a different product, one that domain adaptation alone does not deliver: dense surface height in an absolute geodetic frame under non-central rational polynomial cameras (RPCs). Perspective-pretrained features are not reliably observable along RPC height rays, absolute elevation carries a low-order height--datum gauge exchangeable with sensor bias to first order, and monocular and multi-view cues fail in different regions. \method{} treats all three. Lightweight ray-consistent adapters make a frozen backbone matchable along native RPC rays. An explicit datum mechanism separates relief from absolute level and is equivariant to the vertical origin by construction, so one trained model serves zero-, one-, and sparse-control inference. Calibrated inverse-variance fusion combines the two relief streams. \bench{}, our absolute-frame benchmark of eighteen systems across in-domain, cross-dataset, and cross-city tiers, scores absolute placement without registration or test-reference leakage. On 26 held-out US3D tiles, \method{} attains $2.99$\,m absolute MAE at $91.9\%$ coverage, improves completeness-aware accuracy by $46.4$ points over the strongest compliant feed-forward baseline, remains the most accurate such system under both transfer shifts, and runs in $24$\,s model-forward time per tile. Code and models will be released at https://github.com/HIT-SIRS/GeoRay

</details>


### [121] [A Calibration Audit of Confidence in Feed-Forward 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29705)
*Nanxing Nick Deng,Qing Cheng,Niclas Zeller,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文审计了七个已发布的3D重建模型在十三个数据集上的每像素置信度，发现置信度虽然能较好地排序误差，但在训练分布外条件下预测的不确定性偏低（中位数偏差2.4倍）。作者提出双常数幂律校正可将中位数误差降至1.35倍，但无法修正场景级别的系统性偏差——误差异常呈现形状性而非偏移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈3D重建模型的逐像素置信度被下游系统作为可靠性信号使用，但它仅作为损失权重训练，并非真正的不确定性度量。其能否用于误差预测从未被量化评估，这可能导致下游系统在错误的高置信度下做出危险决策。作者希望通过大规模审计，揭示置信度作为误差预测的真实行为与局限性。

Method: 审计了七个已发布模型主干在十三个数据集上的置信度，从四个维度评估：误差排序质量、平均量级正确性、跨置信度区间的保持性、区间覆盖真实性。提出双常数幂律（per-backbone和per-dataset两个常数）进行量级校正，并尝试多种重缩放方法。同时通过恢复训练实验验证是否在损失最优时仍出现过自信。

Result: 置信度对误差排序良好，但量级在训练分布外场景下系统性偏低，中位数偏差2.4倍，且模型越自信偏差越大。该现象在损失达到最优时仍然存在。双常数幂律校正将中位数偏差降至1.35倍（目标数据集留出时）或1.12倍（少量标注场景重拟合后），但所有校正方法均无法修正场景级别的形状性偏差，三分之二的留出场景仍处于五点区间之外。

Conclusion: 前馈3D重建模型的置信度可作较好的误差排序信号，但不适合直接作为绝对不确定性量级。虽然双常数幂律校正能改善量级准确性，但场景级别的系统性误差呈形状性而非偏移性，无法通过简单重缩放解决，反映出模型在不同场景间缺乏尺度信息的泛化能力。需要更根本的方法来校准置信度或重建尺度知识。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D reconstruction models emit a per-pixel confidence that downstream systems read as a reliability signal. It is trained as a loss weight, not as an uncertainty magnitude, and whether it can be used as an error prediction has not been measured. We audit seven released backbones on thirteen datasets and score the confidence on four properties, how well it ranks error, whether its level is right on average, whether it holds across the confidence range, and whether its intervals cover the truth. The confidence ranks error well, but the predicted uncertainty is too low when it is read under conditions that are not exactly those of training. The median case is off by 2.4x across all seven models, and the error prediction is further off the more confident the model is. We show that this phenomenon can appear even though the loss's optimum is reached.
