A released model resumed under its own loss reaches that optimum on its training data within a few hundred updates and stays overconfident on unseen frames. A power law with two constants per backbone and dataset corrects the overall magnitude of the predicted uncertainty and leaves the ranking untouched. What no rescaling reaches is the scene, which we attribute to the model's missing knowledge of scale across predictions. Every correction we tried is close to right on average and still leaves two thirds of held-out scenes outside a five-point band, because what a scene is missing is a shape rather than a shift. We release the audit protocol, its results, and the fitted constants per model and dataset. Fitted with the target dataset held out, the constants bring the median case from 2.4x off to 1.35x, and a refit on a few labelled scenes of that dataset reaches 1.12x.

</details>


### [122] [XDG: Accelerated Visual Disambiguation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29733)
*Gonglin Chen,Ben Southall,Hanyuan Xiao,Wenbin Teng,Haolin Xiong,Tianwen Fu,Junyi Ouyang,Kshitij Singh Minhas,Supun Samarasekera,Rakesh Kumar,Yajie Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出XDG，一种高效的视觉去歧义模型，用于大规模结构运动（SfM）。核心洞察是3D基础模型已具备跨视角几何推理能力，因此无需在骨干网络上叠加重型transformer分类器。XDG通过轻量LoRA适配器微调Depth Anything 3，并复用其相机标记作为紧凑的成对分类标记，配合轻量MLP头来判断图像对是否观测同一3D表面。相比现有方法，XDG在保持竞争力的同时实现了超过3倍的推理加速，在大规模场景中可节省10小时以上的处理时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉混淆（doppelganger问题）是SfM中的关键挑战，视觉相似但物理不同的表面会产生错误图像匹配并降低重建质量。现有方法虽采用几何感知的基础模型特征，但在骨干网络上堆叠重型transformer分类器，导致大规模去歧义成本高昂。

Method: XDG利用3D基础模型已具备的跨视图几何推理能力，通过轻量级LoRA适配器微调Depth Anything 3，将其相机标记（camera tokens）重新用作紧凑的成对分类标记，并通过紧凑的MLP头预测图像对是否观测同一3D表面，避免了在骨干网络上叠加重型分类解码器。

Result: XDG在成对匹配和重建基准上保持与最先进的去歧义方法相当的性能，同时实现了超过3倍的推理加速。在包含数千张图像的LaMAR场景中，XDG节省了超过10小时的视觉去歧义处理时间。

Conclusion: XDG验证了直接在基础模型表示上进行自适应微调而非重学成对推理的有效性，在准确性和效率之间取得了有利的平衡，为大规模SfM中的视觉去歧义提供了实用且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Visual aliasing, also known as the doppelganger problem, remains a key challenge for structure-from-motion (SfM): visually similar but physically distinct surfaces can produce incorrect image matches and degrade reconstruction quality. Previous work mitigates this issue with geometry-aware foundation-model features, but places a heavy transformer classifier on top of the backbone, making large-scale disambiguation expensive. We introduce XDG, an efficient visual disambiguation model designed for scalable SfM. Our key observation is that a 3D foundation model already performs the cross-view geometric reasoning necessary for visual disambiguation, so doppelganger classification should adapt the backbone representation directly rather than relearn pair reasoning in a separate heavy decoder. XDG fine-tunes Depth Anything 3 with lightweight LoRA adapters and repurposes its camera tokens as compact pair-level classification tokens. A compact MLP head predicts whether a candidate image pair observes the same 3D surface. Extensive experiments show that XDG provides a favorable accuracy-efficiency tradeoff: it remains competitive with the state-of-the-art disambiguation method across pairwise and reconstruction benchmarks and delivers more than a 3x inference speedup. On individual LaMAR scenes containing thousands of images, XDG saves more than 10 hours of visual disambiguation processing. Code is available at https://github.com/xtcpete/xdg.

</details>


### [123] [Drift Calibration in Geometric Eye Tracking Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29739)
*Jiaqi Liu,Zixuan Wang,Yuhong Zhang,Dingkang Liang,Jane Hanqi Li,Tzyy-Ping Jung,Gert Cauwenberghs*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个包含163次试验、12名参与者的校准聚焦数据集，采用18点拟合和32点测试网格，在统一的空间外推协议下评估全局、局部和复合校正函数，并引入了一个轻量级神经精炼器。结果显示，厂商后校正将平均角度误差从1.53°降至1.03°（最强经典复合方法），神经精炼器进一步降至0.96°。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何眼动仪能提供视线交互所需的精度，但测量结果对残留的会话特定校准误差敏感。现有校正方法研究难以比较，因为各方法使用不同的设备、目标布局和误差定义进行评估。

Method: 构建校准聚焦数据集（163次试验、12名参与者，18点拟合与32点测试网格），在统一空间外推协议下评估三类校正函数（全局、局部、复合），并设计一个轻量级神经精炼器，组合来自互补校准器的排名预测。

Result: 厂商后校正后，最强经典复合方法将平均角度误差从1.53°降至1.03°，神经精炼器进一步降至0.96°。在闭环注视任务中，四种在线校正条件下，较低的残余误差与更高的任务表现相关。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个可复现的数据质量基准，用于将注视作为交互建模中的行为信号，证明了统一的评估协议和神经精炼方法在提升眼动校准精度方面的有效性。

Abstract: Geometric eye trackers can provide the spatial accuracy required for gaze-based interaction and multimodal studies, but their measurements remain sensitive to residual session-specific calibration error. Research on correcting this error is difficult to compare because methods are typically evaluated with different devices, target layouts, and error definitions. We present a calibration-focused dataset containing 163 trials from 12 participants, with separate 18-point fitting and 32-point test grids, and use it to evaluate global, local, and composite correction functions under a common spatial-extrapolation protocol. We further introduce a lightweight neural refiner that combines ranked predictions from complementary calibrators. On this controlled dataset, post-vendor correction reduces the mean angular error from $1.53^\circ$ to $1.03^\circ$ with the strongest classical composite and to $0.96^\circ$ with the refiner. In a closed-loop gaze task, lower residual error is associated with higher performance across four online correction conditions. These results provide a reproducible data-quality benchmark for using gaze as a behavioral signal in interactive modeling.

</details>


### [124] [SynCrash: A Multi-Stage Pipeline for Zero-Shot Accident Detection and Localization in Traffic Surveillance Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29759)
*Arkya Jyoti Bagchi,Ritul Jangir,Varun Raskar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SynCrash，一个用于固定视角CCTV监控视频中零样本事故检测、空间定位和碰撞类型分类的多阶段流水线，在CVPR 2026 ACCIDENT挑战赛中无需标注真实世界训练数据即可工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无标注真实世界训练数据的条件下，需要同时预测事故发生的时机、帧内撞击位置和碰撞类型，这对传统监督学习方法构成挑战。

Method: 采用三阶段解耦流水线：(1) 基于VideoMAEv2-giant骨干网络在CARLA合成数据上微调，结合元数据感知嵌入和密集滑动窗口推理实现时间定位；(2) 使用YOLO目标检测结合物理启发式规则（边界框重叠和轨迹推理）实现空间定位；(3) 基于车辆数量和配置的轻量级规则策略实现碰撞类型分类。

Result: 核心洞察是时间理解受益于合成数据的监督微调，而空间理解更适合利用预训练目标检测器和物理先验，这些方法能自然跨域迁移。

Conclusion: 合成数据微调与预训练检测器及物理先验的组合策略，能够在零样本条件下有效解决多任务事故分析问题。

Abstract: We present SynCrash, a multi-stage pipeline for zero-shot accident detection, spatial localization, and collision-type classification in fixed-view CCTV surveillance video. Our approach addresses the ACCIDENT at CVPR 2026 Challenge, which requires predicting when an accident occurs, where in the frame the impact happens, and what type of collision it is, all without access to labeled real-world training data. The pipeline operates in three decoupled stages: (1) Temporal localization via a VideoMAEv2-giant backbone fine-tuned on CARLA-based synthetic clips with metadata-aware embeddings and dense sliding-window inference; (2) Spatial localization using YOLO for object detection combined with a physics-informed hybrid heuristic that leverages bounding-box overlap and trajectory-based reasoning to predict the impact point; and (3) Collision-type classification using a lightweight rule-based strategy derived from the number and configuration of detected vehicles. The key insight is that temporal understanding benefits from supervised fine-tuning on synthetic data, whereas spatial understanding is better served by pretrained object detectors and physics priors that transfer naturally across domains.

</details>


### [125] [InspectorGPT: A Comparative Reasoning Enhanced VLM for Comprehensive Industrial Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29783)
*Weifei Chen,Honghao Zhang,Zhiyuan You,Xinyi Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出InspectorGPT，一个基于比较推理的视觉语言模型框架，通过参考无缺陷图像与查询图像进行对比来检测工业异常，支持文本描述和像素级分割等多种检测任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无监督工业异常检测方法依赖建模正常特征分布，泛化能力有限；现有基于VLM的零样本方法存在推理导向微调导致异常判别能力退化的问题，且仅提供文本决策或粗糙边界框，缺乏像素级分割能力。

Method: InspectorGPT采用比较推理机制，将正常参考图像与查询图像进行对比以识别差异；通过Chain-of-Thought（CoT）微调和带有可验证奖励的Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）内部化比较推理能力。此外提出InspectorGPT-Seg分支用于像素级异常掩码，两个分支分别训练后通过任务向量融合（task-vector fusion）结合，避免联合训练导致的推理与分割能力失衡。

Result: 大量实验表明InspectorGPT在多个维度上表现优异，并且能够泛化到未见过的基准数据集，验证了比较推理在全面工业检测中的有效性。

Conclusion: 比较推理是实现全面工业异常检测的有效原则，分离训练加任务向量融合的策略能够同时保留语义推理和像素级分割能力，为工业检测提供更完整的解决方案。

Abstract: Industrial anomaly detection is a critical component of modern manufacturing. Most traditional unsupervised methods rely on modelling normal feature distributions, inherently limiting generalization to unknown categories. To improve generalizability, some recent methods incorporate vision-language models (VLMs) for zero-shot detection via text prompts. However, we observe that reasoning-oriented post-training can cause anomaly discrimination to collapse, with some fine-tuned models performing worse than their base VLMs. Existing methods also provide only textual decisions or coarse boxes, without pixel-level segmentation. A more explicit detection principle comes from human inspection: anomalies are identified by comparing a query image with a defect-free reference. Inspired by this, we propose InspectorGPT, a VLM framework centered on comparative reasoning. Given a normal reference and a query image, InspectorGPT compares them to identify discrepancies and perform multiple inspection tasks with detailed reasoning. We internalize this capability through Chain-of-Thought (CoT) fine-tuning and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with tailored, verifiable rewards. We further introduce InspectorGPT-Seg for pixel-level anomaly masks. Segmentation supervision improves anomaly discrimination but weakens semantic reasoning, while joint training fails to balance them. We therefore train the two branches separately and combine them through task-vector fusion. Extensive experiments demonstrate superior multi-dimensional performance and generalization to unseen benchmarks, validating comparative reasoning for comprehensive industrial inspection.

</details>


### [126] [GridFlow: Structured Latent Flow for Seamless City-Scale 3D Point Cloud Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29793)
*Xinyu Wang,Muhammad Ibrahim,Atif Mansoor,Ajmal Mian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出City3D-MultiGen基准及多阶段框架，利用卫星影像、语义分割图和DSM作为条件，生成城市尺度的稠密彩色点云。通过网格对齐VAE实现拓扑保持的潜在编码和跨tile无缝拼接，采用条件整流流模型生成几何潜在变量，并引入朝向感知扩散着色器分别处理水平面和垂直立面。实验证明该方法在所有几何指标上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D点云生成方法仅能处理单物体或有界室内场景，无法应对城市尺度生成中面临的大规模数据、无缝拼接以及部分可观测性等挑战。因此需要一种能够从遥感数据生成真实3D城市环境的方法，以支持仿真、城市规划及混合现实等应用。

Method: 提出多阶段生成框架：1) 网格对齐VAE将每个tile编码为保持拓扑的潜在网格，使token对应固定空间区域，支持多模态条件融合和潜在空间边界一致性；2) 条件整流流模型从融合的多模态条件生成几何潜在变量；3) 朝向感知扩散着色器分别处理卫星可见的水平表面和遮挡的垂直立面。

Result: 构建了包含163K个密集标注tile的City3D-MultiGen基准（覆盖墨尔本和伦敦，含对齐点云、卫星图像、语义图和DEM数据）。实验表明所提方法在所有几何指标上优于适配的点云生成基线，能够生成视觉连贯的彩色点云，并可在任意大规模城市范围上实现无缝边界拼接。

Conclusion: 本文成功实现了城市尺度稠密彩色点云的生成，解决了跨tile无缝拼接和大规模生成的关键技术难题。提出的网格对齐VAE、条件整流流和朝向感知扩散着色器组合方案，为城市级3D生成任务提供了有效的通用框架。

Abstract: Generating realistic 3D city environments from remote sensing data is important for simulation, urban planning, and mixed reality, yet existing point cloud generation methods are limited to single objects or bounded indoor scenes and cannot handle the scale, seamless tiling, and partial observability challenges of city-scale generation. We present \ours{}, a multi-stage framework that generates dense, colored point clouds ($10^5$ points per $150\text{m}{\times}150\text{m}$ tile) at city scale, conditioned on satellite imagery, semantic segmentation maps, and digital surface models (DSM). A \emph{Grid-Aligned VAE} encodes each tile into a topology-preserving latent grid where tokens correspond to fixed spatial regions, enabling spatially coherent multi-modal conditioning and compact latent-space edge consistency that implicitly aligns thousands of boundary points for seamless cross-tile generation. A conditional rectified flow model synthesizes geometry latents from the fused multi-modal conditions, and an orientation-aware diffusion colorizer separately handles satellite-visible horizontal surfaces and occluded vertical façades. To support standardized evaluation, we build on public 3D data sources to introduce \emph{City3D-MultiGen}, a benchmark of $163$K densely annotated tiles from Melbourne and London with aligned point clouds, satellite images, semantic maps, and elevation data. Experiments show that \ours{} outperforms adapted point cloud generation baselines across all geometry metrics and produces visually coherent colored point clouds with seamless boundaries over arbitrarily large urban extents. Our benchmark details are available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/e32/City3D-MultiGen

</details>


### [127] [Source-Dependent Deference in Medical Imaging Agents Under Falsified Findings: A Pilot Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29800)
*Ridam Roy,Md Shahriar Rashid,Md. Rajib Mia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究审计了ReAct风格的工具调用智能体在医学影像问答中面对被证伪发现时的行为：当以放射科医生散文形式呈现否定发现时，智能体放弃原本正确答案的比例远高于通过自身调用的analyze_image工具以JSON呈现时（最强模型层级13例中10例vs1例，精确McNemar检验p=0.0039）。但这是小规模先导研究，预指定停止规则未满足，且属性与传递渠道混杂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用型智能体正被应用于医学影像领域，但其在工具返回虚假发现时的行为尚未被充分测量。研究者希望检验智能体是否会因虚假发现而放弃原本已正确给出的答案，以及这一行为是否取决于虚假发现的呈现方式。

Method: 在20个VQA-RAD封闭式问题上、覆盖四个厂商指定模型层级进行审计。智能体先仅凭图像给出答案；随后否定性发现分别以两种方式传递：一种是通过智能体自行调用的analyze_image工具返回JSON，另一种是以引用的散文形式归因于放射科医生。结果为在所有无工具情况下回答正确病例中的委托错误率，并使用精确McNemar检验进行统计分析。

Result: 散文归因宣称下的服从度显著更高：最强模型层级中，智能体在13例中10例修正了原本正确的答案，而工具条件下仅13例中1例（精确McNemar p=0.0039，Holm校正后0.012）。研究未声称这隔离了来源标签的作用，因为属性与传递渠道在设计上混杂，且工具宣称仅在智能体调用工具时才会到达。

Conclusion: 研究发现了一种联合来源与传递渠道的不对称性，即智能体对归因于放射科医生的散文宣称表现出更高的服从/被误导倾向。但该结论来自规模有限的先导研究，预指定停止规则未满足，需谨慎解读并等待更大规模的验证。

Abstract: Tool-using agents are being proposed for medical imaging, and their behaviour when a tool returns a false finding is largely unmeasured. We audit whether a ReAct-style tool-calling agent abandons an answer it has already given correctly once a falsified finding arrives, and whether that depends on how the finding is presented. On 20 VQA-RAD closed questions across four vendor-designated model tiers, the agent commits to an answer from the image alone; a negated finding is then delivered either as JSON from an analyze_image tool the agent invokes itself, or as quoted prose attributed to a radiologist. Our outcome is the commission-error rate over cases answered correctly without any tool. Deference is much higher under the prose-attributed claim: at the strongest tier the agent revised its correct answer in 10 of 13 cases against 1 of 13 under the tool (exact McNemar p=0.0039, Holm-adjusted 0.012). We do not claim this isolates the source label. Attribution travels with the delivery channel in our design, and exposure differs because the tool claim reaches the agent only when it calls the tool. The finding is a joint source-and-delivery asymmetry from a small-scale pilot whose pre-specified stopping rule was not met.

</details>


### [128] [Foundation and Multimodal Large Language Models for Face Presentation and Morph Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29802)
*Hatef Otroshi Shahreza,Asif Hussain Khan,Peter Lorenz,Alain Komaty,Sébastien Marcel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了通用基础模型（FMs）和多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在人脸呈现攻击检测（PAD）和人脸变形攻击检测（MAD）中的应用，提出了五种不同内部信息访问级别的部署方法，并在8个数据集上对16个MLLM和30个视觉编码器进行了基准测试，实验表明这些通用预训练模型在跨数据集评估中能达到最先进的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PAD和MAD检测器在跨数据集评估中泛化能力有限，性能退化严重。本文旨在探索通用基础模型和多模态大语言模型是否能编码与PAD和MAD相关的信息，以及如何最佳部署这些模型来提升人脸生物识别安全检测的泛化能力。

Method: 提出了五种不同内部信息访问级别的部署方法：（i）零样本提示离架MLLM；（ii）在MLLM输出的下一个token logit概率上训练浅层模型；（iii）在任务特定的问答数据上进行参数高效微调，得到PADLLM和MADLLM两个专用模型，并提供文本推理；（iv）对冻结视觉编码器进行线性探测；（v）对FM和MLLM的视觉编码器进行微调。在4个PAD数据集（MSU-MFSD, CASIA-FASD, Replay-Attack, OULU-NPU）和4个MAD数据集（FFHQ, FRGC, FRLL, FERET）上进行基准测试。

Result: 实验表明FM和MLLM在PAD和MAD任务上能够取得显著的性能表现。微调后的模型在跨数据集评估中达到了最先进的检测性能，表明通用预训练表示蕴含了大量攻击相关信息。

Conclusion: 通用基础模型和多模态大语言模型能够有效编码PAD和MAD相关信息，通过微调可实现跨数据集的强大泛化能力，为构建更可靠的人脸生物识别安全系统提供了新路径。所有实验源代码将公开发布。

Abstract: Face recognition systems are increasingly deployed in security-critical applications, yet they remain vulnerable to presentation and morph attacks. Presentation attack detection (PAD) and morphing attack detection (MAD) are therefore essential components of trustworthy face biometrics. Despite advancements in PAD and MAD methods, existing detectors suffer from limited generalization and degrade in cross-dataset evaluation. In this paper, we systematically investigate whether general-purpose foundation models (FMs) and multimodal large language models (MLLMs) encode PAD-relevant and MAD-relevant information, and how such models can best be deployed for both tasks. We study five approaches with increasing access to the internal information of the model: (i) zero-shot prompting of off-the-shelf MLLMs; (ii) training a shallow model on the next-token logit probabilities at the output of the MLLM; (iii) parameter-efficient fine-tuning on task-specific question-answer data, yielding two specialized MLLMs, called PADLLM and MADLLM, which additionally provide textual reasoning for their decisions; (iv) linear probing of frozen vision encoders; and (v) fine-tuning of vision encoders of FMs and MLLMs. We benchmark 16 open-weight MLLMs and 30 vision encoder backbones on four PAD datasets (MSU-MFSD, CASIA-FASD, Replay-Attack, and OULU-NPU) and four MAD datasets (FFHQ, FRGC, FRLL, and FERET). Our experiments show that FMs and MLLMs can achieve significant performance for PAD and MAD. In addition, the fine-tuned models achieve state-of-the-art detection performance in cross-dataset evaluation, indicating that general-purpose pretrained representations carry substantial attack-relevant information. Source code of all our experiments will be publicly released.

</details>


### [129] [Beyond Global Realism: Virtual Try-On Evaluation and Optimization with Dimension-wise Garment Fidelity Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29804)
*Kaidong Zhang,Yukang Ding,Xiaoyu Liu,Ying Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DAT框架，将虚拟试穿的服装一致性分解为七个可解释维度（轮廓、颜色、领口袖型、装饰结构、材质纹理、细节保真、logo保留），通过两阶段训练（5万弱监督+1万高质量标注）构建评估模型，并可作为强化学习的奖励信号优化Qwen-Image-Edit的虚拟试穿生成。实验表明DAT（8B参数）在多个指标上超越Gemini-3.1等强模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估指标（PSNR、SSIM、KID、FID）难以衡量虚拟试穿中生成服装与参考服装之间的一致性，尤其无法捕捉服装保真度的多维特征。需要一个能够系统分解并评估服装一致性各个维度的专用评估框架。

Method: 将服装一致性分解为七个可解释维度，每个维度建模为离散属性级预测任务；采用两阶段学习范式（5万样本大规模弱监督+1万高质量标注精调，后者通过多模型投票获取）；使用加权交叉熵损失缓解标签不平衡；评估模型可集成到强化学习优化中作为维度级奖励信号。

Result: DAT（8B参数）在均衡准确率、SROCC和PLCC指标上达到最先进水平，超越Gemini-3.1、Qwen3.7-plus和GPT-5.5等强商业模型，同时作为有效的奖励信号提升虚拟试穿生成质量。

Conclusion: DAT不仅是一个更精细的虚拟试穿评估框架，还能作为有效的优化信号，通过维度级奖励引导生成模型在各服装特征维度上持续改进，为虚拟试穿的质量评估与优化提供了一套新范式。

Abstract: Virtual try-on (VTON) requires not only realistic generation but also faithful preservation of garment characteristics. However, existing evaluation metrics such as PSNR, SSIM, KID and FID struggle to measure the consistency between the generated and reference garments, particularly in capturing the multi-dimensional characteristics of garment fidelity. To address this, we propose DAT: a Dimension-wise Assessment framework for virtual Try-on, which decomposes garment consistency into seven interpretable dimensions: silhouette, color, neckline and sleeve shape, major decoration and structure, material texture, fine-detail fidelity, and logo preservation, each formulated as a discrete attribute-level prediction task. To train this specialized assessment model, we adopt a two-stage learning paradigm comprising large-scale weak supervision on 50K samples, followed by refinement on 10K higher-quality annotations obtained via multi-model voting. Furthermore, we employ weighted cross-entropy loss to mitigate the severe label imbalance inherent across evaluation dimensions. Beyond its role as an evaluation framework, the assessment model can be integrated into reinforcement learning optimization of Qwen-Image-Edit for VTON, where dimension-wise rewards are adaptively aggregated to emphasize under-optimized aspects during training. Experimental results show that our method (8B parameters) achieves state-of-the-art performance in terms of balanced accuracy, SROCC, and PLCC, outperforming strong proprietary models such as Gemini-3.1, Qwen3.7-plus, and GPT-5.5, while also serving as an effective optimization signal for reward-guided VTON generation

</details>


### [130] [RegionCache: Semantic-Aware Region Reuse for Efficient Multi-Turn Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29809)
*Peizheng Li,Xin Ai,Hanyuan Liu,Qiange Wang,Yanfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RegionCache，一种针对多轮图像编辑的语义感知重用框架，通过检测连续提示词之间的语义重叠和交叉注意力定位，选择性地重用未变化区域的扩散状态，避免重复计算。在PixArt-alpha上实现了1.43x-2.55x的端到端加速，同时保持图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮图像编辑中大部分区域保持不变，但现有DiT编辑流程每轮都重新计算整张图像，造成大量冗余计算。现有的DiT加速方法忽略提示词间的语义对应关系，导致不必要的重计算或不安全的复用，损害编辑质量。

Method: RegionCache包含两个核心机制：1) 通过连续提示词的语义重叠和交叉注意力定位来检测可复用的未变化区域；2) 基于提示词相似度和上下文一致性采用自适应复用调度策略，决定哪些扩散状态可以被安全复用。

Result: 在PixArt-alpha上的实验表明，RegionCache实现了1.43x-2.55x的端到端加速，同时保持了可比的图像质量。

Conclusion: RegionCache通过语义感知的区域级状态复用，有效解决了多轮图像编辑中的冗余计算问题，在不损害编辑质量的前提下显著提升了推理速度。

Abstract: Real-world image generation often involves multi-turn editing, where users iteratively modify small regions while most image content remains unchanged. However, existing diffusion transformer (DiT)-based editing pipelines recompute the entire image at every turn, causing substantial redundant computation. Existing DiT acceleration methods further ignore semantic correspondence across prompts, leading to unnecessary recomputation or unsafe reuse that harms editing quality. To address this, we propose RegionCache, a semantic-aware reuse framework for multi-turn image editing that selectively reuses diffusion states from unchanged regions. RegionCache detects reusable regions through semantic overlap between consecutive prompts and cross-attention localization, and adopts an adaptive reuse schedule based on prompt similarity and contextual consistency. Experiments on PixArt-alpha demonstrate that RegionCache achieves 1.43x--2.55x end-to-end speedup while maintaining comparable image quality. Code is available at https://github.com/hebutBryant/RegionCache.

</details>


### [131] [PhasorNet: Learning Structure from Frequency for Real-Time Stereo Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29819)
*Md Raqib Khan,Santosh Kumar Vipparthi,Subrahmanyam Murala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PhasorNet，一种轻量级立体匹配框架，通过频域相位信息增强注意力机制、几何上下文融合细化模块和多尺度边导向损失，仅用5.3M参数即在ETH3D基准上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在细结构、反光或透明物体等不适定区域，立体匹配的外观线索常常模糊或不可靠，传统方法难以获得准确的几何判别，需要更鲁棒的解决方案。

Method: 包含三个核心组件：(1) 相位增强Transformer（PAT）将傅里叶相位信息注入注意力机制，增强光度鲁棒性和结构保持；(2) 几何上下文融合细化模块（GCFRM）结合全分辨率卷积流与轻量注意力流（含WQA和CDGA模块）；(3) 多尺度边导向高误差区域（EHR）损失，自适应聚焦优化高误差和边缘区域。

Result: 仅5.3M参数，在ETH3D基准上达到最先进性能，并在KITTI上展现出色的跨域泛化能力。

Conclusion: PhasorNet提供了高效且实用的实时立体匹配解决方案，在保持轻量化的同时显著提升了困难区域的匹配精度。

Abstract: Accurate stereo matching remains challenging in ill-posed regions such as fine structures, reflective, or transparent objects, where appearance cues are often ambiguous or unreliable. To tackle this, we propose PhasorNet, a lightweight yet powerful framework that boosts geometric discrimination via frequency-domain cues. At its core, the Phase-Augmented Transformer (PAT) injects Fourier-derived phase information into the attention mechanism, yielding photometrically robust, structure-preserving features that prioritize structural consistency in difficult areas. Additionally, we develop a Geometry-Context Fusion Refinement Module (GCFRM) that combines a full-resolution convolutional stream with a lightweight attention-based stream (leveraging WQA and CDGA blocks) to efficiently preserve fine details and object boundaries without excessive overhead. Training is further enhanced by a multi-scale Edge-guided High-Error Region (EHR) loss that adaptively focuses optimization on high-error and edge regions, guiding hierarchical cost volume refinement. With only 5.3M parameters, PhasorNet achieves state-of-the-art performance on the challenging ETH3D benchmark while exhibiting excellent cross-domain generalization on KITTI, delivering an efficient and practical solution for accurate real-time stereo matching.

</details>


### [132] [Null-Space Diffusion Restoration with Adaptive Uncertainty-Guided Fusion for Ultrasound Speckle Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29820)
*Juneyong Lee,Jaeyoung Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的无标签超声图像去斑方法UGNS（不确定性引导的零空间扩散框架），通过在稳定正包络域中进行一致性校正，并引入结构先验提取、自适应范围-零重建和不确定性引导融合等机制，解决了监督学习方法在临床场景中缺乏无噪声金标准图像的悖论，以及现有无监督扩散方法在非线性域直接约束导致背景伪影放大等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声B-mode成像常受散斑噪声和伪影影响，需要兼顾对比度、分辨率与解剖结构保真度。监督学习方法受限于"金标准悖论"（体内场景缺乏无噪声参考图像），而现有无监督扩散方法在非线性对数压缩域直接施加数据一致性约束，映射回包络域时会不成比例地放大背景伪影。

Method: 提出UGNS框架：(a)在稳定包络域提取结构先验以获得稳健的信号包络；(b)开发自适应范围-零重建机制，利用自适应权重掩码通过范围空间投影保留组织区域；(c)引入自适应的不确定性引导融合以缓解采样变异性。

Result: 在PICMUS基准和体内数据集上的大量对比实验表明，UGNS在多样化数据集上获得了有竞争力的广义对比噪声比（gCNR）值，且能有效抑制散斑噪声同时保持精细的空间分辨率。

Conclusion: UGNS作为一种新颖的标签无关去斑方法，有效解决了超声图像去斑中的关键挑战，在抑制噪声和保持空间分辨率之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Ultrasound B-mode imaging commonly suffers from speckle noise and artifacts, requiring a delicate balance between contrast, resolution, and preservation of anatomical structures. Although recently developed despeckling methods have achieved some progress, supervised learning approaches remain fundamentally limited by the ground truth paradox, which arises from the absence of noise-free, ground truth reference images in in vivo scenarios. Existing unsupervised diffusion-based methods typically enforce data consistency directly in the nonlinear log-compressed domain, which can disproportionately amplify background artifacts when mapped back to the envelope domain. To overcome these limitations, we propose an uncertainty-guided null-space diffusion (UGNS) framework, a novel label-free solution that enforces consistency correction on a stabilized positive-envelope proxy obtained via inverse log compression. The proposed UGNS introduces several technical novelties: (a) extraction of a structural prior in the stabilized envelope domain to produce a robust signal envelope that preserves anatomical structure, (b) development of an adaptive range-null reconstruction mechanism that uses an adaptive weight mask to preserve tissue regions via range-space projection, and (c) introduction of uncertainty-guided fusion in an adaptive way to mitigate sampling variability. Extensive and comparative experiments were conducted using the PICMUS benchmark and in vivo datasets. The results demonstrate that UGNS achieves competitive generalized contrast-to-noise ratio (gCNR) values across diverse datasets. In addition, it is successfully validated that UGNS effectively suppresses speckle noise while preserving fine spatial resolution. Code is available at https://github.com/yousirong/UGNS.git.

</details>


### [133] [ContextBias: Controlled Evaluation of Bias Persistence Under Context Shift in Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29847)
*Shaghayegh Kolli,Sina Emami,Moreno D'Incà,Pouyan Nejadi,Nicu Sebe,Massimiliano Mancini,Jana Diesner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ContextBias评估框架和ContextBench基准（涵盖92个职业角色和1,656个语义控制提示词），以研究文本到图像模型中职业与视觉属性关联的稳定性。通过对4个模型生成66,240张图像进行评估，发现将职业置于语义无关的上下文中不会抑制角色关联属性，反而会加剧跨角色属性集中度（BI +0.047），表明存在一种传统上下文无关评估难以发现的刻板印象持续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像模型会在专业角色与视觉属性之间建立关联，这可能产生刻板偏见。关键开放问题是：当职业角色的视觉表示被置于不同提示上下文时，这些关联是稳定的还是会变化的？现有评估框架缺乏对上下文变化的受控考量。

Method: 提出ContextBias受控评估框架和ContextBench基准，覆盖92个职业角色和1,656个语义受控提示词，将职业置于上下文无关、相关和不相关三种条件下，评估四个最先进模型生成的66,240张图像中的角色关联属性表现。

Result: 将职业置于语义无关上下文中并不会抑制角色关联属性，反而使跨角色属性集中度增加（BI +0.047）。人口统计学线索、特征服装和角色专属工具在三种条件下均高度流行，且对语义提示改写具有鲁棒性；场景构图和摄像机取景对上下文最为敏感。

Conclusion: 研究发现了一种对上下文无关评估几乎不可见的刻板印象持续性，揭示了当前偏差基准测试中受控上下文变化的必要性，为更全面的跨上下文偏见评估提供了新的框架和资源。

Abstract: Text-to-image models learn associations between concepts - in the case of this paper, people's professions, which we refer to as roles - and visual attributes. These associations can underpin many observed forms of stereotypical bias. A key open question in this area is whether these associations are stable or change when visual representations of people in professional roles are placed in different prompted contexts. We introduce ContextBias, a controlled evaluation framework, and ContextBench, a benchmark spanning 92 roles and 1,656 semantically controlled prompts, designed to isolate the effect of contextual variation on role-linked visual representations. Evaluating four state-of-the-art models on 66,240 generated images, we find that placing a role in a semantically unrelated context does not suppress role-linked attributes; instead, cross-role attribute concentration increases (pooled BI $+0.047$). Demographic cues, characteristic garments, and role-specific tools remain highly prevalent across context-free, related, and unrelated conditions, and are robust to semantic prompt reformulation. Scene composition and camera framing show the greatest context-sensitivity. These findings reveal a form of stereotypical persistence that remains largely invisible to context-free evaluations, highlighting the need for controlled contextual variation in bias benchmarking. Code and dataset: https://huggingface.co/datasets/shaghayegh/ContextBias , https://github.com/Sina-Emami/ContextBias

</details>


### [134] [MariSat: A Maritime Dataset for Instance Segmentation of Objects in Satellite and Aerial Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29852)
*Amir Abbes,Ines Harrabi,Lucas Justin Yirepoa Kinda,Rim Trabelsi,Adnane Cabani,Fatma Abdelkefi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了MariSat，一个包含1260张航空和卫星图像的基准数据集，覆盖多样化的港口和沿海场景，对八类海上目标进行像素级标注，并通过半自动标注管道（SAM 3 + 几何/比色滤波 + 人工校正）构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动海事监测需要大规模、精确标注的数据集，但现有数据集中在实例分割任务上尤其缺乏，特别是船舶拥挤港口环境中的小型船只，这限制了模型的训练和评估。

Method: 采用半自动标注管道：结合text-promptable分割模型SAM 3、级联的几何和比色后处理滤波器，并使用CVAT平台进行人工校正和质量控制，最终生成八类海事目标的像素级标注数据集。

Result: MariSat已用于微调和评估分割/检测模型（SAM 3和YOLO11），报告了详细的定量和逐类结果，并在GitHub上公开。

Conclusion: MariSat为海事实例分割和检测提供了高质量的基准数据集，填补了小船只在复杂港口场景中标注数据的空白，支持实时海事监控应用。

Abstract: Automated maritime surveillance from satellite and aerial imagery requires large, precisely annotated datasets, which remain scarce for the instance-segmentation task, particularly for small vessels in cluttered port environments. We present MariSat, a new benchmark dataset of 1260 aerial and satellite images covering diverse port and coastal scenes, annotated at the pixel level for eight maritime object classes (sailboat, yacht, jet-ski, fishing boat, cruise ship, military vessel, tugboat and cargo ship). The dataset was produced through a semi-automatic annotation pipeline combining the textpromptable segmentation model SAM 3 with a cascade of geometric and colorimetric post-processing filters, followed by a manual correction and quality-control pass performed with the CVAT annotation platform. We describe the image-collection methodology, the annotation and correction process, and the resulting data organization. We also report class-wise statistics for the training, validation, and test splits. MariSat has already been used to fine-tune and benchmark segmentation and detection models (SAM 3 and YOLO11) for real-time maritime monitoring. We report detailed quantitative and per-class results for both tasks. The MariSat dataset is publicly available on GitHub : https://github.com/amirabbes/P2M-Maritime-Segmentation

</details>


### [135] [OptiGeo: Efficient Monocular Geometry for Embodied Perception in Optically Challenging Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29881)
*Muxin Liu,Tianbo Liu,Jing Xia,Xiaoyang Lyu,Xiaoshan Wu,Bo Wang,Peng Dai,Zhongrui Wang,Shaoshuai Shi,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OptiGeo，一种针对单目深度估计中透明、反射和镜面环境的光学失效问题的偏差感知训练框架。通过干净几何教师模型和残差修剪对齐来修复有偏的真实深度监督，仅需30M参数即可超越300M级和十亿级模型在透明场景基准上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目深度估计在透明/反射/镜面环境中不可靠，现有方法依赖场景特定预处理、辅助模块或后期微调，导致架构冗余且过度特化，无法很好地泛化到一般场景。作者识别出传感器诱导的监督偏差是核心瓶颈。

Method: OptiGeo采用偏差感知训练框架：(1) 使用干净几何教师模型提供可靠的几何监督信号；(2) 通过残差修剪对齐修复有偏的真实深度监督；(3) 将透明目标渲染重新定义为清洁光学几何的紧凑来源，而非大规模域特定微调集，仅需小规模渲染数据即可学习透明物体的几何结构。

Result: 仅30M参数的OptiGeo在透明场景基准上超越了300M级单目模型和十亿级多视角基线，同时在通用零样本深度估计和边界锐度上保持竞争力。真实世界导航案例验证了其在光学挑战场景中作为高效感知模块的实用性。

Conclusion: OptiGeo通过将光学失效视为基础模型训练中的局部失效模式，并利用清洁几何教师与残差修剪对齐来修复监督偏差，实现了高效且可靠的光学挑战场景深度估计，无需大型域特定微调集或冗余架构。

Abstract: Monocular depth estimation has achieved strong open-domain generalization, yet reliable robotic deployment remains difficult in transparent, reflective, and specular environments, where depth sensors often produce missing or biased depth. Existing methods often handle such optical failures with scene-specific preprocessing, auxiliary modules, or post-hoc fine-tuning. While effective in constrained settings, these designs increase architectural redundancy and can over-specialize general geometry models to narrow optical scenarios. We revisit this problem as a localized failure mode within base-model training and identify sensor-induced supervision bias as a key bottleneck: models inherit sensor failure patterns from biased real-depth supervision in optically challenging regions. We then introduce OptiGeo, a bias-aware training framework that rehabilitates biased real supervision using a clean-geometry teacher and residual-trimmed alignment. We redefine transparency-targeted rendering as a compact source of clean optical geometry, rather than a large domain-specific fine-tuning set. With only a small targeted rendering set, OptiGeo learns the geometric structure of transparent objects and regions, correcting local geometry distortions that real sensors cannot reliably supervise. Despite only 30M parameters, OptiGeo outperforms substantially larger 300M-scale monocular models and billion-scale multi-view baselines on transparent-scene benchmarks, while remaining competitive on general zero-shot depth and boundary sharpness. Real-world navigation cases further validate its practicality as an efficient perception module in optically challenging scenes.

</details>


### [136] [MASQ: Mask-Aware Spatiotemporal Quantization for Unsupervised Skeleton Action Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29891)
*Xinyao Qin,Linxiang Peng,Youbao Ye,Di Yang,Jiangtao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MASQ框架，通过联合级结构化丢弃（JLSD）和掩码感知速度损失来解耦空间特征推断与时间平滑的任务，解决了无监督骨架动作分割中掩码引起表征歧义和离散量化扩大潜在空间扰动的问题，在三个骨架数据集上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督骨架时间动作分割方法依赖于离散量化来发现动作边界，但空间掩码引入表征歧义，离散量化又放大了潜空间中的微小波动，二者交互导致动作边界附近的代码切换不稳定和时间抖动严重。

Method: 提出MASQ框架：(1) 空间维度引入Joint-Level Structured Dropout (JLSD)机制，对选定关节的整个时间轨迹进行掩码，鼓励模型学习判别性的关节间协调模式；(2) 时间维度设计掩码感知速度损失，仅对可见关节强制执行运动一致性，避免掩码信号引起的梯度冲突并稳定时间预测。

Result: 在HuGaDB、LARa和BABEL三个骨架数据集上，MASQ显著优于现有最先进的无监督方法，尤其在Mean over Frames准确率指标上建立了全面且实质性的领先优势。

Conclusion: 通过解耦空间特征推断与时间平滑这两个冲突任务，MASQ有效缓解了掩码和离散量化对动作分割的负面影响，为无监督骨架动作分割提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Unsupervised skeleton-based temporal action segmentation is a crucial task for understanding human behavior in long untrimmed sequences. Recent approaches often rely on discrete quantization to discover action boundaries from motion representations. However, when spatial masking is introduced for representation learning, it can introduce representation ambiguity, while discrete quantization further amplifies small fluctuations in the latent space. The interaction between these two factors often leads to unstable code switching and severe temporal jitter near action boundaries.To address these limitations, we propose a novel Mask-aware Action Spatiotemporal Quantization (MASQ) framework. Our framework decouples the conflicting tasks of spatial feature inference and temporal smoothing.In the spatial dimension, we introduce a Joint-Level Structured Dropout (JLSD) mechanism that masks the entire temporal trajectory of selected joints, to encourage the model to learn discriminative inter-joint coordination patterns. In the temporal dimension, we design a mask-aware velocity loss that enforces motion consistency only on visible joints, that prevents gradient conflicts caused by masked signals and stabilizing temporal predictions. Extensive experiments on three widely used skeleton datasets, including HuGaDB, LARa, and BABEL, demonstrate that the proposed MASQ framework significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art unsupervised methods. In particular, our model establishes a comprehensive and substantial leading advantage in the Mean over Frames accuracy.

</details>


### [137] [Off-Manifold Refinement: Guiding Video Generators with a Frozen World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29904)
*Hai Nguyen-Truong,Tuan-Anh Vu,Dang Huynh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OMR是一种推理时方法，通过在单个采样轨迹的中间ODE步骤中注入世界模型反馈（V-JEPA 2.1的梯度），在不额外训练视频生成器的情况下提升物理一致性，在VideoPhy-2子集上将语义-物理联合指标从47.0%提升至52.0%，且仅需1.71倍基础推理时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成器在物理动态方面经常失败（物体漂浮、轨迹违反重力、接触消失），而标准的去噪和流匹配目标并不显式惩罚这些物理违规。已有的改进方法要么增加大量推理成本（候选选择、基于梯度的世界模型引导），要么需要额外训练（生成器内部精炼、后训练），缺乏一种低成本且不需要额外训练的方法。

Method: OMR是一种推理时方法，在调度后的中间ODE步骤中，将生成器速度与适配器空间V-JEPA 2.1惊喜能量的梯度相结合。通过一个小的潜在到嵌入适配器使梯度在推理时保持可计算，将潜在状态推向更物理合理的区域，之后生成器从修正后的状态继续渲染。视频生成器和世界模型均保持冻结。

Result: 在固定的400提示VideoPhy-2详细子集上，OMR将联合语义-物理常识指标从47.0%提升至52.0%（绝对+5.0pp，相对+10.6%），超越基线Wan2.2-T2V-A14B采样器。在固定的50提示效率子集上，仅需1.71倍基础运行时间，远低于奖励/搜索类方法的乘法级成本。

Conclusion: OMR通过将冻结世界模型的物理反馈直接注入单个采样轨迹，在不大幅增加推理成本且无需额外训练的情况下，有效提升了视频生成的物理一致性，为推理时物理修正提供了一种高效且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Modern video generators routinely fail at physical dynamics: objects float, trajectories violate gravity, contacts vanish. Standard denoising and flow-matching objectives fit visual data distributions but do not explicitly penalize such physical violations. Existing remedies can improve physical consistency, but typically add substantial inference or training cost. Candidate-selection methods generate and score multiple videos, while gradient-based world-model guidance repeatedly decodes and re-encodes intermediate estimates. Generator-internal refinement adds perturbation and re-denoising loops, whereas post-training requires curated data and additional optimization. We propose Off-Manifold Refinement (OMR), an inference-time method that instead injects world-model feedback directly into a single sampling trajectory. During scheduled middle ODE steps, we augment the generator velocity with the gradient of an adapter-space V-JEPA 2.1 surprise energy. This external correction can move the latent away from the uncorrected sampling trajectory and toward regions ranked as more physically plausible by the frozen predictor, after which the generator continues rendering from the corrected state. A small trained latent-to-embedding adapter keeps the gradient tractable at inference, and both the video generator and the world model remain frozen. On our fixed 400-prompt VideoPhy-2 detailed subset, OMR lifts the joint Semantic-Adherence-and-Physical-Commonsense metric from 47.0% to 52.0% (+5.0pp absolute, +10.6% relative) over the base Wan2.2-T2V-A14B sampler. On a separate fixed 50-prompt efficiency subset, it requires $1.71 \times$ the base runtime rather than the multiplicative cost of reward/search alternatives. Project page: https://itruonghai.github.io/omr.

</details>


### [138] [OrnaStyler: Ornament-Aware Latent Editing for Content-Preserving 3D Stylization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29905)
*Tomohiro Aizawa,Shigeru Kuriyama,Chunzhi Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OrnaStyler是一个基于整流流生成模型的零样本框架，通过倒置引导编辑策略在几何和外观两个层面分阶段恢复内容感知的潜在表示，实现文本引导的、面向精细结构装饰的3D资产风格化编辑，在保持源几何和对象身份的同时整合风格化细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D形状建模虽然进步迅速，但对已有资产进行忠实风格化仍然困难，特别是在需要精细结构化装饰的场景下——既要保留源几何和对象身份，又要连贯整合新的风格特定细节，且内容保留与风格表达之间存在根本性张力。

Method: OrnaStyler采用两阶段策略：(1)几何层面，通过流倒置操作体素表示，合成装饰增强结构同时保留源资产空间身份；(2)外观层面，引入邻域感知特征修复机制，协调新生成装饰与原始内容的连贯性。整体基于整流流生成建模，仅需推理阶段操作，支持对几何增强或外观风格化的选择性编辑。

Result: 在生成和真实世界3D资产上相较于先前方法的大量实验表明，OrnaStyler在内容保留、风格保真度和整体视觉真实性方面达到了最先进的编辑性能。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模风格元素的空间配置，有效缓解了体素空间中内容保留与风格表达之间的张力，实现了几何-外观的连贯整合，且方法仅需推理阶段即可运行。

Abstract: Text-guided style editing of 3D assets is essential for adapting existing objects to diverse visual aesthetics in digital content creation. Despite rapid progress in 3D shape modeling, faithfully stylizing an existing asset remains challenging when the desired stylization involves fine-grained structural ornamentation, which requires the model to preserve the source geometry and object identity, while coherently integrating new style-specific details. We propose \textbf{OrnaStyler}, a zero-shot framework for text-guided ornament-aware 3D stylization. Built upon rectified flow-based generative modeling, OrnaStyler introduces an inversion-guided editing strategy that recovers content-aware latent representations at both geometry and appearance levels in a staged manner to facilitate faithful editing. Our core idea is to explicitly model the spatial configuration of stylistic elements, thereby mitigating the fundamental tension between content preservation and style expression in the voxel space. Specifically, at the geometry level, we manipulate voxel representations through flow inversion to synthesize ornament-enhanced structures while preserving the spatial identity of the source asset. Then, at the appearance level, we introduce an adjacency-aware feature inpainting mechanism to harmonize newly generated ornaments with the original content, yielding coherent geometry-appearance integration. Our approach operates solely in the inference phase and enables selective editing over geometric augmentation or appearance stylization. Extensive experiments on both generated and real-world 3D assets against prior methods demonstrate that OrnaStyler achieves state-of-the-art editing performance in terms of content preservation, style fidelity, and overall visual realism. Code is available at: https://github.com/tomohiro0427/OrnaStyler

</details>


### [139] [Matrix-Game 3.5: Enhancing Real-Time Streaming Interactive World Models with Patch Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29910)
*Runjia Qian,Zile Wang,Jihai Zhang,Kai Zou,Wei Yu,Jiaxing Li,Zexiang Liu,Yaokun Li,Fei Kang,Kaichen Huang,Mengyin An,Haobo Zhang,Biao Jiang,Jiahua Wang,Haofeng Sun,Yang Liu,Yangguang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Matrix-Game 3.5，一个面向实时交互式世界生成的框架，通过几何感知记忆框架、静态-动态解耦表示和两阶段渐进式实时蒸馏三大改进，实现了分钟级、长时间一致性的交互式虚拟世界模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式世界模型在游戏、机器人、具身智能和XR等领域具有广阔应用前景，但实现稳定的长时交互生成面临三大挑战：同时保持场景几何、动态一致性和相机控制，同时支持实时自回归生成。

Method: 提出三项关键改进：(1) 统一几何感知记忆框架——patch-memory和tiled-PRoPE组件无需额外可学习参数，结合显式3D patch检索与投影相机条件实现几何一致的相机控制和长时场景回忆；(2) 静态-动态解耦世界表示——分别建模静态场景几何与动态主体，在长时生成中保持几何一致性和主体身份；(3) 两阶段渐进式实时蒸馏——通过感知流匹配和基于课程的自回放DMD，将双向扩散模型转化为少步因果生成器。

Result: 基于覆盖Unreal仿真环境、开放世界游戏和互联网视频的统一训练语料，Matrix-Game 3.5在长时场景回忆、精确相机控制、主体一致性、提示词驱动世界生成以及稳定的实时开放世界交互方面均取得了优异性能。

Conclusion: Matrix-Game 3.5在实时交互式世界生成领域实现了向几何感知和长时一致仿真方向的重要推进，验证了三大技术方案的有效性与实用性。

Abstract: Interactive world models extend video generation from offline clip synthesis toward persistent simulation of interactive virtual worlds, enabling applications in games, robotics, embodied agents, and XR. Achieving stable long-horizon interactive generation, however, remains challenging, as the model must simultaneously preserve scene geometry, dynamic consistency, and camera control while supporting real-time autoregressive generation. Building upon Matrix-Game 3.0, we present Matrix-Game 3.5, as shown in Figure 1, which advances real-time interactive world generation toward geometry-aware and long-horizon consistent simulation through three key improvements. First, we propose a unified geometry-aware memory framework, whose patch-memory and tiled-PRoPE components introduce no additional learnable parameters, combining explicit 3D patch retrieval with projective camera conditioning to enable geometry-consistent camera control and faithful long-horizon scene recall. Second, we introduce a static-dynamic disentangled world representation that separately models static scene geometry and dynamic subjects, preserving both geometric consistency and subject identity throughout long-horizon generation. Third, we develop a two-stage progressive real-time distillation framework that converts a bidirectional diffusion model into a few-step causal generator through Perceptual Flow Matching and curriculum based Self-Rollout DMD, enabling minute-long real-time interactive generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that, with a unified training corpus spanning Unreal simulation environments, open-world games, and internet videos, MatrixGame 3.5 achieves strong performance in long-horizon scene recall, precise camera control, subject consistency, prompt-driven world generation, and stable real-time open-world interaction.

</details>


### [140] [FoundYou: A Unified Model for Personalized Segmentation and Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29917)
*Gabriele Trivigno,Marcos Alfaro,Claudia Cuttano,Gabriele Berton,Luis Payá,Carlo Masone*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FoundYou，一个基于SAM 2构建的统一框架，将个性化分割和个性化检索统一为同一实例对齐过程，以冻结的SAM 2-small模型为基础仅添加5.9M可训练参数，在多项基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化分割和个性化检索虽然共享相同的实例级目标（识别同一物理对象），但两个任务长期独立发展、使用完全不同的解决方案。作者观察到SAM 2在视频帧间保持对象身份的特性天然蕴含实例级线索，因此希望利用这一特性构建统一框架，同时解锁传统基准之外的新能力。

Method: FoundYou利用SAM 2在视频帧中保持对象身份的能力，将跨独立图像的实例匹配统一起来，使分割和检索成为同一实例对齐过程的两种输出结果。模型冻结SAM 2-small，仅增加5.9M可训练参数。

Result: 在PerMIS上提升+18.4 mIoU，在ILIAS上提升+17.8 mAP；性能随参考图像数量增加而提升，对较弱提示具有鲁棒性；在类别级检索基准上达到SOTA，且模型比先前唯一统一方案快75倍以上、小20倍。

Conclusion: FoundYou证明了基于视频对象身份保持训练的SAM 2可以自然迁移到跨图像的个性化任务中，统一分割与检索是可行且高效的，同时解锁了少样本个性化检索和灵活提示的可提示个性化分割等新能力。

Abstract: Personalized segmentation and personalized retrieval both aim to identify the same physical object across different images. While the former localizes the object within a target image, the latter retrieves images where it appears. Despite this shared instance-level objective, the two tasks have largely evolved separately and are addressed with distinct solutions. In this work, we introduce FoundYou, a unified framework built on the observation that Segment Anything 2 (SAM 2), trained to preserve object identity across video frames, inherently captures instance-level cues. We leverage this property to match objects across independent images, enabling segmentation and retrieval to emerge as two outcomes of the same instance alignment process. This unified view unlocks new capabilities beyond traditional benchmarks, including few-shot personalized retrieval and promptable personalized segmentation with flexible prompts. Extensive experiments show consistent gains over unified and task-specific methods, including +18.4 mIoU on PerMIS and +17.8 mAP on ILIAS. Performance scales with additional references and remains robust to weaker prompts. Beyond personalization, FoundYou achieves state-of-the-art results on category-level retrieval benchmarks. Notably, our approach keeps the SAM 2-small model entirely frozen and adds only 5.9 M trainable parameters, yielding a 52 M-parameter model that is over 75x faster and 20x smaller than the only prior unified solution. Code is available at https://github.com/ga1i13o/FoundYou .

</details>


### [141] [Continual Test-Time Adaptation via Entropy Sensitivity-Guidance in Strict Online Setting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29920)
*Chandler Timm C. Doloriel,Yunbei Zhang,Muhammad Salman Siddiqui,Tor Kristian Stevik,Fadi Al Machot,Kristian Hovde Liland,Habib Ullah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SEGA是一种用于严格在线持续测试时适应（CTTA）的方法，通过结构化擦除来探测预测熵的变化，利用逐样本敏感性轨迹指导恢复和样本选择，在无源数据、批量大小为1的恶劣条件下实现稳健的分布偏移适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 严格的在线TTA（批量大小为1且无源数据访问）极易发生漂移或崩溃，现有方法依赖原始熵或批量统计难以在这种长时间跨度下保持稳定。

Method: SEGA使用少量结构化擦除操作探测信息移除后预测熵的变化，生成逐样本敏感性轨迹作为反馈信号来协调模型恢复和样本选择，替代原始熵或批量统计的依赖。

Result: 在ImageNet-C、CIFAR10/100-C以及水产养殖腐败风格流上，SEGA相比强CTTA基线在鲁棒性和稳定性上均获得一致提升，并通过敏感性门控减少了反向传播次数。

Conclusion: SEGA提供了一种实用的长时间跨度、批量大小为1的自适应反馈信号，无需周期性重置或模型存储库，在严格在线CTTA场景下有效缓解了漂移和崩溃问题。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) promises robustness under distribution shift by updating a pretrained model on unlabeled test data, but strict online TTA with batch size one and no access to source data is especially prone to drift or collapse. We introduce Sensitivity-Guided Erasing Adaptation (SEGA), a method for strict online continual TTA (CTTA) on corruption-style streams. SEGA uses a small number of structured erasures to probe how predictive entropy changes as information is removed, and uses the resulting per-sample sensitivity trajectories to coordinate recovery and sample selection rather than relying on raw entropy or batch statistics. This yields a practical feedback signal for long-horizon batch-size-one adaptation without periodic resets or model reservoirs. In experiments on ImageNet-C, CIFAR10/100-C, and corruption-generated aquaculture streams treated as controlled corruption-style proxies, SEGA yields consistent robustness and stability gains over strong CTTA baselines while reducing backward passes through sensitivity-based gating.

</details>


### [142] [Towards Continual Test-Time Adaptation of Vision-Language Models in Open-Vocabulary Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29923)
*Chandler Timm C. Doloriel,Yunbei Zhang,Sarthak Kumar Maharana,Muhammad Salman Siddiqui,Tor Kristian Stevik,Fadi Al Machot,Kristian Hovde Liland,Habib Ullah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DAF框架，通过边际多样性损失、跨模态锚一致性损失和特征显著性过滤三个组件，解决开放词汇语义分割在持续测试时分布偏移下熵最小化导致的类别坍缩和视觉-语言对齐退化问题，在五个数据集上相比源模型提升3-9个mIoU点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇语义分割依赖视觉-语言对齐来识别任意文本定义的类别，但在持续的测试时分布偏移下这种对齐是脆弱的。诊断分析发现熵最小化会导致patch级别的类别坍缩、持续更新会侵蚀视觉-语言对齐、低偏移样本的冗余梯度浪费计算资源。

Method: DAF框架包含三个核心组件：(1)边际多样性损失，用于抵抗熵最小化导致的类别坍缩；(2)跨模态锚一致性损失，约束特征相对于冻结源模型的漂移；(3)特征显著性过滤，跳过低价值反向传播以抵消源锚定带来的计算开销。

Result: 在覆盖自然场景、自动驾驶、水下影像和遥感等五个数据集及其损坏变体上，DAF在熵最小化失效的场景下保持稳定，相比源模型在Pascal VOC20-C上提升超过8个mIoU点，在LoveDA上提升超过9个点，在Foggy Cityscapes上提升超过3个点，且对激进的适应策略和学习率选择具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DAF通过多样性与锚定约束有效解决了持续测试时适应中的类别坍缩和对齐退化问题，同时通过特征显著性过滤保持了计算效率，为开放词汇语义分割在连续分布偏移下的稳定适应提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary semantic segmentation (OVSS) relies on vision-language alignment to recognize arbitrary text-defined categories, yet this alignment is fragile under continual test-time distribution shift. Our diagnostic analysis reveals that entropy minimization drives patch-level class collapse, continual updates erode vision-language alignment, and redundant gradients from low-shift samples waste computation. We propose Diversify, Anchor, and Filter (DAF), a stabilization framework that augments entropy-based adaptation with a marginal diversity loss that resists collapse, a cross-modal anchor consistency loss that constrains feature drift relative to a frozen source model, and feature salience filtering that skips low-value backward passes to offset part of the source-anchor overhead. We evaluate on five datasets spanning natural scenes, autonomous driving, underwater imagery, and remote sensing with their corrupted variants. Across the evaluated continual shifts, DAF remains stable where entropy minimization collapses, improving mIoU by over 8 points on Pascal VOC20-C, over 9 points on LoveDA, and over 3 points on Foggy Cityscapes compared to the source model, and is robust to aggressive adaptation and learning rate choices.

</details>


### [143] [Hallucination Mitigation for Large Vision-Language Models via Implicit Feature Stabilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29924)
*Aditi Sarker,Rafi Ibn Sultan,Hui Zhu,Dongxiao Zhu,Prashant Khanduri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出INFUSE框架，通过在微调阶段将扰动不变性嵌入LVLM模型权重中（而非推理时的显式干预），来降低视觉语言模型的幻觉问题。在多个LVLM模型上显著减少幻觉，且无推理时开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）容易产生幻觉，即描述图像中不存在的物体、属性和场景。研究发现这与其表征的"特征不稳定性"相关——轻微语义不变的输入扰动会导致嵌入发生较大变化。现有稳定化方法都是显式的，在推理时需要潜在引导或约束解码，每次查询都需付出额外计算代价。

Method: 提出INFUSE框架，采用隐式稳定化策略：1）在微调阶段将视觉和文本表征围绕扰动平均锚点和真实标签锚点进行稳定化；2）通过双向对比目标对齐跨模态的稳定化表征。理论证明锚点与扰动平均表征之间的均方根偏差以1/√K速率随视图数量K收缩，并在Lipschitz解码器假设下证明这能约束扰动对幻觉行为的影响。

Result: 在LLaVA-1.5、LLaVA-1.6和Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct上，INFUSE将AMBER CHAIR相对降低46-63%，改善ObjHal、MMHal、HallusionBench和POPE评测指标，同时保持VQA-v2和TextVQA的性能，且无任何推理时开销。

Conclusion: 通过在训练时将扰动不变性内置到模型权重中，可以有效地降低LVLM的幻觉问题，而无需在部署时付出额外计算代价。隐式稳定化比显式干预更为高效，并且具有理论保障。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) are prone to hallucinations: they fluently describe objects, attributes, and scenes that are not in the image. We connect part of this failure to a measurable property of their representations, feature instability, where mild semantics-preserving perturbations of the input cause large changes in the learned embeddings; hallucination rates rise together with this variability. Existing stability-motivated remedies are explicit, in the sense that they intervene at inference time through latent steering or constrained decoding, and pay for it on every query. We propose implicit stabilization instead: perturbation-invariance is built into the model weights during fine-tuning, and nothing extra runs at deployment. Our framework, INFUSE, first stabilizes visual and textual representations around perturbation-averaged and ground-truth anchors, then aligns the stabilized representations across modalities with bidirectional contrastive objectives. We prove that the anchor's root-mean-square deviation from the perturbation-mean representation shrinks at rate $1/\sqrt{K}$ in the number of views, and that under a Lipschitz decoder, this bounds how much any perturbation can change the model's hallucination behavior. On LLaVA-1.5, LLaVA-1.6, and Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct, INFUSE reduces AMBER CHAIR by 46-63% relative to each base model, improves ObjHal, MMHal, HallusionBench, and POPE, and preserves VQA-v2 and TextVQA, all with no inference-time overhead.

</details>


### [144] [Dior: Drawing the Light of Image via Material-Decoupled Illumination Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29925)
*Xuanpu Zhang,Xuesong Niu,Haoxiang Cao,Ruidong Chen,Jianhao Zeng,Changqian Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Dior-Light，一种基于手绘笔划控制的图像重打光方法，通过引入材料解耦的照明表示（Lumi Map）建立用户笔划与重打光结果之间的显式映射，提升了重打光的准确性和可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于笔划的图像重打光方法未能建立笔划输入与重打光结果之间一致且有效的映射关系，导致对照明强度、色度和复杂空间分布的控制能力有限。

Method: 1) 引入材料解耦的照明表示Lumi Map，建立用户笔划与照明结果间的显式映射；2) 使用渲染器合成源图像-Lumi Map-重打光图像三元组用于模型训练；3) 在真实重打光对上执行重建训练以缓解合成数据的领域差距问题。

Result: 大量实验表明，Dior-Light在重打光准确性上优于现有方法，并能对真实世界图像实现有效的照明强度和色度控制。

Conclusion: 通过Lumi Map显式映射和合成+真实数据的训练策略，Dior-Light成功实现了可控、准确且泛化能力强的笔划驱动图像重打光。

Abstract: Controllable image relighting is an important problem in image editing, and hand-drawn scribbles provide an intuitive interface for specifying the desired illumination. However, existing methods do not establish a consistent and effective mapping between scribble inputs and relighting results, limiting their ability to control illumination intensity, chromaticity, and complex spatial distributions. We address this limitation by introducing a material-decoupled illumination representation, termed the Lumi Map, which establishes an explicit mapping between user scribbles and the resulting illumination, thereby improving both relighting accuracy and controllability. Specifically, we use a renderer to synthesize source image-Lumi Map-relit image triplets and train the model to predict the target relighting result conditioned on the Lumi Map. To mitigate the domain gap introduced by synthetic data, we further perform reconstruction training on real relighting pairs, improving the model's generalization to real-world images. Finally, we present Dior-Light, an image relighting method controlled by hand-drawn strokes. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms existing approaches in relighting accuracy and enables effective control over illumination intensity and chromaticity on in-the-wild images.

</details>


### [145] [Everybody Tracking Every Body](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29927)
*Daeyun Shin,Yunhan Zhao,Shu Kong,Alexander C. Berg,Charless Fowlkes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩散模型的融合方法，用于从多人的自我中心视角（佩戴摄像头和IMU）进行3D人体姿态估计。该方法融合了基于头部运动（来自自我中心相机VIO SLAM）的姿态估计和外部视角（第三人称）的姿态观测，并同时考虑观测内容及其可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多人交互场景下，从各自佩戴的自我中心摄像头估计3D人体姿态面临挑战：外部视角观测稀疏、间歇且可靠性高度可变（因相机和被测者都在移动）。现有方法仅依赖单一数据源（运动或视觉）无法充分利用多源互补信息。

Method: 提出扩散（diffusion-based）融合方法：1）从自我中心相机VIO SLAM获取头部运动姿态估计；2）结合外部视角（第三人称）姿态观测作为条件；3）同时以观测内容和观测可靠性为条件进行融合；4）在单人动作捕捉数据和多人视频的混合数据上训练，以学习身体运动轨迹和视频观测可靠性的丰富先验。

Result: 在具有挑战性的多人数据集上，该方法在绝对和相对姿态精度方面均优于仅运动（motion-only）和仅视觉（vision-only）的基线方法。

Conclusion: 扩散模型能够有效融合自我中心运动线索和外部视觉观测，在多人交互场景下显著提升3D姿态估计精度，验证了多源融合和可靠条件建模的有效性。

Abstract: We address the problem of 3D body pose estimation of multiple interacting people from their egocentric views with centralized coordination. Each individual wears a camera recording egocentric video and IMU data. Processing this video with VIO SLAM provides high-quality tracking of each egocentric camera through space. The first-person view from one individual provides third-person observations of other people, although these exocentric observations are sparse, intermittent, and of highly variable reliability as both cameras and subjects move. To integrate these synchronized data streams, we propose a diffusion-based approach that fuses estimates of pose based on head motion derived from egocentric camera motion with exocentric pose observations, conditioning on both observation content and reliability. Our model is trained on a mixture of single-person motion-capture data and multi-person video in order to learn rich priors for body motion trajectories and video observation reliability. Evaluation on challenging multi-person datasets suggests our fusion approach improves over motion-only and vision-only baselines in terms of both absolute and relative pose accuracy.

</details>


### [146] [Biomechanical 3D Body: Self-Supervised Distillation of Biomechanical Pose from a 3D Body Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29928)
*R. James Cotton,J. D. Peiffer,Lucinda Williamson,John Leske,Georgios Pavlakos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从单张RGB图像直接回归生物力学模型关节角度和尺度的新方法，通过蒸馏SAM-3D-Body网格预测头，无需配对的图像-生物力学数据集即可训练，在多个公共数据集上优于现有的直接回归方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单目人体恢复方法输出网格顶点和运动树角度，但缺乏生物力学定义的关节角度，而临床和生物力学分析下游应用恰恰需要这些输出。此外，配对的图像-生物力学拟合数据集非常有限，直接训练面临数据瓶颈。

Method: 在SAM-3D-Body基础上扩展一个额外的生物力学预测头，从单张RGB图像回归生物力学模型的关节角度和尺度。训练时使用Levenberg-Marquardt求解器逆运动学拟合，以网格预测结果为目标进行循环优化蒸馏，从而在无标签图像上监督生物力学输出。整个模型用JAX和Equinox实现，以兼容MuJoCo中的GPU优化生物力学模型。

Result: 在SAM-3D-Body数据集上训练蒸馏头后，在MoVi、BioCV两个基于标记的公共数据集以及临床队列的多视角无标记动作捕捉数据上验证。该模型优于现有的图像直接回归生物力学方法，仅略逊于需要更昂贵的推理时轨迹优化的最优单目生物力学方法。

Conclusion: 通过蒸馏策略，可以在缺乏配对数据的情况下实现从单张图像到生物力学输出的高效直接回归，在保持较好精度的同时大幅降低推理计算成本，为临床和生物力学分析提供了实用的工具。

Abstract: State-of-the-art monocular body recovery methods predict mesh vertices and angles on the corresponding kinematic tree, but their outputs lack biomechanically defined joint angles that downstream applications like clinical and biomechanical analyses require. We extend an existing foundation model, SAM-3D-Body, with an additional biomechanical prediction head that, from a single RGB image, regresses the joint angles and scales of a biomechanical model. Training this model presents a challenge, as there are limited datasets of paired images and biomechanical fits. To overcome this, we supervise biomechanical outputs with in-loop optimized targets from a Levenberg-Marquardt solver performing inverse kinematics fits against markers from the mesh predictions. This allows distilling the biomechanical head from the mesh head, even from unlabeled images. To make this work with GPU-optimized biomechanical models in MuJoCo, the entire model was implemented in JAX using Equinox. We trained this distilled output head on the publicly released SAM-3D-Body dataset. We then validated this model on biomechanical fits to two publicly available marker-based datasets, MoVi and BioCV, as well as movements from a clinical cohort captured with multiview markerless motion capture. The resulting model outperforms existing models for direct regression of biomechanics from images while only slightly underperforming the state-of-the-art monocular biomechanics method that performs more costly inference-time optimization of entire trajectories.

</details>


### [147] [Evaluating 2D and 3D-Aware Vision Foundation Models for Vehicle Attribute Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29929)
*Alexandre V. Delazeri,Gabriel E. Lima,Eduil Nascimento,Rayson Laroca,David Menotti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对14个最先进的2D和3D感知视觉基础模型进行了实证基准评测，使用UFPR-VeSV真实世界数据集，通过线性探测评估车型、品牌和型号识别性能，并进一步在少样本学习和分布外域偏移场景下进行压力测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆属性识别是智能交通系统中的重要任务，但视觉基础模型在细粒度车辆分类中的有效性尚未被充分探索；此外，车辆的3D结构特性使得3D感知基础模型是否优于标准2D架构成为一个待解问题。

Method: 使用UFPR-VeSV数据集，对14个2D和3D感知视觉基础模型作为冻结特征提取器进行线性探测评估，覆盖车型、品牌和型号三类识别任务；对最优模型进一步进行少样本学习和OOD域偏移压力测试。

Result: 标准2D自监督模型（尤其是DINOv3）在细粒度任务中大幅超越3D感知模型，品牌和型号识别达到93%以上的Macro-Accuracy；但3D感知的Depth Anything v2在车型分类中对视角变化表现出更强的不变性。

Conclusion: 2D和3D先验各有优势，研究结果鼓励采用结合2D和3D先验的混合方法来实现稳健的车辆识别。

Abstract: Vehicle attribute recognition is an important task in intelligent transportation systems, particularly when Automatic License Plate Recognition (ALPR) is unavailable or unreliable. Although vision foundation models have shown strong transferability across domains, their effectiveness for fine-grained vehicle classification remains underexplored. Moreover, given the inherently three-dimensional structure of vehicles, it is unclear whether emerging 3D-aware foundation models offer advantages over standard 2D architectures. This paper presents an empirical benchmark of 14 state-of-the-art 2D and 3D-aware vision foundation models. Using the challenging real-world UFPR-VeSV dataset, we evaluate these models as frozen feature extractors via linear probing for vehicle type, make, and model recognition. We further stress-test the best-performing models under few-shot learning and Out-of-Distribution (OOD) domain shifts. Our results show that standard 2D self-supervised models, particularly DINOv3, substantially outperform 3D-aware models in fine-grained tasks, achieving over 93% Macro-Accuracy for make and model recognition. However, the 3D-aware Depth Anything v2 exhibits stronger invariance to viewing angles in vehicle type classification. These findings motivate hybrid approaches that combine 2D and 3D priors for robust vehicle recognition. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/UFPR-IPASPPR/3D-Vision-Benchmark/.

</details>


### [148] [On the Role of MRI Sequences in Cross-Dataset Generalization for Brain Tumor Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29944)
*Henrique Zan Grande,João G. Pitol,Lucas B. Schuck,Rafael V. Serenato,Rayson Laroca,Andre Gustavo Hochuli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了不同MRI序列对脑肿瘤分割模型跨数据集鲁棒性的影响，发现T2f/FLAIR序列在跨数据集表现最佳（Dice>75%），多序列训练能进一步提升性能，且有限的领域自适应即可带来快速性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑肿瘤MRI分割中，深度学习模型（如U-Net）在不同数据集间存在性能退化问题，尤其在域偏移和标注数据有限的情况下表现不佳，因此需要系统性地理解各MRI序列对模型鲁棒性的影响。

Method: 采用ResUNet框架，将每个MRI模态独立训练以隔离其单独效果，在跨数据集评估协议下进行受控实验，包括肿瘤尺寸分层、无目标域训练或有限领域自适应设置。

Result: T2f/FLAIR序列在跨数据集表现最佳（Dice>75%），在大多数肿瘤尺寸范围内优于其他模态；多序列联合训练进一步改善性能；即便有限的领域自适应也能带来快速初始性能增益。

Conclusion: 本研究为脑肿瘤分割中的模态选择提供了实证指导，表明T2f/FLAIR序列对跨数据集的鲁棒性最为重要，同时证明了有限的领域自适应可以有效降低对大量标注数据和昂贵重训练的需求，代码已开源。

Abstract: Brain tumor segmentation in magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) is a critical task for diagnosis and treatment planning. Despite the success of deep learning architectures such as U-Net and its variants, performance degradation across datasets remains a major challenge, particularly under domain shift and limited annotated data. To address this issue, this study systematically evaluates how individual MRI sequences influence model robustness across two well-known datasets. A ResUNet-based framework is employed, where each modality is trained independently to isolate its effect under a controlled cross-dataset evaluation protocol with tumor size stratification, without target-domain training, or with limited domain adaptation. Results show that the T2f/FLAIR sequence achieves the best cross-dataset performance, with Dice scores exceeding 75%. It consistently outperforms other modalities across most tumor size ranges, while multi-sequence training further improves performance. Additionally, even limited target-domain adaptation yields rapid initial gains, reducing the need for extensive annotations and costly retraining. Our source code is publicly available at https://github.com/henrique-zan/brain_tumor_segmentation/.

</details>


### [149] [RIDGE: Region-Informed Derivative-Guided Evidence Selection for Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29958)
*Shanqing Xu,Meng Luo,Mengchen Qian,Yuhui Gao,Siyue Peng,Xiaohan Zhong,Xiaojin Zhang,Zhongyu Wei,Wei Chen,Xiang Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RIDGE框架，将帧-查询相似度曲线视为时间信号，利用局部变化和曲率划分时间线为结构区域，并按区域选择帧以保留事件核心、过渡、铺垫、余波和上下文帧，在固定视觉令牌预算下实现高效帧选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频包含的视觉内容远超LVLMs在固定视觉令牌预算下能处理的范围，帧选择至关重要。现有查询感知选择器虽然机制各异，但仅将相似度序列视为用于排序或采样的数值，而忽略了其形状反映的查询相关证据随时间的出现、峰值和消退模式，导致位于相关峰值上升或下降侧的上下文帧被遗漏。

Method: RIDGE框架：(1) 将帧-查询相似度曲线作为时间信号读取；(2) 利用局部变化和曲率将时间线划分为结构区域；(3) 应用区域特定的选择策略，在固定预算下保留事件核心帧、过渡帧、铺垫帧、余波帧和上下文帧；(4) 作为预计算帧-查询分数的轻量级后处理步骤，无需训练或迭代LVLM调用。

Result: 在四个长视频基准和三个骨干模型上，RIDGE在大多数设置下达到最佳性能，在其余设置中也保持竞争力。

Conclusion: RIDGE验证了将相似度序列的结构形态作为选择依据的可行性，相比仅按得分排序的方式，能更全面地捕捉查询相关证据的时间动态，是一种高效且无需额外训练的帧选择方案。

Abstract: Long videos contain far more visual content than Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) can process under a fixed visual-token budget, making frame selection essential. Existing query-aware selectors usually estimate frame-query relevance and build a compact subset from high-scoring frames. Although their mechanisms differ, the similarity sequence is still often treated primarily as values to rank or sample from, rather than as an ordered signal whose shape reflects how query-relevant evidence emerges, peaks, and fades over time. This can obscure frames that explain, contextualize, or follow an event, because such evidence may lie on the rising or falling sides of a nearby relevance peak and receive lower absolute scores. We propose RIDGE, a frame selection framework that reads the frame-query similarity curve as a temporal signal. By using local changes and curvature, RIDGE partitions the timeline into structural regions and applies region-specific selection to preserve event cores, transitions, buildup, aftermath, and contextual frames under a fixed budget. It is a lightweight post-processing step on precomputed frame-query scores and requires neither training nor iterative LVLM calls. Across four long-video benchmarks and three backbones, RIDGE achieves the best performance in most settings and remains competitive in the others.

</details>


### [150] [Confidence-Aware Ensemble and Long-Word Refinement for Artistic Text Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29970)
*Lucas A. Dias,Henrique A. Schulz,Rafaela de Miranda,Guilherme L. Peres,Pedro L. Bittencourt,Rayson Laroca*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对艺术文本识别（ATR）这一挑战性任务，基于WordArt-V1.5基准测试，提出了一种置信度感知的集成方法，结合SVTRv2、PARSeq和MAERec三个模型，并通过Needleman-Wunsch对齐和词典引导校正对长单词进行细化，在Test B分割上达到89.90%的单词识别准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 艺术文本识别面临装饰字体、曲线布局、类物体字符、杂乱背景和严重变形等多重挑战，现有模型在此场景下性能不足，且缺乏标准化的评估基准和统一的测试协议。

Method: 提出置信度感知的集成方法：将微调后的SVTRv2、PARSeq和MAERec三个模型进行集成，在不一致位置选择最小置信度来确定最终预测，强调区分竞争假设的关键字符。对于长单词，增加基于Needleman-Wunsch对齐和词典引导校正的细化阶段。

Result: 在WordArt-V1.5 Test B分割上达到89.90%的单词识别准确率，比最佳单模型提升1.77个百分点；长单词细化模块将长单词子集提升2.72个百分点；错误分析显示48.8%的剩余错误源于标注问题、视觉模糊或无法辨认的样本。

Conclusion: 置信度感知的模型集成能有效提升艺术文本识别性能，长单词细化策略针对性地改进困难子集，同时通过诊断性错误报告揭示了基准本身的标注质量问题，为未来ATR模型和基准的改进提供了洞察。

Abstract: Artistic Text Recognition (ATR) remains challenging because word images often combine decorative fonts, curved layouts, object-like characters, clutter, and severe distortions. This paper studies WordArt-V1.5 as a standardized benchmark for this setting and evaluates recent scene and artistic text recognizers under a common protocol. We propose a confidence-aware ensemble that combines SVTRv2, PARSeq, and MAERec after fine-tuning on the official training split. The ensemble selects predictions using the minimum confidence over disagreement positions, emphasizing characters that separate competing hypotheses. For long words, where a single character error can invalidate the whole prediction, we add a targeted refinement stage based on Needleman-Wunsch alignment and lexicon-guided correction. On the WordArt-V1.5 Test B split, the proposed system reaches 89.90% Word Recognition Accuracy, improving the best individual fine-tuned model by 1.77 percentage points. The long-word refinement produces a modest global gain, but improves the targeted long-word subset by 2.72 percentage points. Finally, an error analysis of all remaining mistakes shows that 48.8% are associated with labeling issues, visual ambiguity, or illegible samples, highlighting the value of diagnostic reporting for future ATR benchmarks and models. Our source code is available at https://github.com/lucas-azdias/Artistic-Text-Recognition/.

</details>


### [151] [SpanCalib-VLM: Calibrated Hallucination Span Detection in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29974)
*Amanuel Gizachew Abebe,Yasmin Moslem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SpanCalib-VLM，一种混合双系统框架，结合多模态序列标注器（XLM-RoBERTa-Large + SigLIP交叉注意力）与微调的生成式VLM（Qwen3.5-4B-SHROOM-SFT），通过Union-Calibrated Fusion策略重评分生成模型候选片段，在SHROOM-Visions英文评测集上实现Pearson校准相关系数0.41、整体IoU 0.39、干净响应IoU 0.91及70.7%的检测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）中的幻觉检测需要同时具备精确的跨度定位和良好的置信度校准，但现有的两类方法各有缺陷：微调生成式VLM能识别幻觉文本跨度却过于自信且推理延迟高；判别式序列标注器速度快、校准好但跨度召回率保守。二者互补，需要融合策略。

Method: 提出SpanCalib-VLM混合双系统：1）多模态序列标注器，由XLM-RoBERTa-Large与SigLIP视觉编码器通过交叉注意力融合构成；2）微调的生成式VLM（Qwen3.5-4B-SHROOM-SFT）。采用Union-Calibrated Fusion策略，将生成模型产生的候选跨度用序列标注器输出的校准概率重新打分。

Result: 在SHROOM-Visions英文评测集上，整体方案取得Pearson校准相关系数0.41、整体IoU 0.39、干净响应IoU 0.91以及70.7%的整体检测准确率。

Conclusion: 融合判别式与生成式方法可以有效弥补各自的短板，兼顾幻觉跨度定位精度与置信度校准，且模型权重和代码已开源。

Abstract: Detecting hallucinations in Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) requires both accurate span localization and well-calibrated confidence scores. Fine-tuned generative VLMs excel at identifying hallucinated text spans but suffer from overconfidence and high inference latency. Discriminative sequence taggers offer deterministic speed and superior calibration but exhibit conservative span recall. We present SpanCalib-VLM, a hybrid dual-system for the SHROOM-Visions Shared Task that combines a multimodal sequence tagger, consisting of XLM-RoBERTa-Large fused with a SigLIP vision encoder via cross-attention, with our fine-tuned generative VLM (Qwen3.5-4B-SHROOM-SFT). Through a Union-Calibrated Fusion strategy, candidate spans from the generative model are re-scored with calibrated probabilities from the sequence tagger. On the SHROOM-Visions English evaluation split, our ensemble achieves a Pearson calibration correlation of 0.41 and an overall IoU of 0.39, with a clean-response IoU of 0.91} and overall detection accuracy of 70.7%. We make our model weights and code publicly available.

</details>


### [152] [FIS-OT: Feature-Induced Optimal Transport for Unsupervised Action Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29980)
*Linxiang Peng,Xinyao Qin,Jinhan Li,Di Yang,Jiangtao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FIS-OT框架，用于无监督动作分割任务。通过特征增强生成器（FEG）模块利用三元组损失捕获局部一致性，结合特征诱导残差结构先验确保时间连续性，并建立循环优化循环对齐局部特征学习与全局结构对齐，在三个数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最优传输（OT）方法使用全局约束，忽视了局部信息的利用；且现有的最优传输架构因过度信任自身生成的伪标签而产生确认偏差，导致模型在训练早期学习到噪声。

Method: 1) 提出FEG模块作为内部正则器，通过三元组损失捕获局部一致性，提供不依赖噪声伪标签的鲁棒监督；2) 提出特征诱导残差结构先验，将固定时间骨干与动态特征相似性结合，确保时间连续性并适应复杂动作结构；3) 建立循环优化循环，对齐局部特征学习与全局结构对齐。

Result: 在三个数据集上的大量实验表明该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: FIS-OT通过引入局部特征监督和结构先验，有效解决了现有最优传输方法在无监督动作分割中忽视局部信息和受确认偏差影响的问题，取得了较好的实验效果。

Abstract: Unsupervised action segmentation is a challenging task. It involves finding action categories and boundaries in videos without labels. Existing Optimal Transport (OT) methods use global constraints. This causes them to overlook the use of local information. Furthermore, existing Optimal transport architectures are prone to confirmation bias because they overly trust the pseudo-labels they generate. This causes models to learn from noise in the early training stages. To address these issues, we propose FIS-OT. It is a novel Feature-Induced Structured Optimal Transport framework. First, we introduce a Feature Enhanced Generator (FEG) module. It serves as an internal regularizer. By using triplet loss, FEG captures local consistency. It provides robust supervision that is independent of noisy pseudo-labels. Second, we propose a Feature-Induced Residual Structural Prior. This combines a fixed temporal backbone with dynamic feature similarities. This design ensures temporal continuity. It also allows the solver to adapt to complex action structures. Finally, we establish a cyclic optimization loop. This aligns local feature learning with global structural alignment. Extensive experiments on the three datasets show the effectiveness of our method.

</details>


### [153] [SVI2LoD3: Agent-Driven Reconstruction of LoD3 Facade Openings in Semantic 3D City Models from Volunteered Street View Imagery using Large Language and Visual Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29992)
*Elmehdi Kanna,Lukas Arzoumanidis,Huynh Duc An Son Nguyen,Youness Dehbi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端的智能体驱动流水线，用于3D城市模型中立面开口的LoD3重建，采用零样本分割策略生成符合CityGML标准的输出，并引入了一种名为Facade Feature Distance (FFD)的新型评估指标来更好地衡量立面重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有立面重建方法依赖监督式语义分割，需要大量人工标注的训练数据，标注成本高昂；同时传统评估指标如mIoU或FRDS主要基于像素级重叠来评估相似性，无法充分捕捉建筑立面的语义正确性和整体布局。

Method: 提出一个智能体驱动的端到端重建流水线，核心包括：(1) 采用零样本分割策略替代监督式语义分割，大幅减少标注需求；(2) 强制实施正确的部分整体层级结构（partonomic hierarchies），生成符合CityGML标准的LoD3建筑模型；(3) 引入基于视觉Transformer高维特征空间的FFD评估指标，同时衡量语义正确性与建筑布局。

Result: 在eTRIMS数据集上的基准测试中表现出强性能，验证了零样本分割策略的有效性；提出的FFD指标相比传统像素级指标能更全面、更准确地评估立面重建质量。

Conclusion: 该流水线与评估策略共同为语义丰富的3D城市模型的自动化生成与分析提供了实用且可扩展的解决方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: This paper presents an end-to-end, agent-driven pipeline for the LoD3 reconstruction of facade openings in 3D city models, producing directly usable CityGML-conform outputs. In contrast to existing approaches that rely on supervised semantic segmentation and therefore require large amounts of manually annotated training data, the proposed method employs a zero-shot segmentation strategy. This substantially reduces the annotation effort while still achieving strong performance in our benchmark on the eTRIMS dataset. A further key contribution is the enforcement of correct partonomic hierarchies, thereby producing CityGML-conform LoD3 building models. Beyond the reconstruction pipeline itself, this work also introduces a novel evaluation metric for facade reconstruction, termed Facade Feature Distance (FFD). Unlike conventional metrics such as mIoU or FRDS, which assess similarity primarily through pixel-wise overlap, FFD measures distance in a high-level feature space derived from a vision transformer. In doing so, it captures both semantic correctness and architectural layout, providing a more suitable assessment of facade reconstruction quality. The proposed pipeline and evaluation strategy together offer a practical and scalable contribution toward the automated generation and analysis of semantically enriched 3D city models. The developed code is published at: https://github.com/hcu-cml/citydb-SVI2LoD3-ai.

</details>


### [154] [Partition-Aware Unlearning for Removing Spurious Correlations in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29996)
*Aditi Sarker,Nazreen Shah,Rafi Ibn Sultan,Rhongho Jang,Dongxiao Zhu,Prashant Khanduri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PURGE框架，用于构建、评测和缓解大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）中由虚假的对象-背景相关性引发的预测错误，通过结构化数据构建和分区感知解学习技术，在多个模型和基准上显著减少幻觉，同时保持或提升整体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLMs常利用虚假的对象-背景相关而非对象相关的视觉证据进行预测，现有基准难以控制模型预测是真正基于目标对象还是被背景线索误导，缺乏针对性评测和缓解手段。

Method: PURGE框架包含两部分：(1) 结构化数据集构建——提出三种互补的数据构建策略，将样本按对象相关证据和虚假背景线索进行分区，实现对捷径依赖的可控诊断；(2) 分区感知解学习——利用分区结果选择性移除虚假的对象-背景关联，同时保留基于对象的推理能力。

Result: 在LLaVA-1.6-7B、Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct、Qwen3.5-9B及CLIP等多个模型上，以及CHAIR、POPE、Causal-HalBench、MM-SpuBench、AMBER、MMHal和Waterbirds等基准上，PURGE一致性地减少了幻觉和虚假相关驱动误差，在大多数评测场景中保持或提升整体性能。

Conclusion: PURGE既提供了可复用的评测协议，也提供了有效的缓解框架，有助于构建更可靠的LVLMs。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) achieve strong performance across many multimodal tasks; however, they often exploit spurious object-background correlations, resulting in predictions driven by contextual shortcuts rather than object-relevant visual evidence. Despite growing interest in hallucination and robustness evaluation, existing benchmarks provide limited control over whether model predictions are grounded in the target object or induced by correlated background cues. In this work, we introduce PURGE (\underline{P}artition-aware \underline{U}nlearning for \underline{R}emoving spurious-correlation \underline{G}enerated \underline{E}rrors), a framework for constructing, benchmarking, and mitigating spurious-correlation-induced failures in LVLMs. The framework consists of: -- (1) Structured dataset construction wherein we develop three complementary structured data construction strategies that partition examples by object-relevant evidence and spurious background cues, enabling controlled diagnosis of shortcut reliance; and -- (2) Partition-aware unlearning, which uses these partitions to selectively remove spurious object-background associations while preserving object-based reasoning. We evaluate the \algo~framework across multiple LVLMs, including LLaVA-1.6-7B, Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct, and Qwen3.5-9B, together with CLIP as a vision-language encoder, on a diverse suite of benchmarks, including CHAIR, POPE, Causal-HalBench, MM-SpuBench, AMBER, MMHal, and Waterbirds. Our results show that PURGE consistently reduces hallucinations and spurious-correlation-driven errors while maintaining or improving overall performance in most evaluated settings, providing both a reusable evaluation protocol and an effective mitigation framework for more reliable LVLMs.

</details>


### [155] [Discrete Diffusion Bridges for Spatiotemporally Aligned Image Translation and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29997)
*Xing Xie,Jiawei Liu,Shijun Zhou,Huijie Fan,Zhi Han,Yandong Tang,Liangqiong Qu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出离散扩散桥（DDB）框架，解决标准离散扩散在图像翻译和生成中的时空错位问题，通过混合吸收机制和信息引导噪声调度实现高效、高质量的图像生成与翻译。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准离散扩散的前向过程将数据破坏为纯掩码状态，导致双重错位：空间上丢失源图像的结构先验，时间上与推理阶段的"易先难后"解码机制矛盾。

Method: DDB在空间上引入混合吸收机制，将吸收态重新定义为掩码与源token的随机混合，向潜空间注入源图像先验作为空间锚点；在时间上设计信息引导的噪声调度，量化语义变化以优先破坏早期时间步的高信息区域。

Result: 实验验证了DDB在多种生成范式中的通用性和鲁棒性，在文本引导语义操作和纯结构图像翻译中均实现了编辑对齐与结构保真的平衡，并补充了文生图能力，在极低采样步数下保证高质量的鲁棒解码。

Conclusion: DDB有效解决了离散扩散在图像翻译中的时空错位问题，兼顾语义操控与结构保真，同时适用于文生图任务并在低采样步数下保持高质量输出。

Abstract: We propose Discrete Diffusion Bridges (DDB), a novel framework designed to resolve the fundamental spatiotemporal misalignment of standard discrete diffusion in image translation and generation. By corrupting data into a pure mask state via a random schedule, the conventional forward process induces a twofold misalignment: spatially, this pure-mask destination entirely discards the rich structural priors of the source image; temporally, the random masking order inherently contradicts the ``easy-first, hard-last'' decoding mechanism used during inference. To address this, DDB constructs a direct and efficient trajectory between domains. Spatially, we introduce a hybrid absorption mechanism that redefines the absorbing state to a stochastic mixture of mask and source tokens, effectively injecting source prior as spatial anchors into the latent space.
