Temporally, we design an information-guided noise schedule that quantifies semantic variation to prioritize the corruption of high-information regions at earlier timesteps. This ensures the model learns to resolve difficult semantic changes using robust context from invariant regions. Extensive experiments validate the versatility and robustness of our framework across diverse generative paradigms. DDB effectively balances edit alignment with structural fidelity across both text-guided semantic manipulation and pure structural image translation, while inherently complementing text-to-image generation and guaranteeing robust high-quality decoding under extremely low sampling steps. Code and models are available at \href{https://github.com/HKU-HealthAI/DDB}{https://github.com/HKU-HealthAI/DDB}.

</details>


### [156] [OPAL: Orthonormal Prototype Alignment Learning for Interpretable Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30003)
*Ilán Carretero,Gustavo Jesús Angulo,Rocío del Amor,Valery Naranjo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OPAL提出了一种单阶段端到端的可解释分类框架，通过预定义正交基在潜在空间中锚定每个类别的子空间，并利用空间竞争机制实现精确的部位定位，无需辅助损失即可在细粒度基准上超越不可解释模型和现有部位原型方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于原型部位的可解释模型存在训练流程复杂、多阶段进行，且高度依赖辅助正则化来防止原型坍缩的问题，限制了其实际应用和性能表现。

Method: OPAL采用单阶段端到端框架，使用预定义正交基锚定潜在空间，将每个类别嵌入由固定部位原型张成的独立子空间中；通过跨特征图的空间竞争机制强制精确的部位定位，使每个原型在不同图像中持续关注同一语义概念；将分类任务重构为直接的表征对齐问题，从而消除对辅助损失的需求。

Result: 在多个细粒度基准数据集上的大量实验表明，OPAL在性能上超越不可解释的对应方法以及最先进的部位原型方法，同时能提供细粒度的视觉解释，明确揭示驱动每个预测的具体图像区域。

Conclusion: OPAL通过简化训练流程并注入空间竞争机制，成功解决了可解释分类中原型坍缩和多阶段训练的痛点，在保持高性能的同时提供了更加精细和直观的视觉解释能力，是可解释AI领域的有效进展。

Abstract: Prototypical part-based models provide explainable predictions by comparing input regions to learned prototypes. However, current approaches are burdened by complex, multi-stage training pipelines and heavily rely on auxiliary regularization to prevent prototype collapse. To overcome these limitations, we introduce Orthonormal Prototype Alignment Learning (OPAL), a single-stage, end-to-end framework that simplifies interpretable classification. Our approach anchors the latent space using predefined orthonormal bases, embedding each class within a dedicated subspace spanned by fixed part-prototypes. To achieve precise part localization, OPAL enforces spatial competition across feature maps. This mechanism isolates sparse, discriminative regions, directing each prototype to consistently attend to the same semantic concept across different images. By framing classification as a direct representation alignment task, our method eliminates the need for auxiliary losses. Extensive experiments on fine-grained benchmarks demonstrate that OPAL outperforms both its non-interpretable counterparts and state-of-the-art part-prototype methods, delivering granular visual explanations by explicitly revealing the specific image regions driving every prediction. Code is available at https://github.com/ilancarretero/OPAL.

</details>


### [157] [Input-Adaptive Gating of a Dehazing Front-End for On-Device Perception in Smoke-Obscured Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30034)
*Seongjun Kang,Ishaan Garg,Vishnu Bharadwaj*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了一个消防员辅助管道中"先增强后任务"的两阶段视觉管线，发现去雾网络在浓烟下能提升边缘检测精度，但在清晰或轻度烟雾下反而损害性能。因此提出基于暗通道估计的阈值门控机制，仅在必要时执行去雾，在保持精度的同时大幅降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两阶段视觉流水线通常假设"更干净的输入产生更好的输出"，将增强网络置于任务网络之前。然而这一假设缺乏系统性验证，尤其在资源受限的边缘设备上，无条件执行增强可能既损害下游任务精度又浪费宝贵的计算资源。

Method: 构建一个用于Raspberry Pi 4的去雾-边缘检测两级管道：355K参数的去雾网络（float版PSNR达18.60 dB）和23K参数的边缘检测器（ODS 0.738）。提出基于暗通道雾度估计阈值的门控机制（10.1 ms测试），仅在雾度超过阈值时启用去雾器，模型经TensorFlow Lite量化为UINT8。

Result: 去雾在浓烟下提升边缘提取，但在清晰和轻雾帧上反而降低性能，因为去雾器丢弃的细节多于雾所遮蔽的。门控策略在四个雾度级别上平均ODS达0.675，优于始终去雾（0.664）和从不考虑去雾（0.630）；平均每帧时间从569 ms降至321 ms；清晰帧帧率从1.8 FPS提升至9 FPS（5倍提升）。

Conclusion: "更干净的输入产生更好输出"的假设并不总是成立，无条件执行增强会损害下游任务性能。自适应门控是两阶段视觉管线中兼顾精度与效率的关键设计原则，尤其适用于资源受限的边缘部署场景。

Abstract: Two-stage vision pipelines often place an enhancement network before a task network, on the assumption that a cleaner input produces a better output. We evaluate this in a firefighter assistance pipeline, where a dehazer precedes an edge detector that renders smoke-filled rooms as structural outlines. Both were designed for a Raspberry Pi 4, at 355K and 23K parameters, and quantized to UINT8 via TensorFlow Lite. The float dehazer reaches 18.60 dB peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) on held-out real smoke against 13.60 dB unprocessed and 17.08 dB for an AOD-Net trained on the same data, and the edge detector reaches an F-measure at optimal dataset scale (ODS) of 0.738, outperforming an optimized Canny's result of 0.692. Dehazing improves edge extraction under dense smoke but degrades it on clear and lightly hazed frames, where the dehazer discards more detail than the haze obscures. We therefore run the dehazer only when a dark channel haze estimate exceeds a threshold, a 10.1 ms test that lets the pipeline save 469.6 ms on the dehazing stage. Averaged over four haze levels, gating is more accurate than either fixed decision, at 0.675 mean ODS against 0.664 for always dehazing and 0.630 for never dehazing. It reduces the mean per-frame time on the Raspberry Pi from 569 ms to 321 ms, and on clear frames increases the frame rate fivefold, from 1.8 to 9 frames per second.

</details>


### [158] [TAKE 85: Testing Audiovisual filmmaKer's intEnt across 85 Hours of Film](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30068)
*Kaishuu Shinozaki-Conefrey,Olivier Pascaud,Robin Courant,Xi Wang,Dimitris Samaras,Vicky Kalogeiton*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TAKE 85，首个面向导演意图理解的多模态基准数据集，包含398部短片（85小时）和专家验证的问答对，系统评估了当前最强多模态大模型在视觉和音频意图理解方面的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型的评估几乎只关注内容理解（发生了什么），而忽略了电影中灯光、色彩、构图、剪辑、对话、音乐和声音等要素所传达的导演意图（为什么这样呈现），这一维度被严重忽视。

Method: 构建了TAKE 85基准，包含398部短片（85小时）及专家验证的问答对，覆盖全局和细粒度的视觉与音频意图；通过受控模态消融实验系统评估多模态推理能力。

Result: 最强模型仅达到58/100的成绩；模型能准确描述事件和叙事，但在推断电影制作决策的沟通作用方面持续失败；消融实验表明没有任何单一输入模态足以独立完成任务。

Conclusion: 导演意图理解是多模态理解中此前被忽视的重要维度，当前所有SOTA多模态模型在该方面能力显著不足，需要多模态信息协同推理才能实现。

Abstract: Films communicate through deliberate creative choices, including lighting, color, composition, editing, dialogue, music, and sound. Humans naturally interpret these signals as directorial intent, yet current multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are evaluated almost exclusively on understanding what happens rather than why it is presented that way. We introduce TAKE 85, the first benchmark for directorial-intent understanding, comprising 398 short films (85 hours) with expert-verified question-answer pairs spanning global and fine-grained visual and audio intent. Through controlled modality ablations, TAKE 85 enables systematic evaluation of multimodal reasoning. Experiments on state-of-the-art MLLMs reveal a substantial gap between perceptual recognition and intentional understanding: while models accurately describe events and narratives, they consistently fail to infer the communicative role of filmmaking decisions. Our results establish directorial intent as a previously overlooked dimension of multimodal understanding: even the strongest model reaches only 58 out of 100, and our ablations show that no input modality is sufficient on its own. All code, Q&As, and models are publicly available from https://github.com/KaiShinozakiConefrey/Take-85

</details>


### [159] [The Nearest Target Is the Wrong One: Target Separation in Arc2Face Identity Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30087)
*Zeynel Tok*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了人脸条件生成模型中身份遗忘的失效问题，发现通过重定向条件嵌入来遗忘身份时，目标选择距离是关键变量。最近邻目标最易导致静默失败（生成结果仍被识别为原身份），而选择最不相似的目标可实现完全遗忘（30/30组成功，重识别率从51.9降至0.0），且不会泄漏到无关身份。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 身份遗忘在生成模型中可能因重定向后输出仍被验证为原人物而静默失败，但现有工作未系统研究重定向目标选择对遗忘效果的影响，尤其是最近邻目标这一最直观选择恰恰最易引发失败。

Method: 以Arc2Face为审计对象，使用锁定的ArcFace协议和投影适配器实现重定向。构建由最难的0.5%符合条件的身份组成的硬邻居压力测试，比较四种目标选择策略（从最近到最不相似），并使用AdaFace独立识别器交叉验证结果。

Result: 四种目标选择策略呈单调响应：在最近硬目标下清洁遗忘仅9/30组，而最不相似目标下达到30/30组完全遗忘。遗忘身份的平均重识别从51.9降至0.0，同时保留性能保持稳定。710/720个最不相似目标生成成功到达所选目标，无泄漏。AdaFace验证结果与ArcFace高度一致（r=0.94）。

Conclusion: 目标分离度是身份遗忘中一阶可操控的设计变量，应在报告和评估中明确纳入。最近邻目标虽然直观但最易引发静默遗忘失败，选择足够远的目标可显著提升遗忘可靠性。

Abstract: Unlearning an identity from a face-conditioned generator by redirecting its conditioning embedding can silently fail if the redirected output is still verified as the original person. We show that this failure depends on a controllable choice of how far the redirection target lies from the forget identity in recognition space, and that the most intuitive target, the nearest neighbour, is the one most likely to cause it. We audit Arc2Face with a locked ArcFace protocol and a projection adapter that redirects identity conditioning before generation. On a hard-neighbour stress test built from the hardest 0.5% of eligible identities, four target-selection policies show a monotonic response: clean forgetting rises from 9/30 groups under the nearest hard target to 30/30 under the least similar one. Mean forget-identity re-identification falls from 51.9 to 0.0 while mean retention stays flat. This reflects successful redirection rather than outputs becoming unverifiable: 710 of 720 least-sim-hard generations arrive at the chosen target, with no leakage to unrelated identities. Re-verifying identical images with an independent recogniser (AdaFace) preserves that trend, correlating at r=0.94, arguing against a verifier artefact. Target separation is thus a first-order, reportable design variable for identity unlearning.

</details>


### [160] [A Hybrid State-Space Approach for Census-Tract Population Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30094)
*Jackson R. Ye,Alexandre V. Morozov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MambaPop，一种基于状态空间混合架构（MambaVision）的人口估算方法。与传统方法将人口分配到均匀栅格不同，MambaPop将每个行政单元渲染为多边形掩膜的卫星图像，将街区级人口估算视为序列建模问题，直接学习行政单元图像与其人口标签的映射，消除了人口分解步骤。在全美约84,000个2020年人口普查区上，MambaPop的MAE为1,141人，与最强卷积基线YOLOv11（MAE 1,122）相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有卫星图像人口估算方法存在明显缺陷：主流系统（如WorldPop和LandScan）依赖辅助数据构建权重面将人口普查计数分解到栅格单元，会引入系统性空间偏差；基于CNN的方法也丢弃了人口普查实际采集时所用的行政单元结构。作者希望弥补这一空白，直接用行政单元自身的图像学习人口。

Method: MambaPop将每个行政单元（普查区）渲染为单个多边形掩膜的卫星图像，将其图像块视为序列，通过混合状态空间-注意力架构（MambaVision骨干网络）进行序列建模，将行政单元图像直接与其人口标签配对，完全消除人口分解步骤。

Result: 在2020年人口普查的全美约84,000个连续美国普查区上，MambaPop实现每区MAE为1,141人，与最强卷积基线YOLOv11（MAE 1,122）性能相当。

Conclusion: MambaPop首次直接从行政单元自身图像学习人口，也是首个将基于状态空间（Mamba）的混合架构应用于人口估算任务的方法，在匹配最强卷积基线性能的同时，避免了传统方法中由分解步骤引入的系统性空间偏差。

Abstract: Sequence models---the architecture family behind large language models and, increasingly, state-of-the-art image recognition---have redefined how machines learn from high-dimensional data. Yet population estimation from satellite imagery, a task that underpins infrastructure planning, public health, and disaster response, has scarcely benefited: leading systems still bind population to a uniform raster, disaggregating census counts onto grid cells through weighting surfaces built from ancillary data (e.g., in WorldPop and LandScan), which can introduce systematic spatial bias, and predicting population per grid cell with convolutional neural networks. In this approach, the administrative-unit structure in which the census was actually collected is discarded. We close this gap with MambaPop, which renders each administrative unit as a single polygon-masked satellite image and treats tract-level population estimation as a sequence-modeling problem over its image patches, pairing each tract image directly with its population label and eliminating the disaggregation step entirely. Built on the hybrid state-space--attention MambaVision backbone, MambaPop is, to our knowledge, the first method to learn population directly from an administrative unit's own image as well as the first to apply a state-space based (Mamba) hybrid architecture to the population estimation task. Across all $\sim$84{,}000 contiguous-US census tracts of the 2020 census, MambaPop attains a mean absolute error (MAE) of $1{,}141$ persons per tract, matching the strongest convolutional baseline (YOLOv11, MAE $1{,}122$).

</details>


### [161] [Aligning Multi-Trajectory Supervision with Policy Optimization for VLA Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30122)
*Tian Zhang,Zhuo Huang,Hongrui Ye,Yu Wu,Zengmao Wang,Kaixuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个将多轨迹监督与策略优化对齐的VLA驾驶框架，通过约束增广轨迹到可行域流形附近、采用Pareto最优准则过滤冲突样本，并引入可行性优先优势分配和动态蒸馏机制，最终在NAVSIM v1/v2上达到91.4 PDMS和89.1 EPDMS，恢复440个失败场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA驾驶方法中，多轨迹模仿学习与GRPO结合的轨迹选择对最终性能至关重要，但高分轨迹可能改善模仿却因优势估计与当前策略可行行为分布不一致而降低GRPO效果，导致更新偏离安全合规行为。

Method: 提出一个对齐多轨迹监督与策略优化的框架：(1) 将增广轨迹约束到真实可行区域的邻近流形；(2) 用Pareto最优准则替代传统聚合评分，仅保留非支配候选以过滤冲突样本；(3) 引入可行性优先优势分配和动态蒸馏两个互补机制，前者按无roups可行性构成调整Pareto信用并引导不可行组朝向安全参考，后者在各轮精炼中更新教师轨迹持续传递有效监督。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1和v2上分别达到91.4 PDMS和89.1 EPDMS（单轨迹推理），恢复658个初始失败场景中的440个，比原始GRPO基线高11.1%。

Conclusion: 通过从源头过滤不可行噪声轨迹、并在策略优化中自适应地吸收扩展后的轨迹监督，该框架能有效将多轨迹监督优势转化为安全合规的驾驶策略改进。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) driving methods increasingly combine multi-trajectory imitation learning with group-relative policy optimization (GRPO), making trajectory selection critical to final performance. However, some high-scoring trajectories that improve imitation can degrade subsequent GRPO by inducing advantage estimates misaligned with the current policy's feasible behavior distribution, driving updates away from safe and compliant behaviors. To address this, we propose a novel framework that aligns multi-trajectory supervision with policy optimization. To address the policy gradient bias induced by infeasible noisy trajectories outside the feasible region, augmented trajectories are constrained to a neighboring manifold of the ground-truth feasible region, and a Pareto-optimality criterion is adopted in place of the conventional aggregate score, retaining only non-dominated candidates and thereby filtering out conflicting samples at the source. To ensure that expanded trajectory supervision is effectively absorbed during policy optimization, we introduce two complementary mechanisms: feasibility-first advantage assignment and dynamic distillation. The former adapts Pareto credit to the feasibility composition of each rollout group and guides fully infeasible groups toward safe references. The latter updates teacher trajectories across refinement rounds to continually transfer useful supervision. Together, they progressively translate the benefits of expanded supervision into policy improvement. On NAVSIM v1 and v2, our method achieves 91.4 PDMS and 89.1 EPDMS, respectively, under single-trajectory inference, and recovers 440 of 658 initially failed scenes, 11.1\% higher than the original GRPO baseline.

</details>


### [162] [Efficient and High-Quality Depth Estimation via Pixel-Space Diffusion with Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30129)
*Bingde Liu,Wu Ran,Jinglei Zhang,Huanhuan Yuan,Chao Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Lapis，一种基于线性注意力的像素空间生成框架，通过单步扩散实现高效高保真单目深度估计，在1080P和1440P分辨率下分别将推理延迟降低7.6倍和10.9倍，同时保持最先进的精度和边界清晰度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式框架虽在单目深度估计中取得显著成果，但标准注意力的O(N²)复杂度和多步去噪过程在高分辨率应用中带来难以承受的计算成本，亟需兼顾效率与精度的解决方案。

Method: Lapis采用粗到细的层次结构：(1) Patch级一致性模块通过整合语义和空间先验恢复结构一致性；(2) Pixel级细化模块利用跳过连接对应的像素对应关系恢复锐利几何边界；(3) 利用流形假设采用直接x预测策略，通过目标干净数据流形来缓解单步扩散固有的采样噪声。

Result: 在多个基准上的广泛评估表明，Lapis在各种分辨率下均实现最先进的精度和边界锐利度，相比之前的SOTA生成模型，在1080P分辨率下推理延迟减少7.6倍，在1440P分辨率下减少10.9倍。

Conclusion: Lapis验证了线性注意力与单步扩散在生成式深度估计任务中的可行性，通过层次化粗到细设计和x预测策略有效解决了直接应用线性注意力和单步预测所导致的结构不一致、细节丢失和噪声问题，为高分辨率深度估计提供了高效且高质量的生成式解决方案。

Abstract: This work presents $\textbf{Lapis}$, a $\textbf{l}$inear-$\textbf{a}$ttention-based $\textbf{pi}$xel-$\textbf{s}$pace generative framework that achieves efficient and high-fidelity depth estimation with one-step diffusion. While generative frameworks have significantly advanced monocular depth estimation with superior detail fidelity, the $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$ complexity of standard attention and the multi-step denoising process introduce prohibitive computational costs when scaling them to high-resolution image applications. Although linear attention and one-step prediction are intuitively viable, directly applying them leads to poor structural consistency, detail loss, and noise. Lapis rectifies these limitations through a coarse-to-fine hierarchy. Specifically, a Patch-level Consistency Module restores structural coherence by integrating semantic and spatial priors. Subsequently, a Pixel-level Refinement Module recovers sharp geometric boundaries via skip-connection-based pixel correspondence. Furthermore, to mitigate sampling noise inherent in one-step diffusion, we leverage the manifold assumption and adopt a direct $\mathbf{x}$-prediction strategy to target the clean data manifold. Extensive evaluations on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that Lapis consistently achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) accuracy and boundary sharpness across various resolutions, reducing inference latency by up to 7.6$\times$ at 1080P and 10.9$\times$ at 1440P resolution compared to previous SOTA generative models.

</details>


### [163] [Repeatability Characterisation and Error Budget of a Consumer Structured-Light Scanner for 3-D Wound Geometry:A Rigid-Phantom Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30143)
*Pushkal Kumar,Aadit Aggarwal,Karlen Aleksanyan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文表征了手持式消费级结构光扫描仪在重建三维创面几何描述符时的测量误差。通过9个部位、23次重复扫描发现，重建表面积95%重复性限值高达4.8倍因子，44/45个描述符的组内相关系数低于0.50。误差预算显示操作者捕获的表面范围是主要误差来源，仅创面体积是仪器主导误差。研究还指出未分割管线需克服约2.1的信噪比下限，而统计异常值剔除会引入约11%的系统性面积偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 创面三维几何测量在临床愈合评估中具有重要价值，但手持式消费级扫描仪的测量误差从未被系统表征。刚性创面护理体模可确保重复扫描间差异纯粹源于测量误差，为量化仪器精度、误差来源及管线可复现性提供理想实验条件。

Method: 使用单台手持式结构光扫描仪对9个刚性创面体模部位进行23次重复扫描，利用刚性体模不可变形的特性将所有重复差异归为测量误差。通过计算95%重复性限值、组内相关系数（ICC）、误差预算分解（固定分析区域以隔离传感器与重建误差），并比较四周期面积缩减比率，评估各管线组成部分的误差贡献。

Result: 表面积95%重复性限值为4.8倍因子（CI: 3.0-7.0），44/45描述符ICC<0.50，248种描述符-管线组合无一达0.75。误差预算显示操作者捕获面积贡献了75%表面积、91%包络面积和95%包围盒对角线的方差，仅包络体积以48%多数归因于仪器误差。标准化分析区域可将重复性限值降至2.13，统计离群值剔除引入约11%系统面积亏损。

Conclusion: 手持式消费级扫描仪在重建创面描述符时存在极大的测量不确定度，主要误差来源是操作者捕获的表面范围而非传感器本身，因此仅升级传感器收益有限。临床常用的四周期面积缩减比率2.1是未分割管线须克服的噪声下限，而非创面面积可重复性的真实度量；标准化分析区域和谨慎处理异常值剔除是改善可重复性的关键方向。

Abstract: We characterise the measurement error of a free- hand consumer structured-light scanner used to derive three- dimensional geometric wound descriptors. Rigid wound-care phantoms cannot change, so every difference between repeat scans of one site is measurement error; all repeats come from a single scanner unit, nine sites and 23 scans, so this charac- terises one instrument. The 95 percent repeatability limit for reconstructed surface area is a factor of 4.8, with a confidence interval from 3.0 to 7.0, so rescanning an unchanged site can shift the reading from a 79 percent decrease to a 377 percent increase. Forty-four of 45 descriptors fall below an intraclass correlation of 0.50, and none of 248 descriptor and pipeline combinations reaches 0.75. An error budget formed by holding the analy- sis region fixed, leaving sensor and reconstruction untouched, bounds the share of variance attributable to how much surface the operator captured at 75 percent for surface area, 91 percent for hull area and 95 percent for bounding-box diagonal; only hull volume is majority instrumental, at 48 percent, so a better sensor would buy little. No wound is delineated anywhere in the chain, so the comparison against the four-week area reduction used clinically to predict healing, a ratio near 2.1, is a lower bound on the noise an unsegmented pipeline must overcome, not a measurement of wound-area reproducibility. Standardising the analysis region cuts the limit to 2.13, meeting that ratio rather than clearing it. Statistical outlier removal imposes a measured systematic area deficit near 11 percent.

</details>


### [164] [CedarCypress3D: an annotated UAV-LiDAR dataset of individual trees in planted cedar and cypress forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30149)
*Katsuto Shimizu,Fumiaki Kitahara,Tomohiro Nishizono,Hideki Saito,Masayoshi Takahashi,Shingo Obata,Shunsuke Tei,Naoyuki Furuya,Tomoya Goto,Eiji Kodani,Yusuke Yamada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CedarCypress3D，一个在日本柳杉和日本扁柏人工林中采集的UAV-LiDAR点云数据集，包含34个圆形样地、1,627棵人工标注的树木，以及部分样地的地面LiDAR数据和语义标签，为个体树分割和语义分割研究提供高质量标注数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来机器学习方法对点云个体树分割的需求日益增长，但公开可用的、带标注的温带森林UAV-LiDAR数据集十分有限，这制约了相关算法的开发与评估。

Method: 在日本的柳杉和扁柏人工林中，通过UAV-LiDAR采集点云并结合野外调查数据，在34个圆形样地（两个地形特征不同的地点）中人工标注了1,627棵树，并在22个样地中补充采集地面LiDAR数据，同时为树点分配茎干与非茎干的语义标签。

Result: 构建了一个包含UAV-LiDAR点云、野外测量数据、部分地面LiDAR数据以及语义标签的高质量标注数据集CedarCypress3D，并已公开提供（DOI: 10.5281/zenodo.22168721）。

Conclusion: CedarCypress3D为温带人工林中个体树实例分割和语义分割方法的发展与评估提供了高质量数据支撑，同时可支持树木属性预测和多平台LiDAR分析等研究。

Abstract: Individual tree measurements derived from Light Detection and Ranging (LiDAR) mounted on Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAV) provide valuable information for forest inventory, ecosystem monitoring, and sustainable forest management. Recent advancements in machine learning have increased the demand for annotated datasets to develop and evaluate point cloud-based approaches, especially for individual tree segmentation. However, publicly available annotated UAV-LiDAR datasets in temperate forests are limited. In this article, we present CedarCypress3D, a manually annotated UAV-LiDAR dataset collected in Japanese cedar (Cryptomeria japonica) and Japanese cypress (Chamaecyparis obtusa) plantations in Japan. The dataset consists of UAV-LiDAR point clouds and field survey measurements from 34 circular plots across two sites with different topographic characteristics, along with terrestrial LiDAR point clouds available for a subset of 22 plots. A total of 1,627 trees were measured in the census field survey and manually annotated to match the corresponding trees in the UAV-LiDAR point clouds. For the subset of plots with terrestrial LiDAR data, semantic labels (i.e., stem and non-stem) were additionally assigned to tree points in the UAV-LiDAR data. CedarCypress3D provides high-quality annotated UAV-LiDAR data for developing and evaluating individual tree instance segmentation and semantic segmentation methods in temperate planted forests. The dataset can also support research on tree attribute prediction and multi-platform LiDAR analysis. The dataset is publicly available at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.22168721.

</details>


### [165] [AI-enabled Low-Cost 3D Maize Ear Morphometry Platform at Breeding Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30161)
*Therin Young,Elijah Rodriguez,Lisa Coffey,Talukder Zaki Jubery,Adarsh Krishnamurthy,Patrick Schnable,Baskar Ganapathysubramanian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文开发并验证了一条低成本玉米果穗3D表型高通量分析流程：利用消费级单反相机+电动转台拍摄20秒视频，通过COLMAP初始化NeRF重建水密3D网格。在300个果穗中250个（83.3%）通过自动质控，骨架长度与卡尺测量高度一致（R²=0.964），凸包体积与水置换法高度吻合（R²=0.982），硬件成本约607美元，单穗操作时间从5分钟降至1分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玉米果穗几何特征（长度、宽度、曲率、体积）与产量和灌浆结果密切相关，但现有高通量表型分析流程受限于成本高、劳动密集、依赖专用硬件，亟需开发低成本、自动化的果穗3D重建与测量方案。

Method: 使用消费级DSLR在电动转台上、均匀LED照明下拍摄20秒视频；通过多种子COLMAP程序初始化相机位姿，构建NeRF重建水密3D网格；利用每帧可见的已知直径圆柱支架实现自动公制缩放和质量控制；应用于300个果穗的多样化玉米自交系群体。

Result: 300个果穗中250个（83.3%）通过自动处理和质控；骨架长度与卡尺测量R²=0.964（RMSE=4.68mm）；凸包体积与水置换法R²=0.982（RMSE=5.26mL）；残差长度误差随果穗曲率增大而增大（源于卡尺测弦长vs骨架测弧长的定义差异）；硬件成本约607美元，操作时间从约5分钟降至1分钟/穗，下游处理全自动运行。

Conclusion: 该平台实现了育种规模级的3D玉米果穗表型分析，以极低成本和高自动化程度建立了一套完整流程，为大规模育种中的3D果穗表型研究提供了基础。

Abstract: Maize ear geometry (length, width, curvature, and volume) is closely tied to yield and grain-filling outcomes, but existing high-throughput phenotyping pipelines remain constrained by the cost, labor, and specialized hardware they require. We developed and validated a low-cost pipeline that reconstructs a watertight 3-D mesh of a maize ear from a single 20-second video captured with a consumer-grade DSLR on a motorized turntable under uniform LED illumination. Camera poses from a multi-seed COLMAP procedure initialize a Neural Radiance Field (NeRF), and a cylindrical holder of known diameter, visible in every frame, provides automatic metric scaling with downstream geometric quality control. Applied to 300 ears spanning a diverse maize inbred panel, 250 (83.3%) passed automated processing and quality control. Skeleton length agreed with manual caliper measurements across all 250 ears (R^2 = 0.964, RMSE = 4.68 mm), and convex-hull volume agreed with water-displacement volume on a 15-ear subset spanning the full size range (R^2 = 0.982, RMSE = 5.26 mL). Residual length error grew with ear curvature, whereas bounding-box height, which records the same straight-line chord as calipers, showed no such trend; the discrepancy therefore originates in the measurement definition, since calipers record the chord while skeleton length traces the geodesic arc. The capture hardware costs approximately 607 USD, and operator involvement fell from roughly five minutes to one minute per ear, with all downstream processing running unattended. The platform provides a foundation for breeding-scale 3-D ear phenotyping.

</details>


### [166] [ATGS: Anchored Temporal Gaussian Splatting for Long Volumetric Video Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30184)
*Jiahao Wu,Jie Liang,Die Hu,Jiayu Yang,Kaiqiang Xiong,Xiang Li,Xiaoyun Zheng,Chao Wang,Ronggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出ATGS，一种基于高斯泼溅的体积视频重建框架。核心思想是通过时间条件锚点组织高斯原语，减少长时复杂运动的复杂性，并采用时间窗口策略提升可扩展性和时间一致性，配合多级锚点特征约束高斯生成，在长序列复杂运动场景中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有体积视频方法在处理长序列和复杂运动时存在时间不稳定性和视觉伪影问题，而直接对单个高斯原语进行长时复杂运动追踪本质上是不可靠的。

Method: 提出基于高斯泼溅的框架ATGS：(1) 围绕时间条件锚点组织高斯原语，锚点定位其空间和时间支持域，降低长距运动复杂度；(2) 设计时间窗口策略，仅激活与查询时间相关的锚点，提升可扩展性和时间一致性；(3) 设计紧凑的多级锚点特征集，包含全局特征、局部空间特征和局部时间特征，联合约束高斯生成。

Result: 大量实验表明，ATGS在长序列、复杂运动的体积视频上持续优于现有方法，并有项目页面提供代码。

Conclusion: 通过锚点化高斯组织与时间窗口机制，该框架有效解决了长序列复杂运动场景下的时间稳定性和视觉质量问题，为体积视频重建提供了可行的新思路。

Abstract: Volumetric video enables immersive free viewpoint rendering of dynamic real world scenes, yet existing methods struggle with long sequences and complex motions, often leading to temporal instability and visual artifacts. To address these challenges, we propose \ourname, a Gaussian splatting based framework for volumetric video reconstruction. Our key insight is that explicitly tracking long term complex motion with individual Gaussian primitives is inherently unstable. Instead, we organize Gaussians around time conditioned anchors that localize their spatial and temporal support, thereby reducing long range motion complexity. We further introduce a temporal windowing strategy to activate only anchors relevant to the queried time, which improves scalability and temporal coherence. In addition, to ensure spatial and temporal stability, we design a compact set of multi level anchor features that encode global features, local spatial features, and local temporal features, jointly constraining Gaussian generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that \ourname \ consistently outperforms prior methods on long sequence volumetric videos with complex motions. Project page: https://github.com/WuJH2001/ATGS.

</details>


### [167] [NoisEasier: Test-Time Noise Optimization for Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30194)
*Yujiang Pu,Yu Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出NoisEasier，一种测试时缩放框架，通过可微奖励引导的噪声优化来提升文本到视频（T2V）生成的组合对齐能力，无需修改底层模型，在属性绑定、物体交互和数字理解等挑战性维度上实现了超过10%的平均提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2V生成模型在细粒度组合对齐（如属性绑定、空间关系、物体交互）上表现不佳；虽然基于奖励的微调能改善对齐，但容易产生奖励欺骗且难以适应新的提示分布。

Method: 提出NoisEasier框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 高效短步生成器与多目标奖励公式结合，实现实际推理预算下的稳定测试时优化；(2) 关键洞察是联合优化整个随机轨迹而非仅优化初始潜变量，可加速奖励收敛并改善组合对齐，且额外计算和时间成本可忽略不计。

Result: 在VBench和T2V-CompBench基准上的实验表明，该方法在多个骨干网络上均取得一致性改进，在属性绑定、物体交互和数字理解等挑战性维度上平均提升超过10%。

Conclusion: NoisEasier既可作为奖励微调的灵活替代方案，也可作为其互补增强手段，确立了测试时缩放作为可控文本到视频生成的有效范式。

Abstract: Diffusion models have recently advanced text-to-video (T2V) generation, yet they still struggle with fine-grained compositional alignment, such as attribute binding, spatial relations, and object interactions. While reward-based fine-tuning improves alignment, it is susceptible to reward hacking and adapts poorly to new prompt distributions. In this work, we propose NoisEasier, a test-time scaling framework that improves T2V generation through differentiable reward-guided noise optimization without modifying the underlying model. By combining efficient short-step generators with a multi-objective reward formulation, NoisEasier enables stable and practical test-time optimization under realistic inference budgets. Our key insight is that jointly optimizing the entire stochastic trajectory accelerates reward convergence and improves compositional alignment over optimizing only the initial latent, with negligible additional computational and time cost. Experiments on VBench and T2V-CompBench demonstrate consistent improvements across multiple backbones, achieving over 10% average gains on challenging dimensions such as attribute binding, object interaction, and numeracy. Overall, NoisEasier serves as both a flexible alternative and a complementary enhancement to reward-based fine-tuning, establishing test-time scaling as an effective paradigm for controllable text-to-video generation.

</details>


### [168] [DICS: Exploring Data Intrinsic Consistency for Visual Instruction Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30209)
*Yuyang Hong,Jinhui Guo,Jiaqi Gu,Lubin Fan,Ruixiang Wang,Kun Ding,Yue Wu,Shiming Xiang,Jieping Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出数据内在一致性（DIC）自评分指标，用于量化视觉指令数据中样本内部组件间的一致性，并在此基础上构建DICS自适应数据选择方法，在25%数据量下超越全量数据微调效果，并在多模态指令数据选择任务上达到当前最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉指令数据选择方法多依赖分布多样性或启发式过滤，忽略了单个样本内部组件之间的一致性（即样本内连贯性），这一视角的缺失成为VLM数据高效选择的重要瓶颈。

Method: 提出DIC指标，包含两个模块：视觉信息一致性（VIC）评估视觉内容与指令的对齐程度，响应信息一致性（RIC）评估响应相对于指令的连贯性。基于DIC构建DICS自适应数据选择方法，在固定比率约束下优化样本内一致性与全局分布多样性之间的权衡。

Result: DICS在多种数据集规模和模型架构下均优于现有最先进方法，仅使用LLaVA-1.5-665K数据集的25%即超越全量微调效果。此外构建了DICS-6M（600万样本多模态指令语料），在InternVL3-8B-Instruct上仅用不到25%训练数据即可达到其官方性能的94.52%。

Conclusion: 样本内一致性是视觉指令数据选择中的关键信号，DICS通过自评分方式有效平衡样本质量与分布多样性，实现了高效且可扩展的数据选择，为大规模多模态指令数据的高效利用提供了新路径。

Abstract: Visual instruction tuning is crucial for advancing the vision-language alignment and instruction-following capabilities of Vision-Language Models (VLMs). However, identifying optimal subsets under a fixed ratio constraint from rapidly expanding datasets remains a significant bottleneck. While existing methods largely depend on distribution diversity or heuristic filtering, they often overlook the internal coherence within individual samples. To bridge this gap, we propose Data Intrinsic Consistency (DIC), a self-scoring metric designed to quantify the sample-level inter-component consistency. DIC consists of two modules: Visual Information Consistency (VIC), evaluating the alignment between visual content and instructions, and Response Information Consistency (RIC), assessing response coherence relative to the instruction. Building upon DIC, we introduce Data Intrinsic Consistency Selection (DICS), an adaptive data selection method that optimizes the trade-off between high intra-sample consistency and global distributional diversity under varying data budgets. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DICS consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods across diverse dataset scales and model architectures, surpassing full-dataset fine-tuning while using only 25% of the LLaVA-1.5-665K data. We further curate DICS-6M, a 6M-sample multi-modal instruction corpus that enables the largest-scale visual instruction selection study to date; remarkably, DICS reaches 94.52\% of the official InternVL3-8B-Instruct performance using less than 25\% of its reported training data. Code can be seen at https://github.com/cqu-student/DICS

</details>


### [169] [Towards a Joint Khmer Text Recognition and Word Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30213)
*Marry Kong,Rina Buoy,Sovisal Chenda,Nguonly Taing,Masakazu Iwamura,Koichi Kise*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种统一的联合高棉语文本识别与分词框架，使用CTC解码器实现快速并行解码，通过开关参数控制是否输出分词边界，消除了传统"先识别后分词"两阶段流水线的错误累积和延迟问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高棉语不使用可见的词分隔符，传统的识别-分词两阶段流水线需要两个独立的顺序模型，这不仅容易累积错误，而且在大规模文档处理中引入显著延迟。因此需要一个统一的模型同时完成文本识别和分词任务。

Method: 提出一个统一的联合模型，使用连接时序分类（CTC）解码器实现快速并行解码，通过设置参数b=1表示需要分词，b=0表示不需要分词，使同一模型可以灵活地在两种模式间切换。

Result: 在涵盖文档、场景和手写图像等多种文档模态的基准数据集上，该模型不仅能识别文档图像中的字符，还能定位词边界，消除了传统顺序流水线中额外分词步骤的需求。

Conclusion: 所提出的联合框架在多个基准数据集上验证了有效性，证明统一的识别-分词模型可以替代传统的两阶段流水线，在保证识别能力的同时实现词边界定位，从而降低错误率并减少处理延迟。

Abstract: Text recognition, or extracting electronic text from document images, has been indispensable for knowledge retrieval tasks, such as retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). For Khmer, extracted text is subject to an extra word segmentation step, as Khmer does not use any visible word delimiters to denote word boundaries. Thus, a recognition-then-segmentation pipeline for Khmer requires two separate sequential models; this is not only error-prone but also adds significant latency for large-scale document processing. This paper proposes a novel joint Khmer text recognition and word segmentation framework in a unified model. The proposed model, using a connectionist-temporal-classification (CTC) decoder for fast, parallel decoding, can be instructed to recognize Khmer text with ($b=1$) and without ($b=0$) word segmentation. Experimental results on different benchmark datasets of different document modalities (document, scene, and handwritten images) show that the proposed model can not only recognize characters in document images but also locate word boundaries, removing the need for an extra word segmentation step in a conventional sequential pipeline.

</details>


### [170] [Amortized Anchor Refinement for Deployable Continuous-Time 4D Gaussian Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30218)
*Jingong Chen,Qingwen Zhang,Sanghyeon Jun,Chulwoo Pack,Kyle Gao,Kwanghee Won*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"摊销锚点细化"（Amortized Anchor Refinement）方法，将冻结骨干网络的前馈预测与短时优化相结合，在固定计算预算下实现XR头显上的连续时间4D重建，并通过持久同调约束剪枝不稳定高斯点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间4D重建在独立XR头显上不可行：逐场景优化需要部署端无法承受的计算量，降低预算会导致重建崩溃而非渐进退化；前馈预测虽快但难以恢复场景特定细节。

Method: 1) 使用冻结骨干网络预测初始高斯表示；2) 在固定计算预算下进行短时优化以实现场景特化，并通过容量下限保持表示密度；3) 训练-free阶段应用持久同调约束，剪枝不稳定高斯点并保留拓扑持久结构；4) 将轨迹直接流式传输为场景流。

Result: 在Stage-Capture基准上达到24.31±2.22dB；部署实验证明可在单张消费级GPU上于目标预算内完成重建，并在独立XR头显上实现播放。

Conclusion: 摊销锚点细化成功弥合了前馈预测与逐场景优化之间的差距，使高质量连续时间4D重建在独立XR头显上变得可行。

Abstract: Continuous-time 4D reconstruction remains impractical on standalone XR headsets. Per-scene optimization demands deployment-infeasible compute, and lower budgets cause collapse rather than degrade gradually. Feed-forward prediction is fast, but struggle to recover scene-specific detail. We present Amortized Anchor Refinement, which uses a frozen backbone to predict an initial Gaussian representation and a short optimization to specialize it under a fixed compute budget, with a capacity floor preserving representational density. A training-free stage then applies a persistent-homology constraint to prune unstable Gaussians while preserving topologically persistent structures, and streams the resulting trajectories directly as scene flow. On the Stage-Capture benchmark, Amortized Anchor Refinement achieves 24.31$\pm$2.22dB, while our deployment experiments demonstrate reconstruction within the target budget on a single consumer GPU and playback on a standalone XR headset.

</details>


### [171] [Semantic-Spatial Discriminability Enhancement for Generalized Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30233)
*Kaiyan Lei,Xu-Yao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出语义-空间判别性增强（SSDE）框架用于广义视觉定位（GVG）任务，通过细粒度语义判别增强（SeDE）模块和空间判别增强（SpDE）模块，在10个数据集上取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖全局语义匹配或粗粒度区域交互，在复杂的多目标场景中难以区分视觉相似的实例，无法建立稳定的实例级决策边界。

Method: 提出SSDE框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) SeDE模块利用空间引导的交叉注意力解耦细粒度的目标相关视觉属性，并与文本主体语义融合，增强查询表示的语义判别性；(2) SpDE模块建模实例中心密度图以表征目标的空间分布，并将其作为辅助监督信号显式构建空间域的实例分离结构。

Result: 在经典和广义视觉定位任务的10个数据集上取得了优越的性能。

Conclusion: SSDE框架有效解决了多目标场景下视觉相似目标混淆的问题，同时提升了跨模态理解和实例级定位的判别能力。

Abstract: Generalized Visual Grounding (GVG) task aims to localize targets in an image based on referring expressions, extends the classical visual grounding paradigm by integrating multi-target and non-target scenarios. Previous methods typically rely on global semantic matching or coarse-grained region interactions for localization, where the discriminative cues are primarily derived from sentence-level semantics or regional context. In complex multi-target scenarios, such approaches tend to confuse visually similar targets, making it difficult to establish stable instance-level decision boundaries. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a novel Semantic-Spatial Discriminability Enhancement (SSDE) framework for generalized visual grounding, which aims to enhance the discriminative ability on fine-grained semantics and spatial localization, improving both cross-modal understanding and instance-level grounding. Specifically, to enhance the semantic discriminability of query representations at the fine-grained level, we propose a Semantic Discriminability Enhancement (SeDE) module, which leverages spatially guided cross-attention to disentangle fine-grained target-relevant visual attributes and integrates them with the textual subject semantics. Furthermore, to strengthen the spatial discriminability of the referred targets, we introduce a Spatial Discriminability Enhancement (SpDE) module, which models an instance center density map to characterize the spatial distribution of targets, and explicitly constructs instance separation structures in the spatial domain by employing them as an auxiliary supervision signal. Extensive experiments show that SSDE achieves superior performance on ten datasets across both classic and generalized visual grounding tasks.

</details>


### [172] [OPUS: A Simple yet Effective Unified Framework for Open-Vocabulary Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30247)
*Xiaoyan Wei,Zhimin Yao,Ruilin Yang,Wei Zhang,Yong Dai,Yi Zhang,Wei Ge*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OPUS是一个统一的开放词汇检测框架，通过语义丰富的视觉表示（DINOv3-ConvNeXt-B骨干网络）和可扩展的接地监督（SAM3数据引擎），支持文本、视觉示例、混合提示等多种提示方式，在保持简单性的同时达到SOTA性能（COCO 68.1、LVIS-minival 69.2、ODinW35 54.7 AP）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的统一开放词汇检测框架虽然支持异构提示（文本查询、视觉示例及其组合），但往往依赖复杂设计，如重型跨模态融合、分阶段训练和迭代标注流程。作者质疑在更强基础模型时代，这种复杂性是否必要，旨在验证统一OVD能否通过更简单的设计实现。

Method: OPUS采用三部分简单设计：（1）模型架构由基于DINOv3-ConvNeXt-B骨干的语义丰富视觉编码器（含高效混合编码）和提示感知解码器组成，避免提示专用分支以实现统一提示推理；（2）采用单阶段文本-视觉联合训练策略，结合实例级对比对齐（ICA）；（3）基于SAM3的单遍数据引擎提供异构接地监督。

Result: 在COCO、LVIS-minival和ODinW35上，OPUS取得Visual-I SOTA性能，分别为68.1/69.2/54.7 AP，同时保持平衡的文本和视觉G准确率。混合提示从干扰变为互补，优于单独使用文本或视觉提示的效果。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，简单性与强大的统一提示能力可以同时实现，挑战了统一开放词汇检测必须依赖复杂设计的传统观点。

Abstract: Recent unified open-vocabulary detection (OVD) supports heterogeneous prompts, including text queries, visual exemplars, and their combinations, but often rely on increasingly complex designs such as heavy cross-modal fusion, staged training, and iterative annotation pipelines. We revisit whether such complexity is necessary in the era of stronger foundation models. Our finding is that unified OVD can be made substantially simpler with semantic-rich visual representations and scalable grounding supervision. We present OPUS (\textbf{O}pen-vocabulary, \textbf{P}rompt-\textbf{U}nified, \textbf{S}imple), a unified detector supporting text, interactive visual, generic visual, and mixed prompting within one framework. OPUS adopts a simple three-part design. Its model architecture combines a semantic-rich visual encoder, built on a DINOv3-ConvNeXt-B backbone with efficient hybrid encoding, with a prompt-aware decoder that avoids prompt-specific branches for unified prompt reasoning. OPUS is trained with a one-stage text-visual training strategy with Instance-level Contrastive Alignment (ICA), and is supported by a SAM3-based single-pass data engine for heterogeneous grounding supervision. Experiments on COCO, LVIS-minival, and ODinW35 show that OPUS achieves state-of-the-art Visual-I performance, reaching 68.1/69.2/54.7 AP, while maintaining balanced Text and Visual-G accuracy. OPUS also turns mixed prompting from interference into complementarity, improving over text or visual prompt alone. These results show that simplicity and strong unified prompting capability can be achieved together.

</details>


### [173] [Centering before Pruning: Lightweight Geometry Correction for Diversity-Based Visual Token Pruning in LVLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30263)
*Shunjie Wen,Jaeyeon Lee,Dong-Wan Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Cen-Prune方法，通过中心化余弦相似度提高视觉token子集多样性度量的分辨率，同时保留原始空间中的token显著性偏好，以提升大规模视觉语言模型的推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模视觉语言模型（LVLMs）因长且高度冗余的视觉token序列而产生高昂推理成本。基于多样性的剪枝方法依赖余弦相似度选择token子集，但原始视觉token之间的相似度高度集中在正值范围，难以有效区分非冗余token，因此需要改进多样性度量的分辨率。

Method: Cen-Prune方法包含两个关键部分：(1) 使用中心化（centering）后的余弦相似度来度量token子集的多样性，以揭示更丰富的成对结构；(2) 保留原始空间中的token显著性作为互补的token级偏好，因为中心化虽然提升了多样性分辨率，但丢失了原始几何中隐含的全局显著token选择偏好。该方法轻量级、即插即用，不改变底层选择机制。

Result: 在多个图像和视频理解基准以及多种LVLM架构上的大量实验表明，Cen-Prune为现有的基于多样性的剪枝器提供了稳健的性能提升，且计算开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 中心化方法单独使用会降低剪枝性能，但结合原始空间显著性作为互补偏好后，能够有效提升多样性与显著性之间的平衡，从而改进基于多样性剪枝的整体性能。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) incur substantial inference costs due to their long and highly redundant visual-token sequences. Diversity-based pruning mitigates this cost by selecting token subsets based on pairwise cosine similarity. We find, however, that similarities between raw visual tokens are strongly concentrated in the positive range, limiting their ability to distinguish non-redundant tokens. A natural way to improve this resolution is to center token features before computing cosine similarity. Centering indeed reveals a substantially richer pairwise structure, yet unexpectedly degrades pruning performance when used alone. We show that this apparent contradiction arises because the raw geometry does more than represent pairwise diversity: it also implicitly favors globally distinctive tokens, which tend to contain semantically informative content. Centering better resolves subset diversity but loses this useful token-wise preference, revealing that diversity and distinctiveness are entangled in the raw geometry. Based on this analysis, we propose the \textbf{Cen}tered Geometry \textbf{Prune}r (Cen-Prune), which measures subset diversity using centered cosine similarity while retaining raw-space distinctiveness as a complementary token-wise preference. This lightweight, plug-and-play correction leaves the underlying selection mechanism unchanged and incurs negligible computational overhead. Extensive experiments across multiple image- and video-understanding benchmarks and LVLM architectures demonstrate that Cen-Prune provides robust improvements in overall performance across existing diversity-based pruners.

</details>


### [174] [Motion-Saliency Complementary Masked Modeling for Point Cloud Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30279)
*Wei Wang,Yiding Sun,Yuyan Wang,Zhuoyue Zhang,Zhengqiao Li,Dongfu Yin,Chen Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MoSaiC框架，一种基于运动显著性互补掩码建模的自监督点云视频表示学习方法，通过课程运动显著性掩码、法向流运动建模和跨视角令牌一致性预测三个组件，在多个下游任务中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点云视频表示学习对于3D动态场景理解至关重要，现有方法往往未能同时有效捕获外观和运动动态信息，缺乏针对性的自监督学习框架来建模点云视频中的运动显著性。

Method: MoSaiC耦合三个核心组件：(1) 课程运动显著性掩码(CMSM)通过课程调度引导掩码过程聚焦运动显著令牌；(2) 法向流运动建模(NFM)在so(3)李代数中监督每个令牌的局部刚性旋转，提供显式几何运动目标；(3) 跨视角令牌一致性预测(CTCP)在令牌级别强制两个互补掩码视角之间的一致性。

Result: 在动作识别、时序动作分割和点级语义分割等多个下游任务上的大量实验证明了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: MoSaiC通过运动显著性引导的掩码建模和显式几何运动监督，能够有效捕获点云视频中的外观与运动动态，为自监督点云视频表示学习提供了新思路。

Abstract: Point cloud video representation learning is crucial for 3D dynamic scene understanding. In this paper, we propose MoSaiC, a novel Motion-Saliency Complementary masked modeling framework for self-supervised point cloud video representation learning. MoSaiC couples three components: Curriculum Motion-Saliency Masking (CMSM), which guides the masking process toward motion-salient tokens under a curriculum schedule; Normal-Flow Motion (NFM) modeling, which supervises the local rigid rotation of each token in the Lie algebra so(3) as an explicit geometric motion target; and Cross-view Token Consistency Prediction (CTCP), which enforces consistency between two complementary masked views at the token level. Together, these components allow MoSaiC to effectively capture both appearance and motion dynamics. Extensive experiments on multiple downstream tasks, including action recognition, temporal action segmentation, and point-level semantic segmentation, demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [175] [SELECT: SELEctive Context Transfer for Class-Incremental Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30281)
*Avi Gupta,Saurabh Yadav,Koteswar Rao Jerripothula,Tammam Tillo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SELECT方法，通过选择性地从语义相似的历史类别中转移上下文信息来初始化新类别，以解决类增量语义分割中的灾难性遗忘和背景偏移问题，在VOC和ADE20K数据集上分别提升mIoU 2.2%和2.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CISS方法在结合稳定性（保留旧知识）与可塑性（学习新知识）时，往往采用无差别的知识迁移或模糊的初始化方式，这会稀释关键的语义信息，无法有效利用先验知识。

Method: 提出SELECT框架，核心包含两部分：(1) 上下文转移注意力机制(Context Transfer Attention)，从语义相似的少数历史类别中聚合学习到的token，为新类别构建结构化初始化；(2) 受控噪声扰动和基于边距的上下文转移损失(margin-based context-transfer loss)，确保转移不会破坏借用的表示，并强制新类别token与其来源token之间保持分离。

Result: 在Pascal VOC和ADE20K数据集上的大量实验表明，SELECT在mIoU指标上分别超过先前最佳方法2.2%和2.8%，有效应对了稳定性-可塑性困境。

Conclusion: SELECT通过选择性上下文转移机制，将每个新类别锚定在一小组语义相似的历史类别上，实现了更精确的知识复用，为类增量语义分割中的稳定性-可塑性平衡提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Class-Incremental Semantic Segmentation (CISS) is fundamentally challenged by catastrophic forgetting and background shift, where learning new concepts degrades performance on previously seen classes. While existing methods attempt to balance stability (retaining old knowledge) and plasticity (learning new knowledge), they often fail to leverage prior knowledge effectively. These approaches typically rely on indiscriminate knowledge transfer or ambiguous initializations, which can dilute crucial semantic information. To overcome this limitation, we propose SELECT, a novel approach for Selective Context Transfer, which instead grounds each new class in a small set of semantically similar past classes. Its core is a Context Transfer Attention mechanism that aggregates the learned tokens from similar classes into a structured initialization for the new class. To ensure this transfer does not corrupt the borrowed representations, we add a controlled noise perturbation and a margin-based context-transfer loss that enforces separation between the new class token and its source tokens. Extensive experiments on Pascal VOC and ADE20K show that SELECT consistently outperforms prior work, achieving mIoU of 2.2% on VOC and 2.8% on ADE, providing an effective handle on the stability-plasticity dilemma. Code is available at https://github.com/avigupta2798/SELECT.

</details>


### [176] [Dynamic Hub-and-Spoke Memory for Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30294)
*Xinru Jiang,Lin Zhao,Xi Xiao,Yunbei Zhang,Janet Wang,Chenrui Ma,Haolin Li,Yanzhi Wang,Yifan Gong,Octavia Camps*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出D-HSM（动态枢纽-辐条记忆）框架，一种无需训练的流式视频理解方法。它将远处的历史视频转换为结构化文本记忆，保留近期帧作为视觉token，通过实体中心的枢纽-辐条结构进行动态检索和证据扩展，实现任意时间点的视频问答。实验证明该方法在流式和长视频基准上大幅优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频理解的核心挑战在于：需要在连续增长的视觉流中回答任意时刻的问题，既要紧凑地记忆长期历史信息，又要高效检索与问题相关的证据。现有方法难以在长时间跨度上兼顾记忆的紧凑性和检索的有效性。

Method: D-HSM包含三个关键设计：(1) 将选定的历史视频片段转化为带类型的文本观察记录；(2) 以实体为中心的枢纽-辐条（hub-and-spoke）记忆结构存储这些记录，实体作为枢纽，相关证据作为辐条；(3) 回答问题时动态检索紧凑的、问题感知的记忆子集，通过枢纽-辐条链接扩展证据，并与近期视觉窗口结合，输入冻结的VLM进行答案预测。

Result: 在流式和长视频理解基准上的大量实验表明，D-HSM能够持续且显著地提升VLM骨干模型的性能，并优于其他最先进的在线和离线视频理解基线方法。

Conclusion: D-HSM通过结构化文本记忆与视觉近期窗口的有机结合，以及训练自由的动态检索机制，为流式视频理解提供了一种高效、紧致且可扩展的解决方案，验证了"文本化历史记忆+视觉实时窗口"的混合记忆范式在长视频理解中的有效性。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding requires answering questions at arbitrary times over a continuously growing visual stream. The central challenge is to compactly remember long-range history while effectively retrieving question-relevant evidence. We propose Dynamic Hub-and-Spoke Memory (D-HSM), a training-free framework that represents distant history as structured textual memory while preserving the recent frames as visual tokens for fine-grained perception. Specifically, D-HSM turns selected historical video chunks into typed textual observations and stores them in an entity-centered hub-and-spoke memory, with entities as hubs and related evidence as spokes. When answering a question, D-HSM dynamically retrieves a compact question-aware memory subset, expands it through hub-and-spoke links, and combines it with the recent visual window for frozen-VLM answer prediction. Extensive experiments on both streaming and long video benchmarks show that D-HSM consistently and substantially improves VLM backbones and outperforms other state-of-the-art online and offline video understanding baselines.

</details>


### [177] [Learning to Restore More: Continual Capability Expansion for Pretrained Image Restoration Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30305)
*Hu Gao,Yulong Chen,Lizhuang Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RestoreMore框架，一种持续能力扩展的图像复原框架，通过冻结预训练模型作为能力锚点，学习残差扩展模块来处理新的退化类型，并采用能力导向的双层路由机制实现新任务对历史知识的复用，在多个复原基准上验证了其能持续获取新能力同时保持并提升已有能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图像复原模型通常以固定的能力集合进行训练，当遇到新的复原需求时，传统的解决方案要么额外训练新模型，要么用新旧数据联合重新训练原模型，这导致了计算资源浪费和灾难性遗忘问题。作者希望探索如何让已经训练好的复原模型持续获取新能力而不遗忘已学到的能力。

Method: RestoreMore框架包含三个关键设计：(1) 将预训练复原模型冻结作为能力锚点，保持已有能力不丢失；(2) 为新出现的退化类型学习残差扩展模块；(3) 在多个特征阶段引入能力导向的双层路由机制——第一层路由识别与当前输入相关的复原能力，第二层路由选择并组合稀疏的互补退化专家，使新任务能选择性复用历史复原知识，并逐步丰富后续任务可用的专家库。

Result: 在广泛的复原基准上的大量实验表明，RestoreMore能够持续获取新的复原能力，同时保持甚至提升先前学习到的能力，验证了该框架在持续学习场景下的有效性。

Conclusion: RestoreMore通过冻结能力锚点+残差扩展模块+双层路由的设计，为图像复原领域的持续能力扩展提供了一种高效且有效的方案，避免了重新训练模型的成本，并实现了新旧能力的协同提升。

Abstract: Image restoration models are typically trained with a fixed set of capabilities. When new restoration requirements emerge, existing solutions usually train additional models or jointly retrain the original model with both new and historical data. Instead of designing another restoration backbone, we investigate how a trained restorer can continually acquire new capabilities without forgetting those learned previously. We propose RestoreMore, a continual capability-expansion framework that preserves the pretrained restoration model as a frozen capability anchor and learns residual expansion modules for newly arriving degradations. RestoreMore introduces a capability-oriented bi-level routing mechanism at multiple feature stages. The first routing level identifies restoration capabilities relevant to the current input, while the second selects and combines a sparse set of complementary degradation experts. This design enables newly introduced tasks to selectively reuse historical restoration knowledge and progressively enriches the expert bank available for subsequent restoration tasks. Extensive experiments on a wide range of restoration benchmarks demonstrate that RestoreMore consistently acquires new restoration abilities while preserving and improving previously learned capabilities.

</details>


### [178] [ScenePilot: Grow-and-Repair Policy for Text-Driven 3D Indoor Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30307)
*Jiawei Zhang,Hongsong Wang,Pan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ScenePilot是一个检索增强的"生长-修复"三维室内场景生成框架，通过分层检索增强规划（HRAP）获取布局先验，结合强化多模态修复（RMR）模块进行轻量级局部和全局修正，并利用SceneReverse-17k修复轨迹数据集训练策略，在保持多样性的同时提升物理合理性和功能连贯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有三维室内场景生成方法存在三大局限：一次性生成器常产生几何无效布局、重后验优化成本高且不稳定、仅依赖提示的规划器缺乏可复用的布局先验来建模功能分组和物体关系。

Method: 提出ScenePilot框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 分层检索增强规划（HRAP）模块，检索房间级、组级和锚点级布局先验以支持功能组规划；(2) 强化多模态修复（RMR）模块，在每组物体插入后进行轻量级局部修正并在完成后进行全局修复。训练时构建SceneReverse-17k数据集，通过对高质量场景施加位置、旋转和缩放的扰动并逆向操作作为可执行的修复目标，策略从渲染视图、场景状态、检索先验和编辑历史中预测结构化的移动-旋转-缩放动作。

Result: ScenePilot结合HRAP与RMR，提供了对比一次性生成和重全场景优化的高效替代方案，在物理合理性、功能连贯性和可控性方面有显著提升，同时保持了生成多样性。

Conclusion: 通过将场景生成建模为先验引导的增量生长与学习式修正，ScenePilot有效解决了现有方法的几何无效、优化成本高和缺乏布局先验等问题，为三维室内场景生成提供了一种更高效、更可控的路径。

Abstract: Text-driven 3D indoor scene generation has advanced from dataset-bound layout modeling to open-vocabulary synthesis with large language and vision-language models. Yet existing methods remain limited: one-pass generators often yield geometrically invalid layouts, heavy post-hoc optimization is costly and unstable, and prompt-only planners lack reusable layout priors for functional grouping and object relations. We propose \textbf{ScenePilot}, a retrieval-augmented \textbf{Grow-and-Repair} framework that formulates scene generation as prior-guided incremental growth with learned rectification. Given a prompt, the Hierarchical Retrieval-Augmented Planning (HRAP) module retrieves room-, group-, and anchor-level layout priors to support functional group planning. A text-driven base generator then inserts object groups sequentially, while the Reinforcement Multimodal Repair (RMR) module performs lightweight local correction after each insertion and a final global repair after completion. To train this policy, we construct \textbf{SceneReverse-17k}, a repair-trajectory dataset built by perturbing high-quality 3D scenes in position, rotation, and scale, then using inverse operations as executable rectification targets. The policy predicts structured \emph{move--rotate--scale} actions from rendered views, scene state, retrieved priors, and edit history. By combining HRAP with RMR, ScenePilot offers an efficient alternative to one-shot generation and heavy full-scene optimization, improving physical plausibility, functional coherence, and controllability while preserving diversity.

</details>


### [179] [Knowing Beyond the Known: Reinforced Knowledge Specification for Multi-Label Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30316)
*Aoting Zhang,Dongbao Yang,Chang Liu,Xiaopeng Hong,Can Ma,Yu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出KBK（Knowing Beyond the Known）框架，用于解决多标签类别增量学习（MLCIL）中因共现和不完整标签导致的已知与未知知识边界模糊问题，通过层次化特征净化、不确定性感知的回忆增强、语义相关性的未知特征合成以及类别平衡梯度补偿损失，在无需回放缓冲区的情况下超越现有最优方法2.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有类别增量学习方法在多标签场景（MLCIL）中表现不佳，根本原因在于共现和不完整标签导致学习目标存在固有矛盾。核心障碍是模型对已知与未知知识的边界模糊，这会破坏历史知识保留、增加当前任务学习难度、限制对未来概念的适应性。

Method: 提出KBK（Knowing Beyond the Known）强化知识规范框架：1）层次化特征净化模块，从全局特征中解耦细粒度类特定特征，用低层视觉特征强化高层语义抽象；2）不确定性感知的回忆增强策略，基于分布先验抑制不可靠预测，提升历史召回质量；3）利用语义相关性在共现条件下合成信息丰富的未知特征，为未来学习预留嵌入空间；4）设计类别平衡梯度补偿损失，根据遗忘速度动态重新加权梯度反向传播以缓解异质遗忘。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上验证了KBK的有效性和鲁棒性，在MS-COCO B0-C10设置下，即使没有使用任何回放缓冲区，平均准确率（Avg Acc）超越了此前最优方法2.7%。

Conclusion: KBK通过显式建模已知与未知知识，统一了历史、当前和前瞻性学习目标，有效解决了MLCIL中的核心挑战，同时无需回放缓冲区即可实现优越性能，展示了其在实际多标签增量学习场景中的实用价值。

Abstract: Existing class-incremental learning methods struggle in multi-label scenarios (MLCIL) due to the inherent contradiction of learning objectives arising from co-occurring and incomplete labels. We argue that the core obstacle is the model's ambiguous boundary between known and unknown knowledge, which undermines historical knowledge retention, complicates current task learning, and limits adaptability to future concepts. To address this, we propose KBK (Knowing Beyond the Known), a reinforced knowledge specification framework that explicitly models what is known or not to unify historical, current, and prospective learning. Specifically, to clarify known knowledge, we develop a hierarchical feature purification module that disentangles fine-grained class-specific features from global features, where high-level semantic abstraction is reinforced with low-level visual features. Additionally, an uncertainty-aware recall enhancement strategy suppresses unreliable predictions based on distribution priors, improving the quality of historical recall. For probing the unknown, KBK leverages semantic correlations to synthesize informative unknown features under co-occurring, preserving embedding space for future learning. Furthermore, to mitigate heterogeneous forgetting, we design a category-balanced gradient compensation loss that dynamically reweights gradient backpropagation according to forgetting speeds. Experiments on multiple benchmarks validate the effectiveness and robustness of KBK, which surpasses prior best methods by 2.7% in Avg. Acc on MS-COCO B0-C10 setting even without any replay buffers.

</details>


### [180] [CapFrame: Text-Instructed Viewpoint Grounding in 3D Gaussian Scenes via Geometric Pseudo Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30342)
*Jirong Li,Satoshi Ikehata,Shuhei Kurita,Ikuro Sato*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个新的任务TIVG（文本指令视角定位），旨在根据文本指令在3D高斯场景中识别对应的6自由度相机位姿。作者提出了CapFrame框架，通过"检索-翻译-精修"流水线，将语言转化为几何伪标签并用可微优化在3DGS中寻优，在38个真实场景上的实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语言引导方法在3D场景中主要关注物体级定位（确定看什么），但很少控制单帧中的外观呈现方式（如主体朝向或画面布局）。在3DGS中放置虚拟相机以捕捉期望画面仍是大量手工操作，缺乏文本驱动的自动视角控制方法。

Method: 提出CapFrame框架，采用Retrieve-Translate-Refine三阶段流水线：1）检索阶段通过MLLM的Question-Evaluation过程检索并排序相关视角；2）翻译阶段将文本指令转化为朝向和布局伪标签；3）精修阶段在3DGS中通过可微优化，结合布局损失和朝向损失来优化相机位姿。

Result: 在38个真实场景和135条指令上的实验表明，CapFrame生成的视角在VLM指标、MLLM评判和用户研究中均优于启发式视角搜索和改编轨迹生成基线方法。

Conclusion: CapFrame有效地将语言指令转化为3D高斯场景中的相机位姿，实现了文本引导的自动视角定位，弥补了语言引导3D场景理解中"如何呈现"这一维度的空白。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables photorealistic real-time novel view synthesis, yet placing a virtual camera to capture a desired frame remains largely manual. Existing language-guided approaches in 3D scenes mainly focus on object-centric grounding, determining what to observe but rarely controlling how it should appear in a single frame, such as subject orientation or frame layout. To address this limitation, we introduce a new task, Text-Instructed Viewpoint Grounding (TIVG), which aims to identify a 6-DoF camera pose in a 3D Gaussian scene whose rendered frame aligns with a text instruction. To solve this task, we propose CapFrame, a partially differentiable framework that converts language into geometric pseudo labels for camera pose optimization. CapFrame follows a Retrieve-Translate-Refine pipeline: it retrieves relevant views and ranks them through a Question-Evaluation process with MLLMs, translates the instruction into orientation and layout pseudo labels, and refines the camera pose via differentiable optimization with layout and orientation losses in 3DGS. Experiments on 38 real-world scenes with 135 instructions indicate that CapFrame produces viewpoints better aligned with texts than heuristic viewpoint search and adapted trajectory generation baselines, validated by VLM metrics, MLLM judges, and user studies. Code is available at: https://github.com/jirongli/CapFrame

</details>


### [181] [Proximity3D: Shape from Capacitive Proximity on Sensing Manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30344)
*Hao Chen,Chenming Wu,Chun Ping Lam,Xiangjia Chen,Guoxin Fang,Charlie C. L. Wang,Yeung Yam,Juncong Lin,Chengkai Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用弯曲电容纺织品作为形状传感器的方法，将传感器表面视为非平面感知流形，通过多视角前馈重建模型聚合电容邻近场以恢复物体形状，模拟和物理实验证明了该方法的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数形状重建方法假设测量定义在平面感知域（如RGB图像或深度图）上，忽略了非平面传感表面在机器人应用中的潜力，因此需要一种能够处理弯曲传感表面的形状重建方法。

Method: 使用弯曲电容纺织品作为形状传感器，将表面建模为非平面感知流形；每次扫描表示为该流形上的电容邻近场；提出多视角前馈重建模型，聚合已知传感器视角下的这些场并恢复物体形状。

Result: 模拟和物理实验均展示了从弯曲传感表面上获取的电容邻近信号中实现稳健的形状重建，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为机器人通过具身感知实现近场几何感知开辟了新途径，展示了非平面电容传感在形状重建中的潜力。

Abstract: Most shape reconstruction methods assume measurements defined over planar sensing domains, such as RGB images or depth maps. In this paper, we use a curved capacitive textile as a shape sensor, treating its surface as a non-planar sensing manifold. Each scan is represented as a capacitive proximity field on this manifold, induced by the interaction between the curved electrode layout and nearby object geometry. We introduce a multi-view feedforward reconstruction model that aggregates these fields across known sensor views and recovers the observed object shape. Simulated and physical experiments demonstrate robust reconstruction from capacitive proximity signals acquired on curved sensing surfaces, pointing toward a new route to robotic near-field geometric awareness via embodied sensing.

</details>


### [182] [Seeing the Unseen: Camouflaged Object Detection Beyond the Visible Spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30355)
*Avi Gupta,Trasha Gupta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MSFormer，一种端到端的伪装目标检测框架，利用多光谱图像代替传统RGB图像来提升低可见性场景下的检测性能。实验表明该方法优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有伪装目标检测方法主要依赖传统三通道RGB图像，限制了可用的光谱信息范围。多光谱图像能够捕获更精细的光谱特征，有望提升低可见性场景中的检测效果。

Method: 提出一个端到端框架MSFormer，以多光谱伪装图像作为输入，直接预测二值掩码。论文还提供了整合多光谱波段用于这一复杂低视觉任务的实证依据。

Result: 大量实验证明该方法有效，性能优于现有伪装目标检测方法。

Conclusion: 通过引入多光谱图像进行伪装目标检测，突破了RGB图像的局限，验证了多光谱信息在低可见性目标检测任务中的潜力。

Abstract: Recent advances in camouflaged object detection (COD) have led to substantial progress in challenging low-visibility scenarios, with pioneering studies demonstrating notable success in localizing objects in camouflaged scenes. Despite these achievements, existing approaches predominantly rely on conventional three-channel RGB imagery, thereby constraining the available visual information to a limited spectral range. Multispectral images offer a wide range of information about a scene by capturing fine-grained spectral signatures. Hence, by leveraging multispectral images for COD, we introduce a novel approach to detect camouflaged objects from the corresponding multispectral inputs. In particular, we propose an end-to-end framework, \textbf{\textit{MSFormer}}, that takes a multispectral camouflaged image as input and predicts a binary mask for it. Additionally, we also provide empirical justification for integrating multispectral bands for this complex low-vision task. Our extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our method, which outperforms existing methods.

</details>


### [183] [MCSeg: Pre-training and Fine-tuning Volumetric Pyramid Transformer for Multi-modal Cardiac Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30371)
*Zhiyu Ye,Hairong Zheng,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MCSeg，一种基于体积Transformer的多模态心脏分割网络，通过创新的Scaling Feature Pyramid（SFP）弥合单尺度3D ViT编码器与多尺度CNN解码器之间的结构不匹配，并结合掩码图像建模自监督预训练与区域互信息损失提升边界分割精度，在多个心脏数据集上超越11种SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动心脏图像分割对心脏疾病诊断和治疗至关重要。现有混合网络存在编码器与解码器之间的结构不匹配问题，且多模态心脏分割的精度和泛化能力仍有提升空间，尤其是在有限数据场景下。

Method: 1) 提出Scaling Feature Pyramid（SFP），将单尺度3D ViT编码器的输出转化为层次化特征金字塔，替代传统跳跃连接，有效桥接单尺度编码器与多尺度CNN解码器；2) ViT编码器先通过掩码图像建模进行自监督预训练；3) 下游任务微调时引入区域互信息（RMI）损失以提升边界分割准确性。

Result: 在CT数据集ImageCHD、多模态数据集MM-WHS、MRI数据集HVSMR-2.0和MSD Heart上，MCSeg一致性地超越了11种SOTA方法；在少样本实验中表现优越，展示了其在有限数据场景中的适应潜力。

Conclusion: MCSeg通过SFP有效解决了混合网络的结构不匹配问题，结合自监督预训练和RMI损失，在多模态心脏分割任务中取得了领先性能，且在少样本场景下具有显著潜力。代码和预训练权重已开源。

Abstract: Automatic cardiac image segmentation is pivotal for diagnosing and treating cardiac diseases. In this work, we introduce MCSeg, a volumetric transformer-based network tailored for multi-modal cardiac segmentation. To overcome the architectural mismatch inherent in existing hybrid networks, we propose a novel Scaling Feature Pyramid (SFP). Unlike conventional skip connections, the SFP effectively bridges the single-scale 3D Vision Transformer (ViT) encoder and the multi-scale CNN decoder by transforming the ViT's output into a hierarchical feature pyramid, ensuring that global contextual information is effectively leveraged. For the training paradigm, the ViT encoder first undergoes self-supervised pre-training via masked image modeling. Subsequently, the network is fine-tuned on downstream tasks, during which a regional mutual information (RMI) loss is integrated to improve boundary segmentation accuracy. In experiments, MCSeg consistently outperforms eleven SOTA methods on CT dataset ImageCHD, multi-modal dataset MM-WHS, MRI dataset HVSMR-2.0 and MSD Heart, highlighting the effectiveness of our MCSeg for multi-modal cardiac segmentation tasks. Furthermore, MCSeg's superior performance in few-shot experiment showcases its significant potential in adapting to limited data scenarios. Codes and pre-trained ViT-B weights are open-sourced at https://openi.pcl.ac.cn/OpenMedIA/MCSeg

</details>


### [184] [PRISM: Predictive Recomposition via Semantic Latent Decomposition for View-invariant Video Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30388)
*Youngchae Chee,Hosu Lee,Sungjune Park,Junho Kim,Yong Man Ro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PRISM提出了一种跨视角视频表征学习方法，通过将视频分解为视角不变和视角可变两种潜在特征，并在语言监督下进行重组合，实现真正的解耦。在EgoExo4D、EgoExoLearn和AE2数据集上取得了最先进的结果，甚至在零样本设置下超越了领域内模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨视角视频表征学习方法将每个视频编码为统一嵌入，导致视角不变和视角可变语义在共现情况下不可避免地纠缠在一起。即使是在专门训练视角不变性的跨视角方法中，这种失败模式依然存在。

Method: PRISM将视频分解为视角不变和视角可变两种潜在特征，并在语言监督下将它们重新组合，鼓励两个流的干净分解。核心洞察是：当视角不变特征能够与任意视角可变特征充分重组合且保持各自独立语义时，它才是真正解耦的。

Result: 在EgoExo4D、EgoExoLearn和AE2三个数据集上取得了最先进的结果，在零样本设置下甚至超越了领域内训练模型。

Conclusion: 通过重组合机制和语言监督实现了视角不变与视角可变语义的干净解耦，验证了该解耦方案在跨视角视频理解任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Cross-view video representation learning aims to capture viewpoint-invariant action semantics despite substantial appearance changes across egocentric and exocentric videos. However, existing methods encode each video as a unified embedding, where view-invariant and view-variant semantics inevitably entangle under co-occurrences - a failure mode we show persists even in cross-view methods explicitly trained for view-invariance. Our key insight is that a view-invariant feature is truly disentangled when it can be sufficiently recomposed with an arbitrary view-variant feature while preserving their independent semantics. Building on this, we propose PRISM, that decomposes video into view-invariant and view-variant latents and recompose them under language supervision encouraging clean decomposition of the two streams. PRISM achieves state-of-the-art results on EgoExo4D, EgoExoLearn, AE2, even surpassing in-domain models under zero-shot setting. Code is available at https://github.com/litcoderr/prism.

</details>


### [185] [Real-Time Scene-Adaptive Tone Mapping for High-Dynamic Range Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30400)
*Gongzhe Li,Linwei Qiu,Peibei Cao,Fengying Xie,Xiangyang Ji,Qilin Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向自动驾驶场景的新型色调映射方法，利用神经光度标定和尺度不变的局部色调映射模型，桥接HDR RAW输入与LDR sRGB检测网络之间的差距，实现端到端优化，同时支持性能迁移微调，可在NVIDIA Jetson平台上实时处理4K高比特深度HDR输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入式系统上大多数神经网络基于LDR输入训练，在处理高比特深度HDR图像时因极端动态范围导致性能显著下降，而传统ISP流程无法有效弥合HDR RAW输入与LDR检测网络之间的差距。

Method: 提出一种新颖的色调映射方法，包括引入神经光度标定来正则化动态范围，以及采用尺度不变的局部色调映射模型保留图像细节；无需传统ISP管线即可实现端到端优化，并支持从LDR sRGB图像到HDR RAW图像的低成本性能迁移微调。

Result: 在具有挑战性的汽车HDR场景中，该方法优于传统色调映射算法和先进的AI-ISP方法，并能在NVIDIA Jetson平台上实时处理4K高比特深度HDR输入。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法有效弥合了HDR RAW与LDR检测网络之间的鸿沟，兼顾了高动态范围保真度与嵌入式实时性，为自动驾驶场景下的HDR视觉应用提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: High-dynamic-range (HDR) images, with their rich tone and detail reproduction, hold significant potential to enhance computer vision systems, particularly in autonomous driving. However, most neural networks for embedded systems are trained on low-dynamic-range (LDR) inputs and suffer substantial performance degradation when handling high-bit-depth HDR images due to the challenges posed by extreme dynamic ranges. In this paper, we propose a novel tone mapping method that not only bridges the gap between HDR RAW inputs and the LDR sRGB requirements of detection networks but also achieves end-to-end optimization with downstream tasks. Instead of relying on the traditional image signal processing (ISP) pipeline, we introduce neural photometric calibration to regularize dynamic ranges and a scaling-invariant local tone mapping model to preserve image details. In addition, our architecture also supports performance transfer finetuning, enabling efficient adaptation from the LDR sRGB images to the HDR RAW images with minimal cost. The proposed method outperforms traditional tone mapping algorithms and advanced AI-ISP methods in challenging automotive HDR scenes. Moreover, our pipeline achieves real-time processing of 4K high-bit-depth HDR inputs on NVIDIA Jetson platforms.

</details>


### [186] [ImageCAS-X: a dataset and benchmark for coronary artery segmentation and centerline extraction in coronary CT angiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30404)
*Kit M. Bransby,Esther Øksnebjerg,Kristoffer Kjær,Jacob Kirkeby,Yasmin El Youssef,Aïda Jiménez,Philip R. Pedersson,Martina C. de Knegt,Klaus F. Kofoed,Rasmus R. Paulsen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提供了一个基于ImageCAS数据集的800例CCTA扫描的冠状动脉管腔像素级标注数据集，包含中心线和网格表面，并对现有管腔分割方法进行了基准测试，按疾病、图像质量、冠脉优势型、冠脉节段、血管直径和管腔衰减等临床因素分层评估性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冠状动脉管腔的准确分割是CCTA中动脉粥样硬化斑块和血管周围脂肪组织定量评估的前提，但现有自动化方法的验证受限于缺乏大规模、高质量、公开可用的标注数据集，且已有评测通常只报告单一聚合指标，缺乏解剖学和临床背景下的性能描述。

Method: 从公开ImageCAS数据集中选取800例扫描，提供管腔和冠脉节段的体素级标注、中心线和网格表面；基于该数据集对已建立的管腔分割方法进行基准测试，并与观察者间变异进行对比，按疾病状态、图像质量、冠脉优势型、冠脉节段、血管直径和管腔衰减等因素进行分层评估。

Result: 提供了大规模带详细标注的冠状动脉管腔分割数据集，并完成了与观察者间变异对照的基准评测，揭示了分割精度在不同解剖和临床情境下的分布差异。

Conclusion: 该数据集支持管腔分割、斑块和血管周围定量分析以及血流动力学建模方法的开发与验证，有助于推动冠状动脉成像分析的标准化和临床落地。

Abstract: Accurate segmentation of the coronary vessel lumen is a prerequisite for quantitative assessment of atherosclerotic plaque and perivascular adipose tissue in coronary computed tomography angiography (CCTA). Cardiologists rely on semi-automated methods for this task because manual vessel tracing and segmentation are labour-intensive. Although many automated methods have been proposed, their validation remains limited by the lack of large, high-quality publicly available datasets. We provide a new dataset of voxel-wise annotations of the vessel lumen and coronary segments, alongside centerlines, and mesh surfaces for 800 scans from the publicly available ImageCAS dataset. Using this dataset, we benchmark established lumen segmentation methods against inter-observer variability, stratifying performance by disease, image quality, coronary dominance, coronary segment, vessel diameter, and lumen attenuation. These labels allow segmentation accuracy to be described in anatomical and clinical context rather than reported as a single aggregate score. The dataset supports the development and validation of methods for lumen segmentation, plaque and perivascular quantification, and haemodynamic modelling.

</details>


### [187] [SePArate: Segmenting Patterns from Defects in Wafer Manufacturing Using Weak Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30410)
*Dain Kwon,Changmin Shin,Sunjong Park,Kanghyun Choi,Hyeyoon Lee,Jaewon Jang,Minseok Choi,Jinho Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SePArate，一种基于弱监督的晶圆缺陷分割方法，仅利用图像级标注即可实现像素级缺陷模式分离，通过三阶段训练（编码器预训练、知识迁移学习空间线索、合成混合缺陷数据训练）在实验中超越基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体制造中的缺陷分析至关重要，但人工检测无法规模化；现有自动化检测方法在根本原因分析和工艺优化方面仍存在不足。

Method: SePArate采用弱监督学习框架，仅需图像级标注即可进行像素级缺陷分割。训练过程分为三个阶段：编码器预训练、知识迁移以学习空间线索、以及在合成混合缺陷数据上进行训练以实现精确分割。

Result: 实验结果表明SePArate在缺陷分割性能上优于各基线方法。

Conclusion: SePArate通过弱监督方式有效解决了晶圆缺陷分割问题，在无需昂贵像素级标注的前提下取得了优于现有方法的性能，为半导体制造中的自动化缺陷分析提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In semiconductor manufacturing, defect analysis is essential, but manual inspection cannot scale. However, existing automated inspection methods remain insufficient for root-cause analysis and process optimization. To this end, we present SePArate, a weakly supervised wafer defect segmentation method. SePArate enables pixel-level separation of patterns by leveraging only image-level annotations. It consists of a three-phase training: encoder pretraining, knowledge transfer to learn spatial cues, and training on synthetic mixed-defect data for accurate segmentation. Experiments demonstrate that SePArate outperforms the baselines.

</details>


### [188] [Whole-Slide Image Analysis under Realistic Few-Shot Annotation Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30420)
*Tiffanie Godelaine,Maxime Zanella,Karim El Khoury,Benoit Macq,Christophe De Vleeschouwer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SlideCRF，将条件随机场适配到全玻片图像（WSI）场景，结合空间和生物线索处理类别缺失问题，并在真实病理学家标注协议（点击和涂鸦）下显著优于现有转导方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的转导式（few-shot transduction）预测细化方法在全玻片图像上存在三个不切实际的假设：(i) 将切片视为独立补丁，忽略组织空间结构；(ii) 假设类别分布均衡，而真实WSI存在严重类别不平衡且多类缺失；(iii) 标注采样方式不反映病理学家的真实交互行为。因此需要重新设计适配真实WSI场景的转导方法。

Method: 提出SlideCRF方法：将条件随机场（CRF）适配到全玻片图像，联合利用空间线索（补丁间位置关系）和生物学线索（组织类型先验），并显式处理给定切片中可能缺失的类别；同时引入基于空间局部点击和涂鸦的多种真实标注协议，模拟病理学家迭代修正模型错误的交互过程。

Result: 在四个数据集上，SlideCRF在macro F1指标上优于现有转导方法；相比零样本预测，在每现存类别1个点击时提升+24.2%，16个点击时提升+37.5%。

Conclusion: 将转导范式与真实WSI特征对齐（空间结构、类别不平衡、真实标注交互）能显著提升病理图像的零样本预测细化效果，SlideCRF为全玻片图像的少样本标注细化提供了更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Automating the analysis of whole-slide images has high clinical value, since characterizing cancers requires examining them in detail. Such analysis increasingly relies on vision-language models that provide patch-level zero-shot predictions. However, these predictions remain noisy and must be refined with a few annotations. A promising paradigm for this refinement is few-shot transduction. Rather than treating each patch independently, these methods leverage the relations between patches, together with a few annotations, to refine all predictions jointly. However, current transductive methods are evaluated under conditions that overlook key properties of whole-slide images: (i) datasets consist of independent patches extracted from multiple slides, ignoring the complex tissue organization; (ii) datasets are mostly balanced, whereas a single whole-slide image exhibits severe class imbalance, with several classes absent; and (iii) annotations are sampled at random, without reflecting how a pathologist annotates a limited number of regions. To align the transduction paradigm to realistic whole-slide settings, we introduce the following contributions. First, we propose SlideCRF, which adapts conditional random fields for whole-slide images by combining spatial and biological cues while accounting for classes that may be absent from a given slide. Second, we provide a set of realistic annotation protocols, based on spatially localized clicks and scribbles, modeling different pathologist interactions, such as the iterative correction of model errors. Across four datasets, we show that SlideCRF outperforms current transductive methods in macro F1, improving over the zero-shot predictions by +24.2% and +37.5% with one and 16 clicks per present class, respectively.

</details>


### [189] [ObjectSplat: Improving Mesh Fidelity and Interactivity for 3D Scenes via Object-Level Mesh Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30423)
*Minhas Kamal,Hiranya Garbha Kumar,Mahedi Kamal,Balakrishnan Prabhakaran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种"先分解后重建"的3D场景重建方法，将场景中的实例分割出来并独立重建背景和每个实例，通过网格splatting技术合成单一场景，显著提升网格保真度和新视角合成质量，同时支持对象级编辑和交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于splatting的3D重建算法将场景表示为单一的整体场，缺乏对象级结构，无法支持下游编辑和交互；此外，未被直接观测到的区域会被周围纹理污染而无法纠正，限制了网格保真度和新视角合成质量。

Method: 提出分解-重建（decompose-before-reconstruct）范式：首先从每一帧中分割出实例对象，将剩余部分视为背景并对其进行修复（inpainting），然后分别使用网格splatting独立重建每个实例和背景，最后将它们合成为一个单一场景。

Result: 相比基线方法，网格F-score提升超过5%，新视角合成质量显著改善，同时支持对象级别的可修改性和交互性。

Conclusion: 通过将场景分解为实例与背景并独立重建，能够有效避免遮挡区域纹理污染问题，在提升重建质量的同时赋予场景对象级结构，为下游编辑和交互应用提供支持。

Abstract: Splatting-based algorithms reconstruct photorealistic, real-time-renderable, and mesh-exportable 3D scenes from regular images, but they represent a scene as a single monolithic field. Therefore, the reconstruction has no object-level structure, leaving it infeasible for downstream editing or interaction. Moreover, regions that are never directly observed in the input scans are contaminated by the surrounding texture and left uncorrected, capping both mesh fidelity and novel-view synthesis. We propose a decompose-before-reconstruct approach: we segment the instances out of every frame, consider the remaining as background and inpaint it, reconstruct each instance and the background independently with mesh splatting, and compose them into a single scene. Our method significantly improves mesh fidelity (over a 5\% gain in F-score) and novel-view synthesis, while supporting object-wise modifiability and interactivity. The code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [190] [FlowVVTON: Flow-Guided Mask-Free Video Virtual Try-On](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30450)
*Shengyao Chen,Xianbing Sun,Liqing Zhang,Jianfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowVVTON是一种无需mask的视频虚拟试穿框架，仅使用光流作为训练时的监督信号，通过流扭曲潜在损失在生成模型所有层强制多尺度时间一致性，并在TikTokDress数据集上大幅超越基线方法，尤其时间一致性提升显著（VFID-R较SwiftTry提升5.7倍），全程无需分割mask、姿态关键点或区域标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频虚拟试穿方法依赖人体解析mask或姿态关键点，在大运动和遮挡场景下易产生边界伪影和时间不一致；且多数方法仅靠注意力机制建模时间关系，缺乏显式运动监督。

Method: 提出FlowVVTON：1) 完全消除解析mask依赖；2) 仅将光流作为训练时监督信号，通过流扭曲潜在损失在生成模型所有层实现多尺度时间一致性对齐；3) 采用两阶段训练策略，先建立无mask空间对齐，再引入光流引导的时间监督。

Result: 在TikTokDress数据集上，FlowVVTON相比基线方法有大幅性能提升，其中时间一致性指标VFID-R较SwiftTry提升5.7倍。

Conclusion: FlowVVTON证明了无需任何分割标注即可实现高质量的视频虚拟试穿，通过显式光流监督有效解决了时间一致性问题，为视频虚拟试穿提供了一种更简洁且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Video virtual try-on aims to transfer a target garment onto a moving person across video frames. Current methods rely on human parsing masks or pose keypoints that frequently fail under large motions and occlusions, causing boundary artifacts and temporal inconsistency. A further limitation is that most approaches rely solely on attention mechanisms for temporal modeling, providing no explicit motion supervision. We propose FlowVVTON, a mask-free framework that eliminates parsing mask dependency entirely. Optical flow is used solely as a training-time supervision signal: a flow-warped latent loss, applied across all layers of the generation model, enforces multi-scale temporal consistency by aligning adjacent-frame features under explicit physical motion constraints. A two-stage training strategy establishes mask-free spatial alignment before introducing flow-guided temporal supervision. Experiments on TikTokDress show that FlowVVTON outperforms baselines by substantial margins, particularly in temporal consistency (5.7$\times$ VFID-R improvement over SwiftTry), while requiring no segmentation masks, pose keypoints, or region annotations at any stage.

</details>


### [191] [SeqAlign3DVG: A Sequence-Aligned Benchmark and Voxel Reasoning Framework for 3D Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30451)
*Yi Zhang,Yi Wang,Yueting Wu,Kaiyue Yang,Yuejiao Su,Lap-Pui Chau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了SeqAlign3DVG基准，专注于时间有序且与观察严格对齐的图像3D视觉定位，并设计了基于体素的统一管线（ROVM+PLVF）在该基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图像3D视觉定位基准存在文本与观察对齐松散、忽略时间顺序两大问题，导致基准无法真实反映具身智能体在时序观察下进行3D定位的实际需求。

Method: 提出SeqAlign3DVG基准（含9,622个单视图和14,493个序列样本），所有表达式经人工验证且严格对齐RGB观察；提出统一体素管线，包含ROVM（保守记忆动态排序和聚合多视图证据以缓解噪声）和PLVF（由粗到细的空间-语言推理以精确消歧）。

Result: 在无深度协议下达到最优性能，显著提升了针对复杂关系和外观线索目标的定位精度。

Conclusion: 通过严格对齐和时间有序的基准设计以及ROVM+PLVF管线的联合作用，有效推动了图像3D视觉定位领域的发展，验证了时序信息与观察一致性对定位精度的重要性。

Abstract: Image-based 3D visual grounding is critical for embodied agents, yet existing benchmarks suffer from loose text-observation alignment and neglect temporal ordering. We introduce SeqAlign3DVG, a novel benchmark dedicated to temporally ordered and strictly observation-aligned image-based 3D visual grounding. Unlike prior works using order-agnostic views or global point clouds, SeqAlign3DVG ensures all expressions are human-verified and strictly grounded in the provided RGB observations (single frames or ordered observation sequences). It comprises 9,622 single-view and 14,493 sequence samples featuring rich descriptions, complex relations, and multi-instance ambiguities. To tackle this benchmark, we propose a unified voxel-based pipeline featuring Relevance-Ordered Voxel Memory (ROVM) and Progressive Language-Voxel Fusion (PLVF). ROVM dynamically ranks and aggregates multi-view evidence via a conservative memory to mitigate noisy observations, while PLVF performs coarse-to-fine spatial-linguistic reasoning for precise disambiguation.
