Our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance under the depth-free protocol, significantly improving localization for targets defined by complex relations and appearance cues.

</details>


### [192] [Federated Multi-Task Learning for Bladder Tumor Segmentation and MIBC Classification Using a Hybrid CNN-Transformer Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30458)
*Malhar Udmale,Divyanshu Dwivedi,Aarohi Dhand,Sachin Dudda Nagaraju,Mayank Rai,Bagesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种联邦多任务学习框架，用于在四个临床中心联合进行膀胱肿瘤分割和MIBC/NMIBC分类。该框架采用Swin混合模型（ResNet-34与Swin-Tiny Transformer结合），并通过分割引导分类机制提升预测性能。在FedBCa数据集上，联邦训练结合Geo+Elastic增强取得了DSC 0.8100和AUC 0.8931的最佳综合表现，证明了无需集中患者数据即可实现跨机构联合训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 膀胱肿瘤分割和肌层浸润评估对治疗规划至关重要，但跨机构开发稳健模型面临两大挑战：患者数据无法集中汇聚，以及不同扫描仪和采集协议导致的成像特征差异。

Method: 采用联邦多任务学习框架，同时优化膀胱肿瘤分割和MIBC/NMIBC分类。核心模型Swin Hybrid结合ResNet-34分支（提取局部纹理和边界信息）与Swin-Tiny Transformer（捕捉全局解剖上下文），并引入分割引导分类机制利用肿瘤定位信息辅助MIBC预测。同时考察了集中式与联邦训练下多种数据增强策略以提升对多中心变异的鲁棒性。

Result: 在FedBCa数据集上，Swin Hybrid在分割与分类之间取得了最佳平衡。联邦训练下，Geo+Elastic增强策略达到DSC 0.8100、患者级别AUC 0.8931，综合得分最高为0.8474。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明，联合分割和分类任务可以在不集中患者数据的前提下，通过联邦训练在多个机构间有效执行，为跨机构医疗影像AI模型开发提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Accurate bladder tumor segmentation and assessment of mus- cle invasion from T2-weighted MRI are important for treatment plan- ning, but developing robust models across institutions is challenging be- cause patient data cannot be centrally pooled and imaging characteristics vary across scanners and acquisition protocols. We propose a federated multi-task learning framework for joint bladder tumor segmentation and MIBC/NMIBC classification across four clinical centers. The proposed Swin Hybrid model combines a ResNet-34 branch for local texture and boundary information with a Swin-Tiny Transformer for global anatomi- cal context. A segmentation-guided classification mechanism further uses tumor localization information to support MIBC prediction. We also investigate several augmentation strategies under both centralized and federated training to improve robustness to multi-center variability. Ex- periments on the FedBCa dataset show that the Swin Hybrid provides the best overall balance between segmentation and classification among the evaluated architectures. Under federated training, Geo+Elastic aug- mentation achieved a DSC of 0.8100 and a patient-level AUC of 0.8931, yielding the highest combined score of 0.8474. These results demonstrate that joint segmentation and classification can be effectively performed across multiple institutions using federated training without centralizing patient data.

</details>


### [193] [Beyond Accuracy: Quantifying Pulmonary Attribution in Anatomy-Guided Chest X-Ray Classification Under Domain Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30467)
*Abdullah Al Mamun,Md. Nasif Osman Khansur,Md Ashraful Hossen Akash,Md. Kishor Morol,Tze Hui Liew*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DBCA-SegNet-MGAP，一个多任务解剖引导的CNN-Transformer框架，通过双向交叉骨干注意力结合互补特征，预测软组织肺掩膜，并采用Mask-Guided Adaptive Global Average Pooling (MGAP)将解剖先验直接融入分类。在COVID-19内部测试和外部结核病转移测试中，MGAP在不损失诊断性能的前提下显著提升了肺部分配集中度（ALR从0.39提升至0.71），表明诊断判别力、校准和肺部分配包含度是可区分的模型属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型可能在未真正依赖肺部图像内容的情况下达到高分类性能，现有研究缺乏对模型预测是否主要来源于肺部区域的评估。作者提出肺部分配包含度（Pulmonary Attribution Containment）作为独立于诊断性能的解剖相关可靠性属性，并设计方法使其可量化、可优化。

Method: 提出DBCA-SegNet-MGAP框架：(1) 采用CNN-Transformer双骨干网络，通过双向交叉骨干注意力融合互补特征；(2) 预测软组织肺掩膜作为解剖先验；(3) 通过MGAP（Mask-Guided Adaptive Global Average Pooling）将掩膜信息直接引导分类。用Anatomical Local Energy Ratio (ALR)和高强度累积ALR (cALR@0.9)量化肺部分配包含度。

Result: COVID-19内测加权F1为0.9615±0.0015，宏ROC-AUC为0.9906±0.0007。架构匹配对比中，用MGAP替代常规GAP使ALR从0.3878提升至0.7086，cALR@0.9从0.5265提升至0.9905，而F1几乎不变。外部迁移到Montgomery数据集时，ROC-AUC保持0.9080，ALR保持0.6466，但F1降至0.7528，ECE升至0.1683。

Conclusion: 诊断判别力、校准和肺部分配包含度是三种不同的模型属性，应在内部测试和外部域偏移条件下联合评估。MGAP能有效增强模型对肺部区域的关注而不牺牲诊断性能，为胸部X光分类模型的可靠性评估提供了新维度。

Abstract: Deep-learning models can achieve strong chest X-ray (CXR) classification performance without establishing whether their predictions predominantly rely on pulmonary image content. This study evaluates pulmonary attribution containment as an anatomy-related reliability property distinct from diagnostic performance. We propose DBCA-SegNet-MGAP, a multi-task anatomy-guided CNN-Transformer framework that combines complementary feature representations through bidirectional cross-backbone attention, predicts a soft lung mask, and incorporates this anatomical prior directly into classification through Mask-Guided Adaptive Global Average Pooling (MGAP). Pulmonary attribution containment is quantified using the Anatomical Local Energy Ratio (ALR) and high-intensity cumulative ALR (cALR@0.9). Experiments were repeated across three training seeds using the COVID-19 Radiography Database for four-class internal testing and a locked Shenzhen-to-Montgomery protocol for zero-shot external tuberculosis testing. On COVID-19, the proposed model achieved a weighted F1 of $0.9615 \pm 0.0015$ and macro ROC-AUC of $0.9906 \pm 0.0007$. In an architecture-matched dual-bridge comparison, replacing conventional GAP with MGAP increased ALR from $0.3878 \pm 0.0098$ to $0.7086 \pm 0.0104$ and cALR@0.9 from $0.5265 \pm 0.0101$ to $0.9905 \pm 0.0018$, while weighted F1 remained essentially unchanged ($0.9618 \pm 0.0015$ vs. $0.9615 \pm 0.0015$). Under locked external transfer to Montgomery, ROC-AUC remained $0.9080 \pm 0.0043$ and pulmonary ALR remained $0.6466 \pm 0.0081$, whereas weighted F1 decreased to $0.7528 \pm 0.0080$ and ECE increased to $0.1683 \pm 0.0055$. These findings show that diagnostic discrimination, calibration, and pulmonary attribution containment are distinct model properties and support their joint evaluation under internal testing and external domain shift.

</details>


### [194] [HorizonNet for visual terrain navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30471)
*Bertil Grelsson,Andreas Robinson,Michael Felsberg,Fahad Shahbaz Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于360度全景图像和CNN的无人水面艇（USV）位置估计方法，通过提取地平线并与数字高程模型（DEM）数据进行傅里叶域相关匹配，实现了GPS级精度的定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: USV在沿海或群岛区域航行时需要进行位置估计，但传统方法可能依赖GPS或精度不足，需要一种替代或补充方案来提高定位精度。

Method: 首先使用第一个CNN架构从360度全景图像中确定近似地平线并推断相机俯仰角和横滚角；然后对全景图像进行扭曲以补偿相机姿态，生成水平相机图像；接着使用第二个CNN架构在扭曲后的图像中提取逐像素地平线；最后将提取的地平线与数字高程模型（DEM）数据在傅里叶域通过MOSSE相关滤波器进行匹配，通过寻找最大相关分数位置来估计USV位置。

Result: 在群岛区域的实地试验中进行了全面实验，所提方法取得了有前景的结果，实现了GPS级精度的位置估计。

Conclusion: 基于全景图像地平线提取与DEM数据相关匹配的两阶段CNN方法能够有效实现USV的位置估计，精度达到GPS水平，验证了该方法的实用性和有效性。

Abstract: This paper investigates the problem of position estimation of unmanned surface vessels (USVs) operating in coastal areas or in the archipelago. We propose a position estimation method where the horizon line is extracted in a 360 degree panoramic image around the USV. We design a CNN architecture to determine an approximate horizon line in the image and implicitly determine the camera orientation (the pitch and roll angles). The panoramic image is warped to compensate for the camera orientation and to generate an image from an approximately level camera. A second CNN architecture is designed to extract the pixelwise horizon line in the warped image. The extracted horizon line is correlated with digital elevation model (DEM) data in the Fourier domain using a MOSSE correlation filter. Finally, we determine the location of the maximum correlation score over the search area to estimate the position of the USV. Comprehensive experiments are performed in a field trial in the archipelago. Our approach provides promising results by achieving position estimates with GPS-level accuracy.

</details>


### [195] [VisER: Visual Evidence and Reliance for Object Hallucination Detection in LVLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30480)
*Afsaneh Hasanebrahimi,Hanxun Huang,Christopher Leckie,Sarah Erfani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VisER，一种无需训练的物体级幻觉检测指标，通过双重视角（视觉证据与视觉依赖）区分物体支持来源，从而更准确地识别大视觉语言模型中的幻觉物体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练无关的幻觉检测方法使用的内部信号（如token似然、注意力等）存在来源混淆问题，无法区分物体支持是来自图像证据还是文本前缀，导致在困难场景下仍会误判幻觉物体。

Method: VisER从两个互补视角评估生成的物体提及：视觉证据（Visual Evidence）衡量物体-上下文兼容性是否得到图像token中物体特定证据的支持；视觉依赖（Visual Reliance）衡量物体更多依赖于图像还是生成前缀。结合两个视角得到更注重来源的接地评分，无需额外的物体级验证生成。

Result: 在多个大视觉语言模型和基准测试上，VisER在AUROC和AUPR指标上均优于一系列基线方法。

Conclusion: VisER通过区分物体支持的来源（图像证据 vs 文本前缀），提供了一种更可靠的物体级幻觉检测方法，有效提升了幻觉检测的准确性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Object hallucination remains a persistent reliability issue in large vision-language models, where generated object mentions may sound plausible but lack visual grounding. Recent training-free detectors use internal signals such as token likelihood, attention, visual confidence, or image-text similarity to identify hallucinated objects. These signals are useful, but they are often source-confounded. They measure how strongly an object is supported inside the model without distinguishing whether that support comes from object-specific visual evidence or the generated text prefix. In difficult cases, a hallucinated object can still receive high internal support because it fits the scene, is associated with nearby visual cues, or follows naturally from the generated text prefix. We propose VisER, a training-free two-sided metric for object-level hallucination detection. VisER evaluates each generated object mention from two complementary views. Visual Evidence measures whether object-context compatibility is backed by object-specific evidence from image tokens. Visual Reliance measures whether the object is supported more by the image than by the generated prefix. Combining these views gives a more source-aware grounding score, while avoiding additional object-level verification generations. Across multiple LVLMs and benchmarks, VisER improves AUROC and AUPR over a range of baselines.

</details>


### [196] [Lot Machine: Multimodal Lot Extraction from Auction Catalogs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30510)
*Mathias Zinnen,Alisha Mund,Sabine Lang,Lukas Hüttner,Thomas Gorges,Vincent Christlein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的自动化流水线，用于从19-20世纪历史拍卖目录中提取结构化的条目级元数据，并评估了不同部署模式（商业API、机构网关、本地量化模型）在提示策略和受约束解码框架下的性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史拍卖目录是溯源研究和艺术品市场研究的重要资源，但其内部格式高度多变，且缺乏机器可读的表示形式，限制了大规模分析的可能性。

Method: 提出一个从German Sales数据库中自动提取结构化条目级元数据的流水线；使用人工标注的代表性目录页面测试集，在多种提示策略和受约束解码框架下评估视觉语言模型（VLM），并跨商业提供商、机构网关和本地量化模型等不同部署模式进行基准测试。

Result: 商业端点达到性能上限；机构网关是可行且保护隐私的替代方案；本地部署可行但必须强制输出结构以保证JSON格式有效；不同程度上仍需人工介入校正。

Conclusion: 基于VLM的流水线能够成功解锁历史拍卖目录，使其可用于大规模自动化分析，尽管仍需一定程度的人工校正。

Abstract: For provenance research and art market studies, auction catalogs are an essential resource to trace specific objects over time and space. While historical auction catalogs follow established domain conventions, their internal formatting remains highly variable, and their large-scale analysis is currently restricted by the lack of machine-readable representations of the auction lots. We propose a pipeline to automatically extract structured lot-level metadata from German Sales, a large database of historical auction and sales catalogs from the 19th and 20th centuries. Using a manually annotated test set of representative catalog pages, we evaluate Vision-Language Models (VLMs) under varying prompt strategies and constrained decoding frameworks. To reflect the practical constraints faced by cultural heritage institutions, including budget, compute resources, and data privacy requirements, we benchmark the methods across different deployment modes ranging from commercial providers to locally hosted, quantized models. We find that commercial endpoints establish the performance ceiling, while institutional gateways offer a viable, privacy-preserving alternative. Local deployments remain feasible, but strictly require enforcing the output structure during generation to guarantee a valid JSON format. While varying degrees of human-in-the-loop correction are still necessary, this work demonstrates that a VLM-based pipeline can successfully unlock historical auction catalogs for large-scale automated analysis.

</details>


### [197] [MEOM: Multi-View Expected-OKS Maximization for Human Pose Triangulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30521)
*Ziliang Xiong,Henglin Shi,Per-Erik Forssen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MEOM框架，利用完整热图进行多视角3D人体姿态估计，通过最大化跨视角概率质量一致性来定位3D关节，并采用HDR校准评估热图可靠性，在有无3D监督两种设置下均取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统代数三角化方法先将多视角2D关键点从预测热图中解码，但热图在遮挡下可能是多模态的，将其折叠为单一峰值会丢失空间分布信息。现有方法未充分利用完整热图信息，导致3D姿态估计精度受限。

Method: 提出多视角期望OKS最大化（MEOM）目标函数，在3D空间中定位使各视角概率质量分布最一致的关节位置；采用最高密度区域（HDR）校准作为概率质量的诊断方法，独立于基于距离的指标。框架覆盖两种设置：无3D监督时通过最大化MEOM优化3D姿态；有3D监督时使用MEOM与MSE联合损失进行端到端训练。

Result: 无3D监督设置下，与依赖更大骨干网络、时序融合和模拟3D数据的SOTA方法性能相当，在模糊的H36MA和遮挡的CMU Panoptic帧上优势显著。有3D监督时，在Human3.6M上达到19.11mm绝对MPJPE，超越SOTA体素方法，且推理成本减半。

Conclusion: 利用完整热图而不是折叠为单点进行多视角3D姿态估计是更优策略，MEOM目标结合HDR校准能有效提升精度与鲁棒性，尤其在遮挡和模糊场景下表现突出，且具备更低计算成本的优势。

Abstract: Conventional algebraic triangulation solves 3D human pose estimation (HPE) from multi-view 2D keypoints. The typical approach, decoding 2D keypoints from predicted heatmaps, is unreliable as heatmaps can be multimodal under occlusion, and collapsing them into single peaks discards their spatial distribution. We seek to use the entire heatmap to estimate 3D poses more accurately, which requires solving two problems: how to robustly fuse heatmaps across views, and how to assess the reliability of heatmaps. For the former, we introduce a novel objective, Multi-viewExpected-OKS Maximization (MEOM), that locates a 3D joint where the views agree in probability mass. For the latter, we adopt highest-density-region (HDR) calibration as a diagnostic of that mass, independently of distance-based metrics. The proposed framework covers two settings, with and without 3D supervision. Without 3D supervision, we optimize 3D poses from pretrained heatmap predictors by maximizing MEOM, achieving comparable performance with state-of-the-art methods that rely on larger backbones, temporal fusion, and simulated 3D data. On ambiguous Human3.6M (H36MA) and occluded CMU Panoptic frames, the advantage is substantial. When 3D labels are available, we train the model end-to-end with a combined MEOM and MSE loss, achieving 19.11 mm absolute MPJPE on Human3.6M outperforming the state-of-the-art volumetric approach on absolute MPJPE at half the inference cost.

</details>


### [198] [Minerals in the Wild: A Hyperspectral-XRF Dataset for Elemental Composition Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30537)
*Eleftheria Tetoula-Tsonga,George Arvanitakis,Theodoros Giannakas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发布了一个名为"Minerals in the Wild"的多用途矿物高光谱数据集，包含1132个岩石样本及其XRF元素表征，并提出了一种基于USGS光谱库剪枝和凸优化的HSI矿物识别方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱成像（HSI）在矿物勘探和工业矿石处理中具有重要应用前景，但由于缺乏带可靠标注的公开数据集，基于HSI的矿物识别方法开发和评估受到制约。

Method: 构建了跨欧洲采集的1132个岩石样本数据集，提供每种样本的HSI采集和XRF元素表征；定义了元素表征任务，并提出一种剪枝机制，在凸优化匹配HSI像素与USGS光谱签名之前，先从USGS字典中移除远距离签名。

Result: 实验表明所提出的方法在元素表征任务上优于更简单的基线方法。

Conclusion: 该数据集和方法的发布填补了HSI矿物识别领域公开数据集的空白，为后续研究提供了基准。

Abstract: Rapid mineral characterization is essential for applications ranging from mineral exploration to industrial ore processing. To this end, Hyperspectral Imaging (HSI) has emerged as a promising sensing modality thanks to its fine spectral resolution, enabling mineral discrimination in both close-range and remote sensing settings. However, the scarcity of publicly available datasets with reliable ground-truth labels hinders the development and evaluation of HSI-based mineral identification methods. We release Minerals in the Wild, a multi-purpose dataset comprising 1,132 rock specimens collected across Europe. For each specimen, we provide an HSI acquisition together with an elemental characterization obtained via an XRF sensor. We define the task of elemental characterization on our dataset and propose a pruning mechanism that removes distant signatures from the USGS dictionary prior to a convex optimization approach for matching HSI pixels with USGS spectral signatures. Finally, we empirically show that our approach outperforms simpler baselines.

</details>


### [199] [Modality Disentangled Learning for Incomplete Multimodal Emotion Recognition: A Primitive Memory Distillation Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30563)
*Jiaqi Zhang,Zheng Pang,Mengting Li,Yiqi Wang,Guangyuan Dong,Chao Xue,Yusen Wu,Zihao Li,Huy Phan,Sicheng Zhao,Björn W. Schuller,Jiachen Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Primitive Memory Distillation (PriMD)框架，从模态内部出发，将跨模态共享语义与模态特有信息解耦，并将后者离散化为可学习的语义原语构建记忆库，在模态缺失时通过动态检索原语来补偿缺失信息，从而提升多模态情感识别的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态情感识别方法在模态缺失时通常将缺失模态作为整体进行生成、对齐或蒸馏，忽略了模态内部信息在可恢复性上的异质性差异，导致共享语义与模态特有细节被混淆处理，产生不稳定的表征并降低鲁棒性。

Method: PriMD首先将跨模态共享语义从模态特有表征中解耦出来，然后将模态特有信息离散化为可学习的语义原语，构建各模态专属的记忆库。在模态缺失时，采用师生框架，学生模型利用可用模态的共享语义作为查询动态检索原语，在受限记忆空间内补偿缺失的模态特有信息，并与教师模型对齐。

Result: 在IEMOCAP、CMU-MOSI和CMU-MOSEI三个基准数据集上，PriMD在各种模态缺失设置下均取得最先进性能，展现出更强的鲁棒性，同时缓解了整体式特征推断带来的不稳定性。

Conclusion: 通过从模态内部视角处理信息可恢复性的异质性，PriMD有效解决了模态缺失场景下多模态情感识别的鲁棒性问题，验证了语义原语记忆库方法的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal Emotion Recognition (MER) systems often suffer from missing modalities in real-world scenarios. Existing methods usually generate, align, or distill missing modalities as a whole, overlooking the heterogeneous nature of the information carried by each modality. Such holistic treatment mixes inferable shared semantics with uncertain modality-specific details, yielding unstable representations and degrading robustness. To address this issue, we propose the Primitive Memory Distillation (PriMD) framework. Unlike existing methods, PriMD takes an intra-modal perspective and focuses on how different types of information within a modality differ in recoverability within each modality. PriMD first disentangles cross-modal shared semantics from modality-specific representations, and then discretizes the latter into learnable semantic primitives to construct modality-specific memory banks. When modalities are missing, PriMD is a teacher-student framework that the student model uses the shared semantics of available modalities as queries to dynamically retrieve primitives. It compensates for missing modality-specific information within a constrained memory space and aligns with the teacher model. Extensive experiments on IEMOCAP, CMU-MOSI, and CMU-MOSEI demonstrate that PriMD achieves state-of-the-art performance and consistently stronger robustness across a wide range of missing-modality settings, while mitigating the instability caused by holistic feature inference. Our code and project website are available at https://github.com/JiaqiZhang-Sengoku/PriMD and https://jiaqizhang-sengoku.github.io/PriMD/, respectively.

</details>


### [200] [Learning Compositional Spatio-Temporal Video Grounding with Synthetic Curriculum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30584)
*Xingjian Wang,Shijian Wang,Yibo Wang,Zihao Yu,Runhao Fu,Xuelian Cheng,Zongyuan Ge*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出组合时空视频定位（CompSTVG）任务，通过构建合成数据引擎STVG-CompBench基准和基于课程强化学习的CurrSTVG框架，解决现有模型在组合文本查询下性能大幅下降的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有STVG评估和训练数据主要集中于简单查询，忽视了真实场景中常见的组合查询（需联合推理属性与关系来消歧目标）。这导致当前模型在复杂组合查询上表现不佳，且这一性能下降常被数据集级别的平均指标所掩盖。

Method: 提出CompSTVG任务；设计合成数据引擎，以时空场景图作为难度度量，将难度可控的查询合成建模为约束规划问题；构建STVG-CompBench基准，按显式难度层级分层（涵盖时间复杂度和空间干扰）；提出CurrSTVG课程强化学习框架用于训练。

Result: 在STVG-CompBench上评估11个代表性STVG模型，发现它们在组合查询上表现较差，出现显著性能下降；使用CurrSTVG框架训练的模型在所有难度级别上均有提升，其中最具挑战性的组合查询上改进最大。

Conclusion: 组合查询是STVG任务中被忽视但至关重要的挑战，当前模型难以应对；通过难度分级的合成数据和课程强化学习可以有效提升模型的组合推理能力。

Abstract: Despite the impressive progress of recent MLLMs on spatio-temporal video grounding (STVG), existing evaluations and training data focus primarily on simple queries. They largely overlook the compositional queries prevalent in real-world scenarios, where a target must be disambiguated by jointly reasoning about its attributes and relations to other entities. To bridge this gap, we propose Compositional Spatio-Temporal Video Grounding (CompSTVG), a task that requires models to process complex textual queries where every intertwined attribute and relational cue is essential for disambiguation. To facilitate this task at scale, we build a synthetic data engine that leverages a spatio-temporal scene graph as a difficulty measure and casts difficulty-controlled query synthesis as a constraint programming problem, producing difficulty-graded data for both evaluation and training. Built on this engine, we introduce STVG-CompBench, a benchmark stratified by explicit difficulty levels that jointly capture temporal complexity and spatial interference. Evaluating 11 representative STVG models on STVG-CompBench reveals that current models perform poorly on compositional queries, exhibiting a sharp performance drop that is typically obscured by overall dataset-level averages. We further construct synthetic training data and propose CurrSTVG, a curriculum reinforcement learning framework that delivers consistent gains, with the largest improvements observed on the most challenging compositional queries.

</details>


### [201] [DiffSAC: Diffusion-guided Sampling for Consensus-based Robust Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30603)
*Chang Nie,Guangming Wang,Zhe Liu,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DiffSAC框架，利用扩散模型学习有效最小集分布，以置信度细化替代排序，仅需评估几十个假设即可达到SOTA性能，显著提升鲁棒估计效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统样本一致性方法在假设评估前难以有效识别最小集，导致采样效率低下，需要评估大量无效假设。

Method: 引入扩散模型学习有效最小集的分布，通过细化每个数据点的置信度（而非排序）来判断其是否属于良好的最小集，并以几何特征作为条件约束细化方向，输出少量高质量的候选最小集。

Result: 在五个经典计算机视觉任务上的大量实验表明，DiffSAC以仅评估几十个假设即可达到SOTA性能（此前方法需评估上万假设），且扩散模型采样加速器可实现实时运行。

Conclusion: DiffSAC通过扩散引导采样大幅提升了样本共识方法的效率，可作为即插即用模块集成到现有方法中，具有广泛的适用性。

Abstract: Robust estimation is a core computer vision task frequently tackled using sample consensus. However, traditional methods suffer from inefficient sampling as they struggle to identify effective minimum sets before hypothesis evaluation. To address these challenges, we propose a novel Diffusion-guided Sampling for Consensus-based Robust Estimation (DiffSAC) framework. DiffSAC introduces a diffusion model to learn the distribution of effective minimum sets. It refines the confidence for each data point, indicating whether it belongs to a good minimum set, rather than ranking the data points as in previous work. This significantly reduces the need to process numerous bad sets. To constrain the refinement direction, geometric features are incorporated as conditions within our diffusion model. Consequently, DiffSAC outputs a small number of high-quality minimum sets, enabling identification of the best hypothesis via consensus evaluation. Notably, compared to previous works requiring evaluating over ten thousand hypotheses, DiffSAC achieves state-of-the-art performance with only dozens, significantly boosting efficiency. Extensive experiments across five classic computer vision tasks demonstrate the superiority of DiffSAC. The diffusion model's sampling accelerators enable real-time operation, and DiffSAC can be used as a plug-and-play module to improve existing sample consensus methods.

</details>


### [202] [OCR-Based Field Extraction for Archaeological Pottery Metadata: The CENTURIA Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30616)
*Gissu Valentina Naghavi,Dominik Hagmann,Martin Kampel,Irene Ballester*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CENTURIA数据集，包含来自罗马Carnuntum遗址的507份陶器记录，用于测试将手写考古档案转换为结构化元数据的光学字符识别（OCR）模型。研究发现零样本OCR模型的转录错误率高达15-32%，领域专用字段恢复率不足3%；而仅使用57个样本进行LoRA微调即可将错误率降至1.5%以下，字段级准确率超过87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 考古学家通过手写技术图纸和元数据记录陶器信息，这些数据对年代测定、来源归属和跨遗址比较至关重要，但手工转录效率极低，无法进行大规模计算分析。作者希望探索先进文档分析模型能否自动化这一转录过程。

Method: 构建CENTURIA数据集（507份陶器记录，含转录文本、边界框和七个元数据类别的结构化字段标签），对五种OCR模型进行基准测试，并采用LoRA（低秩适应）微调技术，仅用57个样本贴近真实档案标注预算进行优化。

Result: 零样本状态下转录错误率为15-32% SpACER-M，领域字段恢复率低于3%；使用57个样本进行LoRA微调后，转录错误率降至1.5%以下，整体字段级准确率提升至87%以上，有效弥合了领域差距。

Conclusion: 少量专家验证的微调样本即可将手写陶器文档高效转换为结构化、可搜索的元数据，适用于考古数据库建设，证明小规模高质量标注在领域专用OCR任务中具有显著价值。

Abstract: Pottery is a primary source for reconstructing the chronological and economic dimensions of past societies. Archaeologists often document ceramic finds through technical drawings and handwritten metadata. This metadata is critical for dating, provenance attribution, and cross-site comparison, but remains inaccessible to computational analysis, requiring manual transcription of every record. We investigate whether state-of-the-art document analysis models can address this task, and introduce CENTURIA, a dataset of 507 pottery records from the Roman site of Carnuntum, providing transcriptions, bounding boxes, and structured field-level labels across seven metadata categories. Benchmarking five OCR models reveals a substantial domain gap: zero-shot transcription error reaches 15-32% SpACER-M, far exceeding rates on printed archival documents, with domain-specific fields recovered in fewer than 3% of cases. LoRA fine-tuning on just 57 samples, reflecting a realistic archival annotation budget, closes this gap, reducing transcription error to below 1.5% and recovering overall field-level accuracy above 87%. Our results show that a small expert-validated fine-tuning set suffices to convert handwritten pottery documentation into structured, searchable metadata ready for archaeological databases.

</details>


### [203] [RealCAD: Towards Real-World Image-to-CAD Reconstruction under Domain Shift and Parameter Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30617)
*Yihe Sun,Ziyu Lu,Kaihua Tang,Xian-Sheng Hua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RealCAD，一个统一的图像到CAD重建框架，从表示层、图像层和特征层三个层面分别解决合成图像与真实图像之间的外观域差距以及DeepCAD局部归一化导致的参数分布偏差问题，并引入包含392个3D打印物体四视图像和真实命令序列的OpenRealCAD数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像到CAD方法主要基于合成渲染开发，面临两大耦合障碍：合成与真实图像之间显著的外观域差距，以及广泛使用的CAD数据中存在的参数偏差问题。DeepCAD的局部归一化使多个几何参数集中于少量离散值，模型可利用频率先验而非从图像推断几何来获得高参数精度，导致评估指标失真。

Method: RealCAD从三个层面解决上述问题：(1) 表示层：重新分配尺度信息到对应几何参数，在共享尺度空间中产生更不集中的参数分布；(2) 图像层：基于几何约束的图像转换，结合物体轮廓将合成渲染转换到真实图像域；(3) 特征层：多正样本对比学习目标，使同一CAD模型在不同视角和图像域间的表示对齐，支持单视角CAD序列预测。

Result: 实验表明，修正后的表示显著降低了可从参数频率先验获得的精度，使参数精度成为图像条件几何推断的更可靠指标。RealCAD在真实域的命令和参数精度上均有提升，同时保持有竞争力的合成域性能。此外引入了OpenRealCAD数据集（392个3D打印物体的四视图照片及其真实命令序列）。

Conclusion: RealCAD通过表示层、图像层和特征层的统一设计，有效缓解了图像到CAD重建中的域差距和参数偏差问题，使参数精度评估更真实地反映模型从图像推断几何的能力。

Abstract: Reconstructing editable Computer-Aided Design (CAD) models from images is essential for downstream modification, manufacturing, and design reuse. However, existing image-to-CAD methods are developed predominantly on synthetic renderings and face two coupled obstacles: a substantial appearance domain gap between synthetic and real images, and a previously overlooked parameter bias in widely used CAD data. We show that the local normalization adopted by DeepCAD concentrates several geometric parameters around a few discrete values while encoding substantial information in a single scale factor. Consequently, a model can achieve deceptively high parameter accuracy by exploiting these frequent values rather than inferring geometry from the input image. In this paper, we propose RealCAD, a unified framework that addresses these limitations at the representation, image, and feature levels. At the representation level, we redistribute scale information to the corresponding geometric parameters, producing less concentrated parameter distributions in a shared scale space. At the image level, geometry-constrained translation converts synthetic renderings toward the real-image domain while conditioning on object contours. At the feature level, a multi-positive contrastive objective aligns representations of the same CAD model across viewpoints and image domains, enabling CAD sequence prediction from each individual view. We further introduce OpenRealCAD, comprising four-view photographs of 392 3D-printed objects paired with ground-truth command sequences. Experiments show that the revised representation substantially reduces the accuracy attainable from parameter-frequency priors, making parameter accuracy a more reliable measure of image-conditioned geometric inference. RealCAD further improves real-domain command and parameter accuracy, while retaining competitive synthetic-domain performance.

</details>


### [204] [AQ3D: Adaptive Query Transformer for 3D Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30618)
*Keno Moenck,Thorsten Schüppstuhl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AQ3D提出了一种适应不同规模室内场景的3D实例分割解码器方法，通过按场景超点比例动态实例化查询集，利用3D RoPE编码度量坐标替代传统学习式位置编码，并引入基于归因的超点池化、掩码细化分支和余弦分类器，在ScanNetV2、ScanNet200和ScanNet++V2上达到最新最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer解码器在3D实例分割中采用固定数量的查询和基于训练分布校准的位置建模，而室内扫描场景在空间范围和物体数量上差异很大，导致固定查询集在小场景下过度初始化、在大场景下初始化不足，同时学习到的绝对和相对位置编码受限于训练场景范围和可能饱和。

Method: AQ3D的核心方法包括：(1) 以场景超点数的固定比例实例化查询，形成过完备集合，将背景拒绝完全交给解码器；(2) 使用3D RoPE对量化度量坐标进行位置编码，替代传统的学习式有界查找表；(3) 改进解码器本身，采用基于归因的超点池化、掩码细化分支和余弦分类器进行背景拒绝。

Result: 在ScanNetV2、ScanNet200和ScanNet++V2数据集的验证集和隐藏测试集上，AQ3D在无需额外数据增强的解码器方法中取得了新的最先进性能。

Conclusion: 通过动态查询实例化和3D RoPE位置编码，AQ3D成功解决了Transformer解码器在变尺度室内场景中的泛化问题，验证了适应场景规模的自适应查询机制对3D实例分割的有效性。

Abstract: Transformer-based decoders for 3D instance segmentation typically commit to a fixed number of queries and positional modeling calibrated on the training distribution rather than on the scene at hand. Indoor scans vary widely in spatial extent and object count, so a fixed query set over-initializes small scenes and under-initializes large ones, while learned absolute and relative encodings are bound to the training scenes' extents and can saturate. We present AQ3D, which is designed to handle scenes of various sizes during training and inference. Queries are instantiated at a fixed ratio of the scene's superpoints, forming an overcomplete set whose background rejection is entirely left to the decoder. Positional information is encoded using 3D RoPE over quantized metric coordinates, replacing learned bounded lookup tables of prior decoders. Further, we improve the decoder itself by using attribution-based superpoint pooling, a mask refinement branch, and a cosine classifier for background rejection. Experiments show our method sets a new state-of-the-art on validation and hidden test splits across the datasets ScanNetV2, ScanNet200, and ScanNet++V2 among decoder methods trained without additional data augmentation. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/kenomo/aq3d}{github.com/kenomo/aq3d}.

</details>


### [205] [Cost-efficient Active Learning for Referring Image Segmentation and Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30621)
*Junbeom Hong,Seonghoon Yu,Hyung Rok Jung,Sundong Kim,Jeany Son*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向视觉定位（VG）的主动学习（AL）框架，在仅有原始图像、无文本标注的现实条件下，利用基础模型生成辅助区域-文本对，并提出"所指区域歧义度"（Referred Region Ambiguity）作为新的采集函数，优先选择具有跨区域竞争歧义的图像进行标注，同时设计高效的标注界面，显著提升标注效率和模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉定位任务中，收集自然语言指代表达与区域标注（如掩码或框）是主要的瓶颈，因为标注者需要撰写能够区分目标区域与视觉相似区域的描述，成本高昂。现有工作假设已有文本可用，但现实场景中仅有原始图像，缺乏文本标注，这使得样本选择难以估计哪些图像包含需要判别性指代表达的歧义区域。

Method: 首先利用基础模型为原始图像生成辅助区域-文本对，以弥补无文本标注的缺口；其次提出新的采集函数"Referred Region Ambiguity"，度量模型置信度是集中在单一区域还是分散在多个候选区域，从而优先选择具有强烈跨区域竞争（即视觉歧义）的图像；最后设计了一个指代表达标注界面，帮助标注者通过少量点击快速聚焦于撰写判别性语言。

Result: 在RIS（Referring Image Segmentation）和REC（Referring Expression Comprehension）基准上的实验表明，所提出的AL框架在多个主动学习基线上持续取得更优性能；用户研究显示标注速度最高可提升1.6倍。

Conclusion: 通过生成辅助数据和设计针对性的歧义度量采集函数，本文有效解决了无文本条件下视觉定位数据标注的瓶颈问题，既提升了模型学习效果，又显著加速了人工标注流程，为视觉定位任务的低成本数据收集提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Collecting natural-language referring expressions along with region annotations, such as masks or boxes, is a major bottleneck in visual grounding (VG), as annotators must write descriptions that distinguish target regions from visually similar ones. We tackle this by formulating active learning (AL) for VG under the realistic setting where only raw images are available without accompanying text. Since ground-truth text is unavailable, sample selection must estimate which images contain ambiguous regions that would require discriminative referring expressions. To address this, we generate auxiliary region-text pairs using foundation models, and introduce Referred Region Ambiguity, a new acquisition function that measures whether the model's confidence collapses onto a single region or disperses across multiple candidates. It allows our method to prioritize images with strong cross-region competition, which are more informative due to their visual ambiguity. We also design a referring-expression annotation interface that helps annotators quickly focus on writing discriminative language with a few clicks. Experiments on RIS and REC benchmarks show that our AL framework consistently outperforms several AL baselines, while a user study shows up to 1.6X faster description labeling of ours.

</details>


### [206] [Quantum-Grassmann-Plucker Token Mixing for Deep Learning-Based Post-Disaster Damage Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30633)
*Kooroush Farahkhah,Umut Lagap,Taha Rezaei,Saman Ghaffarian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次将Grassmann-Plucker（GP）token混合应用于计算机视觉，提出了QGP和HQML-GP两种扩展头，用于灾后卫星图像建筑损伤分类。在xBD龙卷风数据集上，QGP在可见和不可见事件的测试中均取得了最优的准确率和macro-F1，验证了GP token混合作为无需注意力机制的Transformer替代方案的竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾后建筑损伤评估对卫星图像的及时性要求高，但现有方法受限于类别不平衡、中间损伤状态模糊以及跨事件迁移能力不足等问题。

Method: 提出三种基于Grassmann-Plucker token混合的分类头：GP头通过Plucker坐标编码token对形成的子空间；QGP头在GP基础上引入振幅概率特征；HQML-GP头进一步结合模拟量子电路的期望值。采用冻结的六通道Vision Transformer基座编码器处理16x16图像块，并与MLP和Transformer基线进行同协议对比。

Result: QGP在可见事件测试集上达到83.46%准确率和64.50% macro-F1，在不可见事件上达到66.45%准确率和52.70% macro-F1，均优于其他方法。HQML-GP在验证集macro-F1最高（65.63%），但测试性能不及QGP且训练时间显著更长。

Conclusion: GP token混合可作为传统Transformer token混合的竞争性无注意力替代方案，用于配对卫星图像的损伤分类任务。

Abstract: Timely post-disaster building damage assessment from satellite imagery is a critical engineering decision support task, yet it remains constrained by class imbalance, ambiguous intermediate damage states, and limited cross-event transferability. This study presents, to our knowledge, the first application of Grassmann-Plucker (GP) token mixing to computer vision and introduces two extensions for image classification: the Quantum-inspired Grassmann-Plucker (QGP) head and the Hybrid Quantum Machine Learning Grassmann-Plucker (HQML-GP) head. The GP head represents multiscale relationships among image patch tokens by encoding subspaces formed by token pairs with Plucker coordinates; QGP enriches these coordinates with amplitude-derived probability features, whereas HQML-GP incorporates expectation values generated by a simulated quantum circuit into the geometric token representation. Paired pre- and post-event image patches from the xBD tornado dataset were processed using a frozen six-channel Vision Transformer base encoder with 16 x 16-pixel patches. The three GP-based heads were compared with multilayer perceptron and Transformer baselines under identical training, checkpoint selection, and evaluation protocols. Joplin and Moore tornado samples were used for model development and seen-event testing, while Tuscaloosa was reserved for unseen-event evaluation. QGP led both test sets in accuracy and macro-F1: 83.46% and 64.50% for the seen events, and 66.45% and 52.70% for the unseen event. Although HQML-GP obtained the highest validation macro-F1 of 65.63%, it did not surpass QGP on either test set and required substantially more training time per epoch. These results establish GP token mixing as a competitive attention-free alternative to conventional Transformer-based token mixing for paired satellite image damage classification.

</details>


### [207] [Fine-Grained Multi Image Object Hallucination Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30653)
*Joonki Min,Chaeyun Kim,Hyungwook Choi,Yejin Kim,Kihyun Kim,Yohan Jo,Joonseok Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MIOH基准，用于系统评估多模态大语言模型在多图像场景中的对象幻觉问题，覆盖四类基础任务与三种推理模式，并通过29个模型的评估揭示了模型的幻觉根源不仅来自感知失败，更来自多图像整合阶段的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs在多图像推理场景中普遍存在对象幻觉问题，但现有基准主要针对单图像设置或仅提供高层级的多图像评估，无法系统诊断视觉复杂度与推理需求如何触发幻觉。

Method: 构建MIOH基准，通过三类多图像推理模式（综合、比较、选择）× 四类基础任务（存在性、计数、属性、位置）× 三种受控对抗压力（视觉上下文规模、感知难度、上下文偏差），系统评估29个模型的对象幻觉表现。

Result: 即使GPT-5和Gemini-2.5-Pro等最先进系统在不同推理模式和任务上也表现出明显的失败模式；幻觉不仅源于感知失败，更源于跨图像维持对象表征时的整合阶段局限。

Conclusion: MIOH提供了受控的多图像对象幻觉分析框架，是开发更可靠多模态AI系统的重要评测工具。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly deployed in multi-image scenarios requiring complex reasoning across visual contexts. However, current MLLMs remain fundamentally limited by object hallucination-generating plausible yet factually inconsistent descriptions about objects. Existing benchmarks, designed primarily for single-image settings or providing only high-level multi-image assessments, cannot systematically diagnose how visual complexity and reasoning demands trigger hallucination. To address this gap, we introduce MIOH, a fine-grained multi-image object hallucination benchmark that systematically evaluates object hallucination across four foundational tasks (existence, counting, attribute, position) through three multi-image reasoning patterns (comprehensive, comparative, selective) under three controlled adversarial pressures (visual context scale, perceptual difficulty, contextual bias). Through evaluation of 29 models, we reveal that even state-of-the-art systems like GPT-5 and Gemini-2.5-Pro exhibit distinct failure patterns across different reasoning patterns and tasks. Our evaluation reveals that hallucination stems not merely from perceptual failures but from integration-stage limitations when maintaining object representations across multiple images. MIOH provides a controlled framework for analyzing multi-image object hallucination and serves as a critical evaluation tool for developing more reliable multimodal AI systems.

</details>


### [208] [APT: Anchor-aligned Perturbations for Tamper Localization in Fully Regenerated Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30656)
*Suhyeon Ha,Woo Jae Kim,Joonsung Jeon,Sooel Son,Sung-eui Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出APT，一种半脆弱潜空间扰动方法，用于图像篡改定位。与现有方法仅适用于拼接（SP）场景不同，APT针对扩散修复的全重生（FR）场景设计，通过将每个空间特征向量对齐到固定锚点方向，利用合成前景与锚点对齐背景特征之间的对齐差异来定位篡改。在COCO数据集上，APT在FR场景下IoU达0.92，远超最强基线WAM的0.84，而现有方法性能几乎崩溃（AUC 0.5）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动篡改定位方法假设拼接（SP）场景，即合成区域叠加在原背景上，嵌入信号保持完整。但现实中的扩散修复是在完全重生（FR）场景下进行的，整个图像经过去噪过程，背景信号被破坏，导致现有框架失效。因此需要一种能适应扩散修复场景的半脆弱定位方法。

Method: APT采用半脆弱潜空间扰动，嵌入密集的向量级定位信号。核心机制是将每个空间特征向量对齐到固定的锚点方向，篡改检测通过修复后合成前景与锚点对齐背景特征之间的对齐差异来实现。同时引入硬负样本挖掘损失和噪声扰动分支，以增强对齐的一致性。

Result: 在COCO数据集上的实验表明：APT在FR场景下达到0.92的IoU，优于最强基线WAM（0.84），而现有方法几乎退化为随机性能（AUC约0.5），验证了APT在测试时未知篡改类型上的通用性。

Conclusion: APT建立了一个实用的取证框架，能在全重生（FR）场景下有效定位篡改区域，且可泛化到测试时未知的篡改类型，填补了现有主动篡改定位方法在扩散修复场景下的空白。

Abstract: Proactive tamper localization embeds an imperceptible signal into an image prior to distribution, enabling pixel-level manipulation detection. Existing methods assume a spliced (SP) setting, where synthesized regions are composited onto the original background, leaving embedded signals intact. However, real-world diffusion-based inpainting operates in a fully regenerated (FR) setting, where the entire image undergoes denoising, disrupting background signals and rendering existing frameworks ineffective. We propose APT, a semi-fragile latent-space perturbation that embeds a dense, vector-wise localization signal. By aligning each spatial feature vector toward a fixed anchor direction, APT localizes tampering via the alignment disparity between synthesized foreground and anchor-aligned background features after inpainting. The proposed hard negative mining loss and noisy perturbation branch further enforce uniform alignment. Experiments on COCO demonstrate that APT achieves an FR IoU of 0.92, outperforming the strongest baseline (WAM, 0.84), while existing methods collapse to near-random performance (AUC 0.5), establishing APT as a practical forensic framework generalizable across tampering types unknown at test time.

</details>


### [209] [InfraOcc: An Infrastructure Occupancy Benchmark with Static-to-Dynamic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30657)
*Lei Yang,Xiaokai Bai,Boqi Li,Chunmian Lin,Li Wang,Ziying Song,Jiahuan Zhang,Enhui Ma,Haibao Yu,Jiaqi Ma,Kaicheng Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次提出面向固定视角路侧基础设施的语义占用感知基准InfraOcc，并针对静态-动态不对称结构提出了渐进式静到动证据推理方法ProSD-Occ，在各项指标上均取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有占用感知基准和方法均围绕移动自车视角设计，将占用视为扁平的单次体素分类，既未度量也未利用固定视角路侧占用中"近持久静态骨架叠加稀疏短暂动态事件"这一独特结构，导致该问题缺乏专门的数据与建模方案。

Method: 从数据和模型两方面切入：构建InfraOcc基准，包含290个多模态序列的密集体素标注、静动态解耦标注流程、统一多模态评测及静动态诊断指标；提出ProSD-Occ方法，将占用重构为渐进式静到动证据推理，先解释持久布局，再在静态置信度引导下暴露残差动态证据，最终将静态、动态和自由空间证据重组为统一场。

Result: 静态基础设施占据97.3%的被占体素且跨帧持续存在，而动态参与者每位置的被占帧数中位数仅为1.8%，揭示结构性静动态不对称。ProSD-Occ在所有赛道（总体、动态、静态、几何）均排名第一，如纯相机动态mIoU相对最强基线提升23.5%，多模态总体mIoU达65.87。

Conclusion: 固定视角路侧占用是一个与移动自车感知本质上不同的问题，需要专门的推理范式。InfraOcc与ProSD-Occ确立了该问题的首个基准与领先方法，并阐明静态-动态不对称是超越语义长尾的结构性挑战。

Abstract: Fixed-viewpoint infrastructure sensors repeatedly observe the same traffic space, making roadside 3D occupancy structurally different from ego-vehicle perception: a near-persistent static scaffold is overlaid with sparse, short-lived dynamic events. Existing occupancy benchmarks and methods, however, are built around moving ego vehicles and neither measure nor exploit this structure, instead treating occupancy as flat one-shot voxel classification. We address this gap from both data and model perspectives. We build InfraOcc, to our knowledge, the first real-world infrastructure-side semantic occupancy benchmark, with dense voxel annotations for 290 multi-modal sequences in a fixed roadside frame, a static-dynamic decoupled annotation pipeline, unified camera-only, LiDAR-only, and multi-modal evaluation, and diagnostics for static and dynamic occupancy. InfraOcc shows that static infrastructure fills 97.3% of occupied voxels and persists across frames, whereas dynamic participants have a median occupied-frame ratio of only 1.8% per location, revealing a structural static-dynamic asymmetry beyond semantic long-tailedness. We further propose ProSD-Occ, which reformulates occupancy as progressive static-to-dynamic evidence reasoning: it explains persistent layout, exposes residual dynamic evidence under static-confidence guidance, and recomposes static, dynamic, and free-space evidence into a unified field. ProSD-Occ ranks first in overall, dynamic, static, and geometric occupancy on every track, e.g., a 23.5% relative camera-only dynamic-mIoU gain over the strongest baseline and 65.87 multi-modal overall mIoU, establishing fixed-viewpoint roadside occupancy as a distinct problem with its own reasoning paradigm. The benchmark and code will be publicly available at https://github.com/yanglei18/InfraOcc

</details>


### [210] [UFPR-PEs: A Brazilian Face Recognition Benchmark with Self-Declared Race/Color Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30688)
*Alexandre Diano,Bernardo Biesseck,Gabriel Polo,Vinicius Gregorio,Laura Lopes,Diego Addan,David Menotti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了UFPR-PEs基准数据集，用于评估人脸识别系统在真实公共视频条件下的种族/肤色偏差。该数据集基于巴西选举官员的公开视频，采用巴西人口普查的种族/肤色分类法（包括独特的parda类别），并在验证和识别任务中分析了不同子群和难度水平下的性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人脸识别系统虽被广泛部署，但其在不同人口群体间的可靠性和鲁棒性仍未得到充分保障。现有基准主要采用美国或欧洲中心的种族分类框架，缺乏对巴西等多元社会结构的覆盖，尤其是缺少parda这一独特类别。同时，现有基准往往忽略真实压缩视频等困难条件下的评估。

Method: 作者构建了UFPR-PEs基准：从巴西政客的公开压缩视频中采集人脸数据，采用巴西人口普查的官方自我声明种族/肤色分类法（包括parda类别），保留困难样本以反映真实条件。评估涵盖验证和闭集/开集识别任务，并进行种族/肤色和难度级别的子群分析。

Result: 实验结果表明，人脸识别性能随图像质量变化显著，种族/肤色子群间的性能差距必须结合视觉难度进行联合解读，而非单独分析。该基准提供了可复现且具有人口学依据的评估环境。

Conclusion: UFPR-PEs为在具有挑战性的公共视频条件下研究人脸识别偏差提供了一个可复现且人口学基础扎实的基准框架，弥补了现有基准在分类学多样性和真实视觉条件方面的不足。

Abstract: While face recognition systems are widely deployed, ensuring their demographic reliability and robustness under uncontrolled visual conditions remains a critical challenge. To bridge this gap, we present UFPR-PEs, a benchmark for face recognition bias evaluation using public videos of elected Brazilian politicians annotated with official self-declared race/color categories. The dataset adopts the Brazilian census taxonomy, including the parda category, which has no direct equivalent in the U.S.- or Europe-centric schemas commonly used in prior benchmarks. Our benchmark is built from compressed public video and preserves difficult samples so that performance can be analyzed under realistic conditions. We describe the construction pipeline, report dataset statistics, and evaluate face recognition performance across verification and (closed- and open-set) identification settings, including subgroup analysis by race/color and difficulty level. The results show that recognition performance varies substantially with image quality, and that subgroup gaps must be interpreted jointly with visual difficulty rather than in isolation. Overall, UFPR-PEs provides a reproducible and demographically grounded setting for studying face recognition bias under challenging public video conditions.

</details>


### [211] [CANVAS: Consistency-Aware Navigation via Visual Adaptive Sampling for Long-Context Text-to-SVG Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30689)
*Yichen Wu,Haoxuan Qu,Yihang Lou,Hossein Rahmani,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CANVAS是一个无需训练、具备渲染感知的推理框架，通过结合能量锐化轨迹似然与渲染未来视觉反馈，推导出笔画级导航规则，在不增加训练的情况下显著提升自回归模型生成SVG的全局一致性（几何关系、空间布局、遮挡顺序和整体构图）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归大模型在文生SVG任务中虽已能生成复杂长上下文图形，但标准自回归解码在几何、布局、遮挡和构图等全局一致性方面经常失效，亟需一种不依赖额外训练的推理改进方案。

Method: 提出CANVAS框架：1）利用功率锐化的轨迹似然增强候选路径评估；2）引入渲染未来帧的视觉反馈作为采样依据；3）推导笔画级导航规则，在有限的生成与渲染预算下估算每个候选笔画的未来价值；4）根据候选不确定性、决策影响力和回滚成本自适应分配采样资源。

Result: 在多个自回归SVG基座模型和互补基准上的实验表明，CANVAS在全局一致性（几何关系、空间布局、遮挡排序及整体构图）方面均取得改进，且无需额外训练，验证了框架的有效性与泛化能力。

Conclusion: CANVAS证明了一种无需训练、渲染感知的自适应采样推理框架，能够有效提升自回归模型在复杂SVG生成任务中的全局一致性与可泛化性。

Abstract: Autoregressive large models have recently advanced Text-to-SVG generation from simple icons to complex, long-context graphics, yet standard autoregressive decoding often fails to maintain global consistency across geometry, layout, occlusion, and composition. We introduce CANVAS (Consistency-Aware Navigation via Visual Adaptive Sampling), a training-free, render-aware inference framework that combines power-sharpened trajectory likelihood with visual feedback from rendered futures and derives a stroke-wise navigation rule. It effectively estimates each candidate stroke's future value under a limited generation and rendering budget and adaptively allocates samples according to candidate uncertainty, decision influence, and rollout cost. Experiments across multiple autoregressive SVG backbones and complementary benchmarks demonstrate improvements in global consistency, which includes sound geometric relationships, spatial layouts, occlusion ordering, and overall composition, without additional training, demonstrating the effectiveness and generalization ability of our framework.

</details>


### [212] [Failure or Drift? Evaluating Monocular SLAM under Synthetic and Real-World Corruptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30690)
*Abhay Skaria Thomas,Shashank Agnihotri,Margret Keuper*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了单目视觉SLAM系统在受控合成退化条件下的鲁棒性评估问题。作者评估了一个经典基于特征的系统（ORB-SLAM等）和两个学习型跟踪器在图像空间、几何感知和复合退化下的表现，并与真实恶劣天气（4Seasons数据集）进行对比。研究发现，学习型跟踪器倾向于将灾难性失败转化为持续漂移；更重要的是，系统排名会随退化的物理保真度而变化——结构化的雨和雾代理能保持真实世界排序，而简单的光照代理则不能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉SLAM通常在干净轨迹上评测，但实际部署失败常由恶劣天气、光照、模糊和传感器伪影引起。受控退化能隔离这些因素，但合成压力测试只有在引出与真实条件相同的工程结论时才有价值。本文旨在检验这一前提对单目SLAM是否成立。

Method: 在单目SLAM框架下，对经典特征法系统（ORB-SLAM等）和两个学习型跟踪器施加图像空间、几何感知及复合退化，并利用4Seasons数据集中的真实恶劣条件作为对照。评估指标将显式跟踪失败与仍处于激活状态方法累积的漂移分开考量，而非仅使用单一轨迹误差。

Result: 学习型跟踪器主要将灾难性失败转化为持续且有时严重的漂移。更为关键的是，学习算法的相对排名随退化物理保真度改变：结构化的雨/雾代理能保持真实世界排序，而简单的光照代理无法复现真实世界的相对表现。

Conclusion: 受控合成退化在视觉SLAM鲁棒性评估中并非普遍可靠——其有效性取决于代理退化的物理保真度。研究者应谨慎选择具有物理真实感的退化模型，否则合成压力测试可能得出与真实工况相反的工程结论。

Abstract: Visual SLAM is commonly evaluated on clean trajectories, although deployment failures are often caused by adverse weather, illumination, blur, and sensor artifacts. Controlled corruptions are attractive because they isolate such factors, but a synthetic stress test is useful only when it leads to the same engineering conclusion as the condition it is intended to approximate. This work examines that question for monocular SLAM. We evaluate a classical feature-based system and two learned trackers under image-space, geometry-aware, and compound corruptions, and compare their behavior with adverse conditions from 4Seasons. Rather than reducing robustness to a single trajectory error, the evaluation separates explicit tracking failure from drift accumulated by methods that remain active. The results show that learned trackers largely replace catastrophic loss with sustained, and sometimes severe, drift. More importantly, the apparent ordering of the learned systems changes with the physical fidelity of the corruption: structured rain and fog proxies preserve the real-world ordering, whereas a simple illumination proxy does not. Code is available at: https://github.com/abhaythomas/master_thesis_vslamlab_robustness.

</details>


### [213] [Can Video World Models Track Unobserved World States?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30692)
*Joonghyuk Shin,Yicong Hong,Jaesik Park,Xun Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过动作条件视频"Shell Game"任务（视觉版的S5状态跟踪），研究了视频世界模型是否能真正维护世界的隐藏状态，而不仅仅是生成视觉上合理的帧。研究发现双向/自回归Transformer、Mamba和线性注意力（限于非负转移特征值）在训练范围内拟合良好，但在外推更长交换链时退化为随机猜测，尽管仍能生成看似合理的视频。真正能外推的机制有两种：允许负特征值的线性注意力和TTT非线性快速权重，它们都能跨块携带状态并就地更新。像素扩散目标无法监督隐藏状态，因此状态必须存在于架构而非token中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视频世界模型评估主要依赖视觉保真度，但视觉真实并不等同于模型真正维护了世界的隐藏状态。作者希望弥合"视觉逼真"与"状态跟踪能力"之间的差距，并探究哪些架构机制能够在超出训练长度的情景下保持世界状态的连续性。

Method: 设计了一个动作条件视频Shell Game任务，将可视渲染与隐藏状态合成解耦，评估多种序列模型架构。比较了双向和自回归Transformer、Mamba、线性注意力（限制非负与允许负转移特征值两种设置）以及TTT（Test-Time Training）非线性快速权重方法，并测试了额外去噪步骤是否有助于改善状态跟踪。

Result: 所有限制于非负转移特征值的模型在训练范围内（5次交换）表现良好，但在更长交换链的外推中退化至随机水平，同时仍能渲染出视觉合理的视频。额外去噪步骤无济于事。有两种机制成功外推：允许负特征值的线性注意力和TTT非线性快速权重，二者均能跨块携带状态并就地更新。在动态世界探索任务的更困难场景中进一步验证了这些发现。

Conclusion: 视频世界模型仅靠视觉保真度评估是不够的，必须显式关注架构能否维护隐藏状态。像素级扩散目标无法监督隐藏状态，状态信息必须被架构自身承载。传统Transformer的append-only KV缓存无法跨块维护状态，而线性注意力（允许负特征值）和TTT快速权重机制为构建真正的状态化视频世界模型提供了可行方向。

Abstract: Video world models are increasingly used as simulators, yet visual fidelity alone does not show that a model maintains the hidden state of the world. We examine this gap with an action-conditioned video Shell Game, a visual analog of $S_5$ state tracking that decouples visual rendering from compositing the hidden state underneath. Bidirectional and autoregressive Transformers, Mamba, and linear attention restricted to nonnegative transition eigenvalues all fit the training horizon of 5 swaps and then fall toward chance on longer swap chains (extrapolation) while still rendering plausible video with additional denoising steps providing no benefit. The pixel-based diffusion target never supervises the unseen hidden state, so the generated frames cannot carry it and the state has to live inside the architecture rather than in the tokens. For a Transformer, that architectural state is only an append-only KV cache, so the model has to re-derive the hidden arrangement from the whole history at every chunk. We find two mechanisms that do extrapolate, and both carry a state across chunks and revise it in place. Linear attention succeeds once its transition eigenvalues may be negative, and TTT with a nonlinear fast weight succeeds by updating the feature map through which it reads its own state. We further examine harder cases in dynamic world exploration tasks, and discuss the broader implications for building stateful video world models.

</details>


### [214] [TUE-Detector: A Tool-Using Expert MLLM-Based Detector for AI-Generated Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30704)
*Yichen Wu,Haoxuan Qu,Yongxing Dai,Yan Bai,Yihang Lou,Yuqi Lin,Hossein Rahmani,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TUE-Detector，一种基于工具调用的多模态大语言模型（MLLM）AI生成视频检测框架，通过训练模型调用合适的工具收集异常痕迹证据并进行推理，从而实现可靠的AI生成视频检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成视频检测的关键挑战在于准确识别细微但可测量的不自然痕迹，现有方法难以可靠地完成这一任务。

Method: 提出Tool-Using Expert MLLM框架（TUE-Detector），将通用MLLM训练为任务定制的工具使用专家检测器，能够调用合适的工具、收集具体的不自然证据，并基于证据进行推理判断；同时引入新颖设计以保证专家检测器获得高质量且合适的工具。

Result: 大量实验表明该框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 从工具介导的证据发现这一新颖视角来解决AI生成视频检测问题，通过工具调用与证据推理提升了检测的可靠性。

Abstract: AI-generated video detection, which aims to distinguish AI-generated videos from real ones, has recently received increasing research attention. To perform this task reliably, a key challenge lies in accurately identifying subtle-yet-measurable unnatural artifacts. In this work, we address this challenge from a novel perspective of tool-mediated evidence discovery and propose Tool-Using Expert MLLM-based AI-generated Video Detector (TUE-Detector), a novel framework for AI-generated video detection. TUE-Detector trains a general MLLM into a task-tailored tool-using expert detector that learns to invoke suitable tools, collect concrete evidence of unnaturalness, and reason over the evidence for reliable detection. Meanwhile, TUE-Detector further introduces novel designs to equip the expert detector with high-quality and suitable tools. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework.

</details>


### [215] [VisLens: Single-Pass Interpretable Visual Search for Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30705)
*Jingyi He,Sanghwan Kim,Zeynep Akata*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VisLens，一种基于logit lens的视觉搜索方法，通过将隐藏状态投影到LLM头部来解码视觉token语义，利用轻量级tuned-lens从早期层映射到最终隐藏状态空间，实现单次前向传播完成精细视觉搜索，比现有方法快8.5-22.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在细粒度视觉搜索任务中表现不佳，现有解决方案存在两难：基于注意力或置信度的训练-free方法准确但速度慢（需多次查询）；基于强化学习训练的工具使用模型推理快但不可控、难以解释。

Method: VisLens构建在logit lens之上，通过将隐藏状态投影经过LLM head来解码其语义；进一步使用轻量级tuned-lens将早期隐藏状态映射到最终隐藏状态空间，使视觉token可从早期层读出；将视觉token与查询中的目标词匹配以生成相关区域的裁剪图，与原始图像一起输入得到最终答案；整个过程在单次前向传播中完成。

Result: VisLens在性能上匹敌或超越现有基线，同时具有显著延迟优势：比Thyme快8.5-9.9倍，比训练-free多通道搜索方法快高达22.2倍。

Conclusion: VisLens通过logit lens实现可解释且单次前向传播的视觉搜索方法，在保持准确性的同时大幅提升推理速度，克服了现有方法在速度与可解释性之间的权衡。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) struggle with fine-grained Visual Search, the task of locating small or rare objects in high-resolution images. Existing remedies fall into two families: (1) Training-free methods based on attention or confidence scores are accurate but slow, since they require multiple MLLM queries per example. (2) Reinforcement Learning (RL) trained tool-use models are faster at inference but opaque, since their tool calls remain uncontrollable and hard to interpret. To overcome this, we propose \emph{VisLens} (Visual Focus via Logit Lens), a Visual Search method built on the logit lens, which decodes the semantics held in a hidden state by projecting it through the LLM head. VisLens further uses a lightweight tuned-lens that maps early hidden states into the final hidden state space, so visual tokens can be read out from early layers. These tokens are matched to target words in the query to generate a crop of the relevant region, which is fed back in alongside the original image to produce the final answer. The whole process, from decoding to the final answer, completes in a single forward pass without repeated queries. VisLens matches or exceeds prior baselines while delivering a substantial latency advantage, running $8.5$--$9.9\times$ faster than Thyme and up to $22.2\times$ faster than training-free multi-pass search methods.

</details>


### [216] [RailSyn: Diagnosis-Guided Image Generation for Traceable Data Completion in Railway Foreign Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30709)
*Quan Hao,Chenxi Zhang,Ziyang Tao,Yuyuan Zhou,Yudong Wang,Rui Shi,Lechuan Xu,Changhao Liu,Liguo Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RailSyn，一个诊断引导的铁路异物检测（RFOD）合成数据增强框架，包含真实参考的Inspector和需求对齐的Generator，通过经验覆盖率分析和审计驱动的生成策略，将生成数据对真实派生补全区域的覆盖率提升至13.64%，在九个主流检测器上取得最高4.9点的AP50-95提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁路异物检测（RFOD）中真实正样本稀缺，无法完整覆盖任务相关的目标尺度、侵入关系、铁路场景、光照和恶劣天气等变化。现有合成增强方法虽然能提升检测性能，但缺乏对生成数据所补充的任务相关缺陷的显式说明，难以确保合成数据真正弥补真实数据的不足。

Method: RailSyn由两个核心组件构成：一是真实参考的Inspector，通过可变半径经验覆盖方法从有限真实观测中定位候选补全区域并分析合成数据池；二是需求对齐的Generator，通过域适应、智能体规划的放置与物理接触关系、以及计划一致的条件下细化来满足审计识别出的铁路场景、侵入语义和视觉一致性需求。

Result: 完整系统在局部壳层覆盖率上达到13.64%的C_gap指标，实验显示AP50-95最高提升4.9点，并在九个主流检测器上取得一致的性能提升，展示了跨架构的广泛适用性。

Conclusion: RailSyn通过诊断引导的合成数据生成框架，显式地将生成与任务相关缺陷的补全需求对齐，有效提升了RFOD检测性能，证明了审计驱动的合成增强方法的有效性。

Abstract: Railway foreign object detection (RFOD) is critical to safe railway operation, yet scarce real positive samples incompletely represent task-relevant variations in object scale, intrusion relation, railway scene, illumination, and adverse weather. Existing synthetic augmentation can improve RFOD detection, but its gains lack an explicit account of the task-relevant deficiencies complemented by the generated data. We therefore introduce RailSyn, a diagnosis-guided framework comprising a real-referenced Inspector and a requirement-aligned Generator. The Inspector constructs a variable-radius empirical cover from finite real observations to localize candidate completion regions and profile synthetic pools. The resulting audit identifies railway-context, intrusion-semantic, and visual-consistency requirements; the Generator addresses them through domain adaptation, agent-planned placement and physical contact relations, and plan-consistent conditional refinement. Using the Inspector, we further trace representation-space changes across generation variants; the complete system attains a local-shell occupation of $C_{gap}$ to 13.64%, which measures generated coverage of real-derived completion regions. Extensive experiments show AP50--95 gains of up to 4.9 points and consistent improvements across nine mainstream detectors, demonstrating broad cross-architecture utility.

</details>


### [217] [SegWave: Wavelet-Driven Segmentation of Tampered Regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30714)
*Siddhi Pravin Lipare,Vishesh Kumar,Akshay Agarwal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了SegWave混合框架，结合Transformer与离散小波变换（DWT），通过自适应子带注意力模块（ASA）突出高频小波分量，实现图像篡改检测与定位，在多个基准数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像真实性验证日益困难，在新闻、执法和政治领域构成严重风险，而现有取证方法依赖高层视觉伪影且将检测视为简单二分类任务，缺乏对局部、多尺度频率不一致性的捕捉能力。

Method: 提出SegWave混合框架，将Transformer架构与离散小波变换（DWT）相结合以捕获局部多尺度频率不一致性，并引入自适应子带注意力模块（ASA）动态突出信息丰富的高频小波分量。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，SegWave在具有挑战性的评估设置中一致优于最先进的篡改检测方法。

Conclusion: SegWave通过联合利用空间与频域线索，为图像篡改检测提供了更准确、更鲁棒的解决方案，能够有效提升图像取证能力。

Abstract: Verifying image authenticity is increasingly difficult, posing serious risks across journalism, law enforcement, and political domains. Most existing forensic methods rely on high-level visual artifacts and treat frame detection as a simple binary task. To address this, we propose SegWave, a hybrid framework that jointly leverages spatial and frequency-domain cues for image tampering detection. SegWave integrates a transformer-based architecture with the Discrete Wavelet Transform (DWT) to capture localized, multi-scale frequency inconsistencies indicative of manipulation. To further improve localization effectiveness, we introduce an Adaptive Sub-band Attention module (ASA) that dynamically highlights the informative high-frequency wavelet components. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that SegWave consistently outperforms state-of-the-art tampering detection methods in challenging evaluation settings.

</details>


### [218] [RailGen: Improving Railway Intrusion Detection via Agent-Guided Small-Scale Foreign Object Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30727)
*Quan Hao,Ziyang Tao,Chenxi Zhang,Yudong Wang,Rui Shi,Liguo Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多模态图像生成的检测范式，用于解决铁路异物检测中长尾分布下小目标检测的挑战。通过RailGen生成代理自动生成高质量合成样本以丰富尾部类别的特征空间，并设计了FocalDEIM检测框架增强对小目标和困难样本的判别能力，在mAP@50和mAP@(50-95)上分别提升5.6%和7.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长尾数据分布下的小目标检测在多媒体领域是一个基础且具挑战性的问题，铁路异物检测（RFOD）是典型代表，面临小异物易混淆和样本稀缺的双重困境。

Method: 提出生成增强检测范式：(1) RailGen——基于大模型的多模态图像生成代理，在语义约束下自动调用工具生成铁路场景、校准异物位置、提取异物并融合得到逼真的入侵效果；(2) FocalDEIM——检测框架，采用Focal Modulation改进密集匹配以提升小目标判别力，并采用Focal Loss强调困难样本，缓解复杂铁路场景中的类间边界模糊问题。

Result: RailGen能生成高质量小尺度异物，目标像素面积平均减少13.85倍（最高58倍）；相比基线DEIM，mAP@50提升5.6%，mAP@(50-95)提升7.5%，超越现有最先进方法；消融实验验证了RailGen的特征空间丰富效果和FocalDEIM的边界判别能力。

Conclusion: 该范式为安全关键应用中长尾小目标检测提供了一种有效的多模态生成解决方案，通过数据生成与检测框架的协同设计，有效解决了样本稀缺和特征空间不完整的问题。

Abstract: Small-object detection under long-tailed data distributions is a fundamental yet challenging problem in multimedia. Railway Foreign Object Detection (RFOD) epitomizes this challenge with easily confused small intrusions and scarce samples. To address these issues, we propose a generative-augmented detection paradigm that leverages multimodal image generation to enrich the feature space of rare and small objects. We first construct RailGen, a multimodal image generation agent based on large models. Under semantic constraints, RailGen automatically invokes tools to generate railway scenes, calibrate intrusion positions, extract foreign objects, and fuse them into realistic intrusion effects. This process produces high-quality synthetic samples that effectively densify the feature representations of tail classes and complete the small-object feature space. Within this paradigm, we further propose FocalDEIM, a detection framework designed to enhance training with generated data. FocalDEIM improves dense matching with Focal Modulation for better small-object discrimination and adopts Focal Loss to emphasize hard samples, thereby alleviating blurred inter-class boundaries in complex railway scenes. Experimental results demonstrate that RailGen can generate high-quality small-scale foreign objects, reducing the object pixel area by up to 58x and 13.85x on average. Equipped with these challenging samples, our paradigm surpasses the baseline DEIM by 5.6% and 7.5% in mAP@50 and mAP@(50-95), respectively, and outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods. Ablation studies verify RailGen's feature-space enrichment and FocalDEIM's boundary discrimination. The paradigm provides an effective multimodal generative solution for long-tailed small-object detection in safety-critical applications.

</details>


### [219] [CheXGround: Anatomical Region Tokens for Grounded Longitudinal Chest X-ray Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30758)
*Adonay Demewez Gebremedhin,Wessam Shehieb,Sara Alansari,Mohamad Alansari,Muzammal Naseer,Sajid Javed,Naoufel Werghi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CheXGround，一个区域锚定的纵向胸片X光语言模型，通过解剖区域对齐前后影像，并结合时间增强的ROI令牌与全局上下文，显著提升了临床语言质量、时间推理和定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然纵向建模和视觉定位在放射学语言模型中各自取得了进展，但局部视觉证据如何支持纵向影像解读仍未被充分探索。作者旨在将解剖层面的局部证据与纵向时间推理相结合，弥补这一研究空白。

Method: CheXGround从当前和先前胸片中提取解剖区域，编码为时间增强的Region-of-Interest（ROI）令牌，并在生成时与全局时间图像上下文结合。同时提出Temporal Region-Phrase Alignment预训练目标，用于对齐时间解剖表示与局部报告短语。

Result: 在单次研究和纵向VQA、纵向发现生成、时间接地VQA及解剖定位等多项任务上，CheXGround相较于近期基线在临床语言质量、时间推理和定位准确性上均有提升。

Conclusion: 在解剖层面组织纵向证据是一种强有力的接地放射学语言建模表征，能为区域级时间推理提供有效支持。

Abstract: Recent radiology multi-modal language models have made substantial progress in chest X-ray report generation, visual question answering, and temporal reasoning. While longitudinal chest X-ray interpretation compares sequential examinations to describe change, visual grounding aims to connect clinical language with localized image evidence. Although longitudinal modeling and visual grounding have each advanced radiology language models, how localized visual evidence can support longitudinal interpretation remains under-explored. We introduce CheXGround, a region-grounded longitudinal chest X-ray language model that represents paired studies through corresponding anatomical regions. CheXGround extracts anatomical regions from current and prior radiographs, encodes them as temporally enhanced Region-of-Interest (ROI) tokens, and combines them with global temporal image context during generation. To connect these region tokens with clinical text, we propose Temporal Region--Phrase Alignment, a pretraining objective that aligns temporal anatomical representations with localized report phrases. We evaluate CheXGround on single-study and longitudinal Visual Question Answering (VQA), longitudinal findings generation, temporal grounded VQA, and anatomical grounding. Across these tasks, CheXGround improves clinical language quality, temporal reasoning, and localization accuracy over recent baselines. Our results suggest that organizing longitudinal evidence at the anatomical level is a strong representation for grounded radiology language modeling. Project page: https://adonaydem.github.io/chexground-website

</details>


### [220] [ChessQueries: Toward Better Chess Board Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30762)
*Joël Seytre*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ChessQueries方法，将ViT编码器与DETR风格解码器结合用于棋盘识别任务，在ChessReD基准上将最先进水平从15.3%提升至99.2%，平均每个棋盘仅0.01个错误方块，并发布了更难的新数据集SLCC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 棋盘识别任务缺乏性能优异的通用方法，现有基准（ChessCog为合成数据，ChessReD来自单一棋盘设置）存在局限，尤其是在真实、多样化场景下识别准确率较低（ChessReD仅15.3%），亟需更强的模型和方法来突破性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出ChessQueries方法，结合ViT（Vision Transformer）编码器与DETR风格的解码器架构，利用Transformer的注意力机制对棋盘图像进行端到端的棋子与棋盘方格映射。

Result: 在ChessReD基准上，将最先进水平从15.3%大幅提升至99.2%；在两个数据集上平均每棋盘仅0.01个错误方块（对比SotA分别为3.4和0.15），且对分布外数据集展现出强大的泛化能力。

Conclusion: ChessQueries在现有两个基准上已饱和任务性能，证明了该方法的有效性；同时发布了更难的新数据集SLCC（来自顶级赛事转播），将进一步推动棋盘识别领域的研究。

Abstract: Chess board recognition is the task of mapping the image of a chess board to the information of which piece is on which square. So far this task has two established benchmarks: ChessCog is synthetic, and ChessReD comes from smartphone pictures of a single chess board setup. We introduce ChessQueries, a new method combining a ViT encoder with a DETR-style decoder, which outperforms existing methods. On the ChessReD benchmark, we improve the state of the art from 15.3% to 99.2%, and demonstrate strong capabilities on out-of-distribution datasets. Our method saturates the task on the two datasets, with an average 0.01 wrong squares per board (vs. SotA: 3.4 / 0.15 respectively). We also share a new, harder public dataset, parsed from broadcasted top-level chess tournaments. Code, model weights and the SLCC data will be released.

</details>


### [221] [CORAL: A Benchmark for Structure-aware and Brain-wide Neuron Reconstruction in Light Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30768)
*Zekang Yang,Jiamin Li,Zhenghua Li,Jiaqi Fan,Zengcai Guo,Xiaolin Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了CORAL，这是首个从结构和全脑尺度两个层面评估自动神经元重建的基准测试，基于高质量的全脑fMOST数据集，并引入基于纤维预测的结构感知度量以及将局部重建方法扩展到全脑尺度的追踪框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经元重建方法虽然在局部图像块上取得了不错的成果，但能否同时实现结构准确且扩展到全脑尺度的重建仍然不明确。缺乏一个同时评估局部和全脑尺度结构准确性的统一基准。

Method: 构建了基于高质量全脑fMOST数据集的CORAL基准，设计了两级渐进任务（块级重建和全脑重建）；引入基于纤维预测的结构感知度量来评估拓扑正确性；开发了脑宽神经元追踪框架，通过迭代的局部到全局过程将任意局部重建方法扩展到全脑尺度。

Result: 首次对主流局部神经元重建方法进行了结构感知比较，并评估了它们在全脑重建中的表现。结果强调了结构感知评估的重要性，同时揭示了现有方法在完整神经元重建方面仍需更鲁棒的方法。

Conclusion: 结构感知评估是必要的，当前方法在局部和全脑尺度的完整神经元重建上仍存在不足，需要开发更鲁棒的重建方法。

Abstract: Automatic neuron reconstruction from light microscopy images is a central problem in computational neuroanatomy. While recent methods have achieved encouraging results on local image blocks, it remains unclear whether such progress translates to reconstruction that is both structurally accurate and scalable to the whole-brain scale. We present CORAL, the first benchmark for structure-aware evaluation of automatic neuron reconstruction from light microscopy images at both local and whole-brain scales. Built on a high-quality whole-brain fMOST dataset with carefully curated annotations, CORAL establishes two progressive tasks: block-level reconstruction, which evaluates reconstruction methods under limited spatial context, and brain-wide reconstruction, which assesses complete neuron reconstruction at the whole-brain scale. To account for topological correctness beyond geometric distance similarity, we introduce a structure-aware metric based on fiber prediction. To further achieve complete neuron reconstruction across the entire brain, we develop a brain-wide neuron tracing framework that extends arbitrary local reconstruction methods to the whole-brain scale through an iterative local-to-global process. Using this benchmark, we provide the first structure-aware comparison of mainstream methods for local neuron reconstruction and further evaluate their performance in brain-wide reconstruction. Our results underscore the importance of structure-aware evaluation and the need for more robust methods for complete neuron reconstruction.

</details>


### [222] [PixelIR: Fidelity-Perception Decoupling via Pixel-Space Image-Residual Flow Matching for Efficient One-Step Real-World Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30782)
*Bingtian Qiao,Yue Shi,Yong Guo,Wenjun Zhang,Jiezhang Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PixelIR提出了一种基于像素空间图像残差流匹配的保真-感知解耦框架，通过先学习图像流进行忠实重建，再用残差流从噪声中合成感知细节，并蒸馏为单步学生模型，在RealSR和DRealSR上实现领先的PSNR、SSIM和LPIPS指标，同时仅需32.9M参数、89.7G MACs和8.5ms延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Real-ISR方法在共享网络中同时优化保真度和感知质量，导致两个目标在训练过程中相互干扰，平衡难以控制；且一步法虽减少了采样步数，但仍继承了耦合优化行为和昂贵的高分辨率骨干网络。作者认为高效Real-ISR不仅需要更短的采样轨迹，还需要对忠实重建和感知细节合成进行专门建模。

Method: PixelIR框架包含两个阶段：首先学习一个图像流，将退化观测映射到忠实重建；然后利用残差流从噪声中合成缺失的感知细节，无需重复学习或覆盖完整的恢复解。最后将教师模型蒸馏为面向部署的单步学生模型，采用粗到细的金字塔架构。

Result: 在RealSR和DRealSR数据集上，PixelIR在PSNR、SSIM和LPIPS指标上均达到领先水平。最终模型在单次评估中完成像素空间恢复，仅需32.9M参数、89.7G MACs和8.5ms延迟。

Conclusion: PixelIR通过保真-感知解耦框架实现了实际部署中的强保真-感知-效率平衡，验证了专门化建模忠实重建与感知细节合成对高效Real-ISR的有效性。

Abstract: Real-world image super-resolution (Real-ISR) aims to preserve structures supported by the degraded observation while reconstructing perceptually realistic details. However, existing Real-ISR methods largely optimize fidelity and perceptual quality within a shared network, causing the two objectives to interfere throughout training and making their balance difficult to control. Recent one-step methods reduce sampling steps, yet often inherit both this coupled optimization behavior and the expensive high-resolution backbone of their multi-step predecessors. We argue that efficient Real-ISR requires not only a shorter sampling trajectory, but also specialized modeling of faithful reconstruction and perceptual detail synthesis. Based on this insight, we propose PixelIR, a fidelity-perception decoupling framework built upon pixel-space image-residual flow matching. PixelIR first learns an image flow that maps the degraded observation to a faithful reconstruction. Then, a residual flow synthesizes the missing perceptual details from noise without repeatedly relearning or overwriting the complete restoration solution. We further distill the teacher into a deployment-oriented one-step student within a coarse-to-fine pyramid architecture. Extensive experiments show that PixelIR achieves leading PSNR, SSIM, and LPIPS on both RealSR and DRealSR. The final model completes pixel-space restoration in a single evaluation with only 32.9M parameters, 89.7G MACs, and 8.5ms latency, demonstrating a strong practical fidelity-perception-efficiency balance.

</details>


### [223] [Camera trap classification with deep learning under ground truth uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30789)
*Leonard Hockerts,Peter S. Stewart,Sarthak Arora,Tiffany J. Vlaar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在深度学习中利用公民科学志愿者标注（含标签不一致性）训练生态图像分类模型的影响，发现适度的"地面真值"不确定性可提升整体测试准确率，尤其是对志愿者认为困难图像的效果更好，且ImageNet预训练可增强这一优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督式深度学习方法处理生态图像数据高效但依赖昂贵的标注过程，实践中常使用公民科学志愿者提供的标签。然而志愿者之间的标注分歧会给"地面真值"数据带来不确定性，但这类不确定性对模型训练和验证的影响此前未得到充分研究。

Method: 使用两个包含相机陷阱图像及对应志愿者和专家分类的数据集，系统比较在不同地面真值不确定性水平下训练深度学习模型的性能差异，并考察ImageNet预训练、相机陷阱图像额外预训练以及类别不平衡等因素的影响。

Result: 研究发现较高的地面真值不确定性可提高整体测试准确率（尤其是志愿者认为困难的图像），物种级准确率普遍提升，但泛化到不同数据集时无改善。ImageNet预训练增强了这一收益并减少了训练轮次；类别不平衡时仍观察到明显益处，但降低了稀有物种准确率，且误分类模式更接近志愿者的错误。

Conclusion: 实践者应在训练中包含适度水平的标签分歧并采用预训练模型，以提升准确率（尤其是困难样例）。研究为更有效地利用公民科学标签和改进人机联合分类工作流提供了启示。

Abstract: Supervised deep learning methods enable the rapid processing of ecological image data, but depend on a costly annotation process. Consequently, training labels are commonly derived from volunteer citizen science projects. However, disagreement among volunteers introduces uncertainty in the "ground truth" data that are assumed to be correct for model training and validation. Using two datasets containing camera trap images with associated volunteer and expert classifications, we investigated the effects of training under higher ground truth uncertainty. We observed improved overall test accuracy, particularly for images that were more difficult for volunteers. Species-level accuracy also generally improved, but generalisation to a different dataset did not. The benefits of ground truth uncertainty were enhanced by pre-training on ImageNet. Pre-training also reduced the number of training epochs required; further reductions in computational cost, but not gains in accuracy, resulted from additional pre-training on other camera trap images. With unbalanced training data, we still observed a clear benefit of increased ground truth uncertainty for overall accuracy, especially on difficult images. Class imbalance improved accuracy for common species, reduced rare species accuracy, and changed patterns of misclassification to more closely resemble mistakes made by volunteers. Our findings have implications for applying deep learning across ecological image types with multiple labels. Practitioners can improve accuracy, especially on difficult examples, by including moderate levels of label disagreement during training and using models pre-trained on general image data. In addition to improving the use of citizen science-derived labels in model training, our study suggests avenues for more effectively integrating human and deep learning classifications in combined workflows. (abridged)

</details>


### [224] [A Composition-Aware Pretraining Framework for Geospatial Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30817)
*Aryan Kashyap Naveen,Abhishek Srinivas,Pranav Moothedath,Shrutilipi Bhattacharjee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一个基于组成感知预训练的地理空间基础模型框架，通过将每个卫星图像像元映射为分数土地覆盖直方图（"组成目标"），并使用推土机距离进行蒸馏，在区域级理解任务上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的地理空间基础模型预训练方法通过单一概念视角处理影像，未能捕捉复杂卫星场景的高度组成性特征，因而在需要语义相似性判断的区域级任务上表现不足。

Method: 提出组成感知预训练框架，将每个卫星图像像元编码为分数土地覆盖分布直方图作为组成目标，将其作为主要预测目标并使用推土机距离（Earth Mover's Distance）蒸馏到骨干网络中。

Result: 在零样本图像检索和场景分类等区域级任务上显著提升；在36.8M参数下优于303M的SatMAE和600M的Prithvi-EO-2.0；在ForestNet-12上将mAP@10从0.279提升到0.434（55.6%相对提升）。

Conclusion: 显式的组成建模能有效提升地理空间基础模型的区域级理解能力，在保持细粒度空间精度任务竞争力的同时，以更小的模型规模取得更优的区域级任务性能。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation models have emerged as state-of-the-art methods for downstream Earth observation tasks. However, existing pretraining methodologies process imagery through a single-concept lens, failing to capture the highly compositional nature of complex satellite scenes. We propose a composition-aware pretraining framework that explicitly encodes fractional land-cover mixtures. Each satellite image cell is mapped to a histogram representing its fractional land-cover distribution, which we term the "composition target". These targets serve as the primary prediction objective and are distilled into the backbone using Earth Mover's Distance. Experimental evaluation shows that composition-aware pretraining yields substantial gains on region-level understanding tasks requiring semantic similarity judgment, including zero-shot image retrieval and scene classification, while remaining competitive on tasks requiring fine-grained spatial precision, such as segmentation and object detection. With a 36.8M-parameter backbone, our framework outperforms SatMAE and Prithvi-EO-2.0, which contain 303M and 600M parameters, respectively, in most retrieval and scene classification settings. On the fine-grained ForestNet-12 dataset, a rigorous testbed for compositional discrimination, our method boosts baseline mAP@10 from 0.279 to 0.434, a 55.6% relative improvement, providing direct evidence for the effectiveness of explicit composition modeling. The code implementation can be found at https://github.com/05kashyap/GFM_Composition_Pretraining

</details>


### [225] [RealOOB: A Definition-Consistent Real-World Oriented Occlusion Boundary Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30820)
*Lintao Xu,Yinghao Wang,Chenchu Rong,Xuchong Qiu,Chaohui Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了RealOOB，一个包含426万标注的真实世界遮挡边界基准数据集，涵盖物体间遮挡和自遮挡边界，并提供有效性感知的遮挡方向标注。基于该基准评估了40种边界/边缘检测器和6种单目深度估计器，发现现代边缘检测器在定位上与遮挡边界方法相当，但方向预测仍是所有方法的挑战；深度估计器在真实遮挡边界处也缺乏可测量的几何一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遮挡边界估计受限于碎片化监督：基准存在覆盖有限、类别特定设计、缺少自遮挡标注或标注定义不一致等问题。同时，现代边缘检测器和单目深度估计器虽已成为强大的边界和几何预测器，但它们与定义一致的遮挡边界之间的关系尚未被充分探索。

Method: 构建RealOOB基准，包含426万定义一致、几何驱动的遮挡边界标签，覆盖物体间遮挡和自遮挡边界；设计有效性感知的遮挡方向图，仅对跨边界深度排序可靠可测的像素施加监督。基于RealOOB对40种遮挡边界/边缘估计器和6种单目深度估计器进行系统评估。

Result: 评估揭示了遮挡推理的明显差距：现代边缘检测器在边界定位上与遮挡边界方法表现相当，但所有方法在遮挡方向预测上仍面临挑战；即使是强深度估计器在真实遮挡边界处也常缺乏可测量的几何一致性。

Conclusion: RealOOB为遮挡边界估计社区提供了强参考基准，也为评估更广泛低层视觉任务中的深度不连续性和几何保真度提供了真实世界的测试平台。

Abstract: Occlusion boundaries (OBs) are pixel-level image boundaries corresponding to surface visibility discontinuities caused by occlusion. Through precise boundary localisation and occlusion orientation, OBs encode local surface layout and depth ordering, providing geometry-driven mid-level cues for scene understanding. However, progress in pixel-level OB estimation has been limited by fragmented supervision: Existing benchmarks often suffer from limited coverage, category-specific designs, missing self-occlusion annotations, or inconsistent annotation definitions. Meanwhile, modern edge detectors and monocular depth estimators have become strong boundary and geometry predictors, yet their relationship to definition-consistent OBs remains underexplored. We introduce RealOOB, a carefully annotated real-world benchmark with 4.26M definition-consistent, geometry-grounded OB labels covering both inter-object and self-occlusion boundaries, together with validity-aware occlusion-orientation maps that restrict supervision to pixels whose cross-boundary depth ordering is reliably measurable. Based on RealOOB, we evaluate forty OB estimators and edge detectors alongside six monocular depth estimators. Our evaluation reveals a clear gap in occlusion reasoning: modern edge detectors perform competitively with OB methods in localisation, whereas orientation prediction remains challenging for all evaluated methods. Meanwhile, even strong depth estimators often fail to exhibit measurable geometry at true OBs. We believe RealOOB provides a strong reference benchmark for the OB estimation community and a real-world testbed for assessing depth discontinuities and geometry fidelity in broader low-level vision tasks. Dataset and code will be released.

</details>


### [226] [Lucida: Parse, Generate, and Place for Composable Real-to-Sim Scene Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30821)
*Minghan Qin,Yuang Wang,Xiuyu Yang,Yushi Long,Yujian Zhang,Ruihuan Wang,Kai Ye,Yangang Zhang,Hang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Lucida提出了一种可组合场景建模的新管线，将视频解析为场景图、基于多视图证据生成完整物体资产，并用VLM策略（GizmoAct）以GUI交互方式闭合循环地放置物体，在多项基准上大幅超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可组合场景建模管线将任务分解为实例解析、资产生成和资产放置三个步骤，但每一步都假设了真实杂乱场景难以提供的输入条件（如精确的实例几何、无遮挡视角、与观测匹配的资产），导致在真实捕捉场景中精度受限。

Method: Lucida保持原有三步顺序但重新分配各步骤的要求：1）将视频解析为场景图，节点携带每实例的多视图证据；2）从每实例的证据中生成完整资产；3）使用GizmoAct（一种VLM策略）将资产放置建模为多轮GUI交互，通过闭合循环操作物体的gizmo并自行判定对齐是否完成。

Result: 在场景级3D物体检测上，Lucida在R2S-Scene上相较Boxer提升mAP达69%；在物体姿态估计上，CA-1M的ADD-SB@0.05从57.8%提升到83.4%；在场景重建上，场景F-Score从SAM3D的0.794提升到0.924。

Conclusion: Lucida验证了通过将精度要求从管线起点转移到终点、让每一步只依赖真实捕捉可可靠提供的信息，可以显著提升可组合场景建模在真实杂乱场景中的表现。
