Abstract: Composable scene modeling aims to recover a real indoor scene as complete, editable object assets arranged as observed, giving robot simulation and embodied AI a simulation-ready replica of the real environment whose objects can be manipulated individually. Existing pipelines decompose the task into three steps---parse the observations into instances, generate an asset for each, and place each asset back---but every step presumes an input that a cluttered capture rarely provides: accurate instance geometry, unoccluded views, and assets that accurately match the observations. We propose Lucida, which keeps this order but redistributes the requirements, so each step consumes only what a real capture reliably provides and precision is reached at the end of the pipeline rather than demanded at its start. Lucida parses the video into a scene graph whose nodes carry per-instance multi-view evidence, generates a complete asset for each instance from its evidence, and places assets with GizmoAct, a VLM policy that casts placement as multi-turn GUI interaction, manipulating the object's gizmo in a closed loop and deciding itself when alignment is reached. Across scene-level 3D object detection, object pose estimation, and scene reconstruction, Lucida improves mAP over Boxer by 69% on R2S-Scene, raises ADD-SB@0.05 from 57.8% to 83.4% on CA-1M, and increases scene F-Score from 0.794 for SAM3D to 0.924.

</details>


### [227] [Whole-Body MRI Classification via Prompt-Based Clinical Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30824)
*Laura Daza,Marta Hasny,Cristina González,Julia A. Schnabel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TACTIC，一种基于提示的多模态框架，通过将临床属性编码为提示（prompt）来整合全身磁共振成像（WB-MRI）和结构化临床数据，支持任意数量的表格输入并自然处理缺失数据，在五个WB-MRI分类任务上均优于仅图像基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身磁共振成像与临床变量结合有望改善系统性疾病的诊断，但结构化临床变量常常不完整或缺失，限制了传统多模态融合方法（假设固定输入）的适用性。

Method: 提出TACTIC（Tabular-Attribute Conditioned Transformer for Image Classification）框架，将临床属性作为提示词编码进视觉特征学习过程，实现WB-MRI图像与结构化临床数据的条件融合；该设计支持任意数量的表格输入，无需数据填补或固定输入结构即可处理缺失数据。

Result: 在五项WB-MRI分类任务（糖尿病、慢性阻塞性肺疾病、乳腺癌、前列腺癌和转移瘤诊断）上，TACTIC在临床信息可用时始终优于仅图像基线，并且在表格输入不完整时仍保持强大的预测能力。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，基于提示的模型是一种灵活有效的方法，可利用临床上下文信息提升WB-MRI分析的性能。模型权重和代码已公开。

Abstract: Combining whole-body magnetic resonance imaging (WB-MRI) with clinical variables has the potential to improve systemic disease diagnosis by leveraging complementary sources of patient information. However, structured clinical variables are often incomplete or missing, limiting the applicability of conventional multimodal fusion methods that assume fixed inputs. In this work, we propose TACTIC (Tabular-Attribute Conditioned Transformer for Image Classification), a prompt-based multimodal framework that integrates WB-MRI and structured clinical data through conditional visual feature learning. By encoding clinical attributes as prompts, TACTIC supports an arbitrary number of tabular inputs and naturally handles missing data without requiring imputation or fixed input structures. We evaluate TACTIC on five WB-MRI classification tasks spanning systemic and oncologic applications, including diabetes, chronic obstructive pulmonary disease (COPD), breast cancer, prostate cancer, and metastasis diagnosis. Across all tasks, TACTIC consistently improves performance over image-only baselines when clinical information is available while maintaining strong predictive capability under incomplete tabular inputs. Our results demonstrate the effectiveness of prompt-based models as a flexible approach for improving WB-MRI analysis using clinical context. The model weights and code are available at https://github.com/lauradaza/TACTIC

</details>


### [228] [Reliable Benchmarking of Artifact Detection in Computational Pathology: A Reproducibility and Uncertainty Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30835)
*Konstantinos Moutselos,Ilias Maglogiannis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对全切片图像质量控制基准的可靠性评估协议，量化测试集采样、训练随机性、划分组成和预处理四个变异性来源，并通过对扩散伪影检测器的独立重建验证，发现其核心机制可复现但比较性结论均落在不确定区间内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全切片图像质量控制方法的基准存在四个问题：独立切片数量少、标注集中在少数切片、使用无闭式标准误差的比率指标、以及单一继承的划分方式，导致方法间报告的性能差异难以解释。需要一个能量化这些变异来源的可靠性协议。

Method: 提出包含四种变异来源的可靠性协议：测试集采样、训练随机性、划分组成和未记录的预处理步骤。声称成立需通过全部四项检验，其中三项仅需数分钟计算。协议应用于对已发表扩散伪影检测器的独立重建，在原始24切片划分上评估并对比监督基线。

Result: 核心机制可复现：辅助对比项使池化F1从0.673提升至0.688，并在第二个种子下复现；但作用于笔迹而非论文所述的伪影类型。比较性声明不成立：设计变体间差异及与监督基线的差异均落在评估不确定区间内。4/24张切片承载70%标注像素，有效样本量仅6.2；继承划分位于第7百分位。未报告的組織限制步骤排除了41.4%的失焦标注（气泡仅2.6%），该门控与模糊天然混淆。

Conclusion: 小队列基准支持的结论远比当前报告方式所暗示的弱。四项检查足够廉价，可伴随任何基于此类资源的评估，以区分可复现效应与评估无法分辨的差异。

Abstract: Background and Objective: Quality control is a prerequisite for whole-slide image analysis, yet the benchmarks on which quality-control methods are compared share four properties that make their reported differences hard to interpret: few independent slides, annotation concentrated in a minority of them, pooled ratio metrics with no closed-form standard error, and a single inherited train/test partition. We propose a reliability protocol for such benchmarks. Methods: The protocol quantifies four sources of variability - test-set sampling, training stochasticity, partition composition, and undocumented preprocessing - a claim is reportable only if it survives all four; three of the four cost minutes of compute. We apply it to an independent reconstruction of a published diffusion-based artifact detector, evaluated on the original 24-slide partition and against a supervised baseline. Results: The method's central mechanism reproduces: the auxiliary contrastive term improves pooled F1 from 0.673 to 0.688 and replicates under a second seed (+0.0156, p = 0.031; +0.0190, p = 0.005), although it acts on pen marking rather than the artifact types cited to motivate it. Its comparative claims do not: differences between design variants, and against the supervised baseline, fall inside the uncertainty of the evaluation. Four of 24 slides carry 70% of scored annotated pixels, giving an effective sample size of 6.2, and the inherited partition sits at the 7th percentile. An unreported tissue-restriction step excludes 41.4% of out-of-focus annotation against 2.6% of air bubble; such a gate is confounded with blur by construction. Conclusions: Small-cohort benchmarks support far weaker conclusions than current reporting implies. The four checks are cheap enough to accompany any evaluation on such a resource and separate reproducible effects from differences the evaluation cannot resolve.

</details>


### [229] [Physical Adversarial Examples for Person Detectors in Thermal Images Based on 3D Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30839)
*Xiaopei Zhu,Siyuan Huang,Zhanhao Hu,Jianmin Li,Jun Zhu,Xiaolin Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于3D建模的红外对抗服装，通过在衣物内侧特定位置粘贴方形气凝胶贴片（在热成像中显示为黑色方块），实现对热红外人体检测器的物理对抗攻击。该方法利用3D建模模拟多角度场景，并结合真实红外照片构建3D纹理图以增强仿真真实感。物理攻击实验中，在室内和室外对YOLOv9检测器的攻击成功率分别达到80.11%和76.85%，远高于随机贴片的26.53%和23.03%，且通过集成攻击方法表现出良好的迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热红外检测在自动驾驶和医疗AI等领域应用广泛，但其安全性问题直到最近才引起关注。现有对抗攻击研究多基于2D建模，难以真实模拟多角度场景，因此需要一种更贴近真实世界的3D建模红外人体验证服装攻击方案。

Method: 本文方法包括：(1)基于3D建模设计红外对抗服装，模拟多角度真实场景；(2)利用对抗样本技术优化3D服装的黑色贴片布局模式；(3)采用气凝胶制作物理对抗服装，将方形气凝胶贴片按特定位置和方向粘贴在衣物内侧（热成像中显示为黑色方块）；(4)提出用真实红外照片构建红外3D模型及纹理图的方法，模拟不同时间和位置的红外特性变化；(5)采用集成攻击方法提升对未知检测器的迁移性。

Result: 物理攻击实验中，该对抗服装对YOLOv9在室内攻击成功率为80.11%，室外为76.85%；而随机放置贴片的成功率仅为室内26.53%和室外23.03%。通过集成攻击方法，对抗服装对未知检测器也表现出良好的迁移性，证明了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究证明基于3D建模的红外对抗服装能够在真实物理场景中有效规避热红外人体检测器，攻击成功率显著高于随机贴片基线条，且具备良好的跨模型迁移能力，揭示了热红外检测系统在物理世界中的安全脆弱性。

Abstract: Thermal Infrared detection is widely used in autonomous driving, medical AI, etc., but its security has only attracted attention recently. We propose infrared adversarial clothing designed to evade thermal person detectors in real-world scenarios. The design of the adversarial clothing is based on 3D modeling, which makes it easier to simulate multiangle scenes near the real world compared to 2D modeling. We optimized the black patch layout pattern of 3D clothing based on the adversarial example technique and made physical adversarial clothing using the aerogel. The idea is to paste a set of square aerogel patches, which display black squares in thermal images, in the inner side of clothing at specific locations with specific orientations. To enhance realism, we propose a method to build infrared 3D models with real infrared photos and develop texture maps for 3D models to simulate varied infrared characteristics over time and location. In physical attacks, we achieved an attack success rate of 80.11\% indoors and 76.85\% outdoors against YOLOv9. In contrast, randomly placed patches yielded much lower success rates (26.53\% indoors and 23.03\% outdoors). The adversarial clothing also showed good transferability to unknown detectors with an ensemble attack method, demonstrating the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [230] [Pretrained, Curriculum-Tuned, and Ensembled: A Tracer-Aware Interactive Segmentation Pipeline for AutoPET V](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30844)
*Xinglong Liang,Chunyao Lu,Tianyu Zhang,Jiaju Huang,Tao Tan,Yunchao Yin,Lishan Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TRIAGE是一个用于全身PET/CT交互式病变分割的框架，通过3D STU-Net骨干网络、异步掩码自编码预训练、器官分割辅助模型以及FDG/PSMA特异性分支，实现对不同示踪剂影像的强初始预测和高效交互式修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式病变分割需要模型在推理时同时提供强初始预测并高效响应稀疏修正笔划，而FDG与PSMA影像在示踪剂分布、生理摄取、病变外观和采集特征上存在显著差异，使得跨示踪剂的分割任务极具挑战性。

Method: 该方法包含四个核心组件：(1) 3D STU-Net骨干网络，通过异步掩码策略的掩码自编码预训练学习可迁移的解剖和跨模态表示；(2) 辅助器官分割模型提供解剖上下文以区分生理摄取与恶性病变；(3) 专用示踪剂分类器将研究路由至FDG或PSMA特异性分支，每个分支含第一阶段的初始分割模型和第二阶段的交互式细化网络；(4) 采用课程式训练和模型集成提升稳健性。

Result: 实验使用官方AutoPET V数据和十折划分进行，定量结果、消融实验和最终测试集性能将在挑战评估后补充完成。

Conclusion: TRIAGE通过示踪剂感知的分支架构和交互式细化机制，构建了针对FDG和PSMA异质性挑战的统一解决方案，代码已在GitHub公开。

Abstract: Interactive lesion segmentation in whole-body PET/CT requires a model to provide a strong initial prediction while also responding efficiently to sparse corrective scribbles during inference. This setting is particularly challenging because tracer distributions, physiological uptake patterns, lesion appearance, and acquisition characteristics differ substantially between FDG and PSMA studies. We present TRIAGE, Tracer-aware Refinement via Interactive Anatomy-Guided sEgmentation. The core backbone is a 3D STU-Net initialized through masked autoencoding pre-training with an asynchronous masking strategy, aiming to learn transferable anatomical and cross-modal representations before task-specific fine-tuning. In parallel, we train an auxiliary organ segmentation model whose predictions provide explicit anatomical context and help distinguish physiological uptake from malignant lesions. A dedicated tracer classifier first routes each study to an FDG- or PSMA-specific branch. Within each branch, a first-stage segmentation model consumes CT, PET, and organ context to generate an initial lesion mask. The initial prediction is then combined with cumulative foreground/background scribbles and refined by a second interactive segmentation network. The FDG and PSMA branches share the same overall processing pipeline but are trained independently to account for tracer-specific appearance and error modes. We additionally employ curriculum-style training and model ensembling to improve robustness across interaction steps and heterogeneous cohorts. Experiments are conducted using the official AutoPET V data and ten-fold split; quantitative results, ablations, and final test-set performance are left as placeholders to be completed after the challenge evaluation. Code: https://github.com/Liiiii2101/AUTOPET2026-MEDAI.

</details>


### [231] [TAMI: Temporally Aligned, Missingness-Aware, and Interpretable Multimodal Fusion for Mental Health Assessment in Older Adults with Mild Cognitive Impairment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30857)
*Merna Bibars,Bolaji Omofojoye,Allan I. Levey,Rachel Hershenberg,Gari D. Clifford,Hyeokhyen Kwon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TAMI多模态融合框架，用于远程临床访谈中老年人轻度认知障碍（MCI）的抑郁和焦虑筛查。TAMI解决了多模态特征时间不对齐、模态缺失值处理不当和缺乏细粒度可解释性三大问题。在49名MCI老年人的访谈中，TAMI在抑郁和焦虑检测上分别达到0.68和0.69的AUROC，并发现仅使用开放式问题即可达到与全访谈相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCI老年人中抑郁和焦虑常因护理资源受限而漏诊，远程临床访谈的多模态分析是可扩展的筛查手段，但现有方法存在三个关键缺陷：多模态特征时间不对齐导致虚假跨模态关联、远程录音中模态缺失值被零填充而无法与有效测量区分、缺乏对模态/问题/访谈时刻的联合归因能力，限制了临床解读。

Method: 提出TAMI（时间对齐、缺失感知、可解释）多模态融合框架：1）在问答片段的共享时间线上对齐语音、语言、面部和生理特征；2）对模态级缺失进行时间编码，区分真实缺失与有效近零测量；3）融合过程以问题上下文为条件。在49名MCI老年人的临床访谈数据上进行实验。

Result: TAMI在抑郁检测上达到0.68 AUROC，在焦虑检测上达到0.69 AUROC。细粒度时间对齐带来最大性能提升（Δ≥0.1）。多层级可解释性分析显示：抑郁分类依赖眼部注视和开放式问题，焦虑分类依赖眼部注视和头部姿态。仅使用开放式问题（5.1分钟）时抑郁模型AUROC为0.67，与完整访谈（19分钟）无显著差异（p>0.05）。

Conclusion: TAMI验证了时间对齐、缺失感知和可解释性设计对多模态心理筛查的有效性。研究结果表明，在MCI老年人的抑郁筛查中，设计以开放式问题为中心的访谈协议更为高效，可在显著缩短访谈时间的同时保持检测性能。

Abstract: Depression and anxiety in older adults with Mild Cognitive Impairment (MCI) are frequently underdiagnosed due to limited access to care. Multimodal analysis of remote clinical interviews is a scalable screening approach, but existing methods have three limitations. First, they do not correct temporal misalignment across multimodal features extracted at different resolutions, inducing spurious cross-modal associations. Second, remote recordings exhibit uneven modality dropout, but missing values are often zero-filled, making them indistinguishable from valid near-zero measurements. Finally, they do not jointly attribute predictions to modalities, questions, and interview moments, limiting fine-grained clinical interpretation. We propose a Temporally-Aligned, Missingness-Aware, Interpretable (TAMI) multimodal fusion framework. TAMI aligns speech, language, facial, and physiological features within question-answer segments on a shared timeline, encodes modality-level missingness over time, and conditions fusion on question context. In interviews with 49 older adults with MCI, TAMI achieved area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) scores of 0.68 (depression) and 0.69 (anxiety). Fine-grained temporal alignment of multimodal features produced the largest performance gain ($Δ{\geq}0.1$). Multi-level interpretability analysis revealed that depression classification relied on eyegaze and open-ended questions, while anxiety classification depended on eyegaze and head pose, with attribution uniformly distributed across questions. Using only responses to the open-ended questions (5.1min), the depression model achieved an AUROC score of 0.67, which was not significantly different from using the full interview (19min) ($p>0.05$). Our findings support designing interview protocols centered on open-ended questions for depression screening in older adults with MCI.

</details>


### [232] [VCAR: Training-Free 3DGS Segmentation via View Completeness and Axis-Aware Boundary Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30870)
*Kun Cao,Di Wang,Haibin Zhu,Haozhi Huang,Xu Wang,Zheng Shi,Guanghua Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了VCAR，一种无需训练的粗到细3D高斯泼溅语义分割策略。通过基于可见性的加权多视角投票进行粗定位，再利用球形螺旋采样生成补充视角进行精细边界优化，并引入轴向感知边界细化（ABR）来抑制各向异性高斯图元导致的边界渗漏，在NVOS和LERF数据集上取得了最先进的精度和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS语义分割方法主要依赖特征蒸馏，存在两个问题：一是每场景训练开销大；二是分割边界模糊。作者识别出边界伪影部分源于视点覆盖不足和各向异性高斯图元的边界溢出，因此希望提出一种无需训练的解决方案来同时提升精度和效率。

Method: VCAR采用粗到细两阶段策略。粗阶段使用基于可见性的加权多视角投票快速定位目标；细阶段基于粗分割结果构造目标中心球体，通过球形螺旋采样（SSS）生成补充视点，在增强视点上进行多视角投票以精确细化边界并抑制无关3D高斯。此外，ABR机制将投影后的2D协方差分解为各轴向贡献，识别导致边界渗漏的主轴并仅沿该轴执行定向各向异性压缩。

Result: 在NVOS和LERF两个公开数据集上的大量实验表明，VCAR在无需训练的情况下达到了最先进的语义分割精度和效率，超越了现有依赖特征蒸馏的方法。

Conclusion: VCAR验证了通过视点完整性和轴向感知的边界细化，可以在完全无需训练的框架下实现高质量的3DGS语义分割，为3D场景理解提供了一条更高效、更精准的新路径。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) is crucial for advancing 3D scene understanding. Existing methods predominantly rely on feature distillation, which incurs substantial per-scene training overhead and often yields blurred segmentation boundaries. We identify that these boundary artifacts are driven in part by insufficient viewpoint coverage and boundary overflow of anisotropic Gaussian primitives. To address these challenges, we propose VCAR, a training-free coarse-to-fine segmentation strategy based on View Completeness and Axis-aware Boundary Refinement. In the coarse stage, a visibility-based weighted multi-view voting scheme rapidly localizes the target. In the fine stage, an object-centric sphere derived from the coarse result generates supplementary viewpoints via Spherical Spiral Sampling (SSS), allowing multi-view voting on the augmented views to precisely refine object boundaries and suppress irrelevant 3D Gaussians. Moreover, we introduce Axis-aware Boundary Refinement (ABR) to mitigate artifacts from anisotropic primitives. By decomposing the projected 2D covariance into per-axis contributions, ABR identifies the dominant axis responsible for boundary leakage and applies targeted anisotropic compression exclusively along that axis. Extensive experiments on NVOS and LERF demonstrate that VCAR achieves state-of-the-art segmentation accuracy and efficiency without training. Our code is available at https://github.com/DDKK0526/VCAR.

</details>


### [233] [SurgSkill-Bench: A Benchmark for Multimodal Surgical Skill Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30872)
*Chaohui Dang,Zheheng Jiang,James Glasbey,David Luke,Theodoros Arvanitis,Le Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SurgSkill-Bench基准数据集，包含214个外科手术训练模拟视频、六维OSATS评分和专家自由文本评论，并定义了两种评估设置（仅视频的OSATS预测和评论辅助预测），通过内容自适应关键帧采样和视频-文本共注意力融合模块实现自动化手术技能评估，最佳平均AUROC达到0.88。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前外科手术技能评估依赖耗时的人工专家评审，现有自动化方法主要聚焦视觉输入，缺乏同时支持操作表现、结构化技能评分和评估者反馈的联合研究框架。

Method: 构建SurgSkill-Bench基准数据集（214个训练模拟视频+六维OSATS评分+专家评论），定义视频专用和评论辅助两种评估设置，采用冻结视觉骨干网络、内容自适应关键帧采样以及视频-文本共注意力融合模块进行受控基线实验。

Result: 内容自适应采样提升了视频专用预测性能；评估者评论在辅助设置中提供了额外的评分相关信号；在数据集特定的中位数二分类下最佳平均AUROC达到0.88。

Conclusion: SurgSkill-Bench为联合研究手术视频、技能评分和专家反馈提供了初步基准，但需注意数据集规模、元数据完整性和评论辅助预测解释方面的局限性。

Abstract: Objective assessment of surgical technical skill is important for surgical training and structured feedback, but current workflows remain dependent on labor-intensive expert review. Existing automated approaches primarily focus on visual inputs and provide limited support for jointly studying operative performance, structured skill scores, and evaluator feedback. We introduce SurgSkill-Bench, an initial video-score-text benchmark-style dataset containing 214 surgical training simulation videos, six-dimensional OSATS scores, and expert free-text comments. We define two evaluation settings: video-only OSATS prediction for automated assessment and post hoc expert-comment-assisted prediction, where evaluator comments are available as auxiliary information. We provide controlled baseline experiments using representative frozen visual backbones, content-adaptive key-frame sampling, and a simple video-text co-attention fusion module. Under internal video-level validation, content-adaptive sampling improves video-only performance in this dataset, while evaluator comments provide additional score-related signal in the assisted setting. The best mean AUROC reaches 0.88 under dataset-specific median dichotomization. We further discuss evaluation constraints related to dataset scale, metadata completeness, and the interpretation of comment-assisted prediction. Code will be released publicly at a later date.

</details>


### [234] [A Controlled Evaluation of Model Rankings and Input Reliance in Surface Water Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30895)
*Kittipat Phunjanna,Kristóf Karacs,Chayut Ngamkhanong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出水面分割中常用全局IoU等聚合指标进行配置排名，但仅靠排名无法解释系统性能差异的原因、排序的稳定性或对输入数据的依赖程度。研究通过Sen1Floods11上的重复配置比较、配对测试芯片分析、固定检查点输入压力测试和地理重加权，以及在GEOID-Flood上的辅助输入配置评估，揭示了跨模态学生模型平均IoU最高但排序不稳定、辅助输入排名因架构而异等现象，最终强调应根据具体声明匹配相应的评估证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前水面分割性能评估过度依赖聚合指标（如全局IoU）进行配置排名，但聚合排名无法回答三个关键问题：为何某系统表现更好、相近排序是否稳定、预测对单个输入有多强依赖。论文旨在阐明聚合指标在配置排名上的适用边界，并呼吁根据具体论断采用相应的证据类型。

Method: 在Sen1Floods11数据集上开展多维度实验：1）重复配置比较（三种子均值IoU）；2）配对测试芯片分析；3）固定检查点输入压力测试；4）地理重加权评估。并在GEOID-Flood数据集上进行有监督输入配置的次级评估，比较Swin-UNet与U-Net架构下的辅助输入效应。

Result: 跨模态学生在Sen1Floods11上获得最高的三种子均值IoU，但相似排序在不同种子和地理权重下不稳定；辅助输入排名在Swin-UNet和U-Net之间不一致。GEOID-Flood评估显示有监督辅助输入效应存在一致性，但精确的架构排序仍取决于配置。固定检查点测试证明对地形和WorldCover有依赖，但未显示干净输入的性能优势，且目标语义及后期WorldCover先验将评估限制在回顾性全水分割场景。

Conclusion: 聚合指标对完整配置排名仍然有用，但排名稳定性、组件归因、输入依赖性和部署范围需要不同类型的证据来支撑。性能评估的方式应与所主张的结论相匹配，不能仅依赖单一聚合指标做出超出其能力的推断。

Abstract: Performance evaluation for surface-water segmentation commonly uses an aggregate metric such as global intersection-over-union (IoU) to rank model configurations. However, a configuration ranking does not by itself establish why one system performs better, whether a close ordering is stable, or how strongly predictions rely on individual inputs. We examine these distinctions primarily on Sen1Floods11 through repeated configuration comparisons, paired test-chip analysis, fixed-checkpoint input stress tests, and geographic reweighting, with a targeted secondary evaluation of supervised input configurations on GEOID-Flood. The cross-modal student achieves the highest three-seed mean IoU on Sen1Floods11, but close orderings vary across seeds and geographic weighting, while ancillary-input rankings differ between Swin-UNet and U-Net. The GEOID-Flood evaluation shows substantial agreement in supervised ancillary-input effects, although the exact architecture ordering remains configuration dependent. Fixed-checkpoint tests further establish reliance on terrain and WorldCover without establishing a clean-input performance benefit, while target semantics and the later WorldCover prior restrict the evaluation to retrospective all-water segmentation. These results show that aggregate metrics remain useful for ranking complete configurations, but ranking stability, component attribution, input reliance, and deployment scope require distinct evidence. Performance evaluation should therefore match the evidence reported to the claim being made.

</details>


### [235] [Rad-R: A Raw-ADC Radar Dataset and Capture-Invariant SSM for Hardware-Fault Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30896)
*Mainak Mallick,Junghwan Yim,Seung-Kyum Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Rad-R，一个使用77GHz四芯片级联雷达（192个虚拟通道）采集的原始ADC数据集，系统地记录了毫米波雷达的硬件故障（振动、天线失准、雷达罩堵塞、接收通道退化）。每个记录都配有校准的故障严重程度、独立的物理测量以及IMU、温度、GPS、相机等同步数据流。文中提出RadrNet原始IQ Mamba SSM架构，在受控跨严重程度基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汽车毫米波雷达在感知前可能发生振动、天线失准、雷达罩堵塞和接收通道退化等硬件故障，这些故障会污染信号。但这些故障数据稀缺，因为每种故障都需要在物理硬件上逐一诱发并测量，缺少系统性标注的数据集阻碍了该方向的研究。

Method: 使用TI MMWCAS-RF-EVM 4芯片77GHz级联雷达采集原始ADC数据，为每个记录配对受控硬件故障及校准严重程度、独立物理严重度测量，以及帧同步的IMU、温度、GPS和摄像头数据。提出RadrNet（原始IQ Mamba SSM），并设计了RadrNet-DS（绝对相位）和RadrNet-DS-CI（每帧标准化幅度+相对线性调频间相位）两种变体进行基准测试。

Result: 在片段内协议下性能接近饱和（macro-F1>0.98），但跨严重程度泛化仍然困难（RadrNet-DS降至0.49 macro-F1）。RadrNet-DS-CI在受控基准上排名第一（0.663 vs 0.628，对比最强的RD-CNN），同时在任意时间和少样本预算上表现领先。跨模态分析发现雷达微多普勒与IMU振动能量共变（合并Spearman ρ=0.41）。

Conclusion: Rad-R填补了毫米波雷达硬件故障原始数据集的空白，RadrNet-DS-CI通过相对相位和标准化幅度设计有效提升了跨严重程度的鲁棒性，但跨严重程度泛化仍是开放性挑战。数据集和代码将公开释放。

Abstract: Automotive mmWave radar can develop vibration, antenna misalignment, radome blockage, and receive-channel degradation that corrupt the signal before perception begins. Data for these faults are scarce because each condition must be induced and measured on physical hardware. We introduce Rad-R, a raw-ADC dataset captured with a 4-chip 77GHz TI MMWCAS-RF-EVM cascade (192 virtual channels). Unlike existing raw-radar datasets, Rad-R pairs each recording with a controlled hardware fault at a calibrated severity, an independent physical severity measurement, and frame-synchronised IMU, temperature, GPS, and camera streams. Rad-R is a single-session dataset, so our generalisation claims are confined to a controlled cross-severity protocol in which train and test use physically distinct captures. A reproducible benchmark evaluates seven representative vision backbones and the proposed raw-IQ Mamba SSM (RadrNet) under within-clip, chirp-wise anytime, few-shot cross-capture, and controlled cross-severity protocols. Within-clip performance is near-saturated ($>0.98$ macro-F1), whereas cross-severity generalisation remains difficult: the absolute-phase RadrNet-DS falls to $0.49$ macro-F1. RadrNet-DS-CI replaces absolute phase with per-frame-standardised magnitude and relative chirp-to-chirp phase and ranks first on the controlled benchmark ($0.663$ vs. $0.628$ for the strongest RD-CNN; three seeds); the RadrNet family also leads on the anytime and few-shot budgets. A descriptive cross-modal analysis further finds that radar micro-Doppler covaries with independently measured IMU vibration energy (pooled Spearman $ρ=0.41$ across conditions). The complete dataset and code will be released publicly under permissive licences.

</details>


### [236] [Uncertainty-Aware Trajectory Forecasting from Imperfect Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30899)
*Stephane Da Silva Martins,Victor Petrovic,Emanuel Aldea,Sylvie Le Hégarat-Mascle*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种即插即用的不确定性感知轨迹预测框架，将跟踪器产生的可靠性信号（定位不确定性和关联歧义）通过高斯分布表示传播给预测器，并结合Ornstein-Uhlenbeck扰动与知识蒸馏实现鲁棒训练，在多个数据集上提升了位移精度和概率预测的可靠性-锐度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹预测模型大多在理想化干净轨迹上训练和评估，但实际部署依赖不完美的多目标跟踪器输出，存在定位抖动、检测丢失和关联歧义等问题。已有方法要么忽略这些问题，要么通过去噪处理去除，而作者认为跟踪衍生的可靠性线索应被视为有价值的信息传递给预测器。

Method: 将每个观测状态编码为高斯分布表示的不确定状态表征，其中协方差通过全方差定律融合检测级定位不确定性与关联级歧义。以最小架构改动适配现有骨干网络：输入轨迹为高斯观测，输出为高斯预测而非确定性坐标。训练时结合时间相关的Ornstein-Uhlenbeck扰动和基于干净轨迹训练的教师模型的响应级知识蒸馏，以增强对结构化观测噪声的鲁棒性。

Result: 在Oxford Town Centre和VIRAT数据集上使用真实跟踪器输出进行实验，并辅以ETH/UCY伪检测协议，结果表明所提方法提升了位移精度以及概率预测的可靠性-锐度权衡。

Conclusion: 将跟踪不确定性显式建模并传播到预测器，而非忽略或去噪，能够有效提升轨迹预测在实际部署场景中的准确性和概率预测质量，为轨迹预测模型的实用化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Most trajectory forecasting models are trained on clean annotated histories, and are often evaluated under the same idealized assumption, although practical deployments rely on trajectories produced by imperfect multi-object trackers. The real-world observations exhibit localization jitter, missed or unstable detections, and data-association ambiguity, which are usually either ignored or removed through denoising. This paper instead treats tracking-derived reliability cues as an informative signal to be propagated to the predictor. We propose a plug-in uncertainty-aware formulation in which each observed state is encoded as an uncertain state representation, modeled by a Gaussian distribution whose covariance combines detection-level localization uncertainty and association-level ambiguity through the law of total variance. Existing backbones are adapted with minimal architectural changes: input trajectories are represented as Gaussian observations, and predicted trajectories are produced as Gaussian forecasts rather than deterministic coordinates. To train predictors that remain robust under structured observation noise, we combine temporally correlated Ornstein-Uhlenbeck perturbations with response-based knowledge distillation from a teacher trained on clean trajectories. Experiments on Oxford Town Centre and VIRAT using real tracker outputs, together with a complementary ETH/UCY pseudo-detection protocol, show that the proposed formulation improves displacement accuracy and the reliability-sharpness trade-off of probabilistic forecasts.

</details>


### [237] [Audio-Driven Adversarial Defense for 3D Talking Face Generation with totally Visual Fidelity Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30951)
*Rui-Qing Sun,Chen-Hao Cui,Hui-Yang Zhao,Tian Lan,Zhijing Wu,Xian-Ling Mao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于心理声学掩蔽的音频防御方法，通过将保护性扰动隐藏在语音信号的感知掩蔽频域内，在不影响视觉质量的前提下有效抑制音频驱动的3D说话人脸生成，从而防止身份冒充和隐私泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉域主动防御方法通过在面部区域注入扰动来破坏身份获取，但这种方法受限于人脸的结构先验和社会敏感性，容易损害视觉质量，且容易被常见的现实变换（如缩放）所削弱。因此需要一种更稳健且不损失视觉质量的防御方案。

Method: 将保护从视觉模态转移到音频模态，利用心理声学掩蔽效应，将保护性扰动嵌入到语音信号中知觉上被掩蔽的频率区域，从而在降低感知失真的同时有效抑制可靠的面部动画生成。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法能够有效降低3D说话人脸生成的性能，同时保持良好的感知质量，验证了心理声学引导的音频扰动作为隐私保护的可行性和有效性。

Conclusion: 心理声学引导的音频扰动是实现隐私保护肖像防护的一个实用且有前景的方向，为音频驱动3D人脸生成中的身份保护提供了一种新范式。

Abstract: The rapid development of generative portrait models has raised growing concerns about privacy leakage and identity misuse. In particular, audio-driven 3D talking face generation can reconstruct a reusable 3D portrait of a target person from a monocular video and animate it with arbitrary speech, making realistic identity impersonation alarmingly practical. Existing proactive defenses mainly operate in the visual domain by injecting subtle perturbations into acial regions to disrupt identity acquisition. However, such perturbations often compromise visual quality due to the strong structural priors and social sensitivity of human faces, and are easily weakened by common real-world transformations such as resizing. To overcome these limitations, we propose an imperceptible audio defense for audio-driven 3D talking face generation by shifting protection from the visual modality to the audio modality. Specifically,we exploit psychoacoustic masking to hide protective perturbations within perceptually masked frequency regions of the speech signal, thereby reducing perceptual distortion while suppressing reliable facial animation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed method effectively degrades 3D talking face generation while preserving favorable perceptual quality. These findings highlight psychoacoustically guided audio perturbations as a practical and promising direction for privacy-preserving portrait protection.

</details>


### [238] [LOCI: A Locator-Critic with Refinement Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30959)
*Walid Bousselham,Mathilde Caron,Arsha Nagrani,Cordelia Schmid*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LOCI框架，将视觉搜索与证据验证解耦，通过Locator和Critic两个智能体的迭代循环来改善VLMs在复杂视觉理解任务中因感知定位不足而产生的错误推理，在多个基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者认为VLMs在复杂视觉理解任务上表现不佳的核心问题不在于高层推理能力，而在于无法准确定位图像中的关键细节。这种感知基础的缺陷导致模型生成看似合理但实际错误的推理。

Method: 提出训练无关的Locator-Critic (LOCI)框架：Locator智能体负责提出候选视觉证据，独立的Critic智能体负责评估证据的相关性和充分性，两者通过迭代精化循环逐步改进证据质量，直至足以回答问题。

Result: LOCI在多个复杂视觉基准上取得最先进结果：开源模型如Qwen3-VL在V*提升+12.1、HR-Bench提升+5.8、VisualProbe-Hard提升+11.2；闭源模型如Gemini 2.5 Pro在V*提升+8.9、HR-Bench提升+4.3、VisualProbe-Hard提升+4.8。

Conclusion: 解耦式的自校正证据收集过程能有效提升VLMs的视觉推理性能，验证了感知定位能力是制约复杂视觉理解的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) still struggle on tasks requiring complex visual understanding. We argue that the core issue is not high-level reasoning, but instead failing to locate critical details in the image. Due to this shortcoming, VLMs generate often plausible but incorrect reasoning based on flawed perceptual grounding. To address this, we propose Locator-Critic (LOCI), a training-free framework that decouples visual search from evidence verification. LOCI employs a Locator agent to propose candidate visual evidence and a separate Critic agent to evaluate its relevance and sufficiency. These agents engage in an iterative refinement loop, progressively improving the evidence until it is adequate to answer the given question. This decoupled, self-correcting process yields substantial performance gains, achieving state-of-the-art results on multiple complex visual benchmarks. LOCI improves accuracy for both open-weight models like Qwen3-VL (+12.1 on V*, +5.8 on HR-Bench and +11.2 on VisualProbe-Hard) and proprietary models like Gemini 2.5 Pro (+8.9 on V*, +4.3 on HR-Bench, +4.8 on VisualProbe-Hard).

</details>


### [239] [Vision Models Predict Urban Scene Appraisal with Limited Neural Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30964)
*Kaizhen Tan,Yuantao Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现预测人类街道场景评分（r=0.87）的预训练视觉嵌入，与大脑EEG数据的表征几何对应度极低（最好模型DINOv2仅达噪声下限的29.6%，Gabor能量描述子与之无显著差异），且两项指标互不相关。预测评价不等于表征对齐，提出了仅需55张图像嵌入、无需训练的公共基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前预训练视觉嵌入被广泛用于模拟人类对城市场景的评估，但仅通过预测人类评分来验证模型，高预测精度并不能证明嵌入组织场景的方式与人类感知一致，需要更严格的神经层面验证。

Method: 利用63名成人观看56个柏林街景时的公开发布EEG数据，估计场景随时间的表征几何结构及其可解释比例，并与17种特征空间（涵盖语言监督、自监督、类别监督、密集预测训练，跨两个数量级规模和可解释控制）进行对应性比较。同时评估嵌入预测未观测评分的表现，并测试将特征权重向神经几何方向重调后的影响。

Result: 最佳模型DINOv2 ViT-B仅达噪声下限的29.6%，全部模型范围11.0%-29.6%；Gabor能量描述子与最佳模型无显著差异且优于所有语言监督模型。深度层对应晚期神经响应这一层级关系仍在。评分预测可达r=0.87，但与神经对应度不相关；向神经几何重加权会降低所有模型的评分预测。

Conclusion: 预测场景评分仅是模型表征与大脑一致的弱证据。构建了仅需55张图像嵌入、无需训练、只用公共数据的基准，可用于评估新表征的神经对应性。

Abstract: Pretrained vision embeddings are increasingly used as general-purpose representations for modelling how people appraise urban scenes, and are validated almost entirely by how well they predict human ratings. High predictive accuracy does not establish that these embeddings organise scenes as human perception does. We test the two properties separately against brain data. Using openly released EEG from 63 adults who viewed and rated 56 Berlin street scenes, we estimate the representational geometry of the scenes over time, the proportion of that geometry that is explainable at all, and its correspondence with seventeen feature spaces spanning language-supervised, self-supervised, category-supervised and dense-prediction training, two orders of magnitude of scale, and interpretable controls. Correspondence is low throughout: the best representation, DINOv2 ViT-B, reaches 29.6% of the lower bound of the noise ceiling, the panel spans 11.0% to 29.6%, and a Gabor energy descriptor is indistinguishable from the best model while outperforming every language-supervised model tested. Within a model, deeper layers still match later neural responses, so the hierarchical correspondence found for object recognition survives even at this low overall level. The same embeddings predict held-out appraisal ratings well, up to r = 0.87, and the two measures do not track each other across models; reweighting features towards the neural geometry lowers appraisal prediction for every model tested, against a control of matched dimensionality. Predicting how a street is appraised is therefore weak evidence that a model represents the street as the brain does. The benchmark uses only public data and requires no training, so evaluating a new representation needs only its embeddings for 55 images.

</details>


### [240] [MR-JEPA: A General Purpose Video Foundation Model for Cardiac MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30975)
*Athira J. Jacob,Puneet Sharma,Dorin Comaniciu,Daniel Rueckert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了MR-JEPA，一个面向心脏磁共振成像（CMR）的自监督视频基础模型，通过将LeJEPA扩展为3D时空输入，在10,505名患者的多序列数据（cine、LGE、mapping）上进行预训练。在六个下游任务评估中，MR-JEPA在所有五个回归任务上均优于其他方法，LV EF MAE为4.79%，GLS MAE为1.87，疾病检测宏AUC为0.868。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CMR产生丰富的时序影像数据（如时序电影视频和空间LGE/映射堆栈），但大多数深度学习方法仅处理单个2D切片，丢弃了重要的上下文信息。此外，以往的CMR视频模型仅限于cine数据，缺乏通用性。

Method: MR-JEPA扩展了LeJEPA到3D时空输入，采用三个关键设计：(1) tubelet令牌化处理3D时空数据；(2) 时空掩码增强；(3) 从2D CMR基础模型初始化。模型在来自两个中心的10,505名患者的多序列数据上进行无标注自监督预训练，并使用统一的多视图门控注意力架构进行评估。

Result: 在五个回归任务上MR-JEPA均优于包括使用文本监督且预训练数据更多的领域特定CMR模型和自然视频基础模型在内的对比方法，LV EF MAE达4.79%（r=0.764），GLS MAE为1.87（r=0.805），应变任务上相比基线MAE降低21-27%。疾病检测宏AUC为0.868，与领域特定基线相当。

Conclusion: 研究证明了统一视频编码器能够鲁棒地利用多样化CMR序列进行多视图临床心脏定量和诊断，展示了全自监督预训练目标在CMR领域达到与基于文本监督的领域特定模型相当性能的潜力。

Abstract: Cardiac magnetic resonance imaging (CMR) produces rich sequential data such as temporal cine videos and spatial LGE/mapping stacks, yet most deep learning approaches process individual 2D slices, discarding this context. We present MR-JEPA, a self-supervised video foundation model for CMR that extends LeJEPA to 3D spatiotemporal inputs through tubelet tokenization, spatiotemporal masking augmentation, and initialization from a 2D CMR foundation model. Unlike prior CMR video models limited to cine data, MR-JEPA is pretrained on multi-sequence data (cine, LGE, mapping) from 10,505 patients across two centers without annotations. We evaluate the frozen encoder on six downstream tasks using a unified multi-view gated attention architecture: LV ejection fraction, RV ejection fraction, three myocardial strains (GLS, GCS, GRS), and four-class disease detection. MR-JEPA outperforms other compared methods on all five regression tasks, including both a domain-specific CMR model pretrained on more data with text supervision and a natural-video foundation model, achieving an LV EF MAE of 4.79% (r =0.764) and a GLS MAE of 1.87 (r=0.805), with 21-27% MAE reductions over baselines on strain tasks. For disease detection, MR-JEPA achieved a macro AUG of 0.868, remaining competitive with the domain-specific baseline despite using a fully self-supervised pretraining objective. These results demonstrate the potential of a unified video encoder for robust, multi-view utilization of diverse CMR sequences in clinical cardiac quantification and diagnosis.

</details>


### [241] [Multi-View Reflective Surface Inspection via Semantic-Saliency Cross-Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30997)
*Van-Giang Nguyen,Thanh-Tuan Tran,Xuan-Hieu Phan,Xiem HoangVan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于反光表面（智能手机盖板玻璃）缺陷检测的多视角检测框架。每个RGB视角由共享的逐视角专家处理：视觉语言模型（VLM）生成类别感知的语义框，法线参考重建分支提供类别无关的显著性图，两者空间一致性作为证据对语义提案进行重新排序。在产品层面无需跨视角配准即可融合证据记录。实验表明，语义-显著性关联使AP50从52.6%提升到62.6%，跨视角证据召回率从最佳单视角的75.5%提升到88.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反光智能手机盖板玻璃的缺陷检测面临两大挑战：缺陷在特定视角下可见性弱，且视觉证据存在空间模糊性。单一固定视角难以可靠检测，因此需要多视角信息融合来解决弱可见性和空间歧义问题。

Method: 提出多视角检测框架：每个RGB观测由共享的逐视角专家处理，包含两个分支——VLM生成类别感知语义框，法线参考重建分支生成类别无关显著性图。通过两者空间一致性作为支持证据对语义提案重新排序（不修改框坐标），在产品层面无需跨视角配准地结合证据记录。

Result: 在282张生产线图像上，语义-显著性关联将AP50从52.6%提升至62.6%；在94个产品上，跨视角证据召回率R_prod@0.5从最佳单视角的75.5%提升至三视角结合的88.3%。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持语义-显著性交叉验证与多视角光学观测在反光表面检测中具有互补作用，该方法能有效提升反光表面缺陷检测性能。

Abstract: Reflective smartphone cover glass is challenging to inspect from a single fixed viewpoint because defect visibility varies with viewing geometry and specular reflections. This gives rise to two practical challenges: defects may be weakly observable from certain viewpoints, while the available visual evidence may remain spatially ambiguous. To address these issues, we propose a multi-view inspection framework in which each RGB observation is processed by a shared per-view expert. A vision-language model (VLM) produces class-aware semantic boxes, while a normal-reference reconstruction branch provides class-agnostic saliency. Their spatial agreement is used as supporting evidence to rank semantic proposals without modifying their coordinates or treating saliency as ground truth. The resulting evidence records are combined at product level without cross-view registration. On 282 production-line images, semantic-saliency association improves $AP_{50}$ from 52.6% to 62.6% by re-ranking fixed semantic proposals. Across 94 products, cross-view evidence recall $R_{\rm prod}@0.5$ increases from 75.5% for the best single view to 88.3% using all three views. These results support the complementary roles of semantic-saliency cross-verification and additional optical observations in reflective-surface inspection.

</details>


### [242] [From Intent to Evidence: Policy-Steered Multi-Strategy Retrieval for Long-Video Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31005)
*Can Zhang,Baofeng Zhang,Xiaotian Han,Junyuan Shang,Yuchen Ding,Shuohuan Wang,Dianhai Yu,Ruirui Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VESTA是一个免训练的长视频代理，通过意图路由器推断证据获取策略（聚焦、召回或对比检索），并采用"获取-验证-整合"循环来提升长视频问答准确性，在多个基准测试上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频代理对所有问题采用统一的证据获取行为，忽略了证据是集中分布、需要广泛覆盖还是需要区分竞争假设的情况差异，这种粗粒度策略可能导致推理开始之前就已失败。而针对每个问题预设精细的解决流程又限制了自主探索能力。

Method: VESTA采用路由条件的"获取-验证-整合"循环：意图路由器在探索前推断证据获取策略（聚焦、召回或对比检索）和证据记账策略；策略导向的检索生成临时参考，多模态证据操作将其转化为观察结果；推理器可自由验证、利用中间发现重新查询或检查检索集外区域；时间证据账本将观察整合为自适应压缩视图，记录时序位置、来源、覆盖度、冲突、验证结果和假设支持度；最终化优先采用已验证的观察。

Result: 在Video-MME-v2上，VESTA比VideoARM平均准确率提升2.7个百分点，并在全部六项指标上获得提升。在LongVideoBench、EgoSchema和LVBench上（共享查询时模型），LongVideoBench长子集提升6.9个百分点，LVBench提升1.5个百分点，EgoSchema上与VideoARM持平。

Conclusion: VESTA通过自适应证据获取策略和证据账本机制，在保持自主探索能力的同时显著提升了长视频理解任务的性能，验证了"先规划证据获取策略再进行推理"这一范式的有效性。

Abstract: Existing long-video agents acquire evidence through one uniform behavior, ignoring whether the required evidence is concentrated, requires broad occurrence coverage, or must discriminate competing hypotheses---which can cause failure before substantive reasoning begins. Prescribing a fine-grained solution procedure for every question is not a satisfactory remedy, as it restricts autonomous exploration. We propose VESTA, a training-free long-video agent organized as a route-conditioned acquire--verify--consolidate loop. Before exploration, an intent router infers an evidence-acquisition policy---focused, recall, or contrastive retrieval over a shared visual--speech scene index---together with an evidence-accounting policy that configures the evidence view maintained during exploration. Policy-steered retrieval yields provisional references that multimodal evidence operations convert into observations, while the Reasoner remains free to verify them, re-query using intermediate findings, or inspect regions outside the retrieved set. A temporal evidence ledger consolidates observations into an adaptive, compressed view of temporal location, provenance, coverage, conflicts, verification outcomes, and hypothesis support, exposing missing and unresolved evidence to guide subsequent acquisition; finalization prioritizes verified observations. On Video-MME-v2, VESTA improves average accuracy by 2.7 points over VideoARM and gains across all six reported metrics. On LongVideoBench, EgoSchema, and LVBench under shared query-time models, it improves by 6.9 points on the LongVideoBench long subset and 1.5 on LVBench, and matches VideoARM on EgoSchema.

</details>


### [243] [SMG: Semantic Motion Graph for Monocular Dynamic Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31023)
*Haozheng Yu,Xinyu Yang,Rundong Luo,Jennifer J. Sun,Bharath Hariharan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出语义运动图（SMG），通过将高斯运动建模为低秩语义运动来解决单目视频动态高斯泼溅中的过拟合和遮挡问题，在复杂真实场景基准上达到最新最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态高斯泼溅在单目视频场景中缺乏可靠的规则化信号，导致在遮挡或复杂运动区域容易过拟合训练视角，模型泛化能力不足。

Method: 构建语义运动图（SMG）来建模场景的结构化运动，利用语义一致性先验（空间邻近且语义相关的区域具有一致动态）约束高斯运动；高斯运动由SMG节点驱动，并通过可靠节点引导不可靠节点的运动以处理不确定性。

Result: 在多个挑战性真实世界基准上取得了单目动态高斯泼溅的最先进性能，并引入了一个新的ego-exo设置下的多视角数据集用于评估。

Conclusion: 语义运动图通过注入语义一致性和低秩结构先验，有效提升了动态高斯泼溅在单目视频下的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: We study dynamic Gaussian Splatting from monocular videos. While recent advancements in dynamic Gaussian splatting offer a promising foundation for modeling dynamic scenes, they often overfit to the training views and fail under occlusion or complex scene motion due to the lack of reliable regularization signals in under-constrained regions. We propose Semantic Motion Graph (SMG), a novel approach models the Gaussian motion as the low-rank semantic motion. Our key insight is that the real-world scene motion is often structured by semantic coherence: regions that are spatially close and semantically related tend to exhibit consistent dynamics. To leverage this prior, we construct SMG to model structured motion of the scene. The Gaussian motion is driven by the motion of SMG nodes. We further observe that the uncertainty of Gaussian motion arises from both unreliable off-the-shelf priors and weakly constrained regions during optimization. SMG addresses this by using reliable graph nodes to guide the motion of nearby unreliable nodes. To evaluate dynamic Gaussian splatting under challenging real-world scenarios, we introduce a new multiview dataset collected under an ego-exo setup. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SMG achieves state-of-the-art performance on monocular dynamic Gaussian splatting across challenging real-world benchmarks. Project page: https://smg-gaussian.github.io/.

</details>


### [244] [Analytic Dynamics: Learning Physics-Grounded Representation for Fast Intrinsic Dynamics Inference from Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31025)
*Jailing Lin,Jikuan Zhang,Jianhua Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Analytic Dynamics，一种前馈式动力学推断框架，通过引入基于物理的中间动力学表示（位置、位移、形变梯度场），弥合视觉观察与内在动力学之间的鸿沟，实现从单目视频高效、准确且可泛化地推断材料模型和参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从视觉观察推断物体内在动力学对智能体理解物理世界至关重要，但视觉证据与内在动力学之间存在根本性鸿沟。现有方法要么依赖昂贵的逐场景优化（效率低、可扩展性差），要么直接映射视觉证据到内在动力学（缺乏中间物理抽象，容易受外观和几何捷径影响）。

Method: 提出前馈式动力学推断框架 Analytic Dynamics，在视觉观察与内在动力学之间引入物理基础的中间动力学表示。利用模拟中可获得的特权物理状态（位置、位移、形变梯度场），学习结构化动力学表示，并通过将视觉表示与该空间对齐，赋予视觉模型物理基础的归纳偏置，指导材料模型分类和参数回归。同时构建包含成对物理状态轨迹、渲染视频和真实材料模型的动力学数据生成流水线与基准。

Result: 大量实验表明 Analytic Dynamics 能从单目视频中实现高效、准确且可泛化的动力学推断。

Conclusion: 通过在视觉观察与内在动力学之间引入物理基础的中间表示，可以有效缩小两者之间的差距，使视觉模型具备物理归纳偏置，从而在不依赖逐场景优化的前提下实现高效的动力学推断。

Abstract: Inferring object dynamics from visual observations is essential for intelligent agents to reason about and interact with the physical world, yet remains challenging due to the fundamental gap between visual evidence and intrinsic dynamics. Existing methods either rely on costly per-scene optimization, limiting efficiency and scalability, or directly map visual evidence to intrinsic dynamics without intermediate physical abstractions, making them prone to appearance and geometry shortcuts. To bridge this gap, we propose Analytic Dynamics, a feed-forward dynamics inference framework that introduces an intermediate physics-grounded dynamics representation between visual observations and intrinsic dynamics. Specifically, we leverage privileged physical states, including position, displacement, and deformation gradient fields, which are available in simulation, to learn a structured dynamics representation that is difficult to discover from visual observations alone. By aligning visual representations with this space, we equip visual models with a physics-grounded inductive bias, guiding them to capture dynamics-relevant patterns for material model classification and parameter regression. To facilitate this research, we develop a dynamics data generation pipeline and benchmark containing paired physical state trajectories, rendered videos, and ground-truth material models and parameters. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Analytic Dynamics achieves efficient, accurate, and generalizable dynamics inference from monocular videos.

</details>


### [245] [Driving on Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31029)
*Christian Löwens,Thorben Funke,Alexandru Paul Condurache*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过将端到端自动驾驶模型的摄像头输入替换为先前同地点驾驶的记忆，发现NAVSIM基准测试中仅凭记忆即可达到甚至超越领先端到端方法的性能，表明该基准的高分并不需要模型真正感知当前交通场景；而Bench2Drive和RealEngine基准上记忆驱动则会导致显著性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动驾驶基准（如NAVSIM、Bench2Drive）用模拟指标评估模型的安全与合规驾驶能力，但高基准分数是否真正反映模型对动态场景的理解和反应尚不明确。为了探究这一问题，作者提出将摄像头输入替换为同地点先前驾驶的记忆，检验模型是否真的需要感知当前交通状态。

Method: 作者设计了一种"记忆驱动"（memory-driven）方法：移除模型的摄像头输入，用同一地理位置先前驾驶的检索记忆替代。这些记忆提供持久性场景信息（如道路布局、位置相关的规律），但不包含当前交通状态。通过比较记忆驱动与正常输入的端到端模型在多个基准（NAVSIM、Bench2Drive、RealEngine）上的性能差异来评估。

Result: 在NAVSIM上，仅靠记忆驱动的模型性能接近甚至超过领先的端到端方法，表明NAVSIM高分并不需要模型对当前交通场景做出反应。但在Bench2Drive和RealEngine上，记忆驱动导致大幅性能下降，说明这一现象具有基准依赖性。

Conclusion: NAVSIM基准的评分存在严重缺陷——高分可能来自位置相关的静态记忆而非对动态场景的实时感知。研究警示对该基准结果应持谨慎态度，并强调需要更全面的基准设计来真正评估模型对动态交通场景的理解能力。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving models plan future trajectories from raw sensor input. While earlier driving benchmarks often measured deviation from the human trajectory, current benchmarks such as NAVSIM and Bench2Drive evaluate models with richer simulation-based metrics intended to capture safe and compliant driving. A high benchmark score should reflect that a model can understand the scene in front of it and act accordingly. But how much of that score specifically comes from reacting to the dynamic part of that scene?
  To probe this, we remove a model's camera input and replace it with memories from prior drives at the same location. The retrieved memories can provide persistent scene information, including road layout and location-conditioned regularities, but not the current traffic state. Surprisingly, memory is nearly sufficient on NAVSIM, reaching or even exceeding the performance of leading end-to-end methods without actually observing the evaluated scene. Our results suggest that a high NAVSIM score does not require a planner to react to the current traffic scene and should be treated with caution. This effect is benchmark-dependent: driving from memory causes substantially larger performance drops on Bench2Drive and RealEngine. We provide our code at https://github.com/boschresearch/MemoryDrivoR .

</details>


### [246] [FaceSnap: Real-Time Personalized Lightstage Facial Performance Capture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31033)
*Rukhshanda Hussain,Noé Artru,Emeline Got,Luiz Gustavo Hafemann,Alexandre Messier,Brandon Dearlove,Rafael M. O. Cruz,Abdallah Dib,Eric Granger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FaceSnap是一个端到端框架，通过两阶段方法将光场面部捕捉从昂贵的多视角优化简化为单目实时捕捉。首先通过一次性多视角优化构建个性化模型（编码几何和表情相关外观），然后仅需单个单目光场摄像头即可实现83fps的高保真实时面部性能捕捉，同时联合估计几何和动态4K纹理。文中还引入了Multi4D公共基准用于评估光场环境中的4D面部重建方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统光场面部捕捉虽然能产生生产级质量数字人，但资源密集、劳动密集，多摄像头设置、数小时计算和大规模数据存储成为迭代工作流的瓶颈。

Method: 采用两阶段方法：(1) 从范围运动序列进行一次性多视角优化，构建编码几何和表情相关外观的个性化模型；(2) 利用该模型从单个单目光场摄像头实现实时捕捉。关键创新是使用个性化的残差上采样器恢复主体特定的高频细节，以生成4K动态纹理。

Result: FaceSnap在83fps下联合估计几何和动态4K纹理；几何精度与完整逐帧多视角优化相当，优于在制片质量3D数据上训练的feed-forward方法，且仅需单一摄像头视角。

Conclusion: FaceSnap证明了通过个性化模型和残差上采样的组合，可以在单目设置下实现与多视角优化相当的质量，同时大幅提高效率。Multi4D基准为4D面部重建提供了拓扑不变的几何比较标准。

Abstract: Lightstage facial capture produces production-quality digital humans, but it is resource and labor-intensive. Multi-camera setups, hours of computation, and massive data storage create bottlenecks that hinder iterative workflows. This paper introduces FaceSnap, an end-to-end framework that streamlines capture via a two-stage approach. First, a one-time multi-view optimization from a range-of-motion sequence builds a personalized model encoding both geometry and expression-dependent appearance. This model then enables high-fidelity real-time facial performance capture from a single monocular lightstage camera, with no further multi-view capture required. FaceSnap jointly estimates geometry and dynamic 4K texture at 83 fps. The 4K texture is produced by a novel personalized residual upscaler that recovers subject-specific high-frequency detail, which generic upscalers fail to capture. FaceSnap achieves geometric accuracy competitive with full per-frame multi-view optimization while outperforming feed-forward methods trained on production-quality 3D data, all from a single camera view. Finally, we introduce Multi4D, a public benchmark for evaluating 4D facial reconstruction methods in lightstage environments, enabling topology-invariant geometric comparison across methods.

</details>


### [247] [Segmentation of Bovid Dentition Under Imperfect Annotations: A Comparative Study of Convolutional and Attention Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31052)
*Keith G. Mills,Evan B. Sanders,Gregory J. Matthews,Juliet K. Brophy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对多种语义分割架构（从卷积骨干网络到视觉Transformer）在B.O.V.I.D.牛科动物牙齿照片数据集上进行对比研究，该数据集的手工分割掩膜并非为机器学习训练设计。研究发现预处理和对齐技术对Dice和mIoU等定量指标影响有限，但对预测掩膜的质量有显著改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义分割DNN依赖专家设计的分割掩膜进行训练，但现实中获取的掩膜往往不完美或不对齐，会干扰模型的学习效果。作者希望在非理想标注条件下评估不同分割架构的表现，并探索预处理手段是否能缓解标注缺陷带来的问题。

Method: 在B.O.V.I.D.数据集（高分辨率牛科动物牙齿照片及其手工分割掩膜）上，对从卷积骨干网络到视觉Transformer的多种分割架构进行对比实验，并评估一系列预处理和对齐技术以缓解标注不完美带来的影响。

Result: 预处理和对齐方法对Dice分数和mIoU等定量指标的提升有限，但对预测掩膜的定性效果（如边缘质量、区域一致性）有显著改善。

Conclusion: 在非ML设计的标注数据集上，定量指标可能无法充分反映预处理技术的价值，定性评估对于衡量分割模型的实际表现至关重要。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation decomposes an image into distinct mask regions corresponding to different object categories, such as people, cars, signs or buildings. Advances in machine learning (ML) have shifted this task away from traditional rule-based heuristics such as edge detection, towards deep neural networks (DNN) that learn to classify pixels directly. However, semantic segmentation DNNs crucially depend on expertly designed mask targets to learn from, and imperfect or misaligned masks can interfere with a model's ability to learn effectively.
  This paper presents a comparative study of segmentation architectures, ranging from convolutional backbones to vision transformers, applied to the B.O.V.I.D. dataset, a corpus of high-resolution bovid dental photographs paired with hand-made segmentation masks not originally designed for ML-based training. We evaluate a range of preprocessing and alignment techniques to mitigate the resulting label imperfections. We find that while these preprocessing choices have limited effect on quantitative metrics such as Dice score and mIoU, their qualitative impact on predicted masks is substantial.

</details>


### [248] [Identity-Conditioned Latent Consistency Distillation for Face Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31053)
*Tiago Kienen Chaves,Bernardo Biesseck,David Menotti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出通过潜在一致性模型（Latent Consistency Model）对扩散模型Arc2Face进行任务特定的蒸馏，将身份条件人脸合成速度提升4.36倍（从2.102秒降至0.4819秒/张），同时通过FID分数验证生成质量与教师模型基本持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在高保真人脸合成中表现出色，但迭代采样过程计算开销巨大，难以满足大规模合成人脸数据集（需要大量身份、多种姿态/表情/年龄）在面部识别任务中的应用需求。作者希望在不牺牲图像质量的前提下大幅加速身份条件人脸生成。

Method: 将Arc2Face（教师模型）的文本到图像（text-to-image）流程改造为嵌入到人脸（embedding-to-face）设置，用ArcFace身份嵌入替代文本提示。然后通过知识蒸馏，将教师模型的知识提炼到潜在一致性模型（学生模型）中，使学生模型能以极少迭代步骤生成高质量的身份条件人脸图像。

Result: 学生模型的平均推理时间从2.102秒/张降至0.4819秒/张，实现4.36倍加速。在FID评估中：CelebA上接近教师（13.921 vs 12.928），WebFace42M上甚至超越教师（9.317 vs 9.802），Synth-500上有适度性能差距，AgeDB上结果相当。

Conclusion: 任务特定的潜在一致性蒸馏可以有效加速Arc2Face，在保持高图像质量的同时实现大规模合成人脸生成的高效推理，代码已开源。

Abstract: Diffusion models have achieved strong results in high-fidelity image synthesis, but their iterative sampling process makes large-scale generation computationally expensive. This limitation is especially relevant when generating synthetic face datasets for face recognition, where a large number of subjects with many samples in different poses, expressions, ages, etc., are required. In this work, we show that identity-conditioned face synthesis can be performed at a substantially lower computational cost by a latent Consistency Model with few iterations, without compromising image quality. For training, we distill knowledge from the foundation Diffusion Model Arc2Face (teacher) by adapting its original text-to-image pipeline to an embedding-to-face setting, replacing textual prompts with ArcFace identity embeddings. Our distilled model (student) generates identity-conditioned face images with an average inference time of 0.4819 seconds per image, compared with 2.102 seconds for Arc2Face, resulting in a 4.36$\times$ speed-up. Quantitative results, based on FID scores, show that the distilled model remains competitive with Arc2Face across all evaluation protocols. On 100k generated images, it achieves near-parity on CelebA (13.921 vs. 12.928) and outperforms the teacher on WebFace42M (9.317 vs. 9.802). Further evaluations on Synth-500 and AgeDB show a moderate performance gap for the former but comparable results for the latter. These results indicate that Arc2Face can be accelerated through task-specific latent consistency distillation while preserving high image quality for large-scale synthetic face generation. Our proposal is publicly available at https://github.com/UFPR-IPASP-PR/FaceRec-IdentityConsistency.

</details>


### [249] [Multimodal Shared Latent Representation of Narration, Microscope and iOCT Images for Phase Recognition in Vitreoretinal Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31065)
*Onur Izmitlioglu,Shervin Dehghani,Tarek Ghannoum,Benedikt Schworm,Nassir Navab*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，以显微镜视图为共享锚点，联合建模手术叙述、术中OCT（iOCT）和显微镜视图三种模态，用于玻璃体视网膜手术的阶段识别。通过对比学习将合成域中的结构先验迁移到缺乏iOCT的真实视频中，并利用双头MS-TCN++进行宏观和微观阶段预测，在真实手术数据上从零样本基线F1 0.38提升至0.53。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外科手术阶段识别对于眼外科手术中的上下文感知计算机辅助反馈至关重要，但同步的多模态术中数据（显微镜视图和iOCT）稀缺，限制了模仿外科医生自然多模态整合的方法。手术叙述在线资源丰富可提供语义监督，但以往工作仅探索两两对比学习（如iOCT-显微镜或显微镜-叙述），缺乏三者联合建模的研究。

Method: 以显微镜视图作为共享锚点桥接手术叙述和iOCT，利用真实的显微镜-叙述视频和合成的显微镜-iOCT同步数据对进行对比学习对齐。通过对比对齐将合成域的结构先验迁移到缺乏iOCT的真实视频中，再使用双头MS-TCN++整合嵌入表示，实现宏观（手术阶段）和微观（器械-组织交互）阶段的同时预测。

Result: 在真实玻璃体视网膜手术评估中，宏观阶段识别从零样本基线的平均F1值0.38提升至0.53；微观阶段估计在合成数据上获得定量验证，并在真实手术中定性展示效果。

Conclusion: 这是首个将显微镜视图、iOCT B-scan和手术叙述统一到共享潜在空间的工作，为玻璃体视网膜手术阶段识别提供了无需完全同步三模态数据集的可行方案，并为难以直接观测的精细器械-组织测量提供了探索性估计路径。

Abstract: Surgical phase recognition is key to context-aware computer-assisted feedback in vitreoretinal procedures, yet the scarcity of synchronized multimodal intraoperative data, particularly microscope views and intraoperative OCT, limits approaches that aim to replicate the multimodal integration surgeons perform naturally. Surgical narration, by contrast, is abundantly available online and offers rich semantic supervision. Prior work has mainly explored pairwise contrastive learning (e.g., intraoperative OCT-microscope or microscope-narration), leaving the joint modeling of all three modalities largely unexplored. We introduce a framework that uses microscope views as a shared anchor to bridge surgical narrations and intraoperative OCT (iOCT) without requiring a fully synchronized tri-modal dataset, leveraging real microscope-narration videos and a synthetic dataset of synchronized microscope video and tool-aligned iOCT pairs. Contrastive alignment transfers structural priors from the synthetic domain to real videos lacking iOCT, and a dual-head MS-TCN++ integrates the resulting embeddings for joint macro- and micro-phase prediction. Evaluated on real vitreoretinal surgeries, our framework improves macro-phase recognition over a zero-shot baseline (mean F1 0.38 to 0.53) and provides an exploratory route to estimating fine-grained instrument-tissue measurements that are not directly observable in real microscope video alone; these micro-phase estimates are validated quantitatively on synthetic data and shown only qualitatively on real surgery. To our knowledge, this is the first work to unify microscope view, iOCT B-scans, and surgical narrations in a shared latent space for surgical phase recognition.

</details>


### [250] [LISynSeg: Data-Centric Label-to-Image Synthesis for Cross-Modality Whole-Heart Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31073)
*Jiacheng Wang,Ivana Isgum,Ipek Oguz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LISynSeg，一种数据驱动的全心脏分割方法，通过标签到图像的合成增强nnU-Net训练。在CARE基准上，校准的真实-合成混合训练在保持架构不变的情况下改善了跨模态（CT/MRI）分割性能，且对MRI的提升更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CT和MRI中的全心脏分割面临采集域偏移和心脏标注异质性的挑战，现有方法多依赖架构设计、迁移学习和通用数据增强，但鲜有系统研究数据增强和训练监督策略对跨模态分割的影响。

Method: LISynSeg采用数据中心的策略：在真实图像nnU-Net训练中加入标签到图像的合成。合成体积由心脏标签图经对比度和采集扰动（校准自训练队列）生成，并与真实图像混合以保留标签中缺失的胸腔上下文。通过心肌壁厚度的受控变化和不确定血管端点的部分监督来建模心脏标签变异。

Result: 在CARE全心脏基准上，纯合成训练性能劣于真实图像nnU-Net基线；而校准的真实-合成混合训练在保持架构不变的情况下改善了跨模态分割，其中MRI的改进大于CT。

Conclusion: 修改训练数据策略能够为异质性心脏数据的模型开发带来收益，验证了数据增强和训练监督调整的潜力。

Abstract: Whole-heart segmentation (WHS) in computed tomography (CT) and magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) is affected by acquisition shifts and heterogeneous cardiac annotations. Existing WHS systems combine architectural design, transfer learning, and generic spatial or intensity augmentation. We investigate whether changes to data augmentation and training supervision can improve cross-modality WHS while the segmentation architecture is held constant. We present LISynSeg, a data-centric approach that augments real-image nnU-Net training with label-to-image synthesis. Synthetic volumes are generated from cardiac label maps using contrast and acquisition perturbations calibrated to the training cohort, then mixed with real images to retain thoracic context absent from the labels (and thus the synthesized images). We model cardiac label variation through controlled changes in myocardial wall thickness and partial supervision of uncertain vessel endpoints. On the CARE Whole-Heart benchmark, synthetic-only training performs worse than the real-image nnU-Net baseline, whereas calibrated real-synthetic training improves cross-modality segmentation without changing the architecture; the improvement is larger for MRI than for CT. The results show that modifying the training data strategy can benefit model development for heterogeneous cardiac data. Code and trained weights will be released at https://github.com/MedICL-VU/Care26_LISynSeg.

</details>


### [251] [Real-Time Video Anomaly Detection Using YOLO Pose Estimation and CLIP-Based Semantic Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31074)
*Vanodhya G. Warnasooriya,Amir Hajian,Watchara Ruangsang,Supavadee Aramvith*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种轻量级两阶段实时视频异常检测框架：第一阶段用YOLO v11n-pose同时检测人物并提取17个骨骼关键点，第二阶段用CLIP ViT-B/32对裁剪的人物区域编码，并与预定义的异常行为文本描述计算余弦相似度。在CUHK Avenue、上海Tech Campus及自定义室内数据集上实现了约51 FPS的吞吐量，较多特征基线有3.36倍加速，帧级AUROC分别为89.26%、70.26%和84.13%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频异常检测方法通常依赖光流、独立姿态估计器和基于密度的评分模块，计算开销大，难以满足实时检测需求。作者旨在通过轻量化架构消除这些重型组件，实现高效实时的异常检测。

Method: 采用两阶段框架：第一阶段使用YOLO v11n-pose在单次前向传播中同时完成人物检测与17个骨骼关键点提取；第二阶段将裁剪的人物区域送入CLIP ViT-B/32进行视觉编码，并与预定义的异常行为文本描述计算余弦相似度作为异常评分。

Result: 在NVIDIA Titan XP GPU上实现了约51 FPS的端到端吞吐量，较多特征基线获得3.36倍加速；帧级AUROC在CUHK Avenue、ShanghaiTech Campus和自定义室内数据集上分别为89.26%、70.26%和84.13%。

Conclusion: 该框架证明了无需光流、独立姿态估计器和密度评分模块即可实现实时视频异常检测，在速度和精度之间取得了良好平衡，尤其适用于实际场景中的实时部署。

Abstract: We propose a lightweight two-stage framework for real-time video anomaly detection. The first stage employs YOLO v11n-pose to detect persons and extract seventeen skeletal keypoints in a single forward pass. The second stage encodes each cropped person region through CLIP ViT-B/32 and computes cosine similarity against predefined textual descriptions of anomalous behaviors. This architecture eliminates the need for optical flow, standalone pose estimators, and density-based scoring modules. Experiments on CUHK Avenue, ShanghaiTech Campus, and a custom indoor dataset collected at Chulalongkorn University demonstrate an end-to-end throughput of approximately 51 FPS on an NVIDIA Titan XP GPU, a 3.36x speedup over the multi-feature baseline, while maintaining frame-level AUROC values of 89.26%, 70.26%, and 84.13%, respectively.

</details>


### [252] [Robust retinal biometrics for patient identity verification and retrieval across age and imaging devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31094)
*Jose D. Vargas-Quiros,Dennis Bontempi,Jeroen Vermeulen,Bart Liefers,Sven Bergmann,Caroline C. W. Klaver*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于视网膜眼底图像的生物识别系统，通过在227,004张图像上训练ConvNeXtV2骨干网络与ArcFace和三元组损失的度量学习编码器，实现对患者身份的验证与检索。在Rotterdam Study、UK Biobank和AREDS三个数据集上，验证AUROC均达0.9997以上，检索Recall@1最高达99.7%，证明视网膜解剖结构是可靠的长期生物特征信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者身份错误会破坏纵向医疗记录和研究数据库的完整性，进而影响下游临床决策。现有身份验证方法在医学图像领域缺乏长时间跨度、多设备环境下的可靠验证，需要一种稳健的生物特征识别方法确保纵向影像记录的准确性。

Method: 构建512维度量学习编码器，采用ConvNeXtV2作为骨干网络，结合ArcFace损失和三元组损失联合训练。训练数据来自Rotterdam Study的227,004张彩色眼底图像（21,851个患者-眼睛身份），覆盖多种成像设备和长达32.6年的随访。评估阶段先用模型筛查身份不一致的疑似图像并人工裁定修正，随后在去除近似重复图像后进行回顾性验证和身份检索测试。

Result: 身份验证AUROC在三个数据集中分别达到0.9998、0.9997和0.9998；身份检索Recall@1分别为99.7%、97.2%和97.6%，Top-5命中率至少98.6%。模型筛查发现Rotterdam Study中0.588%、UK Biobank中0.259%、AREDS中0.164%的图像存在错误身份标注。性能在跨设备和长期随访中保持稳健，主要失败原因为低图像质量和不一致的视网膜视野。

Conclusion: 视网膜眼底解剖结构是一种持久稳定的生物特征信号，可用于保障纵向影像记录的身份完整性。该系统在大型多中心数据集上展现出极高的验证与检索性能，具有实际用于临床数据质量保障和身份安全防护的潜力。

Abstract: Patient identity errors can compromise longitudinal medical records, research databases, and downstream clinical decisions. We present a retinal biometric system for verifying claimed identities and retrieving the correct identity from color fundus images. We trained a 512-dimensional metric-learning encoder combining a ConvNeXtV2 backbone with ArcFace and triplet losses on 227,004 images from 21,851 patient-eye identities in the Rotterdam Study, spanning multiple imaging devices and up to 32.6 years of follow-up. The system was evaluated on held-out Rotterdam Study data and externally on the UK Biobank and Age-Related Eye Disease Study (AREDS). Before evaluation, we used the model to screen for identity inconsistencies and manually adjudicated flagged images, identifying incorrect assignments in 0.588% of Rotterdam Study images, 0.259% of UK Biobank images, and 0.164% of AREDS images. In retrospective-only verification after removing near-duplicate images, the system achieved AUROCs of 0.9998, 0.9997, and 0.9998 in the Rotterdam Study, UK Biobank, and AREDS, respectively. For identity retrieval using only previously acquired images, Recall@1 was 99.7%, 97.2%, and 97.6%, respectively, from galleries averaging 4436-8510 identities; the correct identity appeared among the top five results in at least 98.6% of cases. Performance remained robust across imaging devices and long follow-up intervals, while lower image quality and inconsistent retinal fields accounted for most failures. These findings establish retinal anatomy as a durable biometric signal, useful for safeguarding the integrity of longitudinal imaging records.

</details>


### [253] [One Adapter, Many Tasks: Task-Conditioned Feature Transformations for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31096)
*Yunxiang Fu,Meng Lou,Yizhou Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FACET是一种用于类增量学习（CIL）的新方法，通过任务条件特征变换和条件特征一致性损失，在仅使用单个共享适配器的情况下，既能高效利用参数，又能产生高判别性的特征，在长任务序列（200个任务）和短任务序列（20个任务）上都取得了优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CIL方法存在两难困境：任务特定适配器虽然学习显式每任务表示但参数和计算效率低；而基于LoRA合并的方法将每任务参数合并为单一模型，其静态聚合权重在推理时会导致表示干扰。作者希望解决这些问题，实现参数高效且特征判别性强的CIL方案。

Method: FACET包含两个核心组件：(1) 动态任务条件特征变换——在持续训练时学习单个共享适配器，通过任务条件动态变换特征，将适配器的整体特征分布塑造成"重叠减少的任务特定组件混合分布"；(2) 高效的无重放任务条件特征一致性损失——用于缓解适配器特征空间中已学习混合分布的灾难性遗忘问题。

Result: FACET在超长任务序列（如200个任务）和标准短任务序列（如20个任务）上都取得了优越性能，同时使用的可训练参数和GFLOPs显著更少，展示了仅维护单个适配器时的强大可扩展性。

Conclusion: FACET通过动态任务条件特征变换和任务条件特征一致性损失，在保持单个共享适配器（参数高效）的同时，有效解决了表示干扰和灾难性遗忘问题，在各类任务序列长度下均展现出优越性能和可扩展性。

Abstract: Class-incremental learning (CIL) requires a model to incrementally learn tasks that contain new classes without accessing earlier training data while preserving the ability to recognize all seen classes. Recently, pretrained-model-based approaches have become prevalent by adapting a frozen backbone with additional lightweight trainable modules. Existing methods, however, exhibit limitations: task-specific adapters learn explicit per-task representations but are parameter- and computation-inefficient, while LoRA-based merging methods combine per-task LoRA parameters into a single model whose static aggregated weights cause representation interference during inference. To address these problems, we present \textbf{FACET}: task-conditioned \textbf{F}e\textbf{A}ture transformation with \textbf{C}ondition\textbf{E}d feature consis\textbf{T}ency, achieving excellent parameter efficiency while producing highly discriminative features during inference. When continually trained on a task sequence, FACET learns a single shared adapter that employs a dynamic task-conditioned feature transformation, shaping the overall feature distribution of the adapter into a mixture of overlap-reduced task-specific components. On the other hand, we propose an efficient replay-free task-conditioned feature consistency loss, aiming to mitigate catastrophic forgetting of the learned mixture distribution in the adapter's feature space. Even when maintaining only a single adapter, FACET demonstrates robust scalability. On both very long task sequences (e.g., 200 tasks) and standard short task sequences (e.g., 20 tasks), our method achieves superior performance while using significantly fewer trainable parameters and GFLOPs. The code will be made open source upon acceptance.

</details>


### [254] [DreamX-Creator: Democratizing Native Audio-Video Generation at 2K Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31106)
*Jiashu Zhu,Yanhao Zheng,Ruitian Tian,Rujing Dang,Shen Zhang,Bingze Song,Jiachen Lei,Ruimin Lin,Jiahong Wu,Xiangxiang Chu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DreamX-Creator 1.0是一个紧凑的原生联合音视频生成系统，基于7B生成器，通过门控跨模态注意力实现音视频流的联合去噪，并采用渐进式联合训练、音视频强化学习及自回归1步2K精炼流水线，实现了与最先进开源系统竞争的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成器通常忽略音频或将其作为独立阶段合成，限制了视觉动态与声学事件之间的交互建模能力。作者旨在构建一个原生联合音视频生成系统，实现音视频的同步生成与互惠建模。

Method: 构建以7B生成器为核心的紧凑系统：1) 网络前半部分独立处理音视频流，后半部分通过门控跨模态注意力（Gated Cross-Modal Attention）耦合；2) 设计统一的音视频数据系统，构建并筛选时间连贯片段、生成结构化多模态标注并组织能力导向数据池；3) 采用渐进式联合训练，包含两个音视频预训练阶段和高品质微调；4) 引入音视频强化学习，通过模态感知多模态反馈进行后训练；5) 自回归1步2K精炼流水线，将双向多步教师蒸馏为单步学生。

Result: DreamX-Creator 1.0实现了原生同步的音视频生成，性能与最先进的开源系统相当。系统开源了紧凑的7B生成器和2K精炼器。

Conclusion: 通过发布紧凑的7B生成器和2K精炼器，作者旨在民主化原生音视频生成，为统一音视频生成建模的未来研究提供可访问的基础。

Abstract: Recent video generators often omit audio or synthesize it in a separate stage, limiting reciprocal modeling of visual dynamics and acoustic events. We present DreamX-Creator 1.0, a compact native joint audio-video generation system centered on a 7B generator. Conditioned on a first frame and a text prompt, the generator jointly denoises modality-specialized audio and video streams. The streams are processed independently in the first half of the network and coupled in the latter half through Gated Cross-Modal Attention, whose token- and head-wise output gates modulate each active cross-modal attention-head output. A unified Audio-Video Data System constructs and filters temporally coherent clips, produces structured multimodal annotations, and organizes clips into capability-oriented data pools. Progressive Joint Training comprises two audio-video pre-training stages followed by High-Quality Finetuning. Audio-Video Reinforcement Learning further post-trains the generator with Modality-Aware Multimodal Feedback that routes video-, audio-, and cross-modal feedback to the corresponding streams. For high-resolution output, our Autoregressive 1-Step 2K Refinement pipeline adapts a bidirectional multi-step teacher into an autoregressive multi-step refiner and distills it into a student requiring one denoising evaluation per temporal chunk. Overall, DreamX-Creator 1.0 achieves native, synchronized audio-video generation with performance competitive with state-of-the-art open-source systems. By releasing our compact 7B generator and 2K Refiner, we seek to democratize native audio-video generation and provide an accessible foundation for future research in unified audio-video generative modeling.

</details>


### [255] [VeriCam: A Verification Baseline for the Classification of Unknown Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31107)
*Lucas Wojcik,Gabriel E. Lima,Sergio M. Silva,Eduil Nascimento,David Menotti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VeriCam管道，利用验证任务训练的模型构建关系图，通过图聚类实现未知类别的零样本细粒度分类，在真实交通监控数据集LPLCv2上验证，跨设备场景F1得分93.45，V-Measure得分80.13。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型虽具备强大的零样本泛化能力，但在需要基于微小细节进行细粒度类别区分的真实任务中，图像、文本及视觉-文本混合基础模型都缺乏足够的表征能力，现有文献存在空白。

Method: VeriCam利用面向验证任务（判断图像对是否属于同一类别）训练的模型来构建细粒度特征空间，训练模型区分同类与异类图像对，构建表示数据点间类别关系的关系图，提出朴素图聚类算法和Leiden图聚类算法两种聚类方案。

Result: 在LPLCv2真实交通监控数据集上验证，识别出数据集存在固有的采集设备偏差，通过无标签方法动态识别设备以构建公正基准。跨设备场景下验证基线F1得分93.45，聚类步骤V-Measure得分80.13。

Conclusion: VeriCam通过验证学习与图聚类的组合有效解决了基础模型在细粒度零样本分类上的不足，同时揭示了交通监控数据集的设备偏差问题并提供了应对方案，代码已公开。

Abstract: The advent of foundation models have enabled a new era in zero-shot classification. Yet, key challenges persist. Despite their impressive generalization power that leverages the immense pre-training knowledge, both foundation models for image and text as well as vision-text hybrids lack the representational power needed for fine-grained, minutiae-based class separation that some real-world tasks require. To address the current gaps in the literature, we propose VeriCam, a pipeline designed to learn highly specialized features that enable classification of unknown classes in unseen data. VeriCam works by leveraging the representation power of image models trained for the verification task, where the model develops an intricate feature space that incorporates fine-grained details. By training a model to discriminate between pairs of images from the same and different classes, a relational graph is constructed, representing the class relationships between data points. We then present two approaches for graph clustering: a naive algorithm and a specific setup for the Leiden graph clustering algorithm. The pipeline is validated on the LPLCv2 dataset, which comprises real-world traffic surveillance images. We show that the dataset carries an inherent capture device bias that is posed as a generalization challenge for downstream License Plate recognition tasks such as OCR. As such, we dynamically identify capture devices with a label-agnostic approach, enabling the construction of a fair and unbiased benchmark. In the cross-device scenario, our pipeline reaches an F1-Score of 93.45 in the verification baseline and a V-Measure score of 80.13 in the clustering step. All code is publicly available at https://github.com/lmlwojcik/VeriCam

</details>


### [256] [BLARM: Animating 3D Objects from Video via Blending Latent Rigid Motion Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31113)
*Pradyumn Goyal,Yizhak Ben-Shabat,Hsueh-Ti Derek Liu,Haomiao Jiang,Snehasish Mukherjee,Kyle Spence,Mark Stauber,Evangelos Kalogerakis,Yunze Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BLARM是一种前馈方法，用于从单目视频驱动静态3D网格动画。它通过学习稀疏的时变刚性运动分量和时不变的顶点-分量蒙皮权重，实现紧凑且时间一致的网格动画，无需显式骨架或绑定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视频驱动3D网格动画方法要么依赖显式骨架/绑定等注释，要么直接回归高维顶点运动，导致变形空间复杂、计算成本高且难以泛化。

Method: BLARM将动画表示为紧凑的时变刚性运动分量与时不变顶点-分量蒙皮权重的组合；通过分离的空间-时间注意力将几何派生的变形潜变量与视频特征进行条件化，再解码由预测蒙皮权重混合的刚性变换；训练采用轨迹重建、熵正则化和运动感知对比学习。

Result: BLARM能够从单目视频中生成准确且时间稳定的动画，同时恢复紧凑、可解释的运动结构。

Conclusion: BLARM实现了无需骨架/绑定/蒙皮权重/装备注释的低维变形空间，通过紧凑可解释的运动表征从单目视频中生成高质量且时间一致的3D网格动画。

Abstract: We introduce BLARM, a feed-forward method for video-driven 3D mesh animation. Given a monocular video and a static object mesh, BLARM predicts a temporally coherent animated mesh whose motion follows the video. Rather than relying on explicit rigs or directly regressing high-dimensional vertex motion, we represent animation using a compact set of learned, time-varying rigid motion components and time-invariant vertex-to-component skinning weights. This yields a low-dimensional deformation space without requiring skeletons, cages, skinning weights, or rig annotations. Our architecture conditions geometry-derived deformation latents on video features through factorized spatial-temporal attention, then decodes rigid transformations blended by predicted skinning weights. Trained with trajectory reconstruction, entropy regularization, and motion-aware contrastive learning, BLARM produces accurate and temporally stable animations while recovering compact, interpretable motion structure from monocular video.

</details>


### [257] [BRF-GS: Hyperspectral Bidirectional Reflectance Factor Modeling and Image Generation Based on 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31159)
*Yiling Yao,Wenjuan Zhang,Bowen Wang,Bocheng Li,Wentao Song,Bing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BRF-GS，一种基于3D高斯泼溅(3DGS)的框架，用于双向反射因子(BRF)建模和多角度高光谱反射率图像生成。该框架引入混合BRDF驱动核、选取几何可靠光谱波段进行3D场景初始化，并采用两阶段训练策略解耦几何优化与光谱建模，同时构建了包含三个场景的AIR-BRF数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有三维辐射传输模型需要复杂的场景构建和高计算量的求解器，难以高效生成多角度高光谱反射率图像；而3DGS虽提供高效的神经场景表示，但其低阶球谐函数不足以表达复杂方向反射，且高光谱数据的高维性和波段间质量差异带来额外挑战。

Method: BRF-GS框架包含三个关键设计：(1) 混合BRDF驱动的核函数以表征复杂方向反射特性；(2) 选取几何可靠的光谱波段进行鲁棒的3D场景初始化；(3) 两阶段训练策略，将几何优化与光谱建模解耦。同时构建了AIR-BRF多角度高光谱方向反射数据集。

Result: 实验表明BRF-GS在空间和光谱保真度上均表现优异，能够准确再现具有特征的视角相关BRF响应。

Conclusion: 该框架为遥感场景中的BRF建模和多角度高光谱反射率图像生成提供了一种高效的数据驱动方法。

Abstract: The bidirectional reflectance factor (BRF) characterizes the directional radiative properties of terrestrial surfaces. However, existing three-dimensional (3D) radiative transfer models require complex scene construction and computationally intensive radiative transfer solvers, limiting efficient generation of multi-angle hyperspectral reflectance imagery. 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) offers an efficient framework for neural scene representation and novel view synthesis, but its low-order spherical harmonics representation is insufficient for complex directional reflectance, while the high dimensionality and inter-band quality differences of hyperspectral data introduce additional challenges. To address these challenges, we propose BRF-GS, a 3DGS-based framework for BRF modeling and hyperspectral reflectance image generation. BRF-GS introduces a hybrid BRDF-driven kernel to represent complex directional reflectance, selects geometry-reliable spectral bands for robust 3D scene initialization, and adopts a two-stage training strategy that decouples geometry optimization from spectral modeling. We further construct the AIR-BRF dataset, a multi-angle hyperspectral directional reflectance dataset comprising three scenes with diverse natural and artificial targets. Experiments demonstrate that BRF-GS achieves superior spatial and spectral fidelity and accurately reproduces characteristic view-dependent BRF responses. The proposed framework provides an efficient data-driven approach for BRF modeling and multi-angle hyperspectral reflectance image generation in remote sensing scenes.

</details>
